# Rashi on Proverbs

רש"י על משלי

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Writings

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 1170, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 1158)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:1:1)


**מִשְׁלֵי.** כָּל דְּבָרָיו דֻּגְמוֹת וּמְשָׁלִים; מְשַׁל הַתּוֹרָה בְּאִשָּׁה טוֹבָה, וּמְשַׁל הָעוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים בְּאִשָּׁה זוֹנָה:


**The proverbs of** All his words are illustrations and allegories. He compared the Torah to a good woman, and he compared idolatry to a harlot.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:2:1)


**לָדַעַת חָכְמָה וּמוּסָר.** הַמְּשָׁלִים הָאֵלּוּ אָמַר לְהוֹדִיעַ לַבְּרִיּוֹת **חָכְמָה וּמוּסָר** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֲמֵלִים בַּתּוֹרָה, שֶׁהִיא חָכְמָה וּמוּסָר וּבִינָה:


**To know wisdom and discipline** He stated these proverbs to make known to the people [wisdom and discipline], that they toil in the Torah, which is wisdom, discipline and understanding.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:3:1)


**צֶדֶק וּמִשְׁפָּט וּמֵישָׁרִים.** צֶדֶק, צְדָקָה מִמָּמוֹנוֹ. וּמִשְׁפָּט, לִשְׁפּוֹט אֱמֶת. וּמֵישָׁרִים, הִיא הַפְּשָׁרָה דֶּרֶךְ חָלָק וּמִישׁוֹר שָׁוֶה לָזֶה וְלָזֶה:


**righteousness, justice, and equity** “Righteousness” denotes charity from his money; “justice” means to judge honestly, and “equity” denotes compromise—the smooth and straight road, equal to this one and to that one.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:4:1)


**לָתֵת לִפְתָאיִם עָרְמָה.** מְשָׁלִים אֵלּוּ אָמַר קֹהֶלֶת לִקְנוֹת בָּהֶם הַפְּתָאיִם עָרְמָה, **לְנַעַר דַּעַת**, וְגַם לְנַעַר הַמְנֻעָר מִכָּל לִמּוּד שֶׁלֹּא לָמַד עֲדַיִן כְּלוּם:


**To give prudence to the simple** Koheleth stated these proverbs so that the simple should gain prudence. **knowledge...to the youth** And also to the youth, who is devoid of all [knowledge], who has not yet learned anything.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:4:2)


**מְזִמָּה.** מַחֲשֶׁבֶת עֵצָה:


**and discretion** Thoughts of counsel.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:1)


**יִשְׁמַע חָכָם.** הַמְּשָׁלִים הַלָּלוּ:


**Let the wise man hear** these proverbs.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:2)


**וְיוֹסֶף.** עַל חָכְמָתוֹ:


**and increase** to his wisdom.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:3)


**לֶקַח.** לִמּוּד:


**learning** Heb. לקח, learning.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:4)


**חָכָם.** זֶה בַּעַל הַשְּׁמוּעָה:


**the wise man** This is the one who has heard wisdom.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:5:5)


**וְנָבוֹן.** מוֹסִיף עַל יְדִיעַת חָכָם, שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ לְהָבִין דָּבָר מִתּוֹךְ דָּבָר וּמוֹסִיף עַל שְׁמוּעָתוֹ:


**The understanding man** who has knowledge superior to the knowledge of the wise man, for he knows how to derive one idea from another, and he adds to what he [has] heard.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:6:1)


**לְהָבִין מָשָׁל וּמְלִיצָה.** שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ לֵב לְהָבִין בַּמִּקְרָאוֹת שְׁנֵי הַדְּרָכִים: הַמָּשָׁל וְהַמְּלִיצָה, שֶׁיָּבִינוּ אֶת מָה הִמְשִׁיל לַמְּלִיצָה. וְאַף מִן הַמְּלִיצָה לֹא יָסוּרוּ לִבָּם, שֶׁגַּם הִיא צְרִיכִים לְהָבִין. כְּשֶׁאָמַר ״לְהַצִּילְךָ מֵאִשָּׁה זָרָה״ וְ״נָכְרִיָּה״ (משלי ב:טז) – עַל עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה נֶאֱמַר, הֲרֵי הַמָּשָׁל. וְאַף הַמְּלִיצָה שֶׁהוֹצִיא מְשָׁלוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן ׳אִשָּׁה׳ – הָבֵן בָּהּ וְהִזָּהֵר מֵאִשָּׁה (ס״א: זוֹנָה):


**to understand an allegory and a figure** That they should direct their attention to understand the verses through two methods: the allegory and the figure. They should understand what he compares to the figure, but they should not neglect the figure itself, for that, too, requires understanding. When he states, “To save you from a strange woman and a foreign one” (2:16), idolatry is meant; this is the allegory, and also the figure—for he expressed his allegory in terms of a woman—should be understood by it, [meaning that] you shall beware of a strange woman (other editions: a harlot).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:6:2)


**דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים וְחִידוֹתָם.** דּוֹרְשֵׁי רְשִׁימוֹת: מִקְרָא מָלֵא וְחָסֵר, רֶמֶז, דִּמְיוֹן וְחִידָה:


**the words of the wise and their riddles** Those who interpret the Torah metaphorically, full verses and elliptical ones, allusions, comparisons and riddles.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:7:1)


**יִרְאַת ה׳ רֵאשִׁית דָּעַת.** עַד כָּאן פֵּרֵשׁ לְצֹרֶךְ מָה עָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה הַסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה, וְעַתָּה מַתְחִיל הַסֵּפֶר:


**The fear of the Lord is the beginning of knowledge** Until here, he explained for what purpose Solomon composed this book, and now the book commences.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:7:2)


**יִרְאַת ה׳ רֵאשִׁית דָּעַת.** הִיא תְּרוּמַת עִקַּר הַדַּעַת וְהִיא תִּהְיֶה לְךָ הָרִאשׁוֹנָה לָדַעַת. לִפְנֵי חָכְמָתְךָ הַקְדֵּם לְיִרְאָה אֶת יוֹצְרְךָ, וְהִיא תִּתֵּן בְּלִבְּךָ לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּחָכְמָה וּבְדַעַת, כִּי הָאֱוִילִים, אֲשֶׁר אֵינָם יְרֵאִים אֶת ה׳, הֵם בּוֹזִים אֶת הַחָכְמָה וְאֶת הַמּוּסָר:


**The fear of the Lord is the beginning of knowledge** This is the separation of the fundamentals of knowledge, and what shall be for you first, preceding knowledge: Before your wisdom, first fear your Creator, and that will give your heart the desire to engage in wisdom and in knowledge, for the fools, who do not fear the Lord, despise wisdom and discipline.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:8:1)


**שְׁמַע בְּנִי מוּסַר אָבִיךָ.** מַה שֶּׁנָּתַן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְמֹשֶׁה בִּכְתָב וְעַל פֶּה:


**Hearken, my son, to the discipline of your father** What the Holy One, blessed be He, gave Moses in writing and orally.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:8:2)


**אִמֶּךָ.** אוּמָּתְךָ, כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּמוֹ: ״מָה אִמְּךָ לְבִיָּא״ (יחזקאל יט:ב). וְהֵם דִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, שֶׁחִדְּשׁוּ וְהוֹסִיפוּ וְעָשׂוּ סְיָגִים לַתּוֹרָה:


**your mother** Heb. אמך [like אמתך], your nation, the nation of Israel, as in (Ezek. 19:2): “What a lioness was your mother [meaning your nation]!” These are the words of the Scribes, which they innovated and added and made safeguards for the Torah.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:9:1)


**לִוְיַת חֵן.** חִבּוּר שֶׁל חֵן הֵם לְרֹאשְׁךָ, כְּלוֹמַר: הַתּוֹרָה וְהַמּוּסָר יִהְיוּ לְרֹאשְׁךָ **לִוְיַת חֵן**, וְכַעֲנָקִים שֶׁל עֲדִי זָהָב יִהְיוּ:


**a wreath of grace** They are a wreath of grace for your head; i.e., the instruction and the discipline will be a wreath of grace for your head, and they shall be like the rings of a golden ornament.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:9:2)


**לְגַרְגְּרוֹתֶיךָ.** לְצַוָּארְךָ, וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַקָּנֶה עֲשׂוּיָה טַבָּעוֹת טַבָּעוֹת, קוֹרֵא הַצַּוָּאר בִּלְשׁוֹן רַבִּים:


**for your neck** Heb. לגרגרתיך. Since the trachea is composed of many rings, he refers to the neck in the plural form.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:10:1)


**חַטָּאִים.** חוֹטְאִים:


**sinners** Heb. חטאים, sinners.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:10:2)


**אַל תָּבֹא.** אַל תֹּאבֶה לָהֶם:


**do not consent** Heb. אל תבא, do not consent to them.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:1)


**נֶאֶרְבָה לְדָם.** לִשְׁפּוֹךְ דָּם:


**let us lie in wait for blood** to shed blood.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:2)


**נִצְפְּנָה.** לְשׁוֹן ״מַאֲרָב״ (יהושע ח:ט):


**let us hide** Heb. נצפנה, an expression of ambush.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:11:3)


**נִצְפְּנָה לְנָקִי חִנָּם.** הַכָּתוּב אוֹמֵר שֶׁצְּפִינָתָם לְנָקִי – חִנָּם הוּא:


**let us hide for the innocent, without cause** Scripture states that their hiding for the innocent is without cause.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:1)


**נִבְלָעֵם.** לַנְּקִיִּים כְּשֶׁהֵם חַיִּים:


**let us swallow them up** The innocent when they are alive.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:2)


**כְּשְׁאוֹל.** הַבּוֹלֵעַ כָּל גּוּף שָׁלֵם:


**like the grave** which swallows up the entire body.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:12:3)


**וּתְמִימִים.** אֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן צַדִּיקִים, אֶלָּא מִלְּשׁוֹן שְׁלֵמִים: נִבְלָעֵם כְּשֶׁהֵם שְׁלֵמִים, כְּאָדָם הַיּוֹרֵד בְּתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר כְּשֶׁהוּא שָׁלֵם. כְּלוֹמַר: בְּעוֹדָם בְּעָשְׁרָם נַהַרְגֵם, וְנִירַשׁ נִכְסֵיהֶם:


**and the whole ones** Heb. ותמימים. This is not an expression of righteous men, but an expression of whole ones. Let us swallow them up when they are whole, like a man who descends into a pit when he is whole; i.e., when they are still wealthy, let us slay them and inherit their property.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:14:1)


**גּוֹרָלְךָ תַּפִּיל בְּתוֹכֵנוּ.** אִם תִּרְצֶה תַּחֲלֹק, אוֹ אִם תִּרְצֶה יִהְיֶה בְּשֻׁתָּפוּת:


**Cast your lot among us** If you wish, you may share; if you wish, it will be common property.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:14:2)


**כִּיס אֶחָד יִהְיֶה לְכֻלָּנוּ.** בְּיַחַד:


**we will all have one purse** together.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:16:1)


**לָרַע יָרוּצוּ.** לְרָעַת עַצְמָם הֵם רָצִים, וְאֵינָם יוֹדְעִים לָתֵת לֵב לַדָּבָר:


**run to evil** They run to their own harm, and they do not know to pay heed to the matter.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:17:1)


**כִּי חִנָּם מְזֹרָה הָרָשֶׁת.** כִּי הָעוֹפוֹת הָרוֹאוֹת חִטִּים וְקִטְנִיּוֹת מְזוֹרִים עַל הָרֶשֶׁת, חִנָּם הוּא בְּעֵינֵיהֶם, שֶׁאֵינָם מַכִּירִים עַל מָה הִיא נִזְרֶת, וְיוֹרְדִין בָּהּ וְאוֹכְלִין:


**For the net is scattered without cause** For the fowl that see wheat and legumes spread out upon the net—it appears to them that it is without cause; they do not realize for what reason they are spread, and they go down into it and eat.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:18:1)


**וְהֵם לְדָמָם יֶאֱרֹבוּ.** וְהַצַּיָּדִים יֶאֱרֹבוּ לְדָמָם שֶׁל עוֹפוֹת:


**but they lie in wait for their blood** But the hunters lie in wait for the blood of the birds.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:19:1)


**כֵּן אָרְחוֹת כָּל בֹּצֵעַ בָּצַע.** גּוֹזֵל גְּזֵילָה נָאָה וְנֶחְמֶדֶת הִיא לוֹ, וְחִנָּם הִיא לוֹ (משלי א:יז), וְסוֹפוֹ **אֶת נֶפֶשׁ בְּעָלָיו יִקָּח.** נֶפֶשׁ עַצְמוֹ, שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה עַכְשָׁיו בַּעַל הַמָּמוֹן שֶׁגָּזַל מֵחֲבֵרוֹ:


**So are the ways of everyone who commits robbery** Heb. בצע בצע, who commits robbery. It is beautiful and precious to him, and it is free to him. But his end will be that... **it will take away the life of its owner** His own life, for he has now become the owner of the money that he stole from his neighbor.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:20:1)


**חָכְמוֹת בַּחוּץ תָּרֹנָּה.** הֲרֵי חָכְמוֹתֶיהָ שֶׁל תּוֹרָה בְּחוּצוֹתֶיהָ תִּזְעַקְנָה לְהַזְהִירְכֶם לָסוּר עֲלֵיהֶם. וּמָה הֵן חוּצוֹתֶיהָ? בָּתֵּי מִדְרָשׁוֹת:


**Wisdoms shout in the street** Behold, the wisdoms of the Torah cry out in her streets to admonish [the people] to turn to them [the streets]. Now what are her streets? The study halls.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:20:2)


**בָּרְחוֹבוֹת.** בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁמַּרְחִיבִין אוֹתָהּ. כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בחקותי ד):


**in the squares** Heb. ברחבות, in the place where it is broadened [in the place where Torah is studied intensively and elaborated upon]. In this manner, Rabbi Tanhuma expounded [upon this verse].



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:21:1)


**בְּרֹאשׁ הוֹמִיּוֹת תִּקְרָא.** בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהִיא נִשְׁמַעַת וְנִכְרֶזֶת, שָׁם הִיא קוֹרֵאת וְאוֹמֶרֶת עִנְיָן שֶׁלְּמַטָּה: **עַד מָתַי פְּתָיִם וְגוֹ׳**:


**She calls at the head of the noisy streets** In the place where she is heard and called [and proclaimed Vilna, Warsaw ed.] there she calls out and makes the following statement. “How long will you naive ones, etc?”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:21:2)


**בְּפִתְחֵי שְׁעָרִים.** הֵם מְקוֹם יְשִׁיבַת הַזְּקֵנִים:


**at the entrances of the gates** They are the place where the elders sit.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:22:1)


**פְּתָיִם.** הַמִּתְפַּתִּים עַל יְדֵי מְסִיתִים וּמִינִים:


**you naive ones** Heb. פתים. Those who are enticed by enticers and sectarians.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:22:2)


**תְּאֵהֲבוּ פֶתִי.** הֲסָתָה שֵׁם דָּבָר לְ״פַתְיוּת״ (משלי ט:יג), כְּמוֹ ״כֶּלִי״ (במדבר יט:יז), ״קֶרִי״ (ויקרא כו:כא), (שני סא״א) ״שֶׁפִי״ (במדבר כג:ג):


**naivete** Heb. פתי, enticement, the noun for simplicity; like כלי, a vessel, קרי, a happening, שפי, silence.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:24:1)


**נָטִיתִי יָדִי.** לִרְמֹז לָהֶם לָסוּר אֵלַי, כְּאָדָם הַמְרַמֵּז לַחֲבֵרוֹ בְּיָדוֹ וְנוֹטֶה יָדוֹ אֵלָיו, לָסוּר אֵלָיו:


**I stretched out my hand** to beckon to you to turn to me, like a man who beckons to his friend with his hand, stretching out his hand to him to turn to him.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:25:1)


**וַתִּפְרְעוּ.** וּתְבַטְּלוּ:


**and you have made nothing** Heb. ותפרעו, and you nullified.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:25:2)


**כָּל עֲצָתִי.** שֶׁיָּעַצְתִּי לְגַדֵּל אֶתְכֶם בָּעוֹלָם:


**all my advice** that I advised, in order to aggrandize you in the world.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:1)


**כְּשׁוֹאָה.** כְּעָנָן הָעוֹלֶה פִּתְאוֹם:


**when your fear comes like a storm** Like a cloud that comes up suddenly.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:2)


**כְּסוּפָה.** טוֹרְבִּילוֹ״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**like a whirlwind** Tourbillon in Old French, in German wirbelwind (Cf. Rashi, Jer. 4:13, Ps. 83:16).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:27:3)


**יֶאֱתֶה.** יָבֹא:


**comes** Heb. יאתה, it comes.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:28:1)


**יְשַׁחֲרוּנְנִי.** יְבַקְשׁוּנִי:


**they shall seek me** Heb. ישחרנני, they shall look for me.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:31:1)


**מִפְּרִי דַרְכָּם.** הַפֵּרוֹת שֶׁאוֹכְלִים בְּחַיֵּיהֶם בַּצָּרוֹת הַמּוֹצְאוֹת אוֹתָם, וְהַקֶּרֶן שְׁמוּרָה לָהֶם בַּגֵּיהִנֹּם:


**of the fruit of their way** The fruit of the troubles that befall them they eat in their lifetime, and the principal is preserved for them in Gehinnom.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:32:1)


**מְשׁוּבַת פְּתָיִם.** אֶת אֲשֶׁר לִבָּם שׁוֹבָב:


**for the backsliding of the naive** Heb. משובת פתים. Insofar as their heart backslides.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:32:2)


**וְשַׁלְוַת כְּסִילִים תְּאַבְּדֵם.** לְפִי שֶׁרוֹאִים הָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁהֵם מַצְלִיחִים, נִדְבָּקִים בְּרָעָתָם וְאֵינָם חוֹזְרִין:


**and the tranquility of the fools shall cause them to perish** Since they see the wicked who prosper, they adhere to their evil and do not repent.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:1)


**יִשְׁכָּן בֶּטַח.** בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה:


**shall dwell confidently** in this world.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:2)


**וְשַׁאֲנַן.** לְעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**and shall be tranquil** in the world to come.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:3)


**מִפַּחַד רָעָה.** מִדִּינָהּ שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם:


**from the fear of harm** From the punishment of Gehinnom.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 1:33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/1:33:4)


**וְשַׁאֲנַן.** וִיהִי שָׁלֵיו וְשָׁקֵט, וְשַׁאֲנַן לְשׁוֹן עָתִיד לָכֵן נָקוּד פַּתַּח:


**and shall be tranquil** Heb. ושאנן and shall be tranquil and at rest. The word ושאנן is in the future tense; therefore, [the “nun”] is vowelized with a “pattah.”



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:1:1)


**בְּנִי אִם תִּקַּח אֲמָרָי. בְּנִי** תִּהְיֶה **אִם תִּקַּח אֲמָרָי**:


**My son, if you accept my words** You will be my son if you accept my words.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:2:1)


**לְהַקְשִׁיב לַחָכְמָה.** לַעֲסוֹק בַּתּוֹרָה:


**to make...attentive to wisdom** to toil in [learning] Torah.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:5:1)


**אָז תָּבִין יִרְאַת ה׳.** מוּסָב עַל עִנְיָן שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה, שֶׁאָמַר **כִּי אִם לַבִּינָה תִקְרָא**:


**then you will understand the fear of the Lord** This refers back to the topic above, in which he said, “For if you call for understanding.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:6:1)


**כִּי ה׳ יִתֵּן חָכְמָה.** הָא לָמַדְתָּ שֶׁגְּדוֹלָה הִיא, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִתְּנָה מִפִּי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לְכָךְ אַתָּה צָרִיךְ לִקְנוֹתָהּ:


**For the Lord gives wisdom** Here you have learned that it [wisdom] is great, for it was given from the mouth of the Holy One, blessed be He. Therefore, you must acquire it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:7:1)


**יִצְפֹּן לַיְשָׁרִים תּוּשִׁיָּה.** גְּנָזָהּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶצְלוֹ (תנחומא, לך לך יא) עֶשְׂרִים וָשֵׁשׁ דּוֹרוֹת עַד שֶׁנְּתָנָהּ לְדוֹר הַמִּדְבָּר:


**He lays up sound wisdom for the upright** The Holy One, blessed be He, hid it [wisdom] with Him for twenty-six generations until He gave it to the generation of the desert.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:7:2)


**מָגֵן לְהוֹלְכֵי תֹם.** (יִצְפֹּן מָגֵן לְהוֹלְכֵי תֹם), כְּלוֹמַר: וְהִיא תִּהְיֶה לְךָ לְמָגֵן:


**a shield for those who walk in integrity** (He lays up a shield for those who walk with integrity); i.e., it will be a shield for you.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:8:1)


**לִנְצֹר אָרְחוֹת מִשְׁפָּט.** שֶׁעַל יָדָהּ יִנְצְרוּ אָרְחוֹת מִשְׁפָּט, וְהוּא **דֶּרֶךְ חֲסִידָיו יִשְׁמֹר.** שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּשְׁלוּ:


**to keep the paths of justice** For through it, they will keep the paths of justice, and He will guard the way of His pious so that they do not stumble.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:11:1)


**מְזִמָּה תִּשְׁמֹר עָלֶיךָ.** הַתּוֹרָה תִּשְׁמֹר עָלֶיךָ:


**thought** Heb. מזמה. The Torah shall watch over you.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:12:1)


**מֵאִישׁ מְדַבֵּר תַּהְפֻּכוֹת.** הֵם הָאֶפִּיקוֹרְסִין שֶׁמַּטְעִין אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵאֱמוּנָתָן וּמְהַפְּכִין אֶת הַתּוֹרָה לְרָעָה:


**who speaks perversity** Those are the people who distort the thing. [Rashi as it appears in Lublin and Waxman ed.] Malbim ed. reads: who distorts the words of the Torah after his erroneous view. Vilna and Warsaw editions read: They are the apostates who distort the words of the Torah to evil.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:15:1)


**וּנְלוֹזִים בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתָם.** כָּל לְשׁוֹן ׳נָלוֹז׳ לְשׁוֹן ׳עַקְמִימוּת׳ הוּא, שֶׁבְּכָל מָקוֹם הוּא סָמוּךְ לְ׳עִקֵּשׁ׳ (משלי ד:כד), וְ׳עִקֵּשׁ׳ הוּא לְשׁוֹן ׳עָקוֹם׳, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמַעֲקַשִּׁים לְמִישׁוֹר״ (ישעיה מב:טז):


**and perverse in their paths** Heb. ונלוזים. Every expression of נלוז is an expression of crookedness, as it is always next to עקש, and עקש is an expression of crookedness, as it is stated (Isa. 42:16): “and crooked paths (ומעקשים) into straight ones.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:15:2)


**וּנְלוֹזִים בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתָם.** הֵם עֲקוּמִים בְּדַרְכֵיהֶם הַמְקֻלְקָלִים:


**and perverse in their paths** They are crooked in their corrupt ways.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:16:1)


**מֵאִשָּׁה זָרָה.** מִכְּנֵסִיָּה שֶׁל אֲפִיקוֹרְסוּת וְהִיא הַמִּינוּת. וְלֹא יִתָּכֵן לוֹמַר, שֶׁלֹּא דִּבֵּר אֶלָּא עַל הַמְנָאֶפֶת מַמָּשׁ, כִּי מַה שִּׁבְחָהּ שֶׁל תּוֹרָה שֶׁאָמַר כָּאן **לְהַצִּילְךָ מֵאִשָּׁה זָרָה** וְלֹא מֵעֲבֵירָה אַחֶרֶת? אֶלָּא זוֹ מִינוּת, שֶׁהוּא פְּרִיקַת עֹל שֶׁל כָּל הַמִּצְווֹת:


**To save you from a strange woman** From the assembly of idolatry [apostasy], which is sectarianism. It cannot be said that he spoke only of the actual adulteress, for what is the praise of the Torah, that he says here, “to save you from a strange woman,” and not from any other sin. Rather, this is the casting off of the yoke of all the commandments.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:18:1)


**כִּי שָׁחָה אֶל מָוֶת בֵּיתָהּ.** מוּסָב עַל **לְהַצִּילְךָ וְגוֹ׳**, כִּי תָבֹא אֶל בֵּיתָהּ, יָשַׁח וּמַחֲלִיק כְּמִדְרוֹן הַיּוֹרֵד אֶל מָוֶת. וְהַתּוֹרָה תִּשְׁמֹר עָלֶיךָ מִנְּפִילָה זוֹ, הֲרֵי דָּבָר גָּדוֹל הוּא לְךָ:


**for her house sinks to death** This refers back to “to save you, etc.” for whoever comes to her house will sink and slip as if down an incline that leads to death, and the Torah will guard you from this fall. Hence, it is a great thing for you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:18:2)


**רְפָאִים.** נִרְפִּים מִדֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹב וְנֶעֱזָבִים מֵאֵין סוֹמֵךְ, עַד שֶׁנּוֹפְלִים בַּגֵּיהִנֹּם:


**the dead** Heb. רפאים, those who neglect (נרפים) the way of goodness and are forsaken without support until they fall into Gehinnom.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:19:1)


**לֹא יְשׁוּבוּן.** יִקְשֶׁה בְּעֵינֵיהֶם לִפְרֹשׁ מִמֶּנָּה וְלַחֲזֹר בָּהֶם:


**none...return** It is hard for them to part with it and to repent.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:20:1)


**לְמַעַן תֵּלֵךְ.** מוּסָב עַל הַמִּקְרָא שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה **לְהַצִּילְךָ מִדֶּרֶךְ רָעָה**, לְמַעַן הוֹלִיכְךָ **בְּדֶרֶךְ טוֹבִים**:


**in order that you go** This refers back to the above verse (verse 12): “To save you from an evil way,” in order to lead you in the way of the good.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:21:1)


**כִּי יְשָׁרִים יִשְׁכְּנוּ אָרֶץ.** לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**For the upright shall dwell in the land** In the world to come.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 2:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/2:21:2)


**יִוָּתְרוּ בָהּ.** כְּשֶׁיֵּרְדוּ רְשָׁעִים לְגֵיהִנֹּם:


**shall remain therein** when the wicked descend to Gehinnom.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:2:1)


**אֹרֶךְ יָמִים וְגוֹ׳ וְשָׁלוֹם יוֹסִיפוּ לָךְ.** תּוֹרָתִי וּמִצְווֹתַי:


**they shall add length of days...and peace** The Torah and the commandments.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:5:1)


**בְּטַח אֶל ה׳.** וּפַזֵּר מְעוֹתֶיךָ לְבַקֵּשׁ לְךָ רַב לִלְמֹד מִמֶּנּוּ, **וְאֶל בִּינָתְךָ אַל תִּשָּׁעֵן**:


**Trust in the Lord** and squander your money to seek for yourself a teacher from whom to learn, and do not rely on your understanding.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:7:1)


**אַל תְּהִי חָכָם בְּעֵינֶיךָ.** לִהְיוֹת מוֹאֵס בְּדִבְרֵי מוֹכִיחֶךָ:


**Do not be wise in your own sight** to despise the word of the one who reproves you.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:8:1)


**רְפֻאוּת תְּהִי לְשָׁרֶּךָ.** הַחָכְמָה. **שָׁרֶּךָ** כְּמוֹ ״שָׁרְרֵךְ״ (שיר השירים ז:ג), וְזֶהוּ טִיבּוּרֶךָ:


**It shall be healing for your navel** Heb. לשרך. The wisdom shall be healing. שרך is like (Song 7:4): שררך, which means, your navel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:8:2)


**וְשִׁקּוּי לְעַצְמוֹתֶיךָ.** הוּא הַמֹּחַ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמֹחַ עַצְמוֹתָיו יְשֻׁקֶּה״ (איוב כא:כד):


**and marrow for your bones** Heb. ושקוי, lit. moisture. That is the marrow, as the matter is stated (Job 21:24): “and the marrow of his bones is moistened.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:9:1)


**מֵהוֹנֶךָ.** מִכָּל מַה שֶּׁחֲנָנְךָ, אֲפִלּוּ מִקּוֹל עָרֵב. (אַל תִּקְרִי מֵהוֹנֶךָ אֶלָּא מִגְּרוֹנֶךָ):


**from your substance** From whatever He favored you [with], even from a pleasant voice. (Do not read: from your substance [מהונך] but: from your throat [מגרונך].)


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:9:2)


**וּמֵרֵאשִׁית.** אֵלּוּ תְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת:


**and from the first** (These are the terumoth and the tithes.)



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:11:1)


**מוּסַר ה׳ בְּנִי אַל תִּמְאָס.** אִם יָבוֹאוּ עָלֶיךָ יִסּוּרִין, יִהְיוּ חֲבִיבִין עָלֶיךָ (תנחומא, יתרו טז):


**My son, despise not the discipline of the Lord** Should pains come upon you, they should be dear to you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:11:2)


**וְאַל תָּקֹץ.** מִלְּשׁוֹן ״קַצְתִּי בְחַיַּי״ (בראשית כז:מו):


**and do not abhor** Heb. תקץ, from the expression of (Gen. 27:46) “I abhor (קצתי) my life.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:12:1)


**וּכְאָב אֶת בֵּן יִרְצֶה.** שֶׁרוֹצֶה בִּבְנוֹ לְהֵיטִיב לוֹ וִיפַיְּסֵהוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁמַּכֵּהוּ בַּשֵּׁבֶט, כֵּן יֶעֱרַב לְךָ הַטּוֹבָה אַחַר הַמַּכָּה:


**as a father placates his son** He desires to benefit his son and he placates him after striking him with the staff. So will the benefit be pleasant to you after the smiting.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:13:1)


**אַשְׁרֵי אָדָם מָצָא חָכְמָה וְאָדָם יָפִיק תְּבוּנָה.** שֶׁלּוֹמֵד חָכְמָה עַד שֶׁשְּׁגוּרָה הִיא לְהוֹצִיא מִפִּיו:


**Fortunate is the man who has found wisdom and a man who gives forth discernment** One who learned wisdom until he is so accustomed to it that he can express it with his mouth.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:14:1)


**כִּי טוֹב סַחְרָהּ מִסְּחַר כָּסֶף.** כָּל חִלּוּפִין שֶׁאָדָם מַחֲלִיף בִּסְחוֹרָה, זֶה נוֹטֵל זֶה וְזֶה נוֹטֵל זֶה. אֲבָל הָאוֹמֵר לַחֲבֵירוֹ: שְׁנֵה לִי פִּרְקְךָ וַאֲנִי אֶשְׁנֶה לְךָ פִּרְקִי, נִמְצְאוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּיַד כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד:


**for its commerce is better than the commerce of silver** In any exchange, when a person exchanges [something] for merchandise, this one takes this, and that one takes that; but if one says to his friend, “Teach me your chapter, and I will teach you my chapter,” [then] both chapters are found in the hand of each of them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:14:2)


**חָרוּץ.** מִין זָהָב הוּא:


**than fine gold** Heb. מחרוץ. That is a type of gold.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:15:1)


**וְכָל־חֲפָצֶיךָ.** כָּל חֲמֻדּוֹתֶיךָ:


**and all your desirable things** Heb. חפציך all that you desire.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:15:2)


**לֹא יִשְׁווּ בָהּ.** לֹא יִהְיוּ שָׁוִין בְּשָׁוְיָהּ וְדָמֵיהֶן בְּדָמֶיהָ:


**cannot be compared to it** They are not equal to its worth, nor their price to its price.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:16:1)


**בִּימִינָהּ.** לַמְּיַמִּינִים בָּהּ וְעוֹסְקִים בָּהּ לִשְׁמָהּ, **אֹרֶךְ יָמִים**, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן **עֹשֶׁר וְכָבוֹד.** וְלַמַּשְׂמִאילִים בָּהּ, שֶׁעוֹסְקִים בָּהּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָהּ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם **עֹשֶׁר וְכָבוֹד** יֵשׁ (שבת סג.):


**in its right hand** Those who make the right use of it and engage in it for its own sake will have length of days and surely riches and honor, but those who make the wrong [lit. left] use of it, who engage in it not for its own sake, will still have riches and honor.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:18:1)


**לַמַּחֲזִיקִים בָּהּ.** לָאוֹחֲזִים בָּהּ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּשְׁלַח יָדוֹ וַיַּחֲזֶק בּוֹ״ (שמות ד:ד):


**for those who grasp it** Heb. למחזיקים for those who hold onto it, as in (Ex. 4:4): “and he extended his hand and grasped (ויחזק) it.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:18:2)


**וְתוֹמְכֶיהָ מְאֻשָּׁר.** הַמִּתְקָרְבִים לָהּ, וְכֵן כָּל לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיכָה שֶׁבְּסֵפֶר זֶה – שֶׁהוּא אוֹחֵז בָּהּ:


**and those who draw near it are fortunate** Heb. ותמכיה, those who draw near it. Likewise, every expression of תמיכה in this Book means that he holds onto it.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:19:1)


**ה׳ בְּחָכְמָה יָסַד אָרֶץ.** עַל פִּי הַתּוֹרָה, וְהִיא הַתּוֹרָה וְהִיא הַתְּבוּנָה וְהוּא הַדַּעַת. וְאַחַר שֶׁכָּל הָעוֹלָם נִבְרָא בָהֶם, לְכָךְ:


**The Lord founded the earth with wisdom** According to the Torah. This is the Torah, which is discernment and which is knowledge.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:20:1)


**יִרְעֲפוּ.** כְּמוֹ יַטִּיפוּ:


**drip** Heb. ירעפו, like יטיפו. Now, since the entire world was created with them, therefore...



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:21:1)


**אַל יָלֻזוּ מֵעֵינֶיךָ.** אַל יִתְעַקְּמוּ מִנֶּגֶד עֵינֶיךָ לַהֲסִירָם מִכְּנֶגְדְּךָ:


**let them not depart from your eyes** Let them not be curved away from before your eyes, to remove them from before you.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:22:1)


**וְיִהְיוּ חַיִּים לְנַפְשֶׁךָ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי **עֵץ חַיִּים** הוּא:


**and they shall be life for your soul** for it is a tree of life.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:22:2)


**וְחֵן לְגַרְגְּרוֹתֶיךָ.** שֶׁהֲרֵי זֶה שִׁבְחָהּ הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה ״וַעֲנָקִים לְגַרְגְּרוֹתֶיךָ״ (משלי א:ט):


**and grace for your neck** For this is its praise that is mentioned above (1:9): “and a necklace for your neck.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:23:1)


**לֹא תִגּוֹף.** לְשׁוֹן כִּשָּׁלוֹן, אצופי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן ״פֶּן תִּגּוֹף בָּאֶבֶן״ (תהלים צא:יב), וְכֵן ״בְּטֶרֶם יִתְנַגְּפוּ רַגְלֵיכֶם״ (ירמיה יג:טז), וְכֵן ״וְנָגְפוּ אִשָּׁה הָרָה״ (שמות כא:כב):


**shall not stumble** Heb. לא תגוף. This is an expression of stumbling, (achopper in French, straucheln in German. And so in Jeremiah 13:16 Psalms 91.12.) And so (Ps. 91:12). “Lest your foot stumble (תגף) on a stone”; and so (Jer. 13:16): “And before your feet stumble (יתנגפו)” and so (Ex. 21:22): “and they dash (ונגפו) a pregnant woman.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:24:1)


**וְשָׁכַבְתָּ וְעָרְבָה שְׁנָתֶךָ.** וְתִנְעַם שְׁנָתְךָ כְּשֶׁתִּישַׁן, שֶׁלֹּא תִירָא מִפַּחַד פִּתְאוֹם:


**and when you lie down, your sleep shall be sweet** Your sleep shall be sweet when you sleep, that you will not fear sudden terror.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:25:1)


**וּמִשּׁוֹאַת.** כְּשֶׁתָּבוֹא עַל הָרְשָׁעִים:


**or of the darkness** when it comes upon the wicked.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:26:1)


**כִּי ה׳ יִהְיֶה בְכִסְלֶךָ.** בְּמִבְטַחֲךָ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: בַּדְּבָרִים שֶׁאַתָּה כְּסִיל בָּהֶן. כָּךְ מָצָאתִי בִּירוּשַׁלְמִי (פאה א:א):


**For the Lord shall be your trust** Heb. בכסלך, in your trust. Another explanation: in the things concerning which you are a fool (כסיל). This I found in Yerushalmi.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:27:1)


**אַל תִּמְנַע טוֹב מִבְּעָלָיו.** אִם רָאִיתָ חֲבֵירְךָ חָפֵץ לְהֵיטִיב לָעֲנִיִּים, אַל תִּמְנָעֵהוּ:


**Do not withhold good from the one who needs it** If you see that your friend wishes to benefit the poor, do not prevent him from doing so.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:27:2)


**בִּהְיוֹת לְאֵל יָדְךָ.** לְהַחֲדִילוֹ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **אַל תִּמְנַע טוֹב מִבְּעָלָיו**, אַל תִּמְנַע צְדָקָה מִן הֶעָנִי, **בִּהְיוֹת לְאֵל יָדְךָ** לַעֲשׂוֹת צְדָקָה, שֶׁמָּא יָבוֹא יוֹם וְאֵין לְאֵל יָדְךָ:


**when you have power in your hand** to stop him. Another explanation: **Do not withhold good from the one who needs it** Do not withhold charity from the poor man. **when you have the power in your hand** to do charity; perhaps a day will come when you will not have the power in your hand, and similarly...



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:28:1)


**וְכֵן אַל תֹּאמַר לְרֵעֲךָ.** לֶעָנִי:


**Do not say to your fellow** To the poor man.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:28:2)


**וְיֵשׁ אִתָּךְ.** מַה שֶּׁתִּתֵּן לוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (בבא מציעא קי:): לֵךְ וָשׁוּב; עַל ״שְׂכַר שָׂכִיר״ (דברים טו:יח):


**though you have it with you** what to give to him. But our Sages explained: “Go and return” regarding the wages of a hireling.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:29:1)


**אַל תַּחֲרֹשׁ עַל רֵעֲךָ.** אַל תַּחְשֹׁב. וְאֵינוֹ זָז מִמַּשְׁמָעוּת חֲרִישָׁה: מַה דֶּרֶךְ הַחוֹרֵשׁ – מֵכִין מָקוֹם לִזְמַן הַזְּרִיעָה, אַף הַחוֹשֵׁב רָעָה מֵכִין מָקוֹם תַּחְבּוּלוֹת בְּלִבּוֹ, אֵיךְ יַעֲמֹד וְיַעֲשֶׂנָּה:


**Devise no harm against your fellow** Heb. אל תחרש, do not think—but it does not deviate from the meaning of plowing (חרישה) ; just as it is customary for the plower to prepare a place for the time of sowing, so does one who devises harm prepare a place for devices in his heart, how he will get up and execute it.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:30:1)


**אַל תָּרִיב עִם אָדָם.** לְהִתְלוֹנֵן עָלָיו:


**Do not quarrel with anyone** to complain about him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:30:2)


**אִם לֹא גְמָלְךָ רָעָה.** שֶׁעָבַר עַל הַמִּצְוָה הַכְּתוּבָה בַּתּוֹרָה ״וְאָהַבְתָּ לְרֵעֲךָ כָּמוֹךָ״ (ויקרא יט:יח). וּמִי שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע, רַשַּׁאי אַתָּה לְשָׂנְאוֹ:


**if he did you no harm** i.e., unless he transgressed a commandment written in the Torah. The Torah states (Lev. 19:18): “And you shall love your fellow as yourself,” but one who is wicked, you may hate.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:31:1)


**אַל תְּקַנֵּא בְּאִישׁ חָמָס.** לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּמַעֲשָׂיו, אִם תִּרְאֵהוּ מַצְלִיחַ:


**Do not envy a man of violence** to do as his deeds if you see him prospering.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:32:1)


**כִּי תוֹעֲבַת ה׳ נָלוֹז.** מְעֻוָּת בִּדְרָכָיו:


**for the perverse is an abomination to the Lord** One who is crooked in his ways.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:32:2)


**וְאֶת יְשָׁרִים סוֹדוֹ.** וְעִם יְשָׁרִים סוֹדוֹ:


**but His counsel is with the upright** Heb. ואת, and with, etc.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:34:1)


**אִם לַלֵּצִים.** אָדָם נִמְשָׁךְ אַחֲרֵיהֶם, לְסוֹף אַף הוּא יִהְיֶה לֵץ עִמָּהֶם:


**If [one goes] to the scoffers** If a person is attracted to them, he too will scoff with them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:34:2)


**וְלַעֲנָוִים יִתֶּן חֵן.** אִם לַעֲנָוִים יִתְחַבֵּר, סוֹף שֶׁיִּתְּנוּ מַעֲשָׂיו חֵן בְּעֵינֵיהֶם שֶׁל בְּרִיּוֹת:


**but [if he goes] to the humble, he evokes grace** If he joins the humble, eventually his deeds will evoke grace in people’s eyes.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Proverbs 3:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/3:35:1)


**כָּבוֹד חֲכָמִים יִנְחָלוּ וּכְסִילִים מֵרִים קָלוֹן.** לְעַצְמוֹ מַפְרִישׁ קָלוֹן לְחֶלְקוֹ:


**The wise shall inherit honor, but the fools take disgrace as their portion** For himself, he takes disgrace for his portion.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:1:1)


**שִׁמְעוּ בָנִים מוּסַר אָב.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Children, hearken to the discipline of the Father** The Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:2:1)


**נָתַתִּי לָכֶם.** הַנָּבִיא מִתְנַבֵּא וּמְדַבֵּר בִּשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּפִיו:


**For I gave you** The prophet prophesies and speaks as an agent of the Holy One, blessed be He, and he is like His mouth.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:3:1)


**כִּי בֵן הָיִיתִי לְאָבִי.** אִם תֹּאמַר: שְׁלֹמֹה הָיָה שׂוֹנֵא הַבְּרִיּוֹת, שֶׁהִזְהִירָן עַל הַגָּזֵל וְעַל הָעֲרָיוֹת, דָּבָר שֶׁנַּפְשׁוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם מִתְאַוָּה לָהֶם, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **כִּי בֵן הָיִיתִי וְגוֹ׳ רַךְ וְיָחִיד וְגוֹ׳.** שֶׁהָיָה אוֹהֵב אוֹתִי יוֹתֵר:


**For I was a son to my father** Lest you say that Solomon hated people because he admonished them concerning robbery and immorality, something a person desires, he therefore states: I was a son, etc., a tender one and an only one, etc., that he loved me very much.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:4:1)


**וַיּוֹרֵנִי.** עַל כָּךְ וַיּוֹכִיחֵנִי **וַיֹּאמֶר לִי יִתְמָךְ דְבָרַי לִבֶּךָ.** וּלְפִי שֶׁהוֹכִיחֵנִי בִּדְבָרִים אֵלּוּ, לְכָךְ אֲנִי מַזְהִירְכֶם עַל כָּךְ (רבי יוסף קרא). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כִּי בֵן הָיִיתִי לְאָבִי**, הַנָּבִיא אוֹמֵר: בֵּן הָיִיתִי לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהִשְׁרָה רוּחוֹ עָלַי. וּמָצִינוּ שֶׁקְּרָאוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִשְׁלֹמֹה בֵּן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אֲנִי אֶהְיֶה לּוֹ לְאָב וְגוֹ׳״ (שמואל ב ז:יד):


**And he instructed me** concerning that and he chastised me **and said to me, “May your heart draw near to my words** And since he chastened me with these words, therefore I admonish you concerning this (Rabbi Joseph Kara). Another explanation: **For I was a son to my Father** The prophet says, “I was a son to the Holy One, blessed be He, Who caused His spirit to rest upon me.” Now we find that the Holy One, blessed be He, called Solomon a son, for it is stated (II Sam. 7:14): “I will be to him a father, and he shall be to Me a son.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:4:2)


**רַךְ וְיָחִיד לִפְנֵי אִמִּי.** לְאֻמָּתִי אֲנִי נִבְחָר וְחָבִיב כְּבֵן רַךְ וְיָחִיד, לְכָךְ **וַיּוֹרֵנִי אָבִי**:


**a tender one and an only one before my mother** Heb. לפני אמי, before my nation (אמתי) I am chosen and beloved like a tender and only son. Therefore, my father instructed me.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:6:1)


**אַל תַּעַזְבֶהָ וְגוֹ׳ אֱהָבֶהָ.** אֱהוֹב אוֹתָהּ:


**Do not forsake her, and she will preserve you; love her** Heb. אהבה, love her.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:7:1)


**רֵאשִׁית חָכְמָה קְנֵה חָכְמָה.** תְּחִלַּת חָכְמָתְךָ לְמוֹד מֵאֲחֵרִים, קְנֵה לְךָ שְׁמוּעָה מִפִּי הָרַב, וְאַחַר כָּךְ **בְּכָל קִנְיָנְךָ קְנֵה בִינָה**, תִּתְבּוֹנֵן בָּהּ מֵעַצְמְךָ לְהַשְׂכִּיל הַטְּעָמִים, דָּבָר מִתּוֹךְ דָּבָר:


**The beginning of wisdom [is to] acquire wisdom** At the beginning of your wisdom, learn from others and acquire for yourself the tradition from the mouth of the teacher, and afterwards with all your possession acquire understanding. Concentrate on it by yourself to understand the reasons, thereby deriving one thing from another.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:8:1)


**סַלְסְלֶהָ.** חַפְּשֶׂהָ, הֱיֵה חוֹזֵר עָלֶיהָ לְדַקְדֵּק בָּהּ. כְּמוֹ ״כְּבוֹצֵר עַל סַלְסִלּוֹת״ (ירמיהו ו:ט) – הַחוֹזֵר וְהוֹלֵךְ בַּכֶּרֶם וּמֵשִׁיב יָדוֹ לְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת הָעוֹלֵלוֹת. וּבִלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים (תנחומא, וישב ח): מְסַלְסֵל בִּשְׂעָרוֹ:


**Search for her** Heb. סלסלה search for her. Review it to examine it minutely, as in (Jer. 6:9): “as a vintager over the searchings (סלסלות)” who goes repeatedly through the vineyard and brings back his hand to search for the single grapes. In the expression of the Sages (Rosh Hashanah 26b), curls (מסלסל) his hair.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:9:1)


**תִּתֵּן לְרֹאשְׁךָ לִוְיַת חֵן.** חֶבְרַת חֵן, כְּמוֹ ״לִוְיַת חֵן הֵם לְרֹאשֶׁךָ״ (משלי א:ט):


**She will give your head a wreath of grace** Heb. לוית חן, a wreath of grace, as in (1:9): “They are a wreath of grace (לוית חן) for your head.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:10:1)


**שְׁמַע בְּנִי וְקַח אֲמָרַי וְגוֹ׳**:



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:12:1)


**בְּלֶכְתְּךָ לֹא יֵצַר צַעֲדֶךָ.** מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַרְחִיק רַגְלָיו, קָרוֹב לִיפּוֹל:


**When you walk, your step will not be straightened** One who does not spread out his legs is likely to fall.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:13:1)


**הַחֲזֵק בַּמּוּסָר אַל תֶּרֶף.** אֱחוֹז בַּתּוֹרָה, כְּמוֹ ״וְהַחֲזִיקִי אֶת יָדֵךְ בּוֹ״ (בראשית כא:יח), ״וַיִּשְׁלַח יָדוֹ וַיַּחֲזֶק בּוֹ״ (שמות ד:ד):


**Take fast hold of discipline, do not let it loose** Grasp the Torah, as in (Gen. 21:18) “and grasp (והחזיקי) your hand onto him”; (Ex. 4:4) “and he stretched out his hand and grasped it (ויחזק).”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:14:1)


**בְּאֹרַח רְשָׁעִים אַל תָּבֹא וְאַל תְּאַשֵּׁר.** וְאַל תִּדְרֹךְ, כְּמוֹ ״בַּאֲשֻׁרוֹ אָחֲזָה רַגְלִי״ (איוב כג:יא), וְכֵן ״וְנָבוֹן יָבִין לַאֲשֻׁרוֹ״ (משלי יד:טו):


**You shall not come in the way of the wicked, and do not walk** [as translated,] do not walk, as in (Job 23:11), “My foot has held to His path,” and as in (Proverbs 14:15), “but a cunning man understands his steps.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:15:1)


**פָּרְעֵהוּ.** בַּטְּלֵהוּ:


**Avoid it** Heb. פרע, put it to naught.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:15:2)


**שְׂטֵה מֵעָלָיו.** סוּר:


**turn away from it** Heb. שטה, turn away.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:16:1)


**כִּי לֹא יִשְׁנוּ.** אֵינָם יְכוֹלִין לִישֹׁן:


**For they will not sleep** They are unable to sleep.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:18:1)


**וְאֹרַח צַדִּיקִים כְּאוֹר נֹגַהּ.** שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ וּמֵאִיר מֵעַמּוּד הַשַּׁחַר:


**But the way of the righteous is like the light of dawn** which shines and illuminates from the first ray of dawn.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:18:2)


**עַד נְכוֹן הַיּוֹם.** עַד חֲצוֹת הַיּוֹם, שֶׁהוּא בֵּרוּרוֹ שֶׁל יוֹם:


**until the day is perfect** Until midday, which is the brightest time of day.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:19:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ רְשָׁעִים כָּאֲפֵלָה לֹא יָדְעוּ בַּמֶּה יִכָּשֵׁלוּ.** פִּתְאוֹם יָבֹא לָהֶם מִכְשׁוֹל, וְלֹא יֵדְעוּ לְהִזָּהֵר מִמֶּנּוּ:


**The way of the wicked is like pitch darkness; they do not know on what they stumble** Suddenly, an obstacle will come to them, and they will not know how to beware of it.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:20:1)


**בְּנִי לִדְבָרַי הַקְשִׁיבָה וְגוֹ׳**:



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:22:1)


**לְמוֹצְאֵיהֶם.** לְשׁוֹן מְצִיאָה:


**for those who find them** Heb. למצאיהם, an expression of finding.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:23:1)


**מִכָּל מִשְׁמָר.** מִכָּל מַה שֶּׁאָמְרָה תּוֹרָה הִשָּׁמֶר **נְצֹר לִבְּךָ** (מֵעֲבוֹר עָלָיו), בֵּין עֲבֵרָה קַלָּה בֵּין עֲבֵרָה חֲמוּרָה:


**From every interdict** From whatever the Torah commanded to beware of, guard your heart (from transgressing), regardless whether it is a minor sin or a grave sin.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:23:2)


**כִּי מִמֶּנּוּ תּוֹצְאוֹת חַיִּים.** כִּי מֵאוֹתוֹ שֶׁהוּא קַל יִהְיוּ לְךָ חַיִּים וְשָׂכָר גָּדוֹל אִם תְּקַיְּמֶנּוּ. כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, כי תצא ב):


**for the issues of life [come] out of it** For from even a minor one you shall have life and a great reward if you fulfill it—so did Rabbi Tanhuma expound.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:1)


**הָסֵר מִמְּךָ עִקְּשׁוּת פֶּה.** לֹא תַּעֲשֶׂה דָבָר שֶׁיַּלְעִיזוּ בּוֹ הַבְּרִיּוֹת וְיַעֲקִימוּ עָלֶיךָ פִּיהֶם:


**Take crooked speech away from yourself** Don’t do anything for which people will slander you and make their mouths crooked [when talking] about you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:2)


**עִקְּשׁוּת פֶּה.** לְשׁוֹן עִקּוּם, כְּמוֹ ״תָּם אָנִי וַיַּעְקְּשֵׁנִי״ (איוב ט:כ). וּבַמִּשְׁנָה (חולין נו.): שִׁנֶּיהָ עֲקוּמוֹת וַעֲקוּשׁוֹת:


**crooked speech** Heb. עקשות. This is an expression of עקום, crooked, as in (Job 9:20): “Though I was innocent, He would prove me perverse (ויעקשני),” and in the Mishnah (Hullin 3:3) “its teeth are crooked and twisted (עקושות).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:24:3)


**וּלְזוּת שְׂפָתַיִם.** עִקְּמִימוּת שְׂפָתַיִם, שֶׁלֹּא יַרְחִיבוּ הַבְּרִיּוֹת פֶּה עָלֶיךָ:


**devious lips** Crooked lips, that the people should not open their mouth wide [when talking] about you.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:25:1)


**עֵינֶיךָ לְנֹכַח יַבִּיטוּ.** יִסְתַּכְּלוּ אֶל הָאֱמֶת וְאֶל הַיֹּשֶׁר:


**Let your eyes look forward** Let them look to truth and uprightness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:25:2)


**יַיְשִׁירוּ נֶגְדֶּךָ.** יִסְתַּכְּלוּ עַל הַיְשָׁרָה, לְיַשֵּׁר דַּרְכְּךָ נֶגְדֶּךָ:


**and let your eyelids look straight ahead of you** Let them look at what is upright, to straighten your way before you.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:26:1)


**פַּלֵּס מַעְגַּל רַגְלֶךָ.** שְׁקוֹל דַּרְכְּךָ: הֶפְסֵד מִצְוָה כְּנֶגֶד שְׂכָרָהּ, וּשְׂכַר עֲבֵרָה כְּנֶגֶד הֶפְסֵדָהּ (אבות ב:א), וְאָז **כָּל דְּרָכֶיךָ יִכֹּנוּ**:


**Weigh the path of your feet** Weigh your way: the loss of a commandment against its reward and the reward of a sin against its loss, and then all your ways will be established.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 4:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/4:27:1)


**אַל תֵּט יָמִין וּשְׂמֹאול.** מִן הַמִּשְׁקָל הַטּוֹב:


**Turn neither right nor left** of the good weight.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:1:1)


**בְּנִי לְחָכְמָתִי הַקְשִׁיבָה וְגוֹ׳**:



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:1)


**כִּי נֹפֶת תִּטֹּפְנָה.** לְשׁוֹן ״מָתוֹק״ (משלי כד:יג):


**drip honey** Heb. נפת, an expression of sweetness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:2)


**שִׂפְתֵי זָרָה.** אֲפִיקוֹרְסוּת:


**the lips of a strange woman** Apostasy.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:3:3)


**וְחָלָק מִשֶּׁמֶן חִכָּהּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״חֵךְ״ (איוב יב:יא):


**her palate** Heb. חכה, an expression of חךּ, a palate.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:5:1)


**רַגְלֶיהָ יוֹרְדוֹת מָוֶת שְׁאוֹל צְעָדֶיהָ יִתְמֹכוּ.** לְשׁוֹן קוּרְבָה:


**come near** Heb. יתמכו, an expression of nearness.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:6:1)


**אֹרַח חַיִּים פֶּן תְּפַלֵּס.** כָּךְ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ חֲכָמִים (דברים רבה ו:ב): סִלְסֵל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַתַּן שְׂכָרָן שֶׁל מִצְווֹת וְלֹא פֵּרְשָׁן, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִרְאֶה אָדָם מִצְוָה שֶׁמַּתַּן שְׂכָרָהּ מְרֻבֶּה וְיִדְבַּק בָּהּ, וְיַנִּיחַ שְׁאָר הַמִּצְווֹת. וְזֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תְּפַלֵּס אָרְחוֹת הַתּוֹרָה, אֵי זוֹ לִיקַּח וְאֵי זוֹ לְהַנִּיחַ, לְכָךְ **נָעוּ מַעְגְּלֹתֶיהָ.** הֵנִיעַ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא נְתִיבוֹתֶיהָ וְלֹא הוֹדִיעָם. זֶהוּ מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, עקב ב). אֲבָל לְפִי סֵדֶר הַמִּקְרָאוֹת שֶׁכָּתוּב אַחֲרָיו **הַרְחֵק מֵעָלֶיהָ דַרְכֶּךָ** – נִרְאֶה, שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא הִפְסִיק בְּעִנְיַן אִשָּׁה הַזּוֹנָה, וְכֵן יֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ: **אֹרַח חַיִּים פֶּן תְּפַלֵּס.** אַל תִּשְׁקֹל אֹרַח חַיִּים אֵצֶל אוֹתָהּ זָרָה, לוֹמַר: אֵיזוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת זוֹ אוֹ זוֹ? כִּי מַעְגְּלוֹת הַזּוֹנָה נָעוּ לִשְׁאוֹל, וְלֹא תֵדַע לְהִזָּהֵר עַד אֲשֶׁר תָּנוּעַ וְתִפּוֹל:


**Lest you weigh the path of life** So did the Sages (Tanhuma Ekev 2) expound it: The Holy One, blessed be He, moved away (טלטל) the reward for the commandments and did not explain it [the reward], so that a person should not see a commandment for which the reward is great and cling to it and leave over the other commandments. This is the interpretation: Lest you weigh the ways of the Torah, which one to take and which one to leave, therefore its paths wandered off—God caused them to wander off and He did not make them known. This is the Midrash Aggadah, but according to the sequence of the verses, since after it is written: “And now, children, hearken to me and do not turn away from the sayings of my mouth. Distance your way from her,” it appears that he has not yet ended the section dealing with the harlot, and it should be interpreted as follows: **Lest you weigh the path of life** Do not weigh the path of life in conjunction with that strange one, saying what to do, whether this one or this one [i.e., whether to follow the path of life or the path of the strange woman], for the paths of the harlot have wandered off to the grave, and you will not know how to beware until you wander off and fall.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:7:1)


**וְעַתָּה בָנִים שִׁמְעוּ לִי וְאַל תָּסוּרוּ מֵאִמְרֵי פִי**:



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:9:1)


**פֶּן תִּתֵּן לַאֲחֵרִים הוֹדֶךָ.** פֶּן תַּפְנֶה לִבְּךָ לֵאלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים, לָתֵת לָהֶם תִּפְאֶרֶת הוֹדְךָ וְשִׁבְחֲךָ:


**lest you give others your glory** Lest you turn your heart to other gods to give them the glory of your beauty and your praise.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:9:2)


**וּשְׁנוֹתֶיךָ לְאַכְזָרִי.** לְשַׂר שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם:


**and your years to a cruel one** To the prince of Gehinnom.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:1)


**פֶּן יִשְׂבְּעוּ זָרִים כֹּחֶךָ.** נְבִיאֵי הַבַּעַל הַגּוֹבִין מָמוֹן בְּשִׁקְרֵיהֶם וּבְפַחֲזוּתָם (ירמיהו כג:לב):


**lest strangers be sated with your strength** The prophets of Baal, who collect money with their lies and their hastiness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:2)


**וַעֲצָבֶיךָ.** עֲצָבֶיךָ וִיגִיעֲךָ, שֶׁנֶּעֱצַבְתָּ וְטָרַחְתָּ בּוֹ:


**and your labors** And your toil, with which you were saddened and fatigued.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:10:3)


**בְּבֵית נָכְרִי.** בְּבֵית עוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים:


**in the house of an alien** In the pagan temple of the pagans.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:11:1)


**וְנָהַמְתָּ בְאַחֲרִיתֶךָ.** סוֹף שֶׁתִּנְהוֹם בְּאַחֲרִיתֶךָ:


**And you shall moan when your end comes** The end will be that you will moan when your end comes.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:14:1)


**כִּמְעַט הָיִיתִי בְכָל רָע.** כְּפֶשַׂע בֵּינִי וּבֵין גֵּיהִנֹּם, (כְּלוֹמַר: בִּשְׁבִיל דָּבָר מוּעָט הָיִיתִי עַכְשָׁיו **בְּכָל רָע**, שֶׁלֹּא שָׁמַעְתִּי לְקוֹל מוֹרַי; שֶׁאִלּוּ שָׁמַעְתִּי לָהֶם, לֹא יֶאֱרַע בִּי כָּךְ רבי יוסף קרא):


**I was almost in all evil** There is but a step between me and Gehinnom. (Because of a little thing, I was now “in all evil,” for I did not obey my instructors; had I obeyed them, this would not have happened to me. From Rabbi Joseph Kara.)



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:1)


**שְׁתֵה מַיִם מִבּוֹרֶךָ.** מִבּוֹר שֶׁנָּתַן לְךָ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְחֶלְקְךָ, הִיא תּוֹרַת מֹשֶׁה:


**Drink water from your own cistern** From the cistern that the Holy One, blessed be He, gave you for your share, viz. the Law of Moses.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:2)


**בּוֹרֶךָ.** מַיִם מְכֻנָּסִים:


**your own cistern** This denotes gathered water.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:15:3)


**וְנוֹזְלִים מִתּוֹךְ בְּאֵרֶךָ.** מַיִם חַיִּים. כְּלוֹמַר: תְּחִלָּה כְּמַיִם מְכֻנָּסִים, וּלְבַסּוֹף נוֹבְעִין הֵם וְהוֹלְכִין:


**and running water** Heb. ונזלים, spring water. In the beginning it is like gathered water, and at the end it gushes out more and more.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:16:1)


**יָפוּצוּ מַעְיְנוֹתֶיךָ חוּצָה.** סוֹף שֶׁתִּקְנֶה תַּלְמִידִים, וְתוֹרֶה הוֹרָאוֹת בָּרַבִּים, וְיֵצֵא לְךָ שֵׁם:


**May your springs spread out** In the end you will acquire disciples, and you will promulgate decisions in public, and you will gain a reputation.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:16:2)


**בָּרְחוֹבוֹת.** עִיר תְּפוּצֶינָה פַּלְגֵי מֵימֶיךָ:


**in the squares** of the city the rivulets of your water shall spread.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:17:1)


**יִהְיוּ לְךָ לְבַדֶּךָ.** אַתָּה לְבַדְּךָ תִּתְכַּבֵּד בָּהֶם, וְלֹא יַחֲלֹק אַחֵר עִמְּךָ. לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה **פֶּן יִשְׂבְּעוּ זָרִים כֹּחֶךָ**, אָמַר כָּאן **יִהְיוּ לְךָ לְבַדֶּךָ**; וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ:


**You alone shall have them** You alone will be honored by them, and no one else will share your honor with you. Since he says above, “Lest strangers be satisfied with your strength,” he says here, “You alone shall have them, and thereby...”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:18:1)


**יְהִי מְקוֹרְךָ בָרוּךְ וּשְׂמַח מֵאֵשֶׁת נְעוּרֶיךָ.** הִיא הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁלָּמַדְתָּ מִנְּעוּרֶיךָ:


**Your fountain shall be blessed, and you shall rejoice with the wife of your youth** That is the Torah which you learned in your youth.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:19:1)


**תִּשְׁגֶּה תָמִיד.** רָאִיתִי בְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּי מֹשֶׁה הַדַּרְשָׁן: תִּשְׁגֶּה. תַּעֲסֹק תָּמִיד, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי. וְהֵבִיא רְאָיָה, כְּמוֹ ׳לְבַקֵּשׁ שְׁגִייָה – לְבַקֵּשׁ עֵסֶק׳; וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי אֵיפֹה נִשְׁנֵית. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (עירובין נד:): לְשׁוֹן ׳מַשְׁגֶּה׳ כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: בַּעֲבוּר אַהֲבָתָהּ תִּהְיֶה שׁוֹגֵג בִּשְׁאָר עֲסָקֶיךָ, כִּי הִיא מְשַׁמֶּרֶת עַל שֶׁלְּךָ. אָמְרוּ עָלָיו עַל רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בֶּן פְּדָת, שֶׁהָיָה דּוֹרֵשׁ בַּשּׁוּק הַתַּחְתּוֹן, וְסְדִינוֹ מֻטָּל בַּשּׁוּק הָעֶלְיוֹן. פַּעַם אַחַת בָּא אָדָם אֶחָד וּמָצָא נָחָשׁ כָּרוּךְ עָלָיו:


**you shall always be intoxicated** Heb. תשגה. I saw in the words of Rabbi Moshe Hadarshan that תשגה means “you shall always busy yourself,” and it is an Arabic expression. He cited as proof, “to seek shegiah,” meaning to seek business, but I do not know where it is stated. But our Sages explained it as an expression of intoxication (lit. inadvertence), as its apparent meaning. On account of her love, you shall be inadvertent in your other affairs, because she will guard your things. They said concerning Rabbi Eleazar the son of Pedath, that he was preaching in the lower market and his cloak was lying in the upper market. Once a man came and found a snake coiled on it.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:21:1)


**כִּי נֹכַח עֵינֵי ה׳ דַּרְכֵי אִישׁ וְגוֹ׳ מְפַלֵּס.** – שׁוֹקֵל דְּרָכָיו וְיוֹדֵעַ כַּמָּה עֲוֹנוֹת וְכַמָּה זְכֻיּוֹת בְּיָדוֹ:


**For man’s ways are opposite the Lord’s eyes, and He weighs, etc.** He weighs his ways and knows how many sins and how many merits he has.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:22:1)


**עֲווֹנוֹתָיו יִלְכְּדֻנוֹ.** כְּמוֹ יִלְכְּדוּהוּ:


**His iniquities shall trap** Heb. ילכדנו it. shall trap him, like ילכדוהו.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:22:2)


**וּבְחַבְלֵי חַטָּאתוֹ יִתָּמֵךְ.** יִתָּלֶה, שֶׁהַתָּלוּי נִתְמָךְ בַּחֶבֶל שֶׁהוּא תָּלוּי בָּהּ:


**and he shall be hanged with the ropes of his sin** יתמך, lit. he shall be supported. He shall be hanged, for the one hanged is supported by the ropes with which he is hanged.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 5:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/5:23:1)


**הוּא יָמוּת בְּאֵין מוּסָר.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁלֹּא לָקַח מוּסָר:


**He shall die without discipline** Because he did not accept discipline.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:1:1)


**בְּנִי אִם עָרַבְתָּ.** עֲרֵבוּת מָמוֹן כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (בבא בתרא קעג:):


**My son, if you have stood surety** Our Sages explained this as referring to surety in monetary matters, according to its apparent meaning.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:2:1)


**נוֹקַשְׁתָּ בְאִמְרֵי פִיךָ.** שֶׁתָּקַעְתָּ כַּף לְהִדָּבֵר עִם הַזָּרִים (יומא פז.):



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:1)


**לֵךְ הִתְרַפֵּס.** הַתֵּר לוֹ פַּס יָד, לְשַׁלֵּם לוֹ מָמוֹנוֹ:


**go, humble yourself** Heb. התרפס [a combination of two words, התר פס], open the palm of your hand for him to pay him his money.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:2)


**וּרְהַב רֵעֶיךָ.** וְאִם אֵין לוֹ מָמוֹן עִמְּךָ, אֶלָּא שֶׁנּוֹקַשְׁתָּ בְּאִמְרֵי פִיךָ לְדַבֵּר לוֹ קָשׁוֹת, הַרְבֵּה עָלָיו רֵעִים לְבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ שֶׁיִּמְחוֹל לְךָ:


**and give your fellow superiority** Heb. ורהב. And if he has no money with you, only that you were trapped with the sayings of your mouth by speaking harshly to him, bring many friends to him to beg him to forgive you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:3)


דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בְּנִי אִם עָרַבְתָּ לְרֵעֶךָ.** אַחַר שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂיתָ עָרֵב לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא רֵעֲךָ, כְּדִכְתִיב ״זֶה דוֹדִי וְזֶה רֵעִי״ (שיר השירים ה:טז), וְקִבַּלְתָּ עָלֶיךָ בְּסִינַי וּבְעַרְבוֹת מוֹאָב בְּאָלָה וּבִשְׁבוּעָה לִשְׁמֹר מִצְווֹתָיו:


Another explanation: **[1] My son, if you stood surety for your Friend** After you stood surety for the Holy One, blessed be He, Who is your “friend,” as it is written (Song 5:16): “This is my beloved and this is my friend”; you undertook at Sinai and in the plains of Moab with a curse and with an oath to observe His commandments.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:4)


**תָּקַעְתָּ לַזָּר כַּפֶּיךָ.** שֶׁתָּשׁוּב וְתָסוּר מִדְּרָכָיו וְתִדְבַּק בָּאֶפִּיקוֹרְסִין לָלֶכֶת בְּדַרְכֵיהֶם:


**have given your hand to a stranger** You have repented and turned from His ways and clung to the disbelievers to go in their ways.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:5)


**נוֹקַשְׁתָּ בְּאִמְרֵי פִיךָ.** שֶׁתָּקַעְתָּ כַּף לְהִדָּבֵר עִם הַזָּרִים:


**[2] you have been trapped by the sayings of your mouth** You have given your hand to cling to strangers.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:6)


**עֲשֵׂה זֹאת אֵפוֹא בְּנִי וְהִנָּצֵל.** הוֹאִיל וּבָאתָ בְכַף רֵעֶךָ בְּסִינַי, וְקִבַּלְתָּ אֱלֹהוּתוֹ עָלֶיךָ:


**[3] Do this, then, my son, and be saved** Since you have come into the palm of your Friend at Sinai and you have accepted His Godliness over you,


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:7)


**לֵךְ הִתְרַפֵּס.** הִכָּנַע לְפָנָיו כַּאֲסְקוּפָּה הַנִּרְפֶּסֶת וְנִדְרֶסֶת:


**go, humble yourself** Heb. התרפס. Humble yourself before Him like a threshold, which is trodden (נרפסת) and stepped on.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:3:8)


**וּרְהַב רֵעֶיךָ.** הַרְבֵּה רֵעִים שֶׁיִּתְפַּלְּלוּ עָלֶיךָ לְפָנָיו. וְכֵן נִדְרַשׁ בְּמִדְרַשׁ תְּהִלִּים :


**and increase your friends** Bring many friends who will pray for you before Him. In this manner it is expounded on in Midrash Psalms.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:5:1)


**הִנָּצֵל כִּצְבִי מִיָּד.** מַהֵר וְהִשָּׁמֵט מִשָּׁם כִּצְבִי הַנִּמְלָט מִיַּד הָאָדָם:


**Save yourself like a deer from the hand** Hasten and extricate yourself from there like a deer that extricates itself from a man’s hand.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:6:1)


**לֵךְ אֶל נְמָלָה עָצֵל וְגוֹ׳.** וַחֲכָם – וְהִתְחַכֵּם:


**Go to the ant, you sluggard...and become wise** Heb. וחכם, and wisen yourself.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:7:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אֵין לָהּ קָצִין.** שֶׁיּוֹכִיחַ אוֹתָהּ וִיזָרְזֶנָּה וְיוֹצִיא מִיָּדָהּ אִם תִּגְזֹל דָּבָר מֵחֲבֶרְתָּהּ, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן:


**for she has no chief** who would reprove her and alert her and take out of her hand if she steals anything from her companion, nevertheless...



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:8:1)


**תָּכִין בַּקַּיִץ לַחְמָהּ אָגְרָה בַקָּצִיר מַאֲכָלָהּ.** מְזוֹנָהּ, כָּל אַחַת וְאַחַת אֵינָהּ גּוֹזֶלֶת מֵחֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


**yet she prepares her bread in the summer, she gathers her food in the harvest** She gathers her food, each one of them, and does not rob her companion.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:10:1)


**חִבּוּק יָדַיִם.** הַיָּשֵׁן חוֹבֵק יָדָיו, אַבְרַאצַ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a little folding of the hands** The sleeper folds his hands, embrasser in French (abrasar in Provencal,) umfassen [in German], to embrace. In Rashi ms. plier in French [to fold] in German et was falten, to fold a little.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:11:1)
