**וּבָא כִמְהַלֵּךְ רֵאשֶׁךָ.** אִם תַּעֲשֶׂה כֵּן יָבֹא חֶסְרוֹנְךָ, וְדָבָר שֶׁאַתָּה רָשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ יָבֹא לְךָ מִיָּד, כְּאָדָם הַמְהַלֵּךְ מַהֵר; **וּמַחְסֹרְךָ** יָבֹא וְיִתְמַלֵּא:


**And your poverty shall come like a fast walker** If you do this [keep sleeping], your loss and the thing from which you are impoverished shall come to you immediately, like a man walking fast, and your want will come and fill up


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:11:2)


**כְּאִישׁ מָגֵן.** הַבָּא מַהֵר לְהָגֵן עַל אֲדוֹנָיו. הַמִּקְרָאוֹת אֵלּוּ – עִקָּרָן מָשָׁל עַל הַמִּתְעַצְּלִין לַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה:


**like an armed man.** who comes quickly to protect his master. These verses are mainly an allegory concerning those who are [too] lazy to engage in the Torah.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:12:1)


**הוֹלֵךְ עִקְּשׁוּת פֶּה.** הַהוֹלֵךְ בַּעֲקִימַת שְׂפָתַיִם:


**walks with a crooked mouth** He walks with crooked lips.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:1)


**קוֹרֵץ בְּעֵינָיו.** רְמִיזוֹת שֶׁל מִרְמָה:


**he winks with his eyes** winks of deceit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:2)


**מוֹלֵל בְּרַגְלָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר: מְשַׁפְשֵׁף זֶה עַל זֶה:


**shuffles with his feet** He rubs one on the other.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:13:3)


**וּמוֹרֶה בְּאֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו.** כֻּלָּם מִן לְשׁוֹן רְמָזִים הֵם, זֶה נוֹפֵל עַל הָעַיִן וְזֶה נוֹפֵל עַל הָרֶגֶל וְזֶה נוֹפֵל עַל הָאֶצְבַּע. וְהָעִקָּר – הוּא מְדַבֵּר עַל הָרְשָׁעִים הַמְּסִיתִין אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת לַעֲבוֹדָת גִּלּוּלִים:


**points with his fingers** They are all expressions of hinting: one applies to the eye, one applies to the foot, and one applies to the finger, but the main idea is that it is speaking of the wicked.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:14:1)


**מְדָנִים יְשַׁלַּח.** בֵּין אָדָם לְבוֹרְאוֹ:


**he incites quarrels** between man and his Creator.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:15:1)


**פִּתְאוֹם.** וּפֶתַע לְשׁוֹן תְּכִיפָה הִיא. וְלֹא יָדַע אֶת הַמַּפָּלָה הַקְּרוֹבָה לִפּוֹל עָלָיו:


**suddenly, etc. suddenly** Heb. פתע פתאם. This is an expression of immediacy; he will not be aware of the downfall that is ready to descend upon him.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:16:1)


**שֵׁשׁ הֵנָּה שָׂנֵא ה׳ וְשֶׁבַע.** תּוֹעֲבַת נַפְשׁוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר: גַּם הַשְּׁבִיעִית עִמָּהֶם:


**There are six things that the Lord hates, and the seventh is an abomination of His soul** The seventh, too, is [included] with them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:19:1)


**יָפִיחַ.** לְשׁוֹן דִּבּוּר הוּא, כִּי כָל דִּבּוּר בְּרוּחַ הַפֶּה הוּא:


**[one who] speaks** Heb. יפיח, lit. he blows. This is an expression of speech since all speech is a product of the breath of the mouth.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:20:1)


**נְצֹר בְּנִי מִצְוַת אָבִיךָ**:



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:21:1)


**עָנְדֵם.** לְשׁוֹן קֶשֶׁר הוּא, כְּמוֹ ״אֶעֶנְדֶנּוּ עֲטָרוֹת לִי״ (איוב לא:לו):


**tie them** Heb. ענדם, an expression of tying, as in (Job 31:36) “I will tie it (אענדנו) as crowns to me.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:1)


**בְּהִתְהַלֶּכְךָ.** בְּחַיֶּיךָ:


**When you walk** in your lifetime.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:2)


**תַּנְחֶה אוֹתָךְ.** כְּמוֹ תַּנְהִיגְךָ:


**it shall lead you** Heb. תנחה אותך, like תנהיגך.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:3)


**בְּשָׁכְבְּךָ.** בַּקֶּבֶר:


**when you lie down** in the grave.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:4)


**וְהַקִיצוֹתָ הִיא תְשִׂיחֶךָ.** לִתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים, לַעֲמֹד בַּדִּין:


**and when you awaken** for the resurrection of the dead, to stand in judgment.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:22:5)


**הִיא תְשִׂיחֶךָ.** תָּלִיץ בַּעֲדֶךָ (תנחומא, ויצא ב):


**it shall speak for you** It shall speak on your behalf.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:23:1)


**כִּי נֵר מִצְוָה וְתוֹרָה אוֹר.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָאוֹר מֵאִיר לָעוֹלָם תָּמִיד, כָּךְ הַתּוֹרָה עוֹמֶדֶת זְכוּתָהּ לְעוֹלָם אֶל הָאָדָם; וּזְכוּת מִצְוָה לְפִי שָׁעָה, כְּאוֹר הַנֵּר. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כִּי נֵר מִצְוָה וְגוֹ׳** מִצְוַת הָאָב הוּא נֵר: כָּל מִי שֶׁמְּקַיֵּם מִצְוַת אָבִיו, כְּאִלּוּ נוֹטֵל נֵר בְּיָדוֹ לְהַדְלִיק בִּמְקוֹם חֹשֶׁךְ, וְאִם אָבַד שׁוּם דָּבָר, שָׁם הוּא מוֹצְאוֹ לְאוֹרוֹ. וְכֵן מִי שֶׁהוּא מְקַיֵּם הוֹרָאַת אִמּוֹ, אוֹרָה הִיא לוֹ; וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״אַל תִּטֹּשׁ תּוֹרַת אִמֶּךָ״ (משלי א:ח). וְכֵן הוּא וַדַּאי, שֶׁמִּקְרָא זֶה מְדַבֵּר בְּמִצְוַת אָבִיו וְאִמּוֹ, דִּכְתִיב ״מְקַלֵּל אָבִיו וְאִמּוֹ יִדְעַךְ נֵרוֹ בֶּאֱשׁוּן חֹשֶׁךְ״ (משלי כ:כ). וְאִם נֵר שֶׁל אָדָם יִדְעַךְ (כּוֹהֶה) כְּשֶׁאֵין מְקַיֵּם מִצְוֹת, נִמְצָא כְּשֶׁמְּקַיֵּם מִצְוֹת, מֵאִיר נֵרוֹ (רבי יוסף קרא):


**For a commandment is a candle, and the Torah is light** Just as light always illuminates, so does the merit of the Torah stand for a person forever, but the merit of the commandment is only for a limited time, like the light of a candle. Another explanation: **For a commandment is a candle, etc.** The command of the father is a candle. Whoever fulfills the command of his father is as though he takes a candle in his hand to light a dark place, and if he loses anything there he finds it by its light; similarly, whoever fulfills his mother’s instruction—it is light to him, and so [Scripture] states (verse 20): “and forsake not the instruction of your mother.” It is certain that this verse speaks of the commands of one’s father and mother, for it is written (20:20): “If one curses his father or mother, his candle will be put out in the blackest darkness.” Now if a person’s candle goes out when he does not fulfill the commandments of his parents, conversely when he fulfills the command, his candle lights up (Rabbi Joseph Kara).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:23:2)


**וְדֶרֶךְ חַיִּים תּוֹכְחוֹת מוּסָר.** תּוֹכְחוֹת הַמּוּסָר הֵם מַטִּים אֶת הָאָדָם לְחַיִּים, נִמְצָא שֶׁהֵם דֶּרֶךְ הַחַיִּים:


**and disciplining rebukes are the way of life** Disciplining rebukes incline a person to life. It is found that they are the way of life.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:24:1)


**לִשְׁמָרְךָ מֵאֵשֶׁת רָע.** הַתּוֹרָה תִּשְׁמָרְךָ מֵאִשָּׁה שֶׁל מִנְהָג רָע. וְעַל כָּרְחֲךָ לֹא דִּבֵּר שְׁלֹמֹה אִשָּׁה רָעָה, אֶלָּא כְּנֶגֶד עֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים שֶׁהִיא שְׁקוּלָה כְּנֶגֶד הַכֹּל. שֶׁאִם תֹּאמַר: אִשָּׁה זוֹנָה מַמָּשׁ, וְכִי כָּל שְׂכָרָהּ וְשִׁבְחָהּ שֶׁל תּוֹרָה שֶׁשּׁוֹמֶרֶת מִן הַזּוֹנָה לְבַדָּהּ?! אֶלָּא עַל כָּרְחֲךָ זוֹ עֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים, שֶׁהִיא חֲמוּרָה כְּנֶגֶד הַכֹּל:


**to guard you from an evil woman** The Torah shall guard you from a woman of evil behavior—perforce, Solomon did not speak of an evil woman, but concerning idolatry, [the prohibition of] which is equal in gravity to all [commandments], for if you say that he meant a real harlot, is that all the reward and the praise of the Torah that it guards one only from a harlot? Rather, perforce this is idolatry, [the prohibition of] which is as stringent as all other [commandments].


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:24:2)


**מֵחֶלְקַת לָשׁוֹן.** מִטְּחוֹת עֵינַיִם שֶׁל לָשׁוֹן נָכְרִיָּה:


**from the smoothness of the alien tongue** From the sealing of the eyes by an alien tongue.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:25:1)


**וְאַל תִּקָּחֲךָ.** וְאַל תִּטּוֹל חָכְמָתְךָ מִמְּךָ בְּעַפְעַפֵּי עֵינֶיהָ, שֶׁקּוֹרֶצֶת בָּהֶם, **בְּעַפְעַפֶּיהָ**:


**do not let her captivate you** And let her not take away your wisdom from you with her eyelids that she winks at you.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:26:1)


**כִּי בְעַד אִשָּׁה זוֹנָה עַד כִּכַּר לָחֶם.** לִידֵי עֲנִיּוּת וְחֹסֶר כָּל טוֹב:


**Because a man is brought to a loaf of bread for a harlot** He is reduced to poverty and the lack of all good things.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:26:2)


**נֶפֶשׁ יְקָרָה תָצוּד.** נֶפֶשׁ שֶׁהָיְתָה יְקָרָה קוֹדֶם לָכֵן, בָּאָה וְצָדָה לְגֵיהִנֹּם (סוטה ד:):



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:27:1)


**הֲיַחְתֶּה אִישׁ.** הֲיִשְׁאַב אִישׁ גֶּחָלִים בְּשִׁפּוּלֵי בְגָדָיו וְלֹא יִשָּׂרְפוּ?! כָּל לְשׁוֹן חֲתִיַּת גֶּחָלִים לְשׁוֹן שְׁאִיבָה הוּא כְּמַשְׁמָעָהּ: כְּשֶׁמְּמַלֵּא הַכְּלִי בְּתוֹךְ הַמְּדוּרָה:


**Can a man rake** Can a man pick up live coals with the skirt of his garments without burning them? Every expression of חתיה is an expression of raking according to its apparent meaning, that he fills the vessel in the fire.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:29:1)


**אֶל אֵשֶׁת רֵעֵהוּ.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ. וְאַף עַל עֲבוֹדָת גִּלּוּלִים יֵשׁ לְדָרְשָׁהּ (שמות רבה לג:ז), שֶׁהִיא מְיֻחֶדֶת לַגּוֹיִם וְלֹא לְךָ:


**to his neighbor’s wife** According to its apparent meaning, and it can also be expounded upon as referring to the idolatry of the pagans.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:30:1)


**לֹא יָבוּזוּ וְגוֹ׳ וְנִמְצָא יְשַׁלֵּם וְגוֹ׳ נֹאֵף אִשָּׁה וְגוֹ׳.** שְׁלָשְׁתָּם מְחֻבָּרִים בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד. אִם **יִגְנֹב הַגַּנָּב** גְּנֵבָה אֵין לְבַזּוֹתוֹ כָּל כָּךְ כְּמוֹ הַנּוֹאֵף, לָמָּה כִּי לְרַעֲבוֹן נַפְשׁוֹ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה וְאוּלַי אֵין לוֹ מַה יֹּאכַל, וּכְשֶׁנִּמְצָא יֵשׁ לוֹ תַּקָּנָה בְּתַשְׁלוּמִין, וּלְכָל הַיּוֹתֵר יְשַׁלֵּם שִׁבְעָתַיִם כְּלוֹמַר תַּשְׁלוּמֵי כֶּפֶל הַרְבֵּה וַאֲפִלּוּ חֲמִשִּׁים עַל אֶחָד, וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים **שִׁבְעָתַיִם** כְּמוֹ גּוֹנֵב שׁוֹר וְכֵלָיו וּטְבָחוֹ שֶׁמְּשַׁלֵּם חֲמִשָּׁה בָקָר וְתַשְׁלוּמֵי כֶּפֶל עַל הַכֵּלִים (שמות כא:לז) הֲרֵי שִׁבְעָה: **אֶת כָּל הוֹן בֵּיתוֹ יִתֵּן.** וַאֲפִלּוּ צָרִיךְ לִמְכֹּר כָּל אֲשֶׁר לוֹ עַל זֶה מִכָּל מָקוֹם יֵשׁ לוֹ תַּקָּנָה וּמִתְּחִלָּה מֵחֲמַת רָעָב עָשָׂה, אֲבָל:


**They will not despise a thief,** etc. **And if he is found, he will pay sevenfold,** etc. **One who commits adultery with a woman** The three of them are combined in one statement. If a thief commits a theft, he should not be despised as much as the adulterer. Why? Because he steals in order to sate his hunger, and perhaps he has nothing to eat. And, when he is found, he can rectify his sin with payment; at the most, he will pay sevenfold i.e., many times the double payment and even fifty times for one. Some explain “sevenfold” as referring to one who steals an ox and his trappings and slaughters it, who pays five cattle and double payment of the trappings (totalling seven). **he must give all he owns** lit. he must give all the property of his house. And, even if he must sell all he owns because of this, he can nevertheless rectify it, and from the beginning he did it because of hunger. But...



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:32:1)


**נוֹאֵף אִשָּׁה חֲסַר לֵב.** הוּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא לְרַעֲבוֹן עָשָׂה:


**One who commits adultery with a woman is devoid of sense** since he does not do it because of hunger.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:32:2)


**מַשְׁחִית נַפְשׁוֹ הוּא יַעֲשֶׂנָּה.** הַזִּמָּה:


**one who would destroy his soul—he will do it** The lewd act.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:33:1)


**נֶגַע וְקָלוֹן יִמְצָא.** עַל עֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים וְעַל גִּלּוּי עֲרָיוֹת הַנְּגָעִים בָּאִים:


**He will find wounds and disgrace** Lesions are sent for idolatry and immorality.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:34:1)


**כִּי קִנְאָה.** מִתְקַנֵּא בּוֹ לְהִפָּרַע בּוֹ חֲמָתוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהוּא גִּבּוֹר עַל כֹּל, **וְלֹא יַחְמוֹל בְּיוֹם נָקָם**:


**for jealousy** For the jealousy of the Holy One, blessed be He, Who is the Mighty One over all, will be aroused to mete out retribution upon him, and He will not have pity on the day of vengeance.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Proverbs 6:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/6:35:1)


**לֹא יִשָּׂא.** פָּנִים, לְכָל מָמוֹן לְכַפֵּר עַל אֲשֶׁר כָּפַר בּוֹ וְנִדְבַּק בַּעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (תוספתא בבא קמא ז:ג): לֹא יָבוּזוּ לַגַּנָּב, זֶה הַמִּתְגַּנֵּב מֵאַחַר חֲבֵירוֹ הוֹלֵךְ לְבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ וְעוֹסֵק בַּתּוֹרָה, וְנִמְצָא יְשַׁלֵּם שִׁבְעָתַיִם, סוֹפוֹ שֶׁמִּתְמַנֶּה דַּיָּן וּמוֹרֶה הוֹרָאוֹת שֶׁאֵין שִׁבְעָתַיִם אֶלָּא תּוֹרָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מְזֻקָּק שִׁבְעָתָיִם״ (תהלים יב:ז):


**He will not have regard** for any money to expiate for his denial of Him and his cleaving to idolatry. And our Rabbis expounded (Tosefta Baba Kamma 7:3): They will not despise a thief—This is one who steals away from his friend and goes to the study hall and engages in Torah. If he is found, he will pay sevenfold. [The intention is that] eventually, he will be appointed as a judge, and he will render legal decisions, for “sevenfold” refers only to the Torah, as it is said (Ps. 12:7): “refined sevenfold.”



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:1:1)


**בְּנִי שְׁמֹר אֲמָרַי וּמִצְוֹתַי**:



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:2:1)


**וְתוֹרָתִי כְּאִישׁוֹן עֵינֶיךָ.** שָׁחוֹר שֶׁל עַיִן שֶׁהוּא דּוֹמֶה לַחֹשֶׁךְ, כְּמוֹ **אִישׁוֹן לַיְלָה**:


**like the apple of your eyes** The pupil of the eye, which is like darkness, like the darkness of night.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:4:1)


(**אֲחוֹתִי אָתְּ.** קָרְבֶנָּה אֵלֶיךָ):


**“You are my sister”** (Draw her near to you.)


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:4:2)


**וּמוֹדָע.** קָרוֹב, כְּמוֹ ״בֹּעַז מוֹדַעְתֵּנוּ״ (רות ג:ב) – קְרוֹבֵנוּ. כְּלוֹמַר: קָרְבֶנָּה אֶצְלְךָ תָּמִיד:


**a kinsman** Heb. מדע, a kinsman, as in (Ruth 3: 2): “Boaz our kinsman (מדעתנו),” our close relative; i.e., draw her near to you always.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:7:1)


**אָבִינָה.** הִבְחַנְתִּי וְרָאִיתִי:


**I discerned** Heb. אבינה, I discerned and I saw.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:8:1)


**אֵצֶל פִּנָּה.** פִּנָּה שֶׁל זוֹנָה וַעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים:


**next to her corner** The corner of the harlot and of the pagan house of worship.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:1)


**וְהִנֵּה אִשָּׁה.** כְּמַשְׁמָעָהּ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: אֶחָד מִן אֶפִּיקוֹרְסִים:


**And behold a woman** As its apparent meaning. Another explanation: One of the enticers.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:2)


**שִׁית זוֹנָה.** כְּמוֹ ״שְׁתוֹתֵיהֶם״ (שמואל ב י:ד), כְּלוֹמַר: עֶרְוַת זוֹנָה:


**the nakedness of a harlot** Heb. שית, as in (II Sam. 10:4): “their buttocks (שתותיהם),” i.e., the nakedness of a harlot.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:10:3)


**וּנְצוּרַת לֵב.** כְּמוֹ ״עִיר נְצוּרָה״ (ישעיה א:ח) הַמּוּקֶּפֶת כַּרְכּוֹם, וְכֵן לִבָּהּ שֶׁל זוֹ מוּקֶּפֶת זִמָּה אִוֶּלֶת:


**with her heart besieged** Heb. ונצרת לב. As a besieged city is surrounded by bulwarks, so is this one’s heart surrounded by lewdness and foolishness.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:11:1)


**וְסוֹרָרֶת.** סָרָה מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ:


**and rebellious** Heb. וסררת, turning away from the road.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:14:1)


**זִבְחֵי שְׁלָמִים עָלָי.** כְּלוֹמַר: סְעוּדָּה גְּדוֹלָה הֲכִינוֹתִי, כִּי **הַיּוֹם** הִקְרַבְתִּי נְדָרַי וּשְׁלָמַי:


**I had to bring peace offerings** I prepared a great feast, for today I sacrificed my vows and my peace offering.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:15:1)


**וָאֶמְצָאֶךָּ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁאֶמְצָאֶךָּ:


**and I have found you** In order that I find you.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:1)


**מַרְבַּדִּים.** בִּגְדֵי חֹפֶשׁ וְנוֹי, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מַרְבַדִּים עָשְׂתָה לָּהּ״ (משלי לא:כב):


**covers** Heb. מרבדים. Garments of freedom and beauty; a similar term is found at the end of the book (31:22): “She made covers for herself.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:2)


**רָבַדְתִּי עַרְשִׂי.** קִשַּׁטְתִּי:


**I have bedecked my couch** Heb. רבדתי, I have adorned.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:16:3)


**חֲטוּבוֹת אֵטוּן מִצְרָיִם.** מְהֻלָּלוֹת בִּגְדֵי כְּלֵי פִשְׁתָּן חֲשׁוּבִין הַבָּאִים מִמִּצְרַיִם, שֶׁשָּׁם הַפִּשְׁתָּן מָצוּי, כְּדִכְתִיב בְּסֵפֶר (ישעיהו יט:ט) ״וּבֹשׁוּ עֹבְדֵי פִשְׁתִּים״. **אֵטוּן** תַּרְגּוּם ״מֵיתְרֵיהֶם״ (שמות לה:יח) ״אֲטוּנֵיהוֹן״:


**with superior braided work of Egypt** Heb. חטבות אטון מצרים, praiseworthy, high quality linen garments coming from Egypt, where linen is common, as it is written in the Book of Isaiah (19: 9): “And those who work at flax... shall be ashamed.” **braided work** Heb. אטון. The Aramaic translation of מיתריהם, their ropes (Num. 4:32), is אטוניהון.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:17:1)


**נַפְתִּי מִשְׁכָּבִי.** הֲנִיפוֹתִי הָרֵיחַ, כְּמֵנִיף בְּסוּדָר בְּבֵית הַבֹּשֶׂם, לְהָבִיא הָרֵיחַ מִלְמַעְלָה לְמַטָּה. וְדוּנָשׁ פֵּרֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן קִטּוּר, וְאָמַר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן:


**I fanned** Heb. נפתי. I fanned the scent as one fans with a scarf in a perfumery to bring the scent from above down below. Dunash (Teshuvoth Dunash p. 22) defines it as an expression of smoking, which he states has no comparison.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:19:1)


**כִּי אֵין הָאִישׁ בְּבֵיתוֹ.** רְאִיתֶם שֶׁסִּלֵּק הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שְׁכִינָתוֹ, וְכָל טוֹב נָתַן לְעוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים:


**For the man is not at home** You have seen that the Holy One, blessed be He, has removed His Shechinah and has given all good to the pagans.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:20:1)


**צְרוֹר הַכֶּסֶף.** הַטּוֹבִים שֶׁבָּהֶם הָרַג:


**the bag of money** He has slain the righteous among them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:20:2)


**לְיוֹם הַכֵּסֶא.** לִזְמַן הַמּוֹעֵד הַקָּבוּעַ, וְכֵן ״בַּכֶּסֶה לְיוֹם חַגֵּנוּ״ (תהלים פא:ד):


**on the appointed day** Heb. ליום הכסא. At the fixed appointed time, and similarly (Ps. 81:4), “At the appointed time for the day of our festival.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:1)


**הִטַּתּוּ.** לְאוֹתוֹ חֲסַר לֵב (משלי ז:ז) אֵלֶיהָ:


**She swayed him** the one devoid of sense, to her.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:2)


**בְּרֹב לִקְחָהּ.** שֶׁהִיא לְמוּדָה לְהַרְגִּיל בְּכָךְ אֲנָשִׁים:


**with all her talk** with which she is accustomed to familiarize men.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:21:3)


**בְּחֵלֶק שְׂפָתֶיהָ.** בְּדִבּוּר חֲלַקְלַק תַּדִּיחֶנּוּ מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ:


**she entices him** from the road.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:22:1)


**וּכְעֶכֶס.** זֶה אֶרֶס נָחָשׁ:


**and as a viper** Heb. וכעכס. This is the venom of a snake.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:22:2)


**אֶל מוּסַר אֱוִיל.** כְּנָחָשׁ הַמְּמַהֵר לָרוּץ בִּשְׁלִיחוּת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְיַסֵּר הָאֱוִיל, הַמְחֻיָּב לַמָּקוֹם בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כֵּן זֶה רָץ אַחֲרֶיהָ עַד שֶׁנִּכְשַׁל בָּהּ, וַיְפַלַּח חִצָּהּ אֶת כְּבֵדוֹ:


**to the chastisement of a fool** Like a snake that runs quickly as an agent of the Holy One, blessed be He, to chastise the fool who is condemned by the Omnipresent, blessed be He, so does this one run after her until he stumbles on her, and her arrow splits his liver.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:23:1)


**כְּמַהֵר צִפּוֹר.** לָרוּץ אֶל הַפַּח, וְאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ כִּי הַפַּח נִפְרַשׂ שָׁם בְּנַפְשׁוֹ שֶׁל צִפּוֹר:


**as a bird hastens** to run to a snare, and it does not know that the snare was spread out there for the life of the bird.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/7:24:1)


**וְעַתָּה בָנִים שִׁמְעוּ לִי וְגוֹ׳**:



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:1:1)


**הֲלֹא חָכְמָה תִקְרָא.** הֲלֹא הַתּוֹרָה מַכְרֶזֶת אֲלֵיכֶם דְּבָרִים הָאֲמוּרִים לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן:


**Will not wisdom call out** Does not the Torah announce for you the things mentioned below in this section?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:3:1)


**לְפִי קָרֶת.** תִּקְרָה הַנְּתוּנָה עַל הַשַּׁעַר, וְיוֹשְׁבִים עָלֶיהָ:


**at the entrance of the roof** Heb. קרת, the ceiling above the gate, where people sit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:3:2)


**תָּרֹנָּה.** תִּזְעַקְנָה. וּמָה הִיא אוֹמֶרֶת? **אֲלֵיכֶם אִישִׁים אֶקְרָא**:


**she cries** Heb. תרנה, she cries, and what does she say? “To you, O men, I call.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:6:1)


**שִׁמְעוּ כִּי נְגִידִים אֲדַבֵּר.** דִּבְרֵי נְגִידוּת וַחֲשִׁיבוּת:


**Hearken for I will speak noble things** Words of nobility and importance.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:8:1)


**נִפְתָּל וְעִקֵּשׁ.** עַקְמוּמִיּוּת:


**there is nothing twisted or crooked in them** There is no crookedness.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:10:1)


**מֵחָרוּץ.** מִמִּינֵי הַזָּהָב הוּא:


**gold** Heb. חרוץ. A type of gold.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:11:1)


**מִפְּנִינִים.** מַרְגָּלִיּוֹת:


**than pearls** Heb. מפנינים, pearls.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:11:2)


**לֹא יִשְׁווּ בָהּ.** לֹא יִדְמוּ לְשָׁוְיָהּ:


**cannot be compared to it** They will not equal its value.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:12:1)


**שָׁכַנְתִּי עָרְמָה.** אֵצֶל עָרְמָה, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁלָּמַד אָדָם תּוֹרָה, נִכְנַס בּוֹ עַרְמִימוּת שֶׁל כָּל דָּבָר (סוטה כא:):


**I dwelt [beside] cunning** Beside cunning, for since a man has learned Torah, cunning about every matter enters into him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:13:1)


**יִרְאַת ה׳ שְׂנֹאת רָע.** זֶהוּ הַמּוּסָר שֶׁהַחָכְמָה מַכְרֶזֶת לַבְּרִיּוֹת:


**Fear of the Lord is to hate evil** This is the discipline that wisdom announces to the people.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:15:1)


**בִּי מְלָכִים יִמְלֹכוּ.** שֶׁהַדִּינִים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִים אֲנִי מְלַמְּדָם:


**Kings reign with me** for the judges and the judgments I teach them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:1)


**אֹהַב.** כְּמוֹ אֶאֱהַב:


**I will love those who love me** Heb. אהבי. [This is the reading.] The text is written: אוהביה, those who love her. Said the Holy One, blessed be He: “I will love those who love the Torah.” I heard this from Rabbi Aaron in the name of Rabbi Nathan.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:2)


**אֹהֲבַי.** אֹהֲבֶיהָ כְּתִיב, אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: אֲנִי **אֹהֲבֶיהָ** שֶׁל תּוֹרָה **אֹהָב.** זוֹ שָׁמַעְתִּי מֵרַבִּי אַהֲרֹן, בְּשֵׁם רַבִּי נָתָן:


**I will love** Heb. אהב, like אאהב.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:17:3)


**יִמְצָאֻנְנִי.** נוּ״ן יְתֵרָה: חֲמִשִּׁים שַׁעֲרֵי בִינָה אַמְצִיאֶנּוּ:


**will find me** Heb. ימצאנני. There is an extra “nun,” denoting fifty. I will allow him to find the fifty gates of understanding.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:21:1)


**לְהַנְחִיל אוֹהֲבַי יֵשׁ.** יֵשׁ אִתִּי נַחֲלָה רַבָּה:


**There is substance to give inheritance** Heb. יש, lit. there is. There is with me a great inheritance.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:22:1)


**רֵאשִׁית דַּרְכּוֹ.** קוֹדֶם בְּרִיאָתוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם (פסחים נד.):


**at the beginning of His way** before the creation of the world.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:23:1)


**נִסַּכְתִּי מֵרֹאשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן נְסִיכֵי בְּנֵי אָדָם (מיכה ה:ד):


**I was enthroned, from the beginning** Heb. נסכתי, an expression of (Ezek. 35): “Princes (נסיכי) of the sons of man.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:24:1)


**חוֹלָלְתִּי.** נִבְרֵאתִי:


**I was created** Heb. חוללתי.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:25:1)


**בְּטֶרֶם הָרִים הָטְבָּעוּ.** בְּתוֹךְ הַמָּיִם:


**before the mountains were sunk** within the water.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:26:1)


**אֶרֶץ וְחוּצוֹת.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּשְׁאָר אֲרָצוֹת (תענית י.):


**the land and the outsides** The land of Israel and other lands.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:26:2)


**וְרֹאשׁ עַפְרוֹת תֵּבֵל.** אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן (סנהדרין לח.):


**and the beginning of the dust of the earth** The first man.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:27:1)


**בְּחֻקּוֹ חוּג עַל פְּנֵי תְהוֹם.** כְּשֶׁרָקַע חוּג הָאָרֶץ עַל הַמַּיִם, חֹק גְּבוּל בַּל יַעֲבֹר:


**when He drew a circle over the face of the deep** When He drew the circle of the earth over the water, to draw a boundary that it may not pass.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:27:2)


**חוּג.** לְשׁוֹן הֶקֵּף, כְּמוֹ ״וּבַמְּחוּגָה יְתָאֲרֵהוּ״ (ישעיה מד:יג). קוֹנְפָּא״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


חוג is an expression of encircling, as in (Isa. 44: 13): “and with a compass (ובמחוגה) he rounds it.” (Compas in French, zirkel in German, as in Gen. 29: 17, Isa. 5:22, 44:13, and Job 22: 14.)



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:28:1)


**בַּעֲזוֹז.** כְּשֶׁהִגְבִּיר מַעְיְנוֹת תְּהוֹם:


**when He strengthened the fountains of the deep** When He made mighty the fountains of the deep.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:29:1)


**בְּשׂוּמוֹ לַיָּם חֻקּוֹ.** וְגָזַר עַל יַם סוּף כְּשֶׁבְּרָאוֹ, עַל מְנָת לְהִבָּקַע לִפְנֵי מֹשֶׁה (שמות יד:כא):


**when He gave the sea its boundary** and decreed upon the Reed Sea when he created it, on the condition that it split before Moses.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:29:2)


**בְּחֻקּוֹ מוֹסְדֵי אָרֶץ.** בְּחִקָּתוֹ, מִלְּשׁוֹן חֻקּוֹ; כְּמוֹ ״עַל כַּפַּיִם חַקֹּתִיךְ״ (ישעיה מט:טז); וְכֵן ״וּמֵחֵיק הָאָרֶץ״ (יחזקאל מג:יד):


**when He established the foundations of the earth** Heb. בחקו from an expression of engraving (חקוי), as in (Isa. 49:16): “Behold on [My] hands I have engraved you (חקתיך),” and so (Ezek. 43:14): “And from the bottom (מחיק) upon the ground.”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:30:1)


**אָמוֹן.** גְּדֵלָה אֶצְלוֹ, לְשׁוֹן ״הָאֲמוּנִים עֲלֵי תוֹלָע״ (איכה ד:ה):


**a nursling** Heb. אמון, one that was reared beside Him, an expression of (Lam. 4:5): “They that were reared (האמנים) amid crimson.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:30:2)


**יוֹם יוֹם.** אַלְפַּיִם שָׁנָה (תהלים צ:ד; ויקרא רבה יט:א):


**every day** יום יום, lit. a day a day, two thousand years.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:31:1)


**מְשַׂחֶקֶת בְּתֵבֵל אַרְצוֹ.** כָּל דּוֹרוֹת הָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁהָיוּ מֵאָדָם וְעַד נֹחַ וּמִנֹּחַ וְעַד אַבְרָהָם הָיִיתִי מְשַׂחֶקֶת עֲלֵיהֶם:


**playing in the habitable world of His earth** All the generations of the wicked that were from Adam to Noah and from Noah to Abraham, I was laughing at them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:31:2)


**וְשַׁעֲשֻׁעַי.** צָפִיתִי עַד בֹּא דּוֹר הַמִּדְבָּר וִיקַבְּלוּנִי:


**and [having] my delights** I waited until the generation of the desert came and accepted me.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:32:1)


**וְעַתָּה בָנִים שִׁמְעוּ לִי**:



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:33:1)


**וְאַל תִּפְרָעוּ.** וְאַל תְּבַטְּלוּ מוּסָרִי:


**and do not put it to naught** Heb. תפרעו, and do not put my discipline to naught.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:34:1)


**לִשְׁקֹד.** לִשְׁמֹר:


**to watch** Heb. לשקד


###### Rashi on Proverbs 8:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/8:34:2)


**עַל דַּלְתוֹתַי.** לְהִכָּנֵס רִאשׁוֹן לְבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ וּלְבֵית הַכְּנֶסֶת וְלָצֵאת אַחֲרוֹן:


**by my doors** [to be the] first to enter into the study hall and the synagogue and [the] last to leave.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:1:1)


**חָכְמוֹת בָּנְתָה בֵיתָהּ.** בְּחָכְמָה בָּנָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶת הָעוֹלָם:


**Wisdom has built her house** With wisdom has the Holy One, blessed be He, built the world.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:1:2)


**חָצְבָה עַמּוּדֶיהָ שִׁבְעָה.** שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי בְרֵאשִׁית. דָּבָר אַחֵר: שִׁבְעָה סְפָרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בַּתּוֹרָה – ״וַיְהִי בִּנְסֹעַ הָאָרֹן״ (במדבר י:לה) סֵפֶר לְעַצְמוֹ; בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת (שבת קטז.):


**she has hewn her seven pillars** The seven days of Creation. Another explanation: This refers to the seven books of the Torah, since (Num. 10:35f.) “And it came about when the ark traveled...” is an individual book, as is stated in tractate Shabbath (116a).



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:2:1)


**טָבְחָה טִבְחָהּ מָסְכָה יֵינָהּ.** מְזָגָם בְּמַיִם, כְּמוֹ יַיִן חָזָק שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִשְׁתּוֹת חַי:


**She has prepared her meat, she has mingled her wine** She has mixed it with water, like strong wine, that is unfit to drink unmixed.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:2:2)


**אַף עָרְכָה שֻׁלְחָנָהּ.** כָּל יְצִירַת לַח וְיָבֵשׁ:


**she has even set her table** All creations of liquid and solid.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:3:1)


**שָׁלְחָה נַעֲרוֹתֶיהָ.** אָדָם וְחַוָּה (סנהדרין לח.), דָּבָר אַחֵר: מֹשֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן (ויקרא רבה א,ד):


**She has sent her maidens** Adam and Eve.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:4:1)


**מִי פֶתִי יָסוּר הֵנָּה.** וְיִלְמַד אוֹתִי וְיֶחְכַּם:


**“Whoever is simple, let him turn in here”** and learn it and become wise.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:5:1)


**לְכוּ לַחֲמוּ בְלַחֲמִי וּשְׁתוּ בְּיַיִן מָסָכְתִּי.** בְּיַיִן שֶׁמָּזַגְתִּי:


**of the wine I have mingled** Heb. מסכתי.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:6:1)


**עִזְבוּ פְתָאיִם וִחְיוּ.** אֶת דֶּרֶךְ הַפַּתְיוּת:


**Leave, you simpletons** the way of simplicity and live.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:6:2)


**וְאִשְׁרוּ.** לְשׁוֹן פַּעַם, כְּמוֹ ״בַּאֲשֻׁרוֹ אָחֲזָה רַגְלִי״ (איוב כג:יא):


**and step** Heb. מסכתי, an expression of stepping, as in (Job 23:11): “To his steps (באשרו) my foot was held fast.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:7:1)


**וּמוֹכִיחַ לְרָשָׁע מוּמוֹ.** מוּם הוּא לַמּוֹכִיחַ, שֶׁזֶּה מְחָרְפוֹ וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ לוֹ. זוֹ הִיא אַזְהָרָה שֶׁאָסוּר לְדַבֵּר עִם הַמְּסִיתִים מִדֶּרֶךְ הַיְשָׁרָה, אֲפִלּוּ לְהוֹכִיחָם וּלְקָרְבָם:


**and he who reproves a wicked man, that is his blemish** It is a blemish to the one who reproves [him], for this one berates him and does not heed him. This is a warning that it is forbidden to talk with those who entice to worship idols, even to reprove them and to draw them near.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:9:1)


**תֵּן לְחָכָם וְיֶחְכַּם עוֹד.** לְתַלְמִיד הָגוּן שְׁנֵה. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (שמות רבה נ:ב): נֶאֱמַר לְנֹחַ ״מִכָּל הַבְּהֵמָה הַטְּהוֹרָה וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית ז:ב), וּכְשֶׁיָּצָא ״וַיִּקַּח מִכָּל הַבְּהֵמָה וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית ח:כ). אָמַר: מָה רָאָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַרְבּוֹת בְּאֵלּוּ? אֶלָּא שֶׁרָצָה שֶׁאַקְרִיב לוֹ מֵהֶן:


**Give a wise man, and he will become yet wiser** To a worthy pupil, and the Midrash Aggadah (Tanchuma, Vayakhel 6) states: It was said to Noah, “From every clean animal, etc.” (Gen. 7:2), and when he went out, “And he took from all clean animals, etc.” (ibid. 8:20). He said, “What did the Holy One, blessed be He, see to increase the number of these? It is only because He wished that I offer up some of them.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:9:2)


**הוֹדַע לְצַדִּיק.** חָכְמָה וְהוּא **יוֹסֶף לֶקַח.** מִדַּעְתּוֹ וְעַל שְׁמוּעָתוֹ:


**teach a righteous man** wisdom, and he will increase in learning from his own knowledge in addition to what he heard.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:10:1)


**תְּחִלַּת חָכְמָה יִרְאַת ה׳, וְדַעַת קְדוֹשִׁים.** הִיא עִקַּר הַבִּינָה:


**and the knowledge of the holy ones** is the root of understanding.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:11:1)


**וְיוֹסִיפוּ לְךָ שְׁנוֹת חַיִּים.** שָׁנִים שֶׁל חַיִּים הֵם שֶׁל פַּרְנָסָה וְעֹשֶׁר:


**and they will add to you years of life** Years of life are those of sustenance and wealth.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:16:1)


**חֲסַר לֵב, וְאָמְרָה לּוֹ.** הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ, מָה אָמְרָה לוֹ:


**and whoever is devoid of sense** (lit. and one devoid of sense.) **and she says to him** these words. What does she say to him?



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/9:17:1)


**מַיִם גְּנוּבִים יִמְתָּקוּ.** אֵין טַעַם בִּיאַת פְּנוּיָה כְּטַעַם אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ. אַף לְעִנְיַן הַמִּצְוָה, **מַיִם גְּנוּבִים יִמְתָּקוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ יְרֵאִים לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּגָלוּי וְעוֹשִׂין בְּסֵתֶר:


**Stolen water is sweet** The pleasure afforded by intimacy with a single woman does not equal that afforded by intimacy with a married woman. Also, regarding the commandments (other editions: sectarianism), stolen water is sweet, for they were afraid to do it in public, but did it in secret.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:1)


**מִשְׁלֵי שְׁלֹמֹה בֵּן חָכָם יְשַׂמַּח אָב.** זֶה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא (מדרש משלי י:א). דָּבָר אַחֵר: אָבִיו מַמָּשׁ:


**Proverbs of Solomon: A wise son makes his father happy** This is the Holy One, blessed be He (Midrash); another explanation: his actual father.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:2)


**וּבֶן כְּסִיל תּוּגַת אִמּוֹ.** תָּמִיד הוּא עִם אִמּוֹ בַּבַּיִת, וְרוֹאָה אֶת שְׁטוּתוֹ וּמְצֵרָה. וּלְפִי הַמָּשָׁל: **בֵּן כְּסִיל** כְּמוֹ יָרָבְעָם בֶּן נְבָט (ברכות לה:):


**but a foolish son is the grief of his mother** He is always with his mother at home, and she sees his foolishness and is troubled. And according to the allegory, a foolish son, like Jeroboam the son of Nebat,


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:1:3)


**תּוּגַת אִמּוֹ.** תּוּגַת כְּנִסִיּוֹתוֹ:


**is the grief of his mother**, the grief of his nation.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:2:1)


**לֹא יוֹעִילוּ אוֹצְרוֹת רֶשַׁע.** שֶׁהָיָה מִשְׁתַּבֵּחַ בְּעָשְׁרוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וַיֹּאמֶר אֶפְרַיִם אַךְ עָשַׁרְתִּי וְגוֹמֵר״ (הושע יב:ט):


**Treasures of wickedness will not avail** For he was boasting with his riches, as it is stated (Hosea 12:9): “And Ephraim said: Surely I have become rich, etc.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:2:2)


**וּצְדָקָה תַּצִּיל מִמָּוֶת.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר: צַדִּיק שֶׁיְּבַזְבֵּז נְכָסָיו לִצְדָקָה, מֵהֵיכָן יִתְפַּרְנֵס?


**but charity will save from death** And if you ask, “A righteous man who squanders his property for charity—from where will he sustain himself?”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:3:1)


**לֹא יַרְעִיב ה׳ נֶפֶשׁ צַדִּיק, וְהַוַּת רְשָׁעִים יֶהְדֹּף.** יִדְחוֹף אוֹתָם וְתִפּוֹל:


**The Lord will not starve the soul of the righteous** [This is the answer to the previous question.] **but the destruction [wrought by] the wicked will cast [them] down** It will thrust them down and cause them to fall.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:1)


**רֹאשׁ עֹשֶׂה כַף רְמִיָּה.** מִי שֶׁעָנִי בַּתּוֹרָה, מוֹרֶה הוֹרָאוֹת שֶׁקֶר:


**A poor man makes a deceitful scale** Whoever is impoverished in [his knowledge of] Torah promulgates false decisions.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:2)


**כַּף רְמִיָּה.** מֹאזְנַיִם שֶׁל רְמִיָּה. לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ מַשְׁמַע בַּתַּגָּרִים:


**deceitful scales** Scales of deceit. According to its simple meaning, it refers to merchants.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:4:3)


**וְיַד חֲרוּצִים.** יְשָׁרִים, שֶׁחוֹרְצִים דָּבָר בַּאֲמִתּוֹ וּמִשְׁפָּטוֹ בְּלֹא עַוְלָה:


**and the hand of those who make true decisions** The upright, who decide a matter truly and justly without injustice.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:6:1)


**וּפִי רְשָׁעִים יְכַסֶּה חָמָס.** הֶחָמָס יְכַסֶּה עַל פִּיהֶם וְיַהַרְגֵם:


**but violence shall cover the mouth of the wicked** The violence shall cover their mouths and kill them.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:7:1)


**זֵכֶר צַדִּיק לִבְרָכָה.** הַמַּזְכִּיר צַדִּיק מְבָרְכוֹ:


**The mention of a righteous man is for a blessing** Whoever mentions a righteous man blesses him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:7:2)


**וְשֵׁם רְשָׁעִים יִרְקָב.** רִקָּבוֹן עוֹלֶה בִּשְׁמָם, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם חָפֵץ לְהַזְכִּיר שְׁמוֹ וְהוּא מִשְׁתַּכֵּחַ מֵאֵלָיו (יומא לח:):


**but the name of the wicked shall rot** Decay develops in their name, for no one wishes to mention his [the wicked man’s] name, and it is automatically forgotten.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:8:1)


**חֲכַם לֵב יִקַּח מִצְוֹת.** מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ, שֶׁכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ עוֹסְקִין בְּבִזַּת מִצְרַיִם, וְהוּא הָיָה עוֹסֵק בְּמִצְוֹת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּקַּח מֹשֶׁה אֶת עַצְמוֹת יוֹסֵף וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות יג:יט):


**The wise-hearted takes commandments** This alludes to our teacher, Moses, for all Israel were busy with the plunder of Egypt, and he was busy with the commandments, as is it said (Exod. 13:19): “And Moses took Joseph’s bones, etc.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:8:2)


**וֶאֱוִיל שְׂפָתַיִם יִלָּבֵט.** לְשׁוֹן יְגִיעָה. וְיֵשׁ בְּסִפְרֵי בְּפָרָשָׁה ״וַיְהִי הָעָם כְּמִתְאֹנְנִים״ (במדבר יא:א): אָמְרוּ: כַּמָּה נִתְלַבַּטְנוּ בַדֶּרֶךְ:


**but he who talks foolishly will weary** Heb. ילבט, an expression of weariness. It appears in Sifre, in the section commencing (Num. 11:1): “And the people were as complainers.” They said, “How much have we wearied (נתלבטנו) on the way!”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:9:1)


**וּמְעַקֵּשׁ דְּרָכָיו יִוָּדֵעַ.** יִשָּׁבֵר וְיִתְיַסֵּר, כְּמוֹ ״וַיֹּדַע בָּהֶם אֶת אַנְשֵׁי סֻכּוֹת״ (שופטים ח:טז):


**will be broken** Heb. יודע, will be broken and chastised, as in (Jud. 8:16): “And with them he broke (ויודע) the men of Succoth.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:10:1)


**קוֹרֵץ עַיִן יִתֵּן עַצָּבֶת.** זֶה הַמֵּסִית אָדָם בִּקְרִיצוֹתָיו לְרָעָה:


**He who winks his eye causes grief** That is the one who entices a person to evil with his winks.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:11:1)


**מְקוֹר חַיִּים פִּי צַדִּיק וּפִי רְשָׁעִים וְגוֹ׳.** פִּיהֶם יְכַסֶּה חָמָס שֶׁבְּלִבָּם, שֶׁמַּחֲלִיקִין אֶת שִׂפְתֵיהֶם וְשִׂנְאָה טְמוּנָה בְּלִבָּם. וְאֵין פִּתְרוֹן מִקְרָא זֶה דּוֹמֶה לְפִתְרוֹן הָעֶלְיוֹן (משלי י:ו), וְהָעִנְיָן יוֹרֶה עֲלֵיהֶם:


**The mouth of the righteous is a fountain of life, but the mouth of the wicked, etc.** Their mouth conceals the violence that is in their heart, for they talk smoothly with their lips, but hatred is hidden in their heart. Now the interpretation of this verse is not the same as the above verse (8); the context is indicative of their meaning.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:12:1)


**שִׂנְאָה תְּעוֹרֵר מְדָנִים.** אֲפִלּוּ עֲבֵרָה שֶׁנִּתְּנָה לְשִׁכְחָה נִזְכֶּרֶת עַל יְדֵי מַרְבִּית עֲוֹנוֹת; בָּאָה שִׂנְאָה אַחֲרוֹנָה וּמְעוֹרֶרְתָּן. שֶׁכֵּן יְחֶזְקֵאל הוֹכִיחַ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל עֲבֵרוֹת מִצְרַיִם: ״וָאֹמַר אֲלֵהֶם אִישׁ אֶת שִׁקּוּצֵי עֵינָיו הַשְׁלִיכוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל כ:ז) – כַּמָּה שָׁנִים הָיְתָה שִׂנְאָה זוֹ כְּבוּשָׁה וְלֹא הִזְכִּירָהּ לָהֶם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, עַד עַכְשָׁו, שֶׁהִרְבּוּ עֲבֵרוֹת עַל פִּשְׁעֵיהֶם (ויקרא רבה ז:א):


**Hatred arouses quarrels** Even a sin that was forgotten is remembered though additional iniquities. The final hatred comes and arouses them, for Ezekiel reproved Israel for the transgressions of Egypt (Ezek. 20:7), “And I said to them, ‘Every man shall cast away the abominations of his eyes, etc.’” How many (years was) this hatred hidden, that the Holy One, blessed be He, did not mention it to them until now, when they added transgressions to their sins.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:12:2)


**וְעַל כָּל פְּשָׁעִים תְּכַסֶּה אַהֲבָה.** כְּשֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל מֵטִיבִים מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְכַסֶּה עַל פְּשָׁעֵיהֶם:


**but love covers all transgressions** When Israel improves their deeds, the Holy One, blessed be He, conceals their transgressions.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:13:1)


**בְּשִׂפְתֵי נָבוֹן תִּמָּצֵא חָכְמָה.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם מוֹכִיחַ לְנָבוֹן, מֵשִׁיב לוֹ: חָטָאתִי! כְּגוֹן דָּוִד שֶׁאָמַר לְנָתָן: ״חָטָאתִי״ (שמואל ב יב:יג):


**Wisdom is found in the lips of the understanding** When a person reproves an understanding individual, he replies to him, “I have sinned,” e.g. David, who said to Nathan, “I have sinned” (11 Sam. 12:13).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:13:2)


**וְשֵׁבֶט לְגֵו חֲסַר לֵב.** אֲבָל חֲסַר לֵב אֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ עַד שֶׁיִּלְקֶה, כְּמוֹ פַּרְעֹה (שמות ז:יד-יא:י):


**but a rod is for the body of one devoid of sense** But one who is devoid of sense does not listen until he is smitten, like Pharaoh.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:14:1)


**חֲכָמִים יִצְפְּנוּ דָעַת.** יִשְׁמְרוּהוּ בְּלִבָּם, שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁכָּחוּהוּ:


**Wise men store up knowledge** They guard it in their heart so that they do not forget it.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:1)


**הוֹן עָשִׁיר.** בַּתּוֹרָה:


**The wealth of the rich** in Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:2)


**קִרְיַת עֻזּוֹ.** הִיא לוֹ:


**is the city of his strength** to him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:15:3)


**מְחִתַּת דַּלִּים רֵישָׁם.** עֲנִיּוּתָם, שֶׁלֹּא עָסְקוּ בַּתּוֹרָה, הִיא מְחִתָּתָם:


**the destruction of the poor is their poverty** The poverty that they did not engage in the Torah is their destruction.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:16:1)


**פְּעֻלַּת צַדִּיק לְחַיִּים.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (דברים רבה ב:כ): שְׁלֹמֹה עָשָׂה פְּעֻלַּת הַמִּקְדָּשׁ לְחַיֵּיהֶם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְכַפָּרָתָן:


**The act of the righteous is for life** [This is to be understood] according to its apparent meaning, but the Midrash Aggadah states: Solomon performed the construction of the Temple for the life of Israel, for their atonement.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:16:2)


**וּתְבוּאַת רָשָׁע.** תְּבוּאָתוֹ (ס״א: הֲבָאָתוֹ) שֶׁל מְנַשֶּׁה (דברי הימים ב לג:ז), שֶׁהִכְנִיס בּוֹ הַסֶּמֶל, הִיא הָיְתָה לְחַטָּאת:


**what the wicked bring in** What Manasseh brought in, for he brought in the image—that was for sin.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:17:1)


**אֹרַח לְחַיִּים.** הַשּׁוֹמֵר מוּסָר הִיא הַדֶּרֶךְ לְחַיִּים:


**The way to life** He who keeps discipline—that is the way to life.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:17:2)


**וְעוֹזֵב תּוֹכַחַת מַתְעֶה.** אֶת עַצְמוֹ וְאֶת אֲחֵרִים:


**but he who forsakes reproof misleads** himself and others.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:18:1)


**מְכַסֶּה שִׂנְאָה שִׂפְתֵי שָׁקֶר.** הֶחָנֵף שְׂפָתָיו שֶׁקֶר, וּמְכַסֶּה שִׂנְאָה בְּלִבּוֹ:


**He who covers up hatred has false lips** The flatterer has false lips, and he hides hatred in his heart.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:18:2)


**וּמוֹצִיא דִבָּה הוּא כְסִיל.** לַעַז, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ אֲנָשִׁים דּוֹבְבִין בּוֹ עַל חֲבֵירוֹ:


**and he who spreads slander is a fool** Heb. דבה, a rumor about his friend, about which people will converse (דובבין).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:19:1)


**בְּרֹב דְּבָרִים לֹא יֶחְדַּל פָּשַׁע.** הַמַּרְבֶּה דְבָרִים מֵבִיא חֵטְא:


**In a multitude of words, transgression will not be avoided** He who talks too much brings on sin.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:19:2)


**וְחוֹשֵׂךְ שְׂפָתָיו.** הוּא הַמַּשְׂכִּיל:


**and he who holds back his lips** he is wise.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:20:1)


**כֶּסֶף נִבְחָר לְשׁוֹן צַדִּיק.** שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ לְהוֹכִיחַ:


**The tongue of the righteous is choice silver** for he knows how to reprove.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:20:2)


**לֵב רְשָׁעִים כִּמְעָט.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ לְתוֹכַחַת צַדִּיק. וּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא אוֹמֵר (תנחומא, כי תשא ו): עַל עִדּוֹ הַנָּבִיא נֶאֱמַר, שֶׁקָּרָא עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ בְּבֵית אֵל; וְיָרָבְעָם, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיָּבְשָׁה יָדוֹ, לֹא קִבֵּל תּוֹכֵחָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״חַל נָא אֶת פְּנֵי ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים א יג:ו) וְלֹא ׳אֱלֹהַי׳; וְאוֹמֵר ״וַתָּשָׁב יָדוֹ כְּבָרִאשֹׁנָה״ (מלכים א יג:ו) – עוֹמֵד וּמַקְטִיר לַעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים, וְאַף בַּסּוֹף כֵּן:


**the heart of the wicked is worth little** for he does not heed the reproof of the righteous man. Midrash Rabbi Tanhuma (Ki Thissa 6) states: This was stated concerning Iddo the prophet, who called [in prophecy] about the altar in Beth-El, and Jeroboam, although his hand had become stiff, did not heed the reproof, as it is said (I Kings 13:6): “Entreat now the Lord your God, etc.”; but not “my God, etc”; (ad loc.): “and the king’s hand was drawn back to him, and it was as before.” Just as before, he was standing and burning sacrifices to idols, so was it at the end.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:21:1)


**שִׂפְתֵי צַדִּיק יִרְעוּ רַבִּים וְגוֹ׳.** שֶׁרַבִּים אוֹכְלִים בִּזְכוּתוֹ וּבִתְפִלָּתוֹ:


**The lips of the righteous will feed many, etc.** Many eat in his merit and because of his prayer.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:22:1)


**בִּרְכַּת ה׳ הִיא תַעֲשִׁיר, וְלֹא יוֹסִיף עֶצֶב עִמָּהּ.** אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהִתְיַגֵּעַ לְהַעֲשִׁיר בּוֹ, דַּי בַּבְּרָכָה מַה שֶּׁהוּא מְבָרְכוֹ:


**The blessing of the Lord will bring riches, etc.** One need not toil to gain wealth, for it is enough with the blessing that He blesses him.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:23:1)


**כְּשְׂחוֹק לִכְסִיל עֲשׂוֹת זִמָּה.** עֲצַת חַטָּאִים:


**As it is sport for a fool to carry out a sinful plot** Heb. זמה, a plot of sins.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:23:2)


**וְחָכְמָה** כִּשְׂחוֹק **לְאִישׁ תְּבוּנָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: קַלָּה הִיא בְּעֵינָיו לַעֲשׂוֹת:


**[so is] wisdom** like sport **to a man of understanding**; i.e., in his eyes, it is easy to do.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:24:1)


**מְגוֹרַת רָשָׁע.** מַה שֶּׁהוּא יָרֵא יָבֹא לוֹ. דּוֹר הַפְּלַגָּה יָרְאוּ וְאָמְרוּ ״פֶּן נָפוּץ״ (בראשית יא:ד), וְסוֹפָן כְּתִיב ״וַיָּפֶץ ה׳ אֹתָם מִשָּׁם״ (בראשית יא:ח):


**The dread of a wicked man that will befall him** What he fears will befall him. The generation of the Dispersion said, “Lest we scatter” (Gen. 11:4), and their end was that it is written (ibid. 8), “And the Lord scattered them from there.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:24:2)


**וְתַאֲוַת צַדִּיקִים יִתֵּן.** מִי שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ לִתְּנָהּ (לָתֵת):


**but the desire of the righteous He will grant** He Who has the power to grant it.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:25:1)


**כַּעֲבוֹר סוּפָה וְאֵין רָשָׁע.** פִּתְאוֹם בָּא עֶבְרַת רוּחַ סְעָרָה, נִכְרַת הָרָשָׁע מִמְּקוֹמוֹ:


**When the whirlwind passes, the wicked man is no more** Suddenly the fury of a whirlwind comes, and the wicked man is cut off from his place.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:28:1)


**תּוֹחֶלֶת צַדִּיקִים שִׂמְחָה.** סוֹפָהּ מִתְקַיֶּמֶת וּשְׂמֵחִים:


**The expectation of the righteous [will result in] joy** Eventually, it will be realized, and they will rejoice.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:28:2)


**וְתִקְוַת רְשָׁעִים תֹּאבֵד.** כִּי לֹא תָבֹא:


**but the hope of the wicked shall be lost** for it will not come.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:29:1)


**מָעוֹז לַתֹּם דֶּרֶךְ ה׳ וּמְחִתָּה.** הִיא לְפוֹעֲלֵי אָוֶן שֶׁאֵינָן הוֹלְכִין בָּהּ, וְהִיא נִפְרַעַת מֵהֶם:


**The way of the Lord is a stronghold for the innocent, but ruin for those who work iniquity** For they do not follow it, and it exacts retribution from them.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:30:1)


**צַדִּיק לְעוֹלָם בַּל יִמּוֹט.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מָט אֵין מוֹטָתוֹ מוֹטַת עוֹלָם, כִּי יִפּוֹל וְקָם (משלי כד:טז):


**The righteous will not collapse forever** When he collapses, his collapse is not a permanent collapse, but he will fall and rise.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:31:1)


**פִּי צַדִּיק יָנוּב חָכְמָה.** יְדַבֵּר, לְשׁוֹן ״נִיב שְׂפָתָיִם״ (ישעיהו נז:יט):


**The mouth of the righteous man speaks wisdom** Heb. ינוב, speaks, an expression of (Isa. 57: 9): “the speech (ניב) of the lips.”



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 10:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/10:32:1)


**שִׂפְתֵי צַדִּיק יֵדְעוּן רָצוֹן.** יוֹדְעִים לְרַצּוֹת וּלְפַיֵּס אֶת בּוֹרְאָם, וְיוֹדְעִים לְרַצּוֹת אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת וּלְהַטִּיל שָׁלוֹם בֵּינֵיהֶם:


**the lips of a righteous man know how to please** They know how to please and placate his Creator, and they know how to please the people and how to make peace among them.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:2:1)


**בָּא זָדוֹן וַיָּבֹא קָלוֹן וְאֶת צְנוּעִים חָכְמָה.** תָּבוֹא חָכְמָה:


**When willful wickedness comes, etc. but with the modest is wisdom** comes wisdom.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:3:1)


**תֻּמַּת יְשָׁרִים תַּנְחֵם.** תַּנְהֲלֵם:


**The innocence of the upright leads them** Heb. תנחם, will guide them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:3:2)


**וְסֶלֶף בּוֹגְדִים יְשָׁדֵּם.** יְשָׁדְדֵם:


**but the distortion of the treacherous robs them** Heb. ישדם, like ישדדם



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:6:1)


**צִדְקַת יְשָׁרִים תַּצִּילֵם וּבְהַוַּת בֹּגְדִים יִלָּכֵדוּ.** בַּהַוּוֹת שֶׁהֵם עוֹשִׂים הֵם נִלְכָּדִים. וְהַמִּקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: **וּבוֹגְדִים בְּהַוַּת יִלָּכֵדוּ**:


**but in the destruction** In the destruction that they perpetrate, they are caught; the verse is transposed. [It means] “and the treacherous in the destruction will be caught.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:7:1)


**בְּמוֹת אָדָם רָשָׁע תֹּאבַד תִּקְוָה.** תִּקְוַת כָּל הַבּוֹטְחִים בּוֹ:


**When a wicked man dies, hope is lost** The hope of all who trust in him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:7:2)


**וְתוֹחֶלֶת אוֹנִים אָבָדָה.** תּוֹחֶלֶת בָּנָיו, שֶׁהֵם אוֹנוֹ, אָבְדָה, כִּי בִּזְכוּתוֹ לֹא תָבוֹא לָהֶם טוֹבָה. אֲבָל בְּמוֹת צַדִּיקִים יֵשׁ לִבְנֵיהֶם מִבְטָח בְּצִדְקָתָם:


**and the expectation of his children is lost** Heb. אונים. The expectation of his children, who are the products of his strength, is lost, for no good will come to them in his merit. But when the righteous die, their children have trust in their righteousness.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:8:1)


**צַדִּיק מִצָּרָה נֶחֱלָץ.** בְּמוֹת רָשָׁע, וְלַמִּקְרָא הָעֶלְיוֹן (משלי יא:ז) זֶה מְחֻבָּר:


**A righteous man is extricated from trouble** when the wicked man dies, and this is connected to the preceding verse.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:9:1)


**בְּפֶה חָנֵף.** חָנֵף הַמֵּסִית חֲבֵרוֹ בְּדֶרֶךְ רַע, **מַשְׁחִיתוֹ בְּפִיו**:


**With his mouth, the flatterer** The flatterer who entices his friend on an evil way, destroys him with his mouth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:9:2)


**וּבְדַעַת צַדִּיקִים יֵחָלֵצוּ.** וְהַצַּדִּיק נֶחֱלָץ מִמֶּנּוּ בְּדַעַת הַתּוֹרָה, שֶׁהִזְהִירָה עָלָיו ״לֹא תֹאבֶה לוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים יג:ט):


**but with knowledge, righteous men are extricated** But the righteous man is extricated from [the flatterer] with the knowledge of the Torah, which warned concerning him (Deut. 13: 9): “You shall not consent to him, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:11:1)


**תָּרוּם קָרֶת.** תִּתְקַיֵּם תִּקְרַת הַבַּיִת בְּגָבְהָהּ מִלִּפּוֹל. בְּעוֹד שֶׁהָיוּ מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה יְשָׁרִים, הֶעֱמִידָה תְּפִלָּתָם אֶת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**the ceiling is raised** The ceiling of the Temple will be preserved at its height from falling. As long as the kings of Judah were upright, their prayer preserved the Temple.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:12:1)


**וְאִישׁ תְּבוּנוֹת יַחֲרִישׁ.** כְּשֶׁמְּבַזֵּהוּ הֶחָסֵר לֵב; כְּמוֹ שָׁאוּל, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיִּבְזֻהוּ וְלֹא הֵבִיאוּ לוֹ מִנְחָה וַיְהִי כְּמַחֲרִישׁ״ (שמואל א י:כז):


**but a man of understanding is silent** when the one devoid of sense despises him, like Saul, as it is written (I Sam. 10:27): “And they despised him and brought him no gift; and he was as one who kept his peace.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:14:1)


**בְּאֵין תַּחְבֻּלוֹת.** כְּשֶׁהַצָּרָה בָּאָה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְאֵינָן נוֹתְנִין לֵב לְהָבִין לְהִתְעַנּוֹת וְלַעֲשׂוֹת תְּשׁוּבָה, **יִפָּל עָם**:


**Without strategy** When trouble befalls Israel, and they do not put their heart to understand, to fast and to repent, the people will fall.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:15:1)


**רַע יֵרוֹעַ.** הָרָשָׁע יִתְרוֹצֵץ, אֲשֶׁר עָרַב לִבּוֹ לַעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים:


**will be broken by it** The wicked man who gave his heart as surety to idolatry.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:15:2)


**וְשׂוֹנֵא תוֹקְעִים.** תּוֹקְעִים כַּף לַעֲשׂוֹת רַע וְלָלֶכֶת בַּעֲצָתָם, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ (יבמות קט:) בְּעַרְבוּת מָמוֹן:


**but he who hates hand-claspers** Those who clasp hands with the enticers to follow their counsel. But our Rabbis expounded this in regard to surety in monetary matters.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:16:1)


**תִּתְמֹךְ כָּבוֹד.** כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּקָרֵב תָּמִיד לִכְבוֹד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְתוֹרָתוֹ:


**draws near to honor** Heb. תתמך. The people of Israel constantly draw near to the honor of the Holy One, blessed be He, and His Torah. (


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:16:2)


**וְעָרִיצִים יִתְמְכוּ עֹשֶׁר.** בְּנֵי אֱדוֹם מִתְקָרְבִים אֵצֶל גְּנֵיבוֹת מָמוֹן וְגָזֵל:


**but strong men draw near to riches** that it should not be lost to them.)



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:17:1)


**גּוֹמֵל נַפְשׁוֹ.** גּוֹמֵל טוֹבָה לִקְרוֹבָיו. **אִישׁ חֶסֶד** אִישׁ שֶׁהוּא חָסִיד:


**does himself good** He does good to his kin. **A kind man** lit. a man of kindness, a man who is kind.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:17:2)


**וְעֹכֵר שְׁאֵרוֹ אַכְזָרִי.** וְהָאַכְזָרִי הוּא עוֹכֵר אֶת קְרוֹבָיו:


**but a cruel one troubles his own flesh** But the cruel one troubles his kin.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:1)


**רָשָׁע עֹשֶׂה פְעֻלַּת שָׁקֶר.** פְּעֻלָּתוֹ שֶׁל רָשָׁע מְשַׁקֶּרֶת לוֹ, כְּסָבוּר שֶׁתִּתְקַיֵּם בּוֹ הַצְלָחָתוֹ, וְהַכֹּל אָבַד:


**A wicked man earns illusory wages** The wage of a wicked man lies to him. He thinks that his prosperity will remain, but all is lost.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:2)


**וְזוֹרֵעַ צְדָקָה שְׂכַר אֱמֶת.** וְהַזּוֹרֵעַ צְדָקָה הִיא פְּעֻלָּה שֶׁל אֱמֶת, כִּי בְּוַדַּאי בָּטוּחַ הוּא שֶׁיְּקַבֵּל פָּעֳלוֹ בַּסּוֹף:


**but he who sows charity [receives] a true reward** But he who sows charity, it is a wage of truth, for he is surely confident that he will receive his wage at the end.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:18:3)


**שָׂכָר.** קְרוֹנִיאָ״ל בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּאָדָם הַסּוֹכֵר אַמַּת הַמַּיִם כְּדֵי לִלְקוֹט דָּגִים, וּבָטוּחַ שֶׁיִּמְצָא שָׁם דָּגִים הַרְבֵּה. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״כָּל עֹשֵׂי שֶׂכֶר אַגְמֵי נָפֶשׁ״ (ישעיהו יט:י):


שכר cornial in Old French. [This appears to mean a weir in several dialects; i.e., a fence placed in the water to catch fish.] Manuscripts of Rashi yield: eclusse or esklusa, which is e’cluse [in modern French]; in German wasserschleuse, a lock or a sluice gate). Like a man who locks a canal in order to gather fish, and he is confident that he will find many fish there. A similar instance is (Isa. 19:10): “all who make dams (שכר) for still ponds.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:19:1)


**כֵּן צְדָקָה לְחַיִּים.** אֲמִתּוֹ שֶׁל צְדָקָה סוֹפָהּ לְחַיִּים, כְּמוֹ ״כֵּן בְּנוֹת צְלָפְחָד דֹּבְרוֹת״ (במדבר כז:ז):


**The truth of charity** Heb. כן, the truth of charity is that its end is for life, as in (Num. 27:7): “The daughters of Zelofchad speak right (כן).”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:21:1)


**יָד לְיָד.** כְּלוֹמַר: מִיַּד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְיָדוֹ תָּבוֹא לוֹ שְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּתוֹ, וְ**לֹא יִנָּקֶה** מִן הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה (סוטה ד:):


**From hand to hand** From the hand of the Holy One, blessed be He, to his hand shall come to him his wage, and he will not be cleansed of the evil that he committed.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:22:1)


**נֶזֶם זָהָב בְּאַף חֲזִיר.** שֶׁמְּלַכְלֵךְ אוֹתָהּ בָּאַשְׁפּוֹת, כֵּן תַּלְמִיד חָכָם הַסָּר מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹבָה:


**[As] a gold ring in a swine’s snout** that bemires it in the dungheap, so is a Torah scholar who turns away from the good way.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:22:2)


**וְסָרַת טָעַם.** פּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


**from whom sense has departed** Who has departed from the Torah.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:23:1)


**תִּקְוַת רְשָׁעִים עֶבְרָה.** בְּטוּחִים וּמְקַוִּים לְגֵיהִנֹּם:


**the hope of the wicked is wrath** They are assured and hope for Gehinnom.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:24:1)


**יֵשׁ מְפַזֵּר.** מְמוֹנוֹ, כְּגוֹן לִצְדָקָה, **וְנוֹסָף עוֹד**:


**There is one who scatters** his money for such things as charity, and he is given more.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:24:2)


**וְחוֹשֵׂךְ.** עַצְמוֹ מִיֹּשֶׁר, **אַךְ לְמַחְסוֹר** יִהְיֶה לוֹ:


**and one who withholds** himself from [giving] what is proper, **only for a loss** it will be to him.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:1)


**נֶפֶשׁ בְּרָכָה.** שֶׁהוּא וַתְרָן בְּמָמוֹנוֹ. וְכָל לְשׁוֹן בְּרָכָה – פִּישׁוֹן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**A generous person** [lit. a soul of blessing,] who is generous with his money, and every expression of blessing is fuison in Old French, excess.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:2)


**וּמַרְוֶה.** שֶׁמַּשְׂבִּיעַ אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים:


**and he who sates** who sates the poor.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:25:3)


**גַּם הוּא יוֹרֶא.** יִשְׂבַּע טוֹב:


**shall himself become sated as well** He shall be sated with good.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:26:1)


**מוֹנֵעַ בָּר.** מְלַמֵּד תּוֹרָה:


**He who keeps back grain** [who refrains] from teaching Torah.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:27:1
