[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:27:1)


**שׁוֹחֵר טוֹב.** הֶחָפֵץ לְהַדְרִיךְ אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת בְּדֶרֶךְ טוֹבָה, וּמוֹכִיחַ וּמְיַסֵּר אוֹתָם:


**He who desires good** Who wishes to lead the people in the good way and reproves and chastises them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:27:2)


**יְבַקֵּשׁ רָצוֹן.** הוּא חָפֵץ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא רוֹצֶה בָּם, וּמִתְפַּיֵּס עִמָּם. **רָצוֹן**, אפיימינ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**seeks acceptance** He desires that the Holy One, blessed be He, accept them and and be reconciled with them. Appaisement in Old French, besanftigen in German.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:29:1)


**עוֹכֵר בֵּיתוֹ יִנְחַל רוּחַ.** אָדָם עָצֵל שֶׁיִּנְחַל רוּחַ תָּמִיד, וְאֵינוֹ יָגֵעַ בַּתּוֹרָה וְלֹא בִמְלָאכָה, סוֹף עוֹכֵר אֶת בְּנֵי בֵיתוֹ, שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם מַה לֶּאֱכֹל:


**He who inherits the wind will trouble his household** A lazy man, who always inherits the wind and does not toil in Torah or in work, will ultimately trouble the members of his household, for they will have nothing to eat.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:30:1)


**פְּרִי צַדִּיק.** גְּמוּל פֵּירוֹת מַעֲשֵׂה הַצַּדִּיקִים **עֵץ חַיִּים** הֵם לָעוֹלָם:


**The fruit of a righteous man** The reward of the fruit of the righteous man’s deeds is a tree of life for the world.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:30:2)


**וְלֹקֵחַ נְפָשׁוֹת חָכָם.** מִי שֶׁהוּא חָכָם, קוֹנֶה לוֹ נְפָשׁוֹת. שֶׁמְּלַמְּדָם דֶּרֶךְ טוֹב, וַהֲרֵי הֵם לוֹ כְּאִלּוּ קְנָאָם, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְאֶת הַנֶּפֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ בְחָרָן״ (בראשית יב:ה):


**and the wise man acquires souls** Whoever is wise acquires souls for himself. he teaches them the good way, and they are his as though he had acquired them, as the matter is stated (Gen. 12:5): “And the souls that they acquired in Haran.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:31:1)


**הֵן צַדִּיק בָּאָרֶץ יְשֻׁלָּם.** לָמָּה יִבְטַח הָרָשָׁע בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁמַּצְלַחַת לוֹ? הֲלֹא הוּא רוֹאֶה שֶׁהַצַּדִּיק מִשְׁתַּלֵּם שְׂכַר הָעֲבֵירָה שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ בְּעוֹדוֹ עַל הָאָרֶץ בַּחַיִּים:


**Behold! The righteous man will be requited on earth** Why should the wicked man feel confident when he prospers? Does he not see that the righteous man is requited the payment of the sins he has committed while he is still on earth, during his lifetime?


###### Rashi on Proverbs 11:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/11:31:2)


**וְאַף כִּי רָשָׁע וְחוֹטֵא.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ שֶׁל רָשָׁע לְהִשְׁתַּלֵּם לוֹ, אוֹ בְּחַיָּיו אוֹ בְּמוֹתוֹ:


**surely a wicked man and a sinner** Surely the wicked will ultimately be requited, either during his lifetime or after his death.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:2:1)


**טוֹב יָפִיק.** מוֹצִיא מִן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא נַחַת רוּחַ, לְהָבִיא טוֹבָה לָעוֹלָם:


**A good man will obtain** Heb. יפיק. He will draw satisfaction from the Holy One, blessed be He, to bring goodness to the world.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:2:2)


**וְאִישׁ מְזִמּוֹת יַרְשִׁיעַ.** בַּעַל עֲלִילוֹת יַרְשִׁיעַ – מְחַיֵּב אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת לְהָבִיא רָעָה, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״וְחוֹטֵא אֶחָד יְאַבֵּד טוֹבָה הַרְבֵּה״ (קהלת ט:יח):


**but a man of evil devices will condemn** One who has evil devices will condemn. He will condemn the people to bring evil [upon them], and so he states (Ecc. 9:18): “but one sinner destroys much good.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:3:1)


**לֹא יִכּוֹן אָדָם בְּרֶשַׁע.** לֹא יִתְבַּסֵּס:


**A man will not be established with wickedness** Heb. יכון, will not be firmly established.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:4:1)


**וּכְרָקָב.** תּוֹלַעַת הַנִּכְנָס תּוֹךְ הָעֲצָמוֹת וְטוֹחֵן אוֹתָם, כֵּן אִשָּׁה מְבִישָׁה וְרָעָה, שֶׁמַּעֲשֶׂיהָ בּוֹשִׁים:


**is like rot** A worm that penetrates the bones and grinds them. So is an embarrassing, bad wife, whose deeds are shameful.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:6:1)


**דִּבְרֵי רְשָׁעִים אֱרָב דָּם.** יוֹעֲצִים לִרְצוֹחַ בְּיָדָם אוֹ עַל יְדֵי עֵדוּת שֶׁקֶר:


**The words of the wicked are to lurk for blood** They take counsel to murder directly or through false testimony.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:6:2)


**וּפִי יְשָׁרִים.** כְּשֶׁשּׁוֹמְעִים אֶת עֲצָתָם, יַצִּילוּם לַנִּרְדָּפִים, שֶׁמְּגַלִּים אֶת עֲצָתָם; אוֹ אִם עֵדִים הֵם, מְזִמִּין אוֹתָם:


**but the mouth of the upright** when they hear [the wicked’s] counsel, will save the victims, for they will reveal [the wicked’s] plot, or if they are witnesses, [the upright] will prove them to be collusive.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:1)


**הָפוֹךְ רְשָׁעִים.** כְּמוֹ ״לַהֲפֹךְ״, כְּמַהְפֵּכַת רֶגַע (איכה ד:ו) נֶהְפָּכִים:


**The wicked will be overthrown** Heb. הפוך, like להפוך, to overthrow. Like a sudden overthrow they are overthrown [lit. like an overthrow in a second].


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:2)


**וְאֵינָם.** כָּלִים, כְּגוֹן סְדוֹם (בראשית יט:כד-כה):


**and they are no more** They are destroyed, e.g. Sodom.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:7:3)


**וּבֵית צַדִּיקִים יַעֲמֹד.** יִתְקַיֵּם:


**but the house of the righteous will stand** Will endure.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:1)


**לְפִי שִׂכְלוֹ יְהֻלַּל אִישׁ.** אִם חָכָם מְעַט אוֹ הַרְבֵּה, הַכֹּל לְפִי שִׂכְלוֹ יִטֹּל שְׂכָרוֹ:


**According to his intelligence a man is praised** Whether he possesses little or much intelligence, he will receive his reward according to his intelligence.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:2)


**וְנַעֲוֵה לֵב.** שֶׁהֵנִיעַ לִבּוֹ לְגַמְרֵי מִן הַתּוֹרָה, הוּא יִהְיֶה לָבוּז:


**but he whose heart has turned away** He who moved his heart completely away from the Torah will be despised.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:8:3)


**נַעֲוֵה.** כְּמוֹ ״נַעֲוֵיתִי מִשְּׁמֹעַ״ (ישעיה כא:ג), וְכֵן ״בֶּן נַעֲוַת הַמַּרְדּוּת״ (שמואל א כ:ל). וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר נַעֲוֵה – מִי שֶׁלִּבּוֹ נָעוּעַ, מִגִּזְרַת ״נָע וָנָד״ (בראשית ד:יב). כַּאֲשֶׁר יֹאמַר ״זַעֲוָה״ (דברים כח:כה) מִגִּזְרַת ״זָע״ (אסתר ה:ט):


Heb. נעוה, as in (Isa. 21:3): “I have become confused (נעויתי) from hearing,” and similarly (I Sam. 20:30): “the son of a straying (נעות) woman deserving of punishment.” And it is possible to say that נעוה means one whose heart has strayed, from the expression נע ונד, moving and wandering, and as one says זעוה, horror, from the root זע, stirred.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:9:1)


**טוֹב נִקְלֶה.** בְּעֵינָיו, וְנַעֲשָׂה עֶבֶד לְעַצְמוֹ:


**Better is he who is lightly esteemed** in his own eyes and became a slave to himself


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:9:2)


**מִמִּתְכַּבֵּד.** בְּעֵינָיו, וְאוֹמֵר: גְּנַאי הוּא לִי לַעֲמֹל בִּמְלָאכָה, שֶׁאֲנִי מִבְּנֵי אָדָם גְּדוֹלִים; וְסוֹפוֹ לִהְיוֹת **חֲסַר לָחֶם**:


**that one who is honored** in his own eyes and says, “It is disgraceful for me to toil at labor, for I am one of the great men,”—he will ultimately lack bread.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:10:1)


**יוֹדֵעַ צַדִּיק נֶפֶשׁ בְּהֶמְתּוֹ.** מַה בְּהֶמְתּוֹ וּבְנֵי בֵיתוֹ צְרִיכִים:


**A righteous man has regard for the desire of his beast** What his beasts and his household members need.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:11:1)


**עוֹבֵד אַדְמָתוֹ יִשְׂבַּע לָחֶם.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, וּלְפִי מְשָׁלוֹ: שֶׁחוֹזֵר עַל תַּלְמוּדוֹ תָּמִיד, שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁתַּכַּח:


**He who tills his soil will be sated with bread** To be understood according to its apparent meaning, but according to its allegorical meaning, [this means] he who constantly reviews his studies lest they be forgotten.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:12:1)


**חָמַד רָשָׁע.** לִהְיוֹת נִזּוֹן וּמִתְפַּרְנֵס מִמְּצוֹד רְשָׁעִים, שֶׁצָּדִין אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת בִּגְזֵלוֹת וְחָמָס:


**The wicked man yearns** to gain sustenance and maintenance from the prey of the wicked, who prey on people with robbery and violence.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:12:2)


**וְשֹׁרֶשׁ צַדִּיקִים יִתֵּן.** מַה שֶּׁהוּא רָאוּי לִיתֵּן, וְהוּא הַפְּרִי:


**but the root of the righteous yields** what it is fit to yield, and that is the fruit.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:13:1)


**בְּפֶשַׁע שְׂפָתַיִם.** בְּפֶשַׁע שִׂפְתֵי דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל, שֶׁאָמְרוּ: ״מַה שַׁדַּי כִּי נַעַבְדֶנּוּ״ (איוב כא:טו), בָּא לָהֶם מוֹקֵשׁ רָע, וְנֹחַ הַצַּדִּיק יָצָא מֵהַצָּרָה (תנחומא, נח טז):


**Because of the transgression of the lips** Because of the transgression of the lips of the generation of the Flood, who said (Job 21:15): “What is the Almighty that we should serve Him?” an evil snare came upon them, and Noah, the righteous man, came out of the trouble.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:14:1)


**מִפְּרִי פִי אִישׁ.** מִשְּׂכַר פִּיהֶם שֶׁל עוֹסְקֵי תוֹרָה, אוֹכְלִים טוֹב בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, וְהַקֶּרֶן קַיֶּמֶת לָהֶם לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**From the fruit of a man’s mouth** From the reward of the mouth of those who engage in the Torah, they eat the good of this world, and the principal remains intact for them for the world to come.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:16:1)


**אֱוִיל בַּיּוֹם יִוָּדַע כַּעְסוֹ.** בְּיוֹם שֶׁהוּא כּוֹעֵס, בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם הוֹדִיעַ כַּעְסוֹ, שֶׁהוּא מִתְגָּרֶה וּמְחָרֵף חֲבֵירוֹ בָּרַבִּים, וְאֵין מַעְצָר לְרוּחוֹ. אֲבָל הֶ**עָרוּם כֹּסֶה קָלוֹן** וְאֵינוֹ מְמַהֵר לָרִיב. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: בְּיוֹם שֶׁנִּבְרָא אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן נוֹדַע סוּרְחָנוֹ (עבודה זרה ח.), וְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהוּא עָרוּם, לֹא רָצָה לְשַׁחֵת בְּרִיּוֹתָיו, כִּסָּה קְלוֹנוֹ (חגיגה יב.) וְדָחָה לִגְזֵרָתוֹ מִיּוֹם שֶׁל אָדָם לְיוֹם שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁהוּא אֶלֶף שָׁנָה (תהלים צ:ד; במדבר רבה יד:יב):


**A fool’s anger is known within the day** On the day he is angry, on that very day he lets his anger be known, for he starts a quarrel and berates his fellow in public, and his anger has no restraint. But the clever man conceals the shame and does not hasten to quarrel. And the Midrash Aggadah states: On the day the first man was created, his sin was known, but the Holy One, blessed be He, Who is clever, did not wish to destroy His creatures. He concealed his shame and postponed His decree from Adam’s day to His day, which is one thousand years.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:17:1)


**יָפִיחַ אֱמוּנָה יַגִּיד צֶדֶק.** מִי שֶׁמְּדַבֵּר אֱמוּנָה מַגִּיד בְּדִין עֵדוּת צֶדֶק לְזַכּוֹת הַזַּכַּאי:


**He who speaks the truth will testify justly** He who speaks the truth will testify just testimony at a trial to justify the innocent.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:1)


**יֵשׁ בּוֹטֶה.** מְדַבֵּר, כְּמוֹ ״לְבַטֵּא בִשְׂפָתַיִם״ (ויקרא ה:ד):


**There is one who speaks** Heb. בוטה, speaks, as in (Lev. 5:4): “to pronounce (לבטה) with the lips.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:2)


**כְּמַדְקְרוֹת חָרֶב.** שֶׁמְּסַכְסֵךְ אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת וְגוֹרֵם לָהֶם לַהֲרֹג:


**like the jabs of a sword** For he stirs up the people and causes them to kill.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:3)


**וּלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים.** הַמֵּבִיא שָׁלוֹם בֵּין אָדָם לַחֲבֵירוֹ:


**but the tongue of the wise** which brings peace between one man and his fellow.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:18:4)


**מַרְפֵּא.** לַמִּבְטָא הַהוּא:


**brings healing** to that speech.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:19:1)


**שְׂפַת אֱמֶת תִּכּוֹן לָעַד.** תִּתְבַּסֵּס וּתְקַיֵּם:


**A true tongue will be established forever** It will be established and will endure.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:19:2)


**וְעַד אַרְגִּיעָה.** לִמְעַט רֶגַע הוּא כָּלֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ, שֶׁהַשֶּׁקֶר אֵין לוֹ רַגְלַיִם:


**just for a moment** In a fleeting moment, it perishes and leaves, for falsehood has no feet.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:20:1)


**מִרְמָה בְּלֶב חֹרְשֵׁי רָע.** וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהֵם טְרוּדִים בְּמַחֲשָׁבוֹת שֶׁל תַּרְמִית, אֵין לָהֶם שִׂמְחָה, **וּלְיוֹעֲצֵי שָׁלוֹם שִׂמְחָה**:


**There is deceit in the heart of those who plot evil** and since they are occupied with their thoughts of deceit, they have no joy—but for the counselors of peace, there is joy.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:21:1)


**לֹא יְאֻנֶּה לַצַּדִּיק.** לֹא יֶאֱרַע, לֹא תִזְדַּמֵּן לוֹ עֲבֵרָה בְּלִי דַעַת:


**No wrong shall be caused for the righteous** It will not happen. No sin will chance before him inadvertently.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:23:1)


**אָדָם עָרוּם כּוֹסֶה דָּעַת.** אֲפִלּוּ בְּחָכְמָתוֹ הוּא צָנוּעַ, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן לְדִבְרֵי שְׁטוּת הוּא מְכַסֶּה. אֲבָל **וְלֵב כְּסִילִים יִקְרָא אִוֶּלֶת.** מַכְרִיז אִוֶּלֶת בְּקוֹל רָם:


**A cunning man conceals knowledge** Even in his wisdom, he is discreet—he surely conceals words of foolishness; but the heart of the fool announces foolishness aloud.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:24:1)


**יַד חֲרוּצִים.** יְשָׁרִים:


**The hand of the diligent** The upright.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:24:2)


**תִּמְשׁוֹל.** תַּעֲשִׁיר:


**will rule** Will make wealthy.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:25:1)


**דְּאָגָה בְלֶב אִישׁ יַשְׁחֶנָּה.** יְשִׂיחֶנָּה מִדַּעְתּוֹ:


**If there is concern in a man’s heart, let him cast it down** Let him divert his attention from it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:25:2)


**וְדָבָר טוֹב יְשַׂמְּחֶנָּה.** יַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה, וְהִיא תְּשַׂמַּח אֶת הַדְּאָגָה שֶׁבְּלִבּוֹ וְתַצִּילֶנּוּ מִמֶּנָּה. וּלְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר: יְשִׂיחֶנָּה לַאֲחֵרִים, כָּךְ סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל מִקְרָא: **וְדָבָר טוֹב.** שֶׁיְּנַחֲמֶנּוּ חֲבֵרוֹ, יְשַׂמַּח אֶת הַדְּאָגָה:


**and a good word will make it cheerful** Let him engage in the Torah which will cause the concern in his heart to rejoice and save him therefrom. And according to the one who says, “he should tell it to others,” this is [the meaning of] the end of the verse: And a good word with which his friend consoles him will cause the concern to rejoice.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:26:1)


**יָתֵר מֵרֵעֵהוּ צַדִּיק.** הַצַּדִּיק מְוַתֵּר מִדּוֹתָיו וְעוֹבֵר עֲלֵיהֶם יָתֵר, לְרֵיי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The righteous is more generous than his neighbor** Heb. יתר. The righteous renounces his measures and passes over them. יתר large in French. That is freigebig in German, generous.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:26:2)


**וְדֶרֶךְ רְשָׁעִים.** שֶׁהִיא לְמוּדָה לְהָרַע, הִיא **תַּתְעֵם**:


**the wicked** who is accustomed to harming will lead them astray.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:1)


**לֹא יַחֲרֹךְ.** מְחֻבָּר לַמִּקְרָא שֶׁלְּפָנָיו, כְּלוֹמַר: דֶּרֶךְ רָשָׁע תַּתְעֵהוּ, וְ**לֹא יַחֲרֹךְ אֶת צֵידוֹ שֶׁל רְמִיָּה שֶׁלּוֹ**:


**He will not roast** This is connected to the above verse; that is to say that the way of the wicked will mislead him, and he will not roast the prey of his deceit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:2)


**לֹא יַחֲרֹךְ.** לֹא יַצְלִיחַ בְּתַרְמִיתוֹ. וּלְפִי שֶׁדִּבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן צַיִד, נוֹפֵל בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן חֲרִיכָה, שֶׁהַצַּיָּד הַמַּצְלִיחַ צָד אֶת הָעוֹפוֹת וְחוֹרְכָן בָּאֵשׁ:


**He will not roast;** he will not succeed with his deceit. As he [Solomon] spoke with a figure of prey, an expression of roasting is appropriate, for the successful hunter hunts the birds and roasts them in fire.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:3)


**וְהוֹן אָדָם יָקָר חָרוּץ.** מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא **וְהוֹן אָדָם** שֶׁהוּא **חָרוּץ יָקָר** הוּא, מָמוֹן אָדָם כָּשֵׁר יָקָר הוּא:


**but the wealth of an honest man is precious** Heb. יקר חרוץ. This is an inverted verse. But the wealth of a man who is honest is precious.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:27:4)


**חָרוּץ.** עוֹשֶׂה אֱמֶת, כְּמוֹ ״יַד חֲרוּצִים תַּעֲשִׁיר״ (משלי י:ד):


**honest** Heb. חרוץ, one who deals honestly, as in (10:4): “and the hand of those who make true decisions (חרוצים) will make [them] rich.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:28:1)


**בְּאֹרַח צְדָקָה חַיִּים וְדֶרֶךְ נְתִיבָה.** שֶׁל צְדָקָה:


**In the road of charity is life, and [on] the way of its path** of charity.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 12:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/12:28:2)


**אַל מָוֶת.** לֹא יָמוּת:


**there is no death** He will not die.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:1:1)


**בֵּן חָכָם מוּסַר אָב.** מִקְרָא קָצָר הוּא: **בֵּן חָכָם** שׁוֹאֵל וְאוֹהֵב **מוּסַר אָב.** וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: בִּשְׁבִיל **מוּסַר הָאָב** הוּא **חָכָם**:


**A wise son... his father’s discipline** This is an elliptical verse: a wise son asks for and loves his father’s discipline. Some say: Because of the father’s discipline, he is wise.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:1:2)


**וְלֵץ.** לֹא שָׁמַע גְּעָרָה שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל תּוֹכָחוֹת:


**but a scorner does not listen to rebuke** For he does not accept reproof.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:2:1)


**מִפְּרִי פִי אִישׁ יֹאכַל טוֹב.** מִשְּׂכַר תּוֹרָתוֹ יֹאכַל טוֹב בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, וְהַקֶּרֶן קַיֶּמֶת לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**From the fruit of man’s mouth he will eat good** From the reward of his Torah, he will eat the good in this world and the principal will exist in the world to come.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:2:2)


**וְנֶפֶשׁ בֹּגְדִים חָמָס.** וְחֵפֶץ בּוֹגְדִים חָמָס, כְּמוֹ ״אִם יֵשׁ אֶת נַפְשְׁכֶם״ (בראשית כג:ח), ״אַל תִּתְּנֵנִי בְּנֶפֶשׁ צָרָי״ (תהלים כז:יב):


**but the desire of the treacherous is violence** Heb. ונפש. The desire of the treacherous is violence, as in (Gen. 23:8): “If it is your desire (נפשכם)” and (Ps. 27:12): “Do not give me for the desire of (בנפש) my adversaries.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:3:1)


**נוֹצֵר פִּיו שׁוֹמֵר נַפְשׁוֹ פֹּשֵׂק שְׂפָתָיו.** פּוֹתֵחַ שְׂפָתָיו לְדַבֵּר תָּמִיד כָּל רוּחוֹ, מִן ״וַתְּפַשְּׂקִי אֶת רַגְלַיִךְ״ (יחזקאל טז:כה) לְשׁוֹן רָחָב:


**one who opens his lips wide** Heb. פשק, opens his lips to speak constantly all his desire, from (Ezek. 16:25): “And you spread wide (ותפשקי) your legs,” an expression of wideness.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:4:1)


**מִתְאַוָּה וָאַיִן נַפְשׁוֹ עָצֵל.** מִתְאַוָּה נַפְשׁוֹ לְכָל טוֹב וָאַיִן:


**The soul of the lazy man desires but has nothing** His soul desires all good, but has nothing.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:4:2)


**וְנֶפֶשׁ חֲרֻצִים תְּדֻשָּׁן:** יְשָׁרִים הָאוֹכְלִים יְגִיעַ כַּפֵּיהֶם. זֶהוּ מַשְׁמָעוֹ לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ. וּלְפִי מְשָׁלוֹ: לֶעָתִיד יִרְאֶה בִּכְבוֹד תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים וְיִתְאַוֶּה, וְלֹא יַשִּׂיג לוֹ:


**but the soul of the diligent shall be sated** The upright who eat from the toil of their hands. This is its meaning according to its simple interpretation. According to its allegorical meaning, in the future he will see the glory of the Torah scholar and long for it, but he will not achieve it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:5:1)


**דְּבַר שֶׁקֶר יִשְׂנָא צַדִּיק.** הַצַּדִּיק שׂוֹנֵא דְּבַר שֶׁקֶר, אֲבָל הָרָשָׁע מְקַבְּלוֹ:


**The righteous man hates a false word** The righteous man hates a false word, but the wicked accepts it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:5:2)


**יַבְאִישׁ וְיַחְפִּיר.** אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת בְּכָךְ:


**disgraces and embarrasses** the people therewith.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:6:1)


**וְרִשְׁעָה תְּסַלֵּף חַטָּאת.** כְּמוֹ חוֹטֵא, וְעַל שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע גָּמוּר קוֹרְאֵהוּ חַטָּאת. וּמַשְׁמָעוּתָהּ: אֶת הַחוֹטֵא תְּסַלֵּף רִשְׁעָתוֹ, תְּקַלְקֵל אוֹתוֹ וְתַשְׁפִּילֵהוּ. כָּל סִלּוּף – קִלְקוּל וְכִשָּׁלוֹן הוּא, כְּמוֹ ״אִוֶּלֶת אָדָם תְּסַלֵּף דַּרְכּוֹ״ (משלי יט:ג), וְתָבוֹא עָלָיו רָעָה:


**but wickedness brings ruin upon the sinner** Heb. חטאת, lit. sin. Like חטאת, a sinner, but since he is completely wicked, he calls him חטאת, sin. The meaning is that his wickedness will bring ruin upon the sinner and humble him. Every expression of סלוף means ruin and stumbling, like (below 19:3) “A person’s foolishness ruins (תסלף) his way,” and brings evil upon him.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:7:1)


**יֵשׁ מִתְעַשֵּׁר וְאֵין כֹּל.** מַרְאֶה עַצְמוֹ עָשִׁיר. דָּבָר אַחֵר: יֵשׁ אָדָם שֶׁהוּא מִתְעַשֵּׁר בְּסוֹפוֹ, וְאֵין לוֹ כְּלוּם בִּתְחִלָּתוֹ, וְיֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא בָּא לִידֵי עֲנִיּוּת מִמָּמוֹן גָּדוֹל. דָּבָר אַחֵר: מִתְעַשֵּׁר בְּגֶזֶל עֲנִיִּים, וְסוֹף אֵין לוֹ כְּלוּם, וְיֵשׁ **מִתְרוֹשֵׁשׁ** עַל יְדֵי שֶׁפִּזֵּר וְנָתַן לָאֶבְיוֹנִים, **וְהוֹן רָב** מוּכָן לוֹ:


**There is one who feigns riches** He shows himself to be rich. Another explanation: There is a man who becomes rich in the end but has nothing in the beginning, and there is one who comes to poverty from great riches. Another explanation: There is one who becomes wealthy by robbing the poor, but in the end he has nothing, and there is one who becomes poor by scattering his money and giving it to the poor, but great wealth is in store for him.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:1)


**כֹּפֶר נֶפֶשׁ אִישׁ עָשְׁרוֹ.** עָשְׁרוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם הוּא כֹּפֶר נַפְשׁוֹ, שֶׁעָשָׂה מִמֶּנּוּ צְדָקָה:


**The ransom of a man’s soul is his wealth** A man’s wealth is the ransom of his soul, for he performed charity with it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:2)


**וְרָשׁ לֹא שָׁמַע גְּעָרָה.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יַשְׁמִיעַ גְּעָרָה לָרָשׁ שֶׁנּוֹתֵן לוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מַכְלִימוֹ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כֹּפֶר נֶפֶשׁ אִישׁ עָשְׁרוֹ**, תּוֹרָתוֹ:


**as long as the poor man has not heard a rebuke** As long as he does not let the poor man hear a rebuke, i.e., he gives to him and does not embarrass him. Another explanation: **The ransom of a man’s soul is his wealth** His Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:8:3)


**וְרָשׁ.** בְּדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה **לֹא שָׁמַע גְּעָרָה.** אֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ לָסוּר מִן הָרָעָה, לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא נִזְהָר. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: עַל ״מַחֲצִית הַשֶּׁקֶל״ מְדַבֵּר, שֶׁהִטִּיל הַכָּתוּב עַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְהִשְׁוָה בּוֹ דַּל וְעָשִׁיר (שמות ל:טו); שֶׁאֵין הָרָשׁ שׁוֹמֵעַ גְּעָרָה וּכְלִמָּה מִן הֶעָשִׁיר, לֵאמֹר לוֹ: חֶלְקִי גָּדוֹל מֵחֶלְקְךָ בְּקָרְבְּנוֹת צִבּוּר:


**but a poor man** in the words of Torah, **did not hear a rebuke** He does not know to turn away from evil, since he is not cautious. The Midrash Aggadah interprets it as speaking of the half-shekel, which Scripture levied on all Israel and made the poor and the rich equal in that respect—so that the poor does not hear a rebuke from the rich, saying to him, “My share is greater than your share in the public sacrifices.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:9:1)


**יִדְעָךְ.** לְשׁוֹן קְפִיצָה, שֶׁהַשַּׁלְהֶבֶת קוֹפֶצֶת וְנִכְרֶתֶת:


**will ebb away** Heb. ידעך, an expression of springing, for the flame springs and is cut off.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:10:1)


**רַק בְּזָדוֹן יִתֵּן מַצָּה.** מַחֲלֹקֶת:


**Only with wickedness does one cause quarrels** Heb. מצה, controversy.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:10:2)


**וְאֶת נוֹעָצִים.** וְעִם הַמִּתְיַעֲצִים תָּלִין חָכְמָה:


**but there is wisdom with those who take counsel** But wisdom lies with those who behave with counsel.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:11:1)


**הוֹן מֵהֶבֶל יִמְעָט.** עוֹשֶׂה גִּרְסָתוֹ חֲבִילוֹת חֲבִילוֹת **יִמְעָט**, שֶׁיִּשְׁתַּכַּח מִמֶּנּוּ מְעַט מְעַט (עבודה זרה יט.):


**Wealth gotten by vanity** One who makes his study in bundles. **shall be diminished** For he will forget it little by little.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:1)


**תּוֹחֶלֶת מְמֻשָּׁכָה.** מַבְטִיחַ עַצְמוֹ עַל חֲבֵירוֹ וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה:


**Hope deferred** He relies on his friend and does nothing.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:2)


**מַחֲלָה לֵב.** מְבִיאָה חוֹלִי לַלֵּב, וְאֵין מַחֲלָה זֶה שֵׁם דָּבָר כְּמוֹ ״וַהֲסִרֹתִי מַחֲלָה מִקִּרְבֶּךָ״ (שמות כג:כה) אֶלָּא כְּמוֹ ״מַעֲלֵה גֵרָה״ (ויקרא יא:ה):


**makes the heart sick** Heb. מחלה, brings sickness to the heart. This מחלה is not a noun like (Ex. 23:25): “And I will remove sickness (מחלה) from your midst,” but like (Lev. 11:6): “bringing up (מעלת) the cud.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:12:3)


**וְעֵץ חַיִּים תַּאֲוָה בָאָה.** מְסֹרָס הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: **תַּאֲוָה בָאָה** הֲרֵי הוּא **כְּעֵץ חַיִּים.** תּוֹחֶלֶת שֶׁהוֹחִיל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְצִפָּה שֶׁיָּשׁוּבוּ, וְהֵם לֹא שָׁבוּ, סוֹף בָּאָה לָהֶם לְמַחֲלַת לֵב; וּכְשֶׁתַּאֲוָתוֹ בָאָה, שֶׁהֵם עוֹשִׂין רְצוֹנוֹ, **עֵץ חַיִּים** הִיא לָהֶם:


**but a desire fulfilled is a tree of life** lit. but a tree of life is a desire fulfilled. This is inverted; i.e., a desire fulfilled is like a tree of life. The hope that the Holy One, blessed be He, had hoped for Israel and looked forward for them to repent—brought them ultimately to heartsickness when they did not repent. And when His desire is fulfilled—that they comply with His will—it is a tree of life to them.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:13:1)


**בָּז לְדָבָר יֵחָבֶל לוֹ.** הַבּוֹזֶה אֶחָד מִדִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה, סוֹף מִתְמַשְׁכֵּן עָלָיו:


**He who despises a thing will be pledged to it** Heb. יחבל. He who despises one of the words of the Torah will ultimately be taken for it as a pledge.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:13:2)


**וִירֵא מִצְוָה הוּא יְשֻׁלָּם.** יְקַבֵּל שָׂכָר. וּמִדְרַשׁ תְּהִלִּים דּוֹרֵשׁ **בָּז לְדָבָר יֵחָבֶל לוֹ** עַל דָּוִד שֶׁאָמַר לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, מָה הֲנָאָה בַּשּׁוֹטִים שֶׁבָּרָאתָ? אָמַר לוֹ: חַיֶּיךָ, סוֹף שֶׁתִּצְטָרֵךְ לְשִׁטּוּת; כְּשֶׁבָּא לִפְנֵי אָכִישׁ לְהִשְׁתַּגֵּעַ ״וַיּוֹרֶד רִירוֹ עַל זְקָנוֹ וְגוֹמֵר״ (שמואל א כא:יד):


**but he who reveres a commandment will be rewarded** lit. will be paid. He will receive a reward. Midrash Tehillim expounds: “He who despises a thing will be pledged to it” concerning David, who said before the Holy One, blessed be He, “Of what use are the madmen that You created?” He replied, “By your life, you will ultimately need madness.” When he came before Achish to feign madness, “and he let his saliva run down upon his beard, etc.” (I Sam. 21:14).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:14:1)


**תּוֹרַת חָכָם מְקוֹר חַיִּים.** שֶׁהִיא מְלַמְּדַתּוֹ **לָסוּר מִמֹּקְשֵׁי מָוֶת**:


**The instruction of a wise man is a spring of life** for he teaches him to turn away from the snares of death.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:15:1)


**וְדֶרֶךְ בּוֹגְדִים אֵיתָן.** קָשֶׁה לוֹ וְלַאֲחֵרִים:


**but the way of the treacherous is harsh** It is harsh for him and for others.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:16:1)


**כָּל עָרוּם יַעֲשֶׂה בְדָעַת.** מַעֲשֵׂהוּ, כְּגוֹן דָּוִד: ״יְבַקְשׁוּ לַאדֹנִי הַמֶּלֶךְ נַעֲרָה בְתוּלָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (מלכים א א:ב):


**Every cunning man acts with forethought** He performs his deeds with forethought, like David: “Let them seek for my lord, the king, a young girl, a virgin, etc.” (I Kings 1:2).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:16:2)


**וּכְסִיל יִפְרֹשׂ אִוֶּלֶת.** זֶה אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ, ״וְיַפְקֵד הַמֶּלֶךְ״ (אסתר ב:ג). יוֹדְעִים הָיוּ שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׂא לְכֻלָּם, וּמִי שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ בַת הִטְמִינָהּ (מגילה יב:):


**but a fool exposes [his] stupidity** This is Ahasuerus (Esther 2:3): “Let the king appoint, etc.” They knew that he would not marry them all. Therefore, whoever had a daughter concealed her.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:17:1)


**מַלְאָךְ רָשָׁע יִפֹּל בְּרָע.** כְּגוֹן בִּלְעָם, שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא ״לֵךְ עִם הָאֲנָשִׁים״ (במדבר כב:כ, לה), וְהִרְשִׁיעַ לְהַשִּׂיא לְבָלָק עֵצָה רָעָה; לְכָךְ נָפַל בַּחֶרֶב (במדבר לא:ח):


**A wicked messenger falls into evil** like Balaam, to whom the Holy One, blessed be He, said (Num. 22:35): “Go with the men,” but he dealt wickedly to entice Balak to follow his evil counsel; therefore, he fell by the sword.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:17:2)


**וְצִיר אֱמוּנִים מַרְפֵּא.** זֶה מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ:


**but a faithful emissary brings healing** This is Moses, our teacher.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:18:1)


**רֵישׁ וְקָלוֹן.** דַּלּוּת וְקָלוֹן בָּאִים עַל פּוֹרֵעַ מוּסָר:


**Poverty and disgrace** Poverty and disgrace come upon him who spurns discipline.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:19:1)


**תֶּעֱרַב לַנֶּפֶשׁ.** כְּשֶׁבָּאָה תַּאֲוָתוֹ לוֹ לָאָדָם, הִיא עֲרֵבָה לְנַפְשׁוֹ; וּלְכָךְ תּוֹעֲבַת כְּסִיל לָסוּר מֵרָע, לְפִי שֶׁעָרֵב לָהֶם לְמַלֵּא תַּאֲוָתָם:


**pleases the soul** When a person’s desire comes to him it pleases his soul. Therefore, it is an abomination to a fool to turn away from evil because it pleases him to gratify his desire.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:19:2)


**נִהְיָה.** הֹוֶה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **תַּאֲוָה נִהְיָה** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִתְאַוֶּה שֶׁיַּעֲשׂוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל רְצוֹנוֹ, וּבִהְיוֹת תַּאֲוָתוֹ בָּאָה, עֲרֵבָה לוֹ. וְתוֹעֲבַת רְשָׁעִים – שֶׁיָּסוּרוּ מֵרָעָתָם, לְמַלֹּאות תַּאֲוָתָם:


**fulfilled** Heb. נהיה, lit. is. Another explanation: **a desire fulfilled** The Holy One, blessed be He, desires that Israel do His will, and when His desire is fulfilled, it pleases Him. And it is the abomination of the wicked that they turn away from their evil to gratify their desire.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:20:1)


**הוֹלֵךְ אֶת חֲכָמִים.** יֶחְכָּם, **וְרֹעֶה כְסִילִים** הַמְחַבֵּר לוֹ כְּסִילִים לִהְיוֹת לוֹ רֵעִים:


**He who goes with the wise will become wise, but he who befriends the fools** Who joins fools to be his friends.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:20:2)


**יֵרוֹעַ.** יְרוּצַּץ:


**will be broken** Heb. ירוע, will be shattered.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:21:1)


**חַטָּאִים תְּרַדֵּף רָעָה.** אָדָם רָשָׁע, רִשְׁעָתוֹ רוֹדַפְתּוֹ עַד הִשָּׁמְדוֹ:


**Evil will pursue the sinners** As for a wicked man, his wickedness pursues him to his destruction.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:22:1)


**טוֹב יַנְחִיל.** אֶת זְכוּתוֹ וְאֶת מָמוֹנוֹ לִבְנֵי בָנָיו, אֲבָל חוֹטֵא אֵינוֹ מַנְחִיל לְבָנָיו, כִּי **צָפוּן חֵילוֹ** וּמָמוֹנוֹ **לַצַּדִּיק**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״וַתָּשֶׂם אֶסְתֵּר אֶת מָרְדֳּכַי עַל בֵּית הָמָן״ (אסתר ח:ב):


**A good man will leave an inheritance** of his merit and his property to his sons’ sons, but a sinner does not leave over an inheritance to his sons, for his wealth and his property are laid away for the righteous, as it is written (Esther 8:2): “And Esther set Mordecai over the house of Haman.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:23:1)


**רָב אֹכֶל נִיר רָאשִׁים.** רָאִיתִי בַּמָּסֹרֶת הַגְּדוֹלָה: **רָב אֹכֶל.** שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה ׳רָב׳ – קְמָצִין, וְזֶהוּ חֲטַף קָמַץ, וּמְחֻבָּר עִם אֹכֶל בְּמַקֵּף; וְ׳אֹכֶל׳ רָאִיתִי שָׁם: נָקוּד פַּתַּח (ר״ל סגול שהוא פתח קטן) וְטַעֲמוֹ לְעֵילָא, כְּמוֹ ״אֶת כָּל אֹכֶל הַשָּׁנִים הַטּוֹבוֹת״ (בראשית מא:לה). וּלְפִי נְקֻדָּתוֹ, זֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: הַרְבֵּה תְּבוּאָה בָּאָה לָעוֹלָם עַל יְדֵי נִיר אֲנָשִׁים דַּלִּים, כְּלוֹמַר: הַרְבֵּה תּוֹרָה יוֹצְאָה עַל יְדֵי תַּלְמִידִים, שֶׁרַבּוֹתֵיהֶם לְמֵדִין מֵהֶם מִתּוֹךְ פִּלְפּוּלָם שֶׁמְּפַלְפְּלִים בַּהֲלָכָה:


**An abundance of food is the result of the plowing of the poor** I saw in the great Masorah [on the words רב אכל]: The word רב appears thirty-three times with a “kamatz,” but this one has a short “kamatz,” and it is joined to the word אכל, food, with a “makkaf” (hyphen); and I saw there that אכל is vowelized with a “pattah” (i.e., a “segol” which is a small “pattah”), and it is accented on the first syllable, as in (Gen. 41:35): “all the food (אכל) of the good years.” According to its vowelization, this is its interpretation: Much grain comes to the world through the plowing of the poor people; i.e., much Torah emanates from pupils whose teachers learn from them through their debate concerning the halachah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:23:2)


**וְיֵשׁ נִסְפֶּה.** וְיֵשׁ הַרְבֵּה מֵהֶם שֶׁהֵם כָּלִים מִן הָעוֹלָם בִּשְׁבִיל לֹא מִשְׁפָּט שֶׁאֵינָן נוֹהֲגִין כַּשּׁוּרָה, וּלְעִנְיַן הַתְּבוּאָה יֵשׁ תְּבוּאָה לוֹקָה בִּשְׁבִיל בְּעָלֶיהָ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹצִיא מַעַשְׂרוֹתֶיהָ וּמַתְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים כַּמִּשְׁפָּט, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת חֲגִיגָה (חגיגה ד:-ה.), **וְיֵשׁ נִסְפֶּה** עַל שְׁלוּחוֹ שֶׁל מַלְאַךְ הַמָּוֶת שֶׁמְּחַלֵּף שֵׁם בְּשֵׁם וּמֵמִית אֶת שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ זְמַנּוֹ אֲבָל אִם כֵּן הוּא אֵין עִנְיַן סוֹף הַפָּסוּק לְרֹאשׁוֹ:


**and some perish** And many of them perish from the world because [they have] no propriety, for they do not behave properly; and concerning the grain, some grain suffers because of its owner, who does not separate its tithes and its gifts for the poor as is proper. Our Sages explained in tractate Hagigah (4b) “And some perish without justice,” concerning the messenger of the angel of death, who changes one name for another and causes the death of one whose time has not come. But, if it [this explanation] is so, the end of the verse has nothing to do with its beginning.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:24:1)


**חוֹשֵׂךְ שִׁבְטוֹ.** סוֹפוֹ שֶׁשּׂוֹנֵא אֶת בְּנוֹ, שֶׁרוֹאֵהוּ בְּסוֹפוֹ יוֹצֵא לְתַרְבּוּת רָעָה:


**He who holds back his rod** His end will be that he will hate his son because he will see him getting into mischief.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:24:2)


**שִׁחֲרוֹ מוּסָר.** תָּמִיד לַבְּקָרִים מְיַסְּרֵהוּ:


**disciplines him early** He always chastises him in the morning.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:25:1)


**לְשֹׂבַע נַפְשׁוֹ.** דּוֹמֶה שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׂבֵעַ (סא״א ונכתב תחתיו):


**to sate his appetite** It always seems to him that he is satisfied. (


###### Rashi on Proverbs 13:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/13:25:2)


**תֶּחְסָר.** אֵינוֹ דּוֹמֶה שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׂבֵעַ:


**shall feel want** It does not seem to them that they are satisfied.)



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:1)


**חָכְמוֹת נָשִׁים בָּנְתָה בֵיתָהּ.** חָכְמוֹת נָקוּד פַּתָּח, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֵׁם דָּבָר, אֶלָּא הַחֲכָמוֹת שֶׁבַּנָּשִׁים בּוֹנוֹת אֶת בָּתֵּיהֶם – שֶׁמִּתְקַיְּמוֹת עַל יְדֵיהֶן; כְּמוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁל אוֹן בֶּן פֶּלֶת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּפֶרֶק ׳חֵלֶק׳ (סנהדרין קט:-קי.):


**The wisest of women** Heb. חכמות, vowelized with a “patach,” is not a noun, but [it means that] the wisest of women build their houses, for they are preserved by them, like the wife of On the son of Peleth, as is explained in the chapter entitled “Chelek.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:2)


**וְאִוֶּלֶת.** וְאִשָּׁה שׁוֹטָה:


**but a foolish one** Heb. ואולת. But a foolish woman.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:1:3)


**בְּיָדֶיהָ תֶּהֶרְסֶנּוּ.** תַּהֲרֹס בֵּיתָהּ; זוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁל קֹרַח (במדבר טז:א):


**tears it down with her hands** She tears down her house. This is Korah’s wife.]



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:2:1)


**הוֹלֵךְ בְּיָשְׁרוֹ יָרֵא.** מִי שֶׁהוּא יְרֵא ה׳, וּמִי שֶׁהוּא נְלוֹז דְּרָכִים, כְּלוֹמַר: עוֹקֵם דְּרָכָיו:


**He who fears the Lord goes in his uprightness** Heb. ירא ה', he who fears the Lord, and he who is crooked in his ways...


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:2:2)


**בוזהו.** לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**despises Him** The Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:3:1)


**בְּפִי אֱוִיל חֹטֶר גַּאֲוָה.** מַקֵּל שֶׁל גַּאֲוָה, כְּגוֹן פַּרְעֹה, שֶׁאָמַר ״מִי ה׳ אֲשֶׁר אֶשְׁמַע בְּקוֹלוֹ״ (שמות ה:ב):


**In a fool’s mouth is a staff of haughtiness** A stick of haughtiness, like Pharaoh, who said (Ex. 5: 2): “Who is the Lord that I should hearken to His voice?”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:3:2)


**תִּשְׁמוּרֵם.** כְּמוֹ ״תִּשְׁמְרֵם״ (משלי כב:יח) – תִּשְׁמֹר אֶת הַחֲכָמִים:


**but the lips of the wise guard them** Heb. תשמורם, like תשמרם. They guard the wise.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:4:1)


**בְּאֵין אֲלָפִים אֵבוּס בָּר.** בְּאֵין שְׁוָורִים הָאֵבוּס רֵיקָם, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ תֶּבֶן אֵין מָצוּי בַּבַּיִת:


**Without oxen the manger is empty** Without oxen, the manger is empty, for not even straw is found in the house.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:4:2)


**בָּר.** נָקִי וְרֵיקָם, כְּלוֹמַר: בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים אֵין הוֹרָאָה נִמְצֵאת כַּהֲלָכָה:


**empty** Heb. בר, clean and empty. This means that without Torah scholars, there is no proper instruction.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:5:1)


**וְיָפִיחַ כְּזָבִים.** וְתָמִיד מְדַבֵּר כָּזָב **עֵד שָׁקֶר**:


**but he who speaks lies** and always speaks lies, is a false witness.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:6:1)


**בִּקֵּשׁ לֵץ חָכְמָה.** כְּשֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ אֶל הַחָכְמָה אֵינוֹ מוֹצְאָהּ בְּלִבּוֹ:


**The scorner sought wisdom** When he needs wisdom, he does not find it in his heart.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:7:1)


**לֵךְ מִנֶּגֶד לְאִישׁ כְּסִיל.** מֵהִתְחַבֵּר תָּמִיד אֶצְלוֹ:


**Go far away from a foolish wisdom.** Do not always associate with him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:7:2)


**וּבַל יָדַעְתָּ.** סוֹפְךָ שֶׁלֹּא יָדַעְתָּ חָכְמָה:


**or you will know lips of knowledge** You will eventually not know wisdom.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:8:1)


**חָכְמַת עָרוּם.** מִי שֶׁהוּא חָכָם **הָבִין דַּרְכּוֹ** לְפַלֵּס אָרְחוֹתָיו:


**The wisdom of a cunning man** Heb. ערום, he who is wise. **is to understand his way** To weigh his paths.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:8:2)


**וְאִוֶּלֶת כְּסִילִים.** הִיא הַמִּרְמָה שֶׁבְּלִבָּם, סוֹפָהּ שֶׁמְּבִיאָתָם לִידֵי טִפְּשׁוּת:


**but the folly of the fools** That is the deceit in their hearts. It will ultimately bring them to foolishness.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:1)


**אֱוִילִים יָלִיץ אָשָׁם.** שֶׁהֵם חוֹטְאִים, שֶׁצְּרִיכִין לָתֵת מָמוֹן לְמִי שֶׁחָטְאוּ לוֹ, וְיָלִיץ בַּעֲדָם אֲשָׁמָם, שֶׁהֵם מְבִיאִים מְמוֹנָם לוֹ; כְּמוֹ הַפְּלִשְׁתִּים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״קְחוּ לָכֶם חֲמִשָּׁה טְחוֹרֵי זָהָב וַחֲמִשָּׁה עַכְבְּרֵי זָהָב וְגוֹמֵר וּנְתַתֶּם לֵאלֹהֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כָּבוֹד אוּלַי יָקֵל אֶת יָדוֹ מֵעֲלֵיכֶם״ (שמואל א ו:ד-ה):


**Amends for guilt plead for the fools** For they sin and have to give money to the one against whom they sinned. And their amends, which they bring him with their money, will plead for them, like the Philistines, as it is stated (I Sam. 6:4f.): “five hemorrhoids of gold, etc. and you shall give honor to the God of Israel. Perhaps He will lighten His hand from upon you.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:2)


**אָשָׁם.** אַמֶנְדָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


אשם (amende in French, geldstrafe in German) a fine.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:9:3)


**וּבֵין יְשָׁרִים רָצוֹן.** אַפֵּיְימִינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְרֻצֶּה לָהֶם:


**but there is good will among the upright** apaisement in French (cf. above 11:27), appeasement. The Holy One, blessed be He, is pleased with them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:10:1)


**לֵב יוֹדֵעַ מָרַּת נַפְשׁוֹ.** טָרְחוֹ וִיגִיעוֹ שֶׁעָמַל בַּתּוֹרָה, לְפִיכָךְ **בְּשִׂמְחָתוֹ לֹא יִתְעָרַב זָר** כְּשֶׁיְּקַבֵּל שְׂכָרוֹ לֶעָתִיד. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **לֵב יוֹדֵעַ מָרַּת נַפְשׁוֹ** יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהֵם מָרֵי נֶפֶשׁ בַּגָּלוּת וְהָיוּ נֶהֱרָגִים עַל קְדֻשַּׁת הַשֵּׁם **וּבְשִׂמְחָתוֹ לֹא יִתְעָרַב זָר** לֶעָתִיד. **בֵּית רְשָׁעִים.** בֵּית אֲרַם **יִשָּׁמֵד.**


**One’s heart knows the bitterness of his soul** His toil and labor that he toiled with the Torah. Therefore, in his joy a stranger shall not mingle. Another explanation: **One’s heart knows the bitterness of his soul** Israel, who are of embittered spirit in exile, for they are killed for the sanctification of the Name—in their joy no stranger shall mingle in the future.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:12:1)


**יֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ יָשָׁר לִפְנֵי אִישׁ.** עוֹבֵר עֲבֵירָה וְאוֹמֵר אֵין בָּהּ עֲבֵירָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **יֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ יָשָׁר לִפְנֵי אִישׁ.** דֶּרֶךְ עָצֵל יְשָׁרָה הָיְתָה בְּעֵינֵי עֵשָׂו שֶׁהָיָה אִישׁ שָׂדֶה (בראשית כה:כז), **וְאַחֲרִיתָהּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**There is a way that seems right to a man** He commits a transgression and says, “There is no transgression in it.” Another explanation: **There is a way that seems right to a man** The way of laziness seemed right to Esau, who was a man of the field, “but its end etc.“



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:13:1)


**גַּם בִּשְׂחוֹק.** שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְשַׂחֵק עִמָּהֶם בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה, יִכְאַב לִבָּם לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא. וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר ״וְאַתֶּם תִּצְעֲקוּ מִכְּאֵב לֵב״ (ישעיהו סה:יד):


**Even with laughter** that the Holy One, blessed be He, laughs with the heathens in this world. Their heart will ache in the future, and so Scripture says (Isa. 65:14): “And you shall cry out from sorrow of heart.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:14:1)


**מִדְּרָכָיו יִשְׂבַּע סוּג לֵב.** עֵשָׂו:


**The one with an impure heart shall have his fill from his ways** Esau.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:14:2)


**וּמֵעָלָיו אִישׁ טוֹב.** יַעֲקֹב:


**and above him is a good man** Jacob.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:1)


**פֶּתִי יַאֲמִין.** לְדִבְרֵיהֶם וְנִסָּת אַחֲרֵיהֶם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בִּשְׂחוֹק יִכְאַב לֵב,** ״שְׂחוֹק לִכְסִיל עֲשׂוֹת זִמָּה״ (משלי י:כג), וְסוֹפוֹ **יִכְאַב לֵב**. **סוּג לֵב** – רָשָׁע, כְּמוֹ ״כֶּסֶף סִיגִים״ (משלי כו:כג). סוּג הוּא שֵׁם הַפְּסֹלֶת, סוּג הוּא הַכֶּסֶף הַמְעֻרְבָּב בַּפְּסֹלֶת:


**A fool believes** their words and is enticed after them. Another explanation: **with laughter the heart aches** It is sport for a fool to carry out a sinful plot (above 10:23), and ultimately, his heart will ache. The one with an impure heart is a wicked man, as in (below 26:23) “Impure (סיגים) silver.” סיג is the name of the dross. סוג is the silver mixed with the dross.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:2)


**וּמֵעָלָיו אִישׁ טוֹב.** וּמִמַּעַל לוֹ לָרָשָׁע, יִהְיֶה הַצַּדִּיק:


**and above him is a good man** And above the wicked man will be the righteous man.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:3)


**פֶּתִי יַאֲמִין.** לְאַנְשֵׁי רָכִיל:


**A fool believes** talebearers,


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:15:4)


**וְעָרוּם יָבִין לַאֲשׁוּרוֹ.** לִפְסִיעוֹתָיו, כְּלוֹמַר: יְצַפֶּה מִלָּרִיב, וְיַמְתִּין עַד יִוָּדַע לוֹ אֶל נָכוֹן (שמואל א כו:ד):


**but a cunning man understands his steps** Heb. לאשרו, his steps. He waits to quarrel until he knows the matter correctly.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:1)


**חָכָם יָרֵא.** מִן הַפּוּרְעָנוּת:


**A wise man fears** retribution.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:2)


**וְסָר מֵרָע.** מִן הָרָעָה:


**and turns away from evil** From the evil.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:3)


**וּכְסִיל מִתְעַבֵּר.** מִתְחַזֵּק לַעֲבֹד בְּחָזְקָה:


**but a fool passes vigorously** He strengthens himself to pass vigorously.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:16:4)


**וּבוֹטֵחַ.** וּמַחֲלִיק וְנוֹפֵל בָּאָרֶץ, כְּמוֹ ״בְּאֶרֶץ שָׁלוֹם אַתָּה בוֹטֵחַ״ (ירמיה יב:ה), וְתִרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אַתְּ מִתְבַּטַּח וְנָפוֹל״ – מַחֲלִיק. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים: בִּטָּחוֹן מַמָּשׁ, בְּהֵיכָלוֹ; בּוֹטֵחַ לוֹמַר: לֹא תְבוֹאֵנִי רָעָה:


**and slips** Heb. ובוטח, and slips and falls to the ground, as in (Jer. 12:5): “and in the peaceful you slip (בוטח),” and Jonathan renders: את מתבטח ונפיל, meaning “you slip and fall.” Others explain this as confidence. In his palace, he is confident, saying, “The evil will not befall me.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:17:1)


**קְצַר אַפַּיִם.** הַמְּמַהֵר לִנְקֹם כַּעֲסוֹ:


**A quick tempered man** who hastens to avenge his anger.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:17:2)


**וְאִישׁ מְזִמּוֹת.** מַחֲשָׁבוֹת עֵצָה רָעָה:


**and a man of sinful plots** Thoughts of counsels of evil.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:18:1)


**יַכְתִּירוּ דָעַת.** יַעֲשׂוּהוּ כֶּתֶר לְרֹאשָׁם, וְסוֹף:


**make knowledge a crown** Heb. יכתרו, they make it a crown for their head, and ultimately...



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:19:1)


**שָׁחוּ רָעִים לִפְנֵי טוֹבִים.** לֶעָתִיד:


**The evil bend down before the good** in the future.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:20:1)


**גַּם לְרֵעֵהוּ יִשָּׂנֵא רָשׁ.** אֲפִלּוּ לְשׁוֹשְׁבִינָיו וְאוֹהֲבָיו. **גַּם** לְשׁוֹן אֲפִלּוּ:


**The poor man is hated even by his friend** Even by his friends who accompany him to his wedding and by his close friends. **even** Heb. גם, an expression meaning even.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:20:2)


**יִשָּׂנֵא רָשׁ.** עַם הָאָרֶץ שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ לִנְהוֹג כַּשּׁוּרָה:


**The poor man is hated** The ignoramus, who does not know how to behave properly.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:23:1)


**בְּכָל־עֶצֶב.** בְּכָל יְגִיעַ מְלָאכָה יִהְיֶה רֶוַח, אֲבָל דִּבְרֵי הֶבֶל **אַךְ לְמַחְסוֹר**:


**In every toil** In every toil of work, there is a gain, but in words of vanity, there is only loss.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:24:1)


**עֲטֶרֶת חֲכָמִים עָשְׁרָם.** שֶׁהֵם עֲשִׁירִים בַּתּוֹרָה:


**The crown of the wise is their wealth** that they are rich in Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:24:2)


**וְאִוֶּלֶת כְּסִילִים אִוֶּלֶת.** קַלְקַלְתָּן שֶׁל כְּסִילִים הִיא הָאִוֶּלֶת, שֶׁנִּתְעַצֵּל מִן הַחָכְמָה:


**the folly of the foolish is folly** The ruin of the fools is the folly that they neglected to learn wisdom.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:1)


**בְּיִרְאַת ה׳ מִבְטַח עֹז.** נֶאֱמַר בְּאַבְרָהָם ״כִּי עַתָּה יָדַעְתִּי כִּי יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים אַתָּה״ (בראשית כב:יב), וְהִבְטִיחוֹ ״כִּי בָרֵךְ אֲבָרֶכְךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית כב:יז):


**In the fear of the Lord is a strong promise** It is stated concerning Abraham (Gen. 22:12): “Now I know that you are a God-fearing man,” and He promised him: “For I will bless, you, etc.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:2)


**וּלְבָנָיו יִהְיֶה** הוּא:


**and he will be... for his sons** He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:26:3)


**מַחֲסֶה.** שֶׁיֵּחָסוּ וְיִתְחַבֵּא בְּצֵל זְכוּתוֹ:


**a shelter** For they will take shelter and hide in the shadow of his merit.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:1)


**בְּרָב עָם.** שֶׁהַצִּבּוּר זַכָּאִים – הַדְרַת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא הוּא:


**in a multitude of people** that the people are righteous is the glory of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:2)


**וּבְאֶפֶס לְאֹם.** כְּשֶׁאֵינָם דְּבֵקִים בּוֹ:


**but...in lack of people** When they do not cleave to Him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:28:3)


**מְחִתַּת רָזוֹן.** חֶסְרוֹן רַזְנוּתוֹ הוּא, כִּבְיָכוֹל נוֹתֵן מִכְּבוֹדוֹ לֵאלֹהֵי נֵכָר וּמַשְׁלִיט אֶת הָאֻמּוֹת עַל בָּנָיו:


**the ruin of His Princedom** It is the absence of His Princedom. So to speak, He gives of His honor to others.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:29:1)


**מֵרִים אִוֶּלֶת.** מַפְרִישָׁהּ לְחֶלְקוֹ:


**selects folly** Heb. מרים, he separates it for his share.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:30:1)


**חַיֵּי בְשָׂרִים לֵב מַרְפֵּא.** לֵב בָּשָׂר, שֶׁהוּא רוֹפֵא אֶת הָרָעָה וְעוֹבֵר עַל מִדּוֹתָיו, הוּא חַיֵּי בְּרִיּוֹתָיו שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהֵן בָּשָׂר וָדָם:


**A healing heart is the life of the flesh** A heart of flesh, which heals the evil and passes over his retaliations, is the life of the creatures of the Holy One, blessed be He, who are flesh and blood.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:30:2)


**וּרְקַב עֲצָמוֹת קִנְאָה.** אָדָם בַּעַל חֵמָה, רִקָּבוֹן עֲצָמוֹת הוּא לַכֹּל:


**but anger is the rot of the bones** A man who is often angry is the rot of everyone’s bones.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:32:1)


**וְחֹסֶה בְמוֹתוֹ צַדִּיק.** כְּשֶׁיָּמוּת, הוּא בָּטוּחַ שֶׁיָּבֹא לְגַן עֵדֶן:


**but a righteous man is confident in his death** When he dies, he is confident that he will come to Paradise.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:33:1)


**בְּלֵב נָבוֹן תָּנוּחַ חָכְמָה.** תִּשְׁכֹּן וְתִשְׁקֹט בְּנַחַת, לְשׁוֹן מְנוּחָה וּמַרְגּוֹעַ:


**Wisdom rests in the heart of an understanding man** Heb. תנוח, it dwells and rests tranquilly; an expression of rest and tranquility.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:33:2)


**וּבְקֶרֶב כְּסִילִים תִּוָּדֵעַ.** מְעַט חָכְמָה שֶׁבְּלִבּוֹ מַכְרִיזָה; אִיסְתְּרָא בְּלָגִינָא קִישׁ קִישׁ קָרְיָא (בבא מציעא פה:):


**but in the midst of the fools it is known** The little wisdom that is in his heart calls out. This parallels the Talmudic maxim (Baba Mezia 85b): “A coin in a bottle makes a sound of kish kish.”



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:34:1)


**צְדָקָה תְרוֹמֵם גּוֹי.** יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Charity will elevate a nation** Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:34:2)


**וְחֶסֶד לְאֻמִּים חַטָּאת.** הֵם עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁגּוֹזְלִים מִזֶּה וְנוֹתְנִים לָזֶה:


**but the kindness of the kingdoms is sin** They are the heathens, who rob one to give another.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Proverbs 14:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/14:35:1)


**וְעֶבְרָתוֹ** תִּהְיֶה לְעֶבֶד מֵבִישׁ (בבא בתרא י:):


**but his wrath will be** upon a shameful servant.



## Chapter 15


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:4:1)


**וְסֶלֶף בָּהּ שֶׁבֶר בְּרוּחַ.** כְּשֶׁיְּסַלֵּף בַּלָּשׁוֹן, סוֹפָהּ לְהָבִיא עָלָיו שֶׁבֶר הַבָּא בְּרוּחַ קָדִים, הַמּוּכָנָה לְהִפָּרַע מִן הָרְשָׁעִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר ״בְּרוּחַ קָדִים עַזָּה״ (שמות יד:כא), ״בְּרוּחַ קָדִים אֲפִיצֵם״ (ירמיה יח:יז), ״בְּרוּחַ קָדִים תְּשַׁבֵּר אֳנִיּוֹת תַּרְשִׁישׁ״ (תהלים מח:ח):


**but if there is perverseness in it, it causes destruction by wind** When one is perverse with his tongue, it will ultimately bring upon him destruction that comes from the east wind, which is prepared to mete out retribution upon the wicked, as it is stated (Ex. 14:21): “with a strong east wind”; (Jer. 18: 17): “Like an east wind I will scatter them”; and (Ps. 48:8): “With an east wind, You break the ships of Tarshish.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:6:1)


**בֵּית צַדִּיק חֹסֶן רָב.** בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁבָּנָה דָּוִד הַצַּדִּיק חֹסֶן רַב וּמִגְדַּל עֹז לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**The house of a righteous man possesses great strength** The Temple that David the Righteous built is a great strength and a mighty tower for Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:6:2)


**וּבִתְבוּאַת רָשָׁע נֶעְכָּרֶת.** וּבַהֲבָאַת הַצֶּלֶם שֶׁהֵבִיא מְנַשֶּׁה (דברי הימים ב לג:ז) בָּהּ נֶעְכְּרָה (דברים רבה ב:כ):


**but it becomes ruined with the bringing of the wicked man** But with the bringing of the idols that Manasseh brought into it, it was ruined.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:7:1)


**שִׂפְתֵי חֲכָמִים יְזָרוּ דָעַת.** יַכְתִּירוּ דַעַת, כְּמוֹ ״זֵר זָהָב״ (שמות כה:יא):


**The lips of the wise crown knowledge** יזרו, they crown knowledge, as in (Ex. 25: “a gold crown (זר).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:7:2)


**לֹא כֵן.** אֵינוֹ אֱמֶת:


**is not so** It is not true gold.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:8:1)


**זֶבַח רְשָׁעִים.** תּוֹעֵבָה בָּלָק וּבִלְעָם (במדבר כב-כד):


**The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination of the Lord** Balak and Balaam.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:8:2)


**וּתְפִלַּת יְשָׁרִים.** זֶה מֹשֶׁה :


**but the prayer of the upright** This is Moses.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:10:1)


**מוּסָר רָע לְעוֹזֵב אֹרַח.** יִסּוּרִים קָשִׁים מוּכָנִים לְעוֹבֵר אֹרַח שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Harsh discipline will come to him who forsakes the way** Harsh suffering is ready for him who transgresses the way of the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:11:1)


**שְׁאוֹל וַאֲבַדּוֹן נֶגֶד ה׳.** גָּלוּי לְפָנָיו כָּל אֲשֶׁר בְּתוֹכָהּ:


**The grave and Destruction are opposite the Lord** It is revealed to Him all that are therein.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:11:2)


**אַף כִּי.** קַל וָחֹמֶר הוּא:


**surely people’s hearts** This is an inference from a major to a minor.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:13:1)


**לֵב שָׂמֵחַ יֵיטִיב פָּנִים.** אִם תְּשַׂמַּח לִבּוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּלֶכְתְּךָ בִּדְרָכָיו, וְיֵיטִיב לְךָ פָּנָיו לַעֲשׂוֹת כָּל רְצוֹנְךָ. וְאִם תְּעַצְּבֵהוּ, יַרְאֶה לְךָ **רוּחַ נְכֵאָה,** כְּמָה דְּאַתְּ אָמַר (בראשית ו:ו-ז) ״וַיִּתְעַצֵּב אֶל לִבּוֹ וַיֹּאמֶר ה׳ אֶמְחֶה אֶת הָאָדָם וְגוֹמֵר״. **רוּחַ נְכֵאָה** טלנ״ט, רוּחַ שֶׁל זַעַף:


**A merry heart makes a cheerful face** If you cheer up the heart of the Holy One, blessed be He, by following His ways, He will show you a cheerful face to do your will, but if you sadden Him, He will show you a breaking spirit, as it is stated (Gen. 6:6f): “And He became saddened to His heart. And the Lord said, ‘I will erase man, etc.’“ **a breaking spirit** (Talant in Old French, explained in Ecc. 1:6) a spirit of fury.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:1)


**כָּל יְמֵי עָנִי רָעִים.** וַאֲפִלּוּ שַׁבָּתוֹת וְיָמִים טוֹבִים, דְּאָמַר שְׁמוּאֵל (סנהדרין ק:): שִׁנּוּי וֶסֶת תְּחִלַּת חֳלִי מֵעַיִם:


**All the days of a poor man are wretched** Even Sabbaths and festivals, as Samuel said. A change of diet is the beginning of intestinal disorders.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:2)


**וְטוֹב לֵב.** מִי שֶׁלִּבּוֹ טוֹב בְּעָשְׁרוֹ:


**but he who has a cheerful heart** One whose heart is cheerful with his wealth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:15:3)


**מִשְׁתֶּה תָמִיד.** כָּל שְׁנוֹתָיו דּוֹמוֹת לוֹ יְמֵי מִשְׁתֶּה, לְלַמֶּדְךָ שֶׁיְּהֵא אָדָם שָׂמֵחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ (אבות ד:א). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ מַה שֶּׁדָּרְשׁוּ בְּחֵלֶק (סנהדרין ק:-קא.):


**always has a feast** All his years seem to him like days of feasting to teach you that a person should be happy with his lot (Avoth 4:1). And our Sages expounded [on this] in the chapter entitled Chelek (Sanh. 100b, 101a).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:16:1)


**טוֹב מְעַט בְּיִרְאַת ה׳ מֵאוֹצָר רָב וּמְהוּמָה בוֹ.** קוֹל בְּנֵי אָדָם צוֹעֲקִים, שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה הָאוֹצָר מִגֶּזֶל וְחָמָס. כְּמוֹ ״וּרְאוּ מְהוּמוֹת רַבּוֹת בְּתוֹכָהּ וַעֲשׁוּקִים בְּקִרְבָּהּ״ (עמוס ג:ט):


**Better a little with the fear of the Lord, than a great treasury and turmoil with it** The voice of people shouting that the treasury was made from robbery and violence, as in (Amos 3:9): “And see great confusions within it and people being oppressed in its midst.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:17:1)


**מִשּׁוֹר אָבוּס.** שֶׁאוֹבְסִין לוֹ הַמַּאֲכָל בְּפִיו עַל כָּרְחוֹ כְּדֵי לְפַטְּמוֹ, וְכֵן ״בַּרְבֻּרִים אֲבוּסִים״ (מלכים א ד:כג). וְיֵשׁ לְמוֹשְׁלוֹ כְּנֶגֶד קֹמֶץ מְעַט שֶׁל עָנִי שֶׁחָבִיב מִשּׁוֹר חַטָּאת שֶׁל רָשָׁע. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **טוֹב אֲרֻחַת יָרָק** לָתֵת לֶעָנִי, **וְאַהֲבָה שָׁם** לְהַרְאוֹת לוֹ פָּנִים יָפוֹת **מִשּׁוֹר אָבוּס** לְהַאֲכִילוֹ בָּשָׂר שָׁמֵן וְיַרְאֵנוּ פָּנִים זוֹעֲפוֹת:


**than a fattened ox** To feed him fat meat and to show him an angry countenance. than a fattened ox אבוס. An ox into whose mouth they stuff the food against his will, in order to fatten him. Likewise (I Kings 5:3): “fatted (אבוסים) fowl.” And it can be explained allegorically as referring to the handful—the little bit offered up by the poor—[being better] than an ox for a sin-offering offered up by a wicked man. Another explanation: **Better a repast of herbs** To give a poor man. **where there is love** To show him a friendly countenance.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:1)


**אִישׁ חֵמָה יְגָרֶה מָדוֹן.** אָדָם שֶׁאֵין מַעֲצוֹר לְרוּחוֹ לְהַאֲרִיךְ חֲמָתוֹ, **יְגָרֶה מָדוֹן**:


**A man of wrath stirs up quarrel** A man who has no control over his anger, to be slow to anger, stirs up quarrels.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:2)


**וְאֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְמַהֵר לְהִנָּקֵם וְלָרִיב:


**but he who is slow to anger** Who does not hasten to take revenge and to quarrel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:18:3)


**יַשְׁקִיט רִיב.** שֶׁהוּא כָּלֶה וְשָׁקַט מֵעָלָיו (ס״א: מֵאֵלָיו):


**abates strife** It ends and abates by itself.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ עָצֵל כִּמְשׂוּכַת חָדֶק.** דּוֹמֶה בְּעֵינָיו כְּאִלּוּ הַדְּרָכִים גְּדוּרִים לְפָנָיו בְּגֶדֶר חֲדָקִים:


**The way of a lazy man is like a hedge of thorns** In his eyes, it appears as though the roads are fenced before him with a fence of thorns.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:2)


**מְשׂוּכַת.** גָּדֵר, כְּמוֹ ״הָסֵר מְשׂוּכָתוֹ״ (ישעיה ה:ה). **חֵדֶק** – קוֹצִים:


**like a hedge of** Heb. כמשכת. A fence, as in (Isa. 5:5): “I will remove its hedge (משוכתו).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:19:3)


**וְאֹרַח יְשָׁרִים סְלוּלָה.** כְּבוּשָׁה וּמוּפְנָה. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, כי תשא א): דַּרְכּוֹ שֶׁל עֵשָׂו כְּקוֹץ זֶה הַנִּסְבָּךְ בְּגִזַּת צֶמֶר, אִם תִּטְּלֵהוּ מִכָּאן, הוּא מִתְעָרֶה בְּכָאן; כָּךְ אֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לָצֵאת יְדֵי עֲלִילוֹתָיו בְּלֹא מָמוֹן:


**thorns** Heb. חדק, thorns. but the path of the upright is even סללה, trodden and cleared. The Midrash Aggadah (Pesikta d’Rav Kahana 11a) states: The way of Esau is like a thorn entangled in a fleece of wool. If you extract it from here, it catches on here. Likewise, no one can extricate himself from his false accusations without money.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:20:1)


**בֵּן חָכָם יְשַׂמַּח אָב וּכְסִיל אָדָם בּוֹזֶה אִמּוֹ.** גּוֹרֵם לְאִמּוֹ שֶׁמְּבַזִּים אוֹתָהּ:


**A wise son makes his father happy, but a foolish person despises his mother** He causes people to despise his mother.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:21:1)


**אִוֶּלֶת.** שִׂמְחָה הִיא לַחֲסַר לֵב:


**Folly is joy** It is joy for the one devoid of sense.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:22:1)


**הָפֵר מַחֲשָׁבוֹת בְּאֵין סוֹד.** בְּלֹא עֵצָה לֹא תָקוּם מַחֲשָׁבָה:


**Plans are foiled for lack of counsel** Without counsel, the plan will not be established.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:23:1)


**שִׂמְחָה לָאִישׁ בְּמַעֲנֵה פִיו.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: עַל יְדֵי מַעֲנֶה רַךְ וְדִבּוּר נַחַת, הַבְּרִיּוֹת אוֹהֲבִים אוֹתוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ עַל בַּעֲלֵי גִּירְסָא (סנהדרין קא.): אִם מוֹצְאִים בְּפִיהֶם מַעֲנֶה, אָז תִּתְקַיֵּם בָּם וְיִשְׂמְחוּ:


**A man has joy with the response of his mouth** According to its apparent meaning: Through a soft answer and gentle speech, people love him. Our Sages expounded it as referring to those who memorize the Torah; if they utter an answer with their mouth, they will remember it and rejoice with it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:23:2)


**וְדָבָר בְּעִתּוֹ מַה טּוֹב.** שׁוֹאֲלִים בְּהִלְכוֹת הַפֶּסַח וּבְהִלְכוֹת הֶחָג בִּזְמַנּוֹ (פסחים ו.):
