**and how good is a word in time!** They ask concerning the laws of Passover and the laws of Sukkoth in their season.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:24:1)


**אֹרַח חַיִּים לְמַעְלָה לְמַשְׂכִּיל.** כְּמוֹ ״שְׂרָפִים עֹמְדִים מִמַּעַל לוֹ״ (ישעיהו ו:ב), כְּלוֹמַר: לִפְנֵי הֶחָכָם קְשׁוּטָה וּמוּכָנָה אֹרַח חַיִּים:


**The path of life is above the intelligent person** As in (Isa. 6:2): “Seraphim stood above for Him.” Before the wise man, the path of life is arrayed and prepared.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:27:1)


**עוֹכֵר בֵּיתוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא **בּוֹצֵעַ בָּצַע**:


**troubles his own house** he who is greedy of gain.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:27:2)


**וְשׂוֹנֵא מַתָּנוֹת יִחְיֶה.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁהוּא שׂוֹנֵא מַתָּנוֹת, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁשּׂוֹנֵא אֶת הַגָּזֵל:


**but he who hates gifts will live** Since he hates gifts, he surely hates robbery.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:28:1)


**לֵב צַדִּיק יֶהְגֶּה לַעֲנוֹת.** יַחְשֹׁב וְיָבִין מַה יַּעֲנֶה, קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּשִׁיב דָּבָר:


**The heart of a righteous man thinks to answer** He will think and understand what to answer before replying.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:30:1)


**מְאוֹר עֵינַיִם.** בַּתּוֹרָה:


**The light of the eyes** in Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:30:2)


**יְשַׂמַּח לֵב.** שֶׁשּׁוֹאֲלִין מִמֶּנּוּ דָּבָר וְיוֹדֵעַ מַה לְּהָשִׁיב. וּלְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא תַּאֲוָה לְמַרְאוֹת עֵינַיִם, יְשַׂמַּח הַלֵּב וּמְצַחְצֵחַ תּוּגַת הַלֵּב, כְּגוֹן גַּן יָרָק וּנְהָרוֹת הַמּוֹשְׁכִים:


**makes the heart happy** when they ask him something, and he knows what to answer. According to its simple meaning, it is to be interpreted according to its apparent meaning: A thing that is a delight to the sight of the eyes makes the heart happy and cleanses the sadness of the heart, such as a vegetable garden and flowing rivers.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 15:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/15:33:1)


**וְלִפְנֵי כָבוֹד עֲנָוָה.** הָעֲנָוָה גּוֹרֶמֶת שֶׁהַכָּבוֹד בָּא:


**and before honor there is humility** Humility causes honor to come.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:1:1)


**לְאָדָם מַעַרְכֵי לֵב.** הוּא סוֹדֵר עֲצָתוֹ וּדְבָרָיו בְּלִבּוֹ:


**The preparations of the heart are man’s** He arranges his counsel and his words in his heart.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:1:2)


**וּמֵה׳ מַעֲנֵה לָשׁוֹן.** כְּשֶׁבָּא לְהָשִׁיב, הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַשְׂכִּילוֹ בִּדְבָרָיו, אוֹ אִם זָכָה מְזַמֵּן לוֹ מַעֲנֶה טוֹב:


**but the answer of the tongue is from the Lord** When he comes to answer, the Holy One, blessed be He, causes him to stumble with his words—or if he merits, He prepares a good reply for him.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:2:1)


**וְתֹכֵן רוּחוֹת ה׳.** וּמוֹנֶה הַלְּבָבוֹת, מִי הַטּוֹב וּמִי הָרָע:


**but the Lord counts the spirits** and counts the hearts; who is the good one, and who is the bad one.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:3:1)


**גֹּל אֶל ה׳ מַעֲשֶׂיךָ.** גַּלְגֵּל וְהַשְׁלֵךְ עָלָיו צְרָכֶיךָ **וְיִכֹּנוּ מַחְשְׁבֹתֶיךָ.** דָּבָר אַחֵר: **גֹּל אֶל ה׳ מַעֲשֶׂיךָ**, הִתְפַּלֵּל לְפָנָיו עַל כָּל צְרָכֶיךָ:


**Commit your affairs to the Lord** גל. Roll and cast your necessities upon Him, and your plans will be established. Another explanation: **Commit your affairs to the Lord** pray to Him for all your necessities.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:3:2)


**וְיִכֹּנוּ.** יִתְבַּסְּסוּ וְיִתְקַיְּמוּ:


**will be established** Will be founded and will endure.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:4:1)


**כֹּל פָּעַל ה׳ לְמַעֲנֵהוּ.** הַכֹּל עָשָׂה בִּשְׁבִיל קִלּוּסוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״עֱנוּ לַה׳ בְּתוֹדָה״ (תהלים קמז:ז). דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְהָעִיד עָלָיו, כְּלוֹמַר: פָּעֳלוֹ מֵעִיד עָלָיו עַל גְּבוּרוֹתָיו. שְׁנֵיהֶם בְּהַגָּדַת תְּהִלִּים:


**The Lord made everything for His praise** Heb. למענהו. He made everything for His praise, as in (Ps. 147:7): “Praise (ענו) the Lord with thanksgiving.” Another explanation: To testify concerning Him. That is to say that His work testifies concerning Him, about His mighty acts. Both are in the Aggadah of Psalms (Midrash Tehillim 19:1).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:4:2)


**וְגַם רָשָׁע.** עָשָׂה לְהַנִּיחוֹ לְיוֹם רָעָה, וְכָל זֶה לְקִלּוּסוֹ:


**even the wicked man** He made, to leave him for the day of evil. All this is for His praise.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:5:1)


**יָד לְיָד לֹא יִנָּקֶה.** מִיָּד לְיָד בָּא לוֹ גְּמוּל גֹּבַהּ לִבּוֹ:


**hand to hand he will not go unpunished** From hand to hand the punishment for his haughtiness will come to him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:6:1)


**וּבְיִרְאַת ה׳ סוּר מֵרָע.** עַל יְדֵי יִרְאַת ה׳ סוּר מֵרָע:


**and through fear of the Lord turn away from evil** Through the fear of the Lord turn away from evil.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:7:1)


**בִּרְצוֹת ה׳ דַּרְכֵי אִישׁ גַּם אוֹיְבָיו יַשְׁלִם אִתּוֹ.** יֵרָצֶה לוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ שְׁלֵמִים עִמּוֹ:


**When the Lord accepts a person’s ways, He will cause even his enemies to make peace with him** He will appease [them] for him that they should make peace with him.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:9:1)


**לֵב אָדָם יְחַשֵּׁב דַּרְכּוֹ.** לָלֶכֶת דֶּרֶךְ יָשָׁר:


**A man’s heart plans his way** to walk on the straight way.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:9:2)


**וַה׳ יָכִין צַעֲדוֹ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (שבת קד.): הַבָּא לִטָּהֵר מְסַיְּעִין לוֹ:


**but the Lord prepares his step** as we learned: He who comes to purify himself is granted assistance.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:10:1)


**קֶסֶם עַל שִׂפְתֵי מֶלֶךְ.** עַל שִׂפְתֵי חָכָם הַיּוֹשֵׁב בְּדִין:


**There is magic on a king’s lips** On the lips of a wise man sitting in judgment.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:11:1)


**פֶּלֶס וּמֹאזְנֵי מִשְׁפָּט וְגוֹ׳.** לְשַׁלֵּם לָאָדָם כְּפָעֳלוֹ, מִשְׁפָּט יוּשְׁטִיצָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז, פֵּרָעוֹן עֲוֹנוֹת הָאָדָם בְּפֶלֶס וּמֹאזְנַיִם:


**The balance and scales of justice are the Lord’s etc.** to requite a person according to his deed. משפט is (justice in French, in German Gerechtigkeit, and so in Isaiah 7,) the retribution for a person’s sins with a balance and scales.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:11:2)


**מַעֲשֵׂהוּ כָּל אַבְנֵי כִיס.** כַּאֲשֶׁר יֵשׁ בְּאַבְנֵי כִיס מִשְׁקָלוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת וּקְטַנּוֹת, הַכֹּל לְפִי פָּעֳלוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם:


**all the weights in the bag** Just as among the weights in the bag, there are large weights and small ones, so is everything according to a person’s deeds.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:12:1)


**תּוֹעֲבַת מְלָכִים עֲשׂוֹת רֶשַׁע.** דָּבָר תָּעוּב הוּא לַדַּיָּנִין וְאֵין הָגוּן לָהֶם לַעֲשׂוֹת רֶשַׁע:


**It is an abomination to kings to commit wickedness** It is an abominable thing for judges, and it is not proper for them to commit wickedness.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:14:1)


**חֲמַת מֶלֶךְ.** הוּא כְּמַלְאֲכֵי מָוֶת, כִּשְׁלוּחֵי מִיתָתָם:


**The king’s wrath** is like messengers of death, like messengers of death [but not angels of death].


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:14:2)


**וְאִישׁ חָכָם יְכַפְּרֶנָּה.** יְפַיְּסֶנָּה:


**will pacify it** Will placate it.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:15:1)


**בְּאוֹר פְּנֵי מֶלֶךְ חַיִּים.** מִי שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַסְבִּיר לוֹ פָּנִים, חַיִּים הוּא לוֹ, לְכָךְ צָרִיךְ אָדָם לְיַשֵּׁר דַּרְכּוֹ לְפָנָיו:


**In the light of the King’s countenance is life** The one to whom the Holy One, blessed be He, shows a bright countenance—it is life to him. Therefore, a person must straighten his way before Him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:15:2)


**וּרְצוֹנוֹ כְּעָב מַלְקוֹשׁ.** מִי שֶׁהוּא מְרֻצֶּה לוֹ, רְצוֹנוֹ טוֹב לוֹ כְּעָב הַמֵּבִיא אֶת הַמָּטָר:


**and His delight is like a cloud of the late rain** Whoever is accepted by Him—His delight is beneficial to him like the cloud that brings the rain.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:19:1)


**טוֹב שְׁפַל רוּחַ.** טוֹב לְהִתְחַבֵּר **אֶת עֲנָוִים** וְלִהְיוֹת אִתָּם שְׁפַל רוּחַ, מִהֱיוֹת **מְחַלֵּק שָׁלָל אֶת גֵּאִים**:


**It is better to be of humble spirit** It is better to join the lowly and to be of humble spirit with them than to divide spoils with the haughty.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:1)


**מַשְׂכִּיל עַל דָּבָר יִמְצָא טוֹב.** הַנּוֹתֵן לֵב לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן עַל דְּבָרָיו לְפַלֵּס אֶת דְּרָכָיו יִמְצָא טוֹב:


**He who considers a matter** One who sets his heart to consider his matters, to weigh his ways, will find good.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:2)


**וּבוֹטֵחַ בַּה׳ אַשְׁרָיו.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מְפַלֵּס דְּרָכָיו וְרוֹאֶה בָּהּ מִצְוָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ סַכָּנָה אוֹ חֶסְרוֹן כִּיס, וּבוֹטֵחַ בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְעוֹשֶׂה הַטּוֹב:


**and he who trusts in the Lord is fortunate** When he weighs his ways and sees that a commandment involves danger or monetary loss, but he trusts in the Holy One, blessed be He, and [still] does that good deed.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:20:3)


**אַשְׁרָיו.** הֵן אֲשׁוּרָיו שֶׁלּוֹ:


**is fortunate** They are his fortunes.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:1)


**לַחֲכַם לֵב יִקָּרֵא נָבוֹן.** שֶׁלָּמַד חָכְמָה מֵרַבּוֹ:


**The wise-hearted shall be called understanding** He who learned wisdom from his teacher.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:2)


**יִקָּרֵא נָבוֹן.** סוֹפוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה נָבוֹן בִּדְבָרִים, מְפֻלְפָּל בְּחָכְמָתוֹ, וְיִקְרְאוּ אוֹתוֹ נָבוֹן:


**shall be called understanding**. Ultimately, he will be understanding in matters and keen in his wisdom, and they will call him understanding.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:21:3)


**וּמֶתֶק שְׂפָתַיִם יוֹסִיף לֶקַח.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם מַטְעִים דְּבָרָיו לְתַלְמִיד וּמַמְתִּיק דְּבָרָיו בִּטְעָמִים, יוֹסִיף לֶקַח:


**and the sweetness of speech increases learning** When a person explains his words to a pupil and makes his words sweet with reasons, he increases learning.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:22:1)


**מְקוֹר חַיִּים שֵׂכֶל.** לִבְעָלָיו וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹן הַמִּקְרָא: שֵׂכֶל הַבְּעָלִים מְקוֹר חַיִּים הוּא לוֹ:


**Intelligence is a fountain of life to one who has it** lit. a fountain of life is the intelligence of its possessor. And this is the interpretation of the verse: The intelligence of the one who has it is a fountain of life to him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:22:2)


**וּמוּסַר אֱוִילִים אִוֶּלֶת.** יִסּוּרֵי אֱוִילִים עַל יְדֵי אִוֶּלֶת בָּאִים לָהֶם, וְכֵן מַשְׁמָעוֹ: הָאִוֶּלֶת יִסּוּרִין הוּא לָאֱוִילִים:


**but the chastisement of the fools is folly** But the chastisement of the fools comes to them because of folly. And this is the apparent meaning: Folly is chastisement to the fools.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:23:1)


**לֵב חָכָם יַשְׂכִּיל פִּיהוּ.** לִבּוֹ מְלַמֵּד אֶת פִּיו לְדַבֵּר צָחוֹת. (**וְעַל שְׂפָתָיו יוֹסִיף** לִבּוֹ **לֶקַח**):


**The heart of a wise man gives his mouth intelligence** His heart teaches his mouth to speak lucidly.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:24:1)


**צוּף דְּבַשׁ.** מְתוֹק דְּבַשׁ, בְּרִישְׁקָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a honeycomb** Heb. צוף דבש, sweet as honey (bresche in O.F.).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:24:2)


**אִמְרֵי נֹעַם.** דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה:


**Pleasant words** Words of Torah.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:25:1)


**יֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ.** שֶׁהוּא יָשָׁר לִפְנֵי אִישׁ:


**There is a way** that is straight in a man’s view.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:26:1)


**עָמְלָה לוֹ.** לְצָרְכּוֹ הוּא עָמֵל:


**labors for him** It labors for his needs.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:26:2)


**כִּי אָכַף עָלָיו פִּיהוּ.** כְּשֶׁפִּיהוּ כּוֹפֵהוּ וְתוֹבֵעַ לוֹ מַאֲכָל, אָז עֲמָלוֹ עוֹמֶדֶת לוֹ, שֶׁאָכַל מַה שֶּׁעָמַל כְּבָר:


**when his mouth forces him** When his mouth forces him and demands food, then his labor stands up for him, for he eats what he has already worked for.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:1)


**כּוֹרֶה רָעָה.** בְּלִבּוֹ חוֹרֵשׁ הָרָע:


**digs up evil** In his heart, he plots evil.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:2)


**וְעַל.** שְׂפָתָיו כְּאֵשׁ צָרָבֶת הָרָעָה כְּאֵשׁ שׂוֹרֶפֶת עַל שְׂפָתָיו, עַד שֶׁמּוֹצִיאָהּ בְּפִיו וְגוֹמְרָהּ:


**and on his lips is a kind of searing fire** The evil burns on his lips until he expresses it with his mouth and executes it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:27:3)


**כְּאֵשׁ צָרָבֶת.** שׂוֹרֶפֶת, כְּמוֹ ״וְנִצְרְבוּ בָהּ כָּל פָּנִים״ (יחזקאל כא:ג):


**a kind of searing fire** Heb. צרבת burning, as in (Ezek. 21:3): “And all faces shall be scorched (ונצרבו) by it.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:28:1)


**וְנִרְגָּן מַפְרִיד אַלּוּף.** וְעַל יְדֵי רִיגּוּנוֹ וְתַרְעוֹמְתּוֹ מַפְרִיד מִמֶּנּוּ אַלּוּפוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם (בראשית רבה כ:ב):


**and a grumbler alienates the Lord** And through his grumbling, he alienates the Lord of the world from himself.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:30:1)


**עוֹצֶה עֵינָיו.** לְשׁוֹן קְרִיצָה, וְכֵן ״אִיעֲצָה עָלֶיךָ עֵינִי״ (תהילים לב:ח):


**He winks his eyes** Heb. עצה, an expression of winking, and so (Ps. 32:8): “I will wink (איעצה) to you my eye.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:30:2)


**כִּלָּה רָעָה.** כְּמוֹ ״כִּי כָלְתָה אֵלָיו הָרָעָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (אסתר ז:ז), וְגוֹרֵם לָבֹא רָעָה לָעוֹלָם:


**bringing evil to pass** כלה, as in (Esther 7:7) “that the evil was determined (כלתה) against him by the king.” He causes evil to come to the world.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:31:1)


**בְּדֶרֶךְ צְדָקָה תִּמָּצֵא.** עַל יְדֵי הַצְּדָקָה מַאֲרִיכִין יְמֵיהֶם:


**it will be found in the way of righteousness** Through righteousness, they live long.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:32:1)


**וּמוֹשֵׁל בְּרוּחוֹ.** כּוֹבֵשׁ אֶת יִצְרוֹ (אבות ד:א):


**and one who rules over his spirit** He conquers his evil inclination.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:33:1)


**בַּחֵיק יוּטַל אֶת הַגּוֹרָל.** בֵּינוֹ לְבֵין עַצְמוֹ אָדָם מֵטִיל גּוֹרָל:


**The lot is cast in the lap** By himself, a person cast lots.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 16:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/16:33:2)


**וּמֵה׳ כָּל מִשְׁפָּטוֹ.** לִבְרוֹר לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד חֶלְקוֹ:


**but all his judgment** to choose for each one his share.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:1:1)


**טוֹב פַּת חֲרֵבָה וְשַׁלְוָה בָהּ.** טוֹב הָיָה לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַחֲרִיב בֵּיתוֹ וְעִירוֹ, וְהָיָה בְשַׁלְוָה מֵעֲבֵירוֹתֵיהֶן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Better a piece of dry bread and tranquility with it** It would have been better for the Holy One, blessed be He, to destroy His Temple and His city, for He would enjoy tranquility from the sins of Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:1:2)


**מִבַּיִת מָלֵא זִבְחֵי רִיב.** שֶׁהָיוּ מַקְרִיבִין זִבְחֵי רִיב בְּבֵיתוֹ:


**than a house full of sacrifices of strife** For they were sacrificing sacrifices of strife in his Temple.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:2:1)


**עֶבֶד מַשְׂכִּיל וְגוֹ׳.** עַל שֶׁהִשְׂכִּיל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לִפְסֹעַ שָׁלֹשׁ פְּסִיעוֹת לִכְבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, עָלָה לִגְדֻלָּה וּמָשַׁל בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהוֹבִישׁוּ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם **וְחָלַק נַחֲלָתָם לְעֵינֵיהֶם.** דָּבָר אַחֵר: גֵּר צֶדֶק טוֹב מֵאֶזְרָח רָשָׁע, וְלֶעָתִיד לָבוֹא יְחַלֵּק שָׁלָל וְנַחֲלָה בְּתוֹךְ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְהָיָה בַשֵּׁבֶט אֲשֶׁר גָּר הַגֵּר וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל מז:כג):


**An intelligent slave, etc.** Because Nebuchadnezzar was wise enough to take three steps in honor of the Holy One, blessed be He, he achieved greatness and ruled over Israel, who performed disgraceful deeds, and he divided their inheritance before their eyes. Another explanation: A righteous proselyte is better than a wicked person who was home born, and in the future, he will share the spoils and the inheritance among the children of Israel, as it is said (Ezek. 47:23): “And it shall be, in whatever tribe the stranger will live, etc.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:1)


**מַצְרֵף לַכֶּסֶף.** כְּלִי שֶׁצּוֹרְפִין בּוֹ כֶּסֶף וְעוֹשִׂין אוֹתוֹ מֵאֵפֶר מִקְלֶה עַל גַּבֵּי חֶרֶס, קָרוּי **מַצְרֵף**:


**A refining pot is for silver** A vessel in which they refine silver and which is made from burnt ash on earthenware is called מצרף, a refining pot.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:2)


**וְכוּר לַזָּהָב.** כְּלִי שֶׁצּוֹרְפִין בּוֹ אֶת הַזָּהָב, כְּלִי שֶׁזּוֹקְקִין בּוֹ אֶת הַזָּהָב, כְּמוֹ קְדֵרָה שְׁבוּרָה, קְרוּיָה כּוּר:


**and a furnace is for gold** A vessel in which gold is refined, a vessel in which gold is purified, like a broken pot, is called כור, a furnace.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:3:3)


**מַצְרֵף לַכֶּסֶף וְכוּר לַזָּהָב.** הַמַּצְרֵף עָשׂוּי לִצְרֹף כֶּסֶף לִמְלַאכְתּוֹ וְכֵן הַכּוּר לְזַקֵּק הַזָּהָב, אֲבָל הַלְּבָבוֹת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא זוֹקְקָן וּבוֹחֲנָן וְיוֹדֵעַ מַחְשְׁבוֹתָן:


**A refining pot is for silver, and a furnace is for gold** The refining pot is made to refine silver for its work, and so is the furnace to purify the gold, but [peoples’] hearts—the Holy One, blessed be He, purifies them and tests them and knows their thoughts.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:1)


**מֵרַע.** רָשָׁע:


**An evildoer** A wicked man.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:2)


**מַקְשִׁיב עַל שְׂפַת אָוֶן שֶׁקֶר.** מְקַבֵּל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע וְעֵדֵי שֶׁקֶר:


**hearkens to a language of violence and lies** He accepts slander and false testimony.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:4:3)


**מֵזִין.** מַאֲזִין, מִי שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁקֵּר מַאֲזִין **עַל לְשׁוֹן הַוּוֹת**:


**lends an ear** Heb. מזין like מאזין He who lies lends an ear to destructive speech.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:6:1)


**עֲטֶרֶת זְקֵנִים.** כְּשֶׁרוֹאִים בְּנֵי בְנֵיהֶם הוֹלְכִין בְּדֶרֶךְ טוֹבָה:


**the crown of the aged** when they see their children’s children going on the good way.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:6:2)


**וְתִפְאֶרֶת בָּנִים אֲבוֹתָם.** כְּשֶׁאֲבוֹתֵיהֶם צַדִּיקִים, הִיא תִּפְאֶרֶת הַבָּנִים:


**and the glory of the children is their fathers** When their fathers are righteous, it is glory for the children.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:7:1)


**שְׂפַת יֶתֶר.** דִּבְרֵי גַּאֲוָה:


**Proud words** Heb. שפת יתר, words of pride.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:7:2)


**אַף כִּי לְנָדִיב.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁלֹּא נָאֶה **שְׂפַת שֶׁקֶר לְנָדִיב**:


**surely not lying speech a generous one** And surely, lying speech does not befit a generous one.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:8:1)


**אֶבֶן חֵן.** כְּשֶׁהָאָדָם בָּא לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וּמְפַיְּסוֹ בִּדְבָרִים וְשָׁב לוֹ, **אֶבֶן חֵן** וּמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת הִיא בְּעֵינָיו:


**a precious stone** When a person comes before the Holy One, blessed be He, and placates Him with words and returns to Him, it is as **a precious stone** and as pearls in His eyes.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:8:2)


**אֶל כָּל אֲשֶׁר יִפְנֶה יַשְׂכִּיל.** בְּכָל מַה שֶּׁיְּבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ מַצְלִיחוֹ:


**wherever he turns, he prospers** In whatever he asks of Him, He makes him prosper.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:1)


**מְכַסֶּה פֶּשַׁע מְבַקֵּשׁ אַהֲבָה.** אִם יֶחֱטָא אִישׁ לְאִישׁ, וְזֶה מְכַסֶּה עָלָיו וְאֵינֶנּוּ מַזְכִּיר לוֹ חַטָּאתוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מַרְאֶה לוֹ פָּנִים זוֹעֲמוֹת, גּוֹרֵם לוֹ שֶׁיֹּאהַב אוֹתוֹ:


**He who conceals transgression seeks love** If one man sins against another, and the latter conceals it for him, does not remind him of his sin, and does not show him an angry face, he causes him to love him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:2)


**וְשׁוֹנֶה בְדָבָר.** שֶׁנּוֹטֵר אֵיבָה וּמַזְכִּירוֹ: כָּךְ וְכָךְ עָשִׂיתָ לִי! מִיָּד:


**but he who harps on a matter** Who bears a grudge and reminds him, “You did such and such a thing to me.” Immediately...


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:9:3)


**מַפְרִיד אַלּוּף.** אָז מַפְרִיד מִמֶּנּוּ אַלּוּפוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם שֶׁהוּא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהִזְהִירוֹ ״לֹא תִקֹּם וְלֹא תִטֹּר״ (ויקרא יט:יח):


**alienates the Lord** Then he alienates the Lord of the world.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:10:1)


**תַּחַת גְּעָרָה בְמֵבִין.** תַּחַת זוֹ טַעֲמוֹ לְמַעְלָה בַּתָּי״ו, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בְּכָל הַמִּקְרָא, לְכָךְ אֲנִי אוֹמֵר שֶׁהִיא שֵׁם דָּבָר כְּמוֹ ״חִתַּת״ (בראשית לה:ה), וְכֵן פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: הַכְנָעַת גְּעָרָה נִכֶּרֶת בְּמֵבִין יוֹתֵר מִמֵּאָה מַכּוֹת שֶׁמַּכִּין אֶת הַכְּסִיל:


**The humility caused by the rebuke of an understanding person** Heb. תחת. The accent in the word תחת is above on the “tav,” unlike the rest of the Scriptures. Therefore, I say that it is a noun, like תחת and its explanation is as follows. The humility caused by a rebuke is recognizable in an understanding person more than one hundred blows that they strike the fool.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:11:1)


**אַךְ מְרִי יְבַקֶּשׁ רָע.** כְּלוֹמַר: מִי שֶׁכָּל דְּבָרָיו מְרִי וְסַרְבָנוּת הוּא יְבַקֵּשׁ רָע תָּמִיד:


**He who is only rebellious seeks evil** One whose every word is rebelliousness and stubbornness constantly seeks evil.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:12:1)


**פָּגוֹשׁ דֹּב שַׁכּוּל בְּאִישׁ.** טוֹב לָאָדָם שֶׁיִּפְגַּע בּוֹ דֹּב מְשַׁכֵּל, וְאַל יִפְגַּע בּוֹ אֶחָד מִן הַכְּסִילִים הַמְּסִיתִים אוֹתוֹ לָסוּר מֵה׳ וּמִתּוֹרָתוֹ:


**May a bereaving bear encounter a person** It is better for a person that a bereaving bear encounter him rather than one of the foolish heathens, who entice him to idolatry.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:14:1)


**פּוֹטֵר מַיִם רֵאשִׁית מָדוֹן.** הַמַּתְחִיל בִּמְרִיבָה הוּא כְּפוֹתֵחַ חוֹר בְּגִדְרֵי אַגְפֵי אַמַּת הַמַּיִם וְהַמַּיִם יוֹצְאִין בּוֹ, וְהַחוֹר הוֹלֵךְ וּמַרְחִיב. כֵּן הַמָּדוֹן הוֹלֵךְ וְגָדֵל תָּמִיד:


**The beginning of strife is like letting out water** Whoever commences a quarrel is like one who opens a hole in the stone walls of a water canal, and the water goes out through it, and the hole becomes progressively wider; so does the quarrel constantly escalate.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:14:2)


**וְלִפְנֵי הִתְגַּלַּע.** קוֹדֶם שֶׁתִּתְגַּלַּע חֶרְפָּתְךָ נְטוֹשׁ אֶת הָרִיב (סנהדרין ו:):


**and before you are exposed** Before your shame is exposed, abandon the quarrel.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:16:1)


**לָמָּה זֶּה מְחִיר בְּיַד כְּסִיל לִקְנוֹת חָכְמָה.** לִלְמֹד תּוֹרָה:


**Why is there a price in the fool’s hand to buy wisdom** To learn the Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:16:2)


**וְלֶב אָיִן.** וְאֵין בְּלִבּוֹ לְקַיְּמָהּ, וְאֵינוֹ לוֹמֵד תּוֹרָה אֶלָּא לִקְנוֹת שֵׁם:


**when the heart is not here** In his heart, he has no intention of fulfilling it, and he learns only to gain a reputation.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:17:1)


**בְּכָל עֵת אוֹהֵב הָרֵעַ.** לְעוֹלָם הֱוֵי אוֹהֵב רֵעִים, לִקְנוֹת אוֹהֲבִים:


**At all times, love a friend** You should always love friends, i.e., to acquire people who love you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:17:2)


**וְאָח לְצָרָה יִוָּלֵד.** לְעֵת הַצָּרָה יִוָּלֵד לְךָ הָרֵעַ כְּאָח, לַעֲזֹר לְךָ וּלְהִשְׁתַּתֵּף בְּצָרָתְךָ:


**for he is born a brother for adversity** At the time of adversity, the friend will be born to you as a brother, to help you and to participate in your adversity.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:18:1)


**אָדָם חֲסַר לֵב תּוֹקֵעַ כָּף.** עֲרֵבוּת מָמוֹן. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **תּוֹקֵעַ כַּף** לָרְשָׁעִים כְּדֵי לָלֶכֶת בְּדַרְכֵיהֶם, וַהֲרֵי כְּבָר **עֹרֵב עֲרֻבָּה לִפְנֵי רֵעֵהוּ.** כְּבָר עָרַב לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִשְׁמֹר מִצְווֹתָיו:


**A person without sense clasps hands** [This refers to] surety of money. Another explanation: He clasps with the wicked to go in their ways, while he is already a guarantor for his friend. He already **accepted surety** for the Holy One, blessed be He, to keep His commandments.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:1)


**אוֹהֵב פֶּשַׁע.** לִפְשׁוֹעַ בַּחֲבֵירוֹ:


**He who delights in transgression** to sin against his friend.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:2)


**אוֹהֵב מַצָּה.** לְהִתְקוֹטֵט וְלָרִיב:


**delights in quarrels** To quarrel and strive.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:19:3)


**מַגְבִּיהַּ פִּתְחוֹ.** מְדַבֵּר בְּגַאֲוָה, כְּמוֹ ״שְׁמֹר פִּתְחֵי פִיךָ״ (מיכה ז:ה):


**he who speaks haughtily** lit. he who raises his opening, speaks with haughtiness, as in (Micah 7:5) “guard the openings of your mouth.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:22:1)


**לֵב שָׂמֵחַ יֵיטִיב גֵּהָה.** כְּשֶׁהָאָדָם שָׂמֵחַ בְּחֶלְקוֹ, פָּנָיו מְאִירִין:


**A happy heart enhances one’s brilliance** Heb. גהה. When a person is satisfied with his lot, his countenance shines.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:23:1)


**שֹׁחַד מֵחֵיק רָשָׁע יִקָּח.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְקַבֵּל דִּבְרֵי הַכְנָעָה וּפִיּוּס מֵחֵיק הָרְשָׁעִים, כְּלוֹמַר: בַּסֵּתֶר בֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָם:


**He will take a bribe from a wicked man’s bosom** The Holy One, blessed be He, accepts words of humility and appeasement from the bosom of the wicked; i.e., in secret, between Him and them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:23:2)


**לְהַטּוֹת אָרְחוֹת מִשְׁפָּט.** לַהֲפֹךְ דִּינוֹ מֵרָעָה לְטוֹבָה:


**to pervert the roads of justice** To overturn his verdict from evil to good.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:24:1)


**אֶת פְּנֵי מֵבִין חָכְמָה.** הַחָכְמָה לִפְנֵי מֵבִין הוּא:


**Wisdom is directly in front of an understanding man** The wisdom is directly in front of an understanding man.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:24:2)


**וְעֵינֵי כְסִיל בִּקְצֵה אָרֶץ.** לוֹמַר: אֵין הַחָכְמָה מְצוּיָה לְפָנַי כִּי רְחוֹקָה הִיא מִמֶּנִּי, אֵיךְ אוּכַל לִשְׁנוֹת סֵדֶר נְזִיקִין שֶׁהוּא שְׁלֹשִׁים פְּרָקִים, מַסֶּכֶת כֵּלִים – שְׁלֹשִׁים פְּרָקִים, מַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת – עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה פְּרָקִים. אֲבָל לֶחָכָם הוּא דָּבָר קַל: הַיּוֹם שׁוֹנֶה שְׁנֵי פְרָקִים וּמָחָר שְׁנַיִם, וְאוֹמֵר: כָּךְ עָשׂוּ אוֹתָן שֶׁהָיוּ לְפָנַי מֵעוֹלָם:


**but the eyes of a fool are at the end of the earth** saying, “Wisdom is inaccessible to me [lit. The wisdom is not found before me] because it is far from me. How will I be able to learn the Order Nezikin, which consists of thirty chapters; Tractate Kelim, consisting of thirty chapters; Tractate Shabbath, consisting of twenty-four chapters?” But, for the wise man, it is an easy matter: “Today I learn two chapters and tomorrow two chapters.” And he says, “This is what those who preceded me from time immemorial did.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:25:1)


**כַּעַס לְאָבִיו בֵּן כְּסִיל.** כְּגוֹן יָרָבְעָם כַּעַס הוּא לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא (מלכים א יב:כו-לג):


**A foolish son causes anger to his father** [A foolish son] like Jeroboam causes anger to the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:25:2)


**וּמֶמֶר לְיוֹלַדְתּוֹ.** לִכְנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהֶחֱטִיא אוֹתָם:


**and bitterness to her who bore him** To the people of Israel, whom he caused to sin.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:1)


**גַּם עֲנוֹשׁ לַצַּדִּיק לֹא טוֹב.** לֹא אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִמְחוֹת שֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ, כִּי לֹא טוֹב בְּעֵינָיו לַעֲנוֹשׁ כֻּלָּם:


**To punish also the righteous man is not good** The Holy One, blessed be He, did not decide to obliterate the name of Israel from upon the earth, as it was not good in his sight to punish all of them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:2)


**לְהַכּוֹת נְדִיבִים עֲלֵי יֹשֶׁר.** לְהַכּוֹת הַטּוֹבִים שֶׁבָּהֶם שֶׁהָיוּ נְדִיבִים עֲלֵי יֹשֶׁר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וְהִשְׁאַרְתִּי בְיִשְׂרָאֵל שִׁבְעַת אֲלָפִים כָּל הַבִּרְכַּיִם אֲשֶׁר לֹא כָרְעוּ לַבַּעַל״ (מלכים א יט:יח):


**to strike the generous who possess uprightness** To strike the good people among them, who were generous and possessing uprightness, as it is said (I Kings 19:18): “And I will leave over in Israel seven thousand—all the knees that did not kneel to the Baal.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:26:3)


**נְדִיבִים עֲלֵי יֹשֶׁר.** דֻּגְמַת הָאָמוּר בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר ״וְנָדִיב נְדִיבוֹת יָעָץ וְהוּא עַל נְדִיבוֹת יָקוּם״ (ישעיהו לב:ח), נִמְצָא ״נָדִיב״ עַל ״נְדִיבוֹת״:


**the generous who possess uprightness** Heb. נדיבים עלי ישר, an analogy of what is stated elsewhere (Isa. 32:8): “But the generous person plans generous deeds, and he, because of generous deeds, shall stand.” We find “a generous man because of generous deeds.”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:27:1)


**חוֹשֵׂךְ אֲמָרָיו.** מִי שֶׁהוּא יוֹדֵעַ דַּעַת אֵינוֹ מַרְבֶּה בִּדְבָרִים:


**keeps back his words** He who has knowledge does not talk excessively.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 17:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/17:27:2)


**יְקַר רוּחַ.** מוֹנֵעַ דְּבָרִים אִישׁ תְּבוּנָה:


**he whose breath is dear** (Whose words are dear; Salonica ed.) He who keeps back his words is a man of understanding.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:1:1)


**לְתַאֲוָה יְבַקֵּשׁ נִפְרָד.** מִי שֶׁהוּא נִפְרָד מֵהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמֹר מִצְווֹתָיו, לְתַאֲוַת לִבּוֹ וְיִצְרוֹ הָרַע הוּא רוֹדֵף, וְסוֹף **בְּכָל תּוּשִׁיָּה יִתְגַּלָּע.** בֵּין חֲכָמִים תִּגָּלֶה חֶרְפָּתוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ (נזיר כג:) בְּלוֹט שֶׁנִּפְרַד מֵאַבְרָהָם עַל תַּאֲוַת לִבּוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּבְחַר לוֹ לוֹט אֵת כָּל כִּכַּר הַיַּרְדֵּן״ (בראשית יג:יא). כָּל הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה עַל שֵׁם נִיאוּף נֶאֱמַר, וְסוֹפוֹ נִתְגַּלָּה קְלוֹנוֹ בְּבָתֵּי כְנֵסִיּוֹת וּבְבָתֵּי מִדְרָשׁוֹת: ״לֹא יָבֹא עַמּוֹנִי וּמוֹאָבִי״ (דברים כג:ד):


**He who is separated seeks lust** He who is separated from the Holy One, blessed be He, not keeping His precepts, pursues the lust of his heart and his evil inclination, and finally... **in all sound wisdom, he is exposed** Among the wise, his disgrace will be revealed. Our Sages expounded this as referring to Lot, who separated from Abraham because of the lust of his heart, as it is stated (Gen. 13:11): “And Lot chose for himself the entire plain of the Jordan, etc.” This whole verse is stated concerning adultery. His end was that his shame was exposed in the synagogues and in the study halls, [when people read] (Deut. 23:4): “Neither an Ammonite nor a Moabite may enter [the congregation of the Lord].”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:2:1)


**כִּי אִם בְּהִתְגַּלּוֹת לִבּוֹ.** כִּי אִם בְּגִלּוּי לִבּוֹ, הוּא חָפֵץ לְגַלּוֹת מַה שֶּׁבְּלִבּוֹ:


**but in revealing his heart** But in the revelation of his heart. He wishes to reveal what is in his heart.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:3:1)


**וְעִם קָלוֹן.** תָּבוֹא חֶרְפָּה – מִי שֶׁבּוֹחֵר בְּקָלוֹן וְנִאוּף, חֶרְפָּה הִיא לוֹ:


**and with disdain** disgrace comes. He who chooses disdain and adultery—it is a disgrace for him.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:4:1)


**דִּבְרֵי פִי אִישׁ.** כָּל אִישׁ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן גִּבּוֹר גָּדוֹל בִּגְבוּרָה הוּא:


**the wellspring of wisdom** is like a flowing stream and like deep water.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:4:2)


**מְקוֹר חָכְמָה.** כְּנַחַל נוֹבֵעַ וּכְמַיִם עֲמֻקִּים:


**The words of a man’s mouth** Heb. איש. Every instance of איש in Scripture is an expression of a mighty man, one who is great in might.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:5:1)


**שְׂאֵת פְּנֵי רָשָׁע לֹא טוֹב.** כַּאֲשֶׁר פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (יומא פז.): לֹא טוֹב לָהֶם לָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁנּוֹשְׂאִים לָהֶם פָּנִים בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וְנִפְרָעִין מֵהֶן לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**It is not good to be partial to the wicked** As our Sages explained: It is not good for the wicked, that they are favored in this world and are requited in the next world.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:5:2)


**טוֹב וְגוֹ׳.** טוֹב לָהֶם לַצַּדִּיקִים שֶׁמַּכְרִיעִין לָהֶם כַּף מֹאזְנַיִם לְחַיְּבָם בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וְנִפְרָעִין מֵהֶן בְּחַיֵּיהֶן לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא. וּלְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ – כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ:


**to subvert the righteous in judgment** It is good for the righteous that the scale is weighed down for them to make them guilty in this world, where they are requited during their lifetime, and they merit the world to come. Its simple meaning, however, is according to its apparent meaning.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:6:1)


**שִׂפְתֵי כְסִיל יָבֹאוּ בְרִיב.** כָּל דְּבָרָיו בָּאִים בִּלְשׁוֹן תִּגְרָה:


**The lips of a fool will enter a quarrel** All his matters come about with an expression of quarreling.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:6:2)


**וּפִיו לְמַהֲלֻמוֹת יִקְרָא.** קוֹרֵא לְיִסּוּרִין לַהֲבִיאָם עָלָיו:


**and his mouth calls out for blows** He calls pains to come upon himself.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:8:1)


**דִּבְרֵי נִרְגָּן כְּמִתְלַהֲמִים.** מַהֲלוּמוֹת:


**The words of a grumbler are like blows** Heb. כמתלהמים.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:9:1)


**גַּם מִתְרַפֶּה בִמְלַאכְתּוֹ.** פּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַתּוֹרָה וְהוּא תַּלְמִיד חָכָם:


**Even one who is slack in his work** If he is a Torah scholar who separated himself from the Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:9:2)


**לְבַעַל מַשְׁחִית.** לַשָּׂטָן:


**to the destroyer** To Satan.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:10:1)


**מִגְדַּל עֹז שֵׁם ה׳ בּוֹ יָרוּץ צַדִּיק וְנִשְׂגָּב.** וְהוּא מִתְחַזֵּק:


**The name of the Lord is a tower of strength; the righteous runs into it and is strengthened** Heb. ונשגב.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:11:1)


**וּכְחוֹמָה נִשְׂגָּבָה.** עָשְׁרוֹ לוֹ בְּחַדְרֵי מַשְׂכִּיתוֹ. מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַבַּיִת נִרְצָף בְּרִצְפַּת אֲבָנִים הַסּוֹכְכִים עַל הָאָרֶץ, קָרוּי אֶבֶן מַשְׂכִּית:


**and like a strong wall** his wealth is to him in his chambers. Since the house is paved with a floor of stones that cover the earth, it is called אבן משכית, a covering stone.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:14:1)


**רוּחַ אִישׁ.** רוּחַ גֶּבֶר שֶׁהוּא אִישׁ גִּבּוֹר וְאֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן דְּאָגָה בְּלִבּוֹ וּמְקַבֵּל כָּל הַבָּא עָלָיו בְּשִׂמְחָה וּבְחִבָּה:


**A man’s spirit** The spirit of a גבר, who is a mighty man, does not take worry to heart, but accepts with joy and love whatever befalls him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:14:2)


**יְכַלְכֵּל מַחֲלֵהוּ.** אֵין כֹּחוֹ סָר מֵעָלָיו:


**will sustain his illness** He does not lose his strength.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:16:1)


**מַתַּן אָדָם יַרְחִיב.** כִּפְשׁוּטוֹ, וּמִדְרָשׁוֹ (במדבר רבה יג:יז): בְּנוֹתְנֵי צְדָקָה מְדַבֵּר, שֶׁ**מַּרְחִיב לוֹ** חֶלְקוֹ לֶעָתִיד לָבוֹא, וְגַם בְּחַיָּיו **לִפְנֵי גְדוֹלִים יַנְחֶנּוּ**, וְאוֹמְרִים עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּב:


**A man’s gift will make room** [This is to be understood] according to its simple meaning, but, according to its midrashic interpretation, it deals with those who give (sic) charity, which widens his share in the future world, as well as during his lifetime, and it will lead him before the great, who say that he is esteemed.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:18:1)


**וּבֵין עֲצוּמִים יַפְרִיד.** אֵלּוּ בַּעֲלֵי דִינִין, כְּמוֹ ״הַגִּישׁוּ עֲצוּמוֹתֵיהֶם״ (ישעיה מא:כא). וּבַמִּשְׁנָה (סנהדרין לא:): שְׁנַיִם שֶׁנִּתְעַצְּמוּ בַדִּין. עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמְּרִיבוֹתָם עֲצוּמוֹת קְרוּיִים עֲצוּמִים, אוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳חוֹזֶק׳ אוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳אוֹטֶם׳, כְּמוֹ ״עֹצֵם עֵינָיו״ (ישעיה לג:טו):


**and it separates contentious people** Heb. עצומים, these are the litigants, as in (Isa. 41: 21): “‘present your strong points (עצמותיכם).’” As their pleas are strong, they are called עצומים, either an expression of strength or an expression of closing, as in (ibid. 33:15) “and closes (עצם) his eyes.”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:19:1)


**אָח נִפְשָׁע מִקִּרְיַת עֹז.** יֵשׁ אָח נִפְשָׁע מֵאָחִיו וּמְאַבֵּד בְּמִרְדּוֹ קִרְיַת עֹז, כְּגוֹן לוֹט בְּאַבְרָהָם (בראשית יג), עֵשָׂו בְּיַעֲקֹב (בראשית כז:מא-מג):


**A rebellious brother...of a strong city** There is a brother who rebels against his brother and loses a strong city, e.g. Lot against Abraham and Esau against Jacob.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:19:2)


**וּמִדְיָנִים כִּבְרִיחַ אַרְמוֹן.** וּמְרִיבָה שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶם מַפְרִידָתָם לְעוֹלָם כִּבְרִיחַ זֶה שֶׁנּוֹעֲלִים בּוֹ שַׁעֲרֵי אַרְמוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנְסוּ בּוֹ (נזיר כג,א-ב):


**and the quarrels are like the bolt of a castle** The quarrel between them separates them forever, like a bolt with which they lock the gates of a castle, so that no one should enter it.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:21:1)


**וְאֹהֲבֶיהָ יֹאכַל פִּרְיָהּ.** אוֹהֵב אֶת לְשׁוֹנוֹ וּמַרְגִּיל לַתּוֹרָה אוֹכֵל שְׂכָרָהּ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה (תנחומא, מצרע ב):


**and those who love it will eat its produce** He who loves his tongue and accustoms it to [speaking words of] Torah, partakes of its reward in this world.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:22:1)


**מָצָא אִשָּׁה מָצָא טוֹב.** מָצָא תּוֹרָה, וּכְמַשְׁמָעוֹ: אִשָּׁה טוֹבָה:


**He who has found a wife has found good** He who has found the Torah; and according to its apparent meaning, a good wife.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:22:2)


**וַיָּפֶק רָצוֹן.** וְיוֹצִיא; זֶה פְּשׁוּטוֹ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: אָדָם שֶׁ**מָּצָא אִשָּׁה** וּ**מָצָא טוֹב וַיָּפֶק רְצוֹנוֹ**, אוֹתוֹ הָאִישׁ מוֹצִיא רָצוֹן מֵהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. רַבִּי יוֹסֵף קָרָא:


**and has obtained favor** And has obtained. This is its simple meaning. Another explanation: A man who found a wife and found good, has obtained favor. That man obtains favor from the Holy One, blessed be He. [This is in the name of] Rabbi Joseph Kara.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:23:1)


**תַּחֲנוּנִים יְדַבֵּר רָשׁ.** דֶּרֶךְ זֶה בְּכָךְ וְדֶרֶךְ זֶה בְּכָךְ; לִמֶּדְךָ דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ, שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי **שֶׁעָשִׁיר יַעֲנֶה עַזּוֹת**, **הָרָשׁ יְדַבֵּר תַּחֲנוּנִים.** וְכֵן הָעִנְיָן הָרַב לַתַּלְמִיד:


**A poor man speaks with supplications** This one is accustomed to [speak] in this manner, and that one is accustomed to [speak] in that manner. He teaches you a rule of conduct; that although the rich man answers with impudence, the poor man should speak with supplications, and so is the matter of a teacher to a pupil.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 18:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/18:24:1)


**אִישׁ רֵעִים לְהִתְרֹעֵעַ.** אָדָם שֶׁקּוֹנֶה לוֹ רֵעִים עוֹד יָבוֹא יוֹם שֶׁיִּצְטָרֵךְ לָהֶם וִיקָרְבֵהוּ. וְאִם תֹּאמַר: מַה בְּכָךְ – **יֵשׁ אוֹהֵב דָּבֵק מֵאָח**, שֶׁיְּקָרְבֵהוּ יוֹתֵר מִקְּרוֹבָיו וְאֶחָיו:


**A man acquires friends with whom to associate** A man who acquires friends for himself [will find] that the day will arrive when he will need them, and they will befriend him. Now, if you ask, “What of it?” there is a friend who sticks closer than a brother, for he will befriend him more than his kin and his brothers.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:1)


**גַּם בְּלֹא דַעַת נֶפֶשׁ לֹא טוֹב.** אֵין טוֹב לָאָדָם שֶׁהוּא בְּלֹא תּוֹרָה:


**It is also not good that a soul be without knowledge** It is not good for a person to be without Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:2)


**וְאָץ בְּרַגְלַיִם חוֹטֵא.** הַחוֹטֵא רוֹפֵס וְדָשׁ הָעֲווֹנוֹת בַּעֲקֵבָיו, וְאוֹמֵר: קַל הוּא זֶה, אֶעֱבוֹר עָלָיו. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (עירובין ק:): **גַּם בְּלֹא דַעַת**, בְּכוֹפֶה אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ לְתַשְׁמִישׁ הַמִּטָּה בְּעַל כָּרְחָהּ:


**and he who hastens with his feet sins** The sinner treads and tramples the sins with his heels and says, “This is of minor import; I will transgress it.” And our Sages explained: It is also...without knowledge, as referring to one who compels his wife to copulate with him against her will.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:2:3)


**וְאָץ בְּרַגְלַיִם חוֹטֵא.** זֶה הַבּוֹעֵל וְשׁוֹנֶה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: זֶה הַמְהַלֵּךְ עַל גַּבֵּי עֲשָׂבִים בְּשַׁבָּת (שבת צה.):


**and he who hastens with his feet sins** This refers to one who performs the sex act and repeats it. Another explanation: One who walks on grass on the Sabbath.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:3:1)


**אִוֶּלֶת אָדָם תְּסַלֵּף דַּרְכּוֹ.** בַּעֲוֹנוֹ בָּא לוֹ הָרָע, כִּי בְּאִוַּלְתּוֹ תְּסַלֵּף דַּרְכּוֹ וְעָבַר עֲבֵירוֹת וְנִפְרָעִין מִמֶּנּוּ, וּבְבוֹא לוֹ הַצָּרָה זוֹעֵף לִבּוֹ עַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וּמְמַהֵר אַחַר מִדַּת הַדִּין, כְּגוֹן אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף שֶׁאָמְרוּ ״מַה זֹּאת עָשָׂה אֱלֹהִים לָנוּ״ (בראשית מב:כח):


**A man’s folly perverts his way** Harm comes upon him because of his sin, because his folly perverts his way and he commits sins for which he is punished; and when the trouble befalls him, his heart is wroth with the Holy One, blessed be He, and he questions the Divine standard of justice, e.g. Joseph’s brothers, who said (Gen. 42:28): “What is this that God has done to us?”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:4:1)


**הוֹן יוֹסִיף רֵעִים רַבִּים.** אוֹהֲבִים רַבִּים. וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּמִי שֶׁלָּמַד תּוֹרָה:


**Wealth adds many friends** Heb. רעים רבים, many lovers. And it can be interpreted as concerning one who learned Torah.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:6:1)


**רַבִּים יְחַלּוּ פְנֵי נָדִיב וְכָל הָרֵעַ.** הַכֹּל נַעֲשִׂים רֵעִים **לְאִישׁ מַתָּן.** יֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּנוֹתְנֵי צְדָקָה וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּמַרְבִּיצֵי תוֹרָה:


**The great will beg the favor of a generous man, and everyone is a friend** All become friends to a man who gives gifts. This may be interpreted as referring to those who give charity, and it may also be interpreted as referring to those who disseminate the Torah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:1)


**כָּל אֲחֵי רָשׁ.** קְרוֹבָיו:


**All the kinsmen of a poor man** lit. the brothers, the kinsmen.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:2)


**אַף כִּי מֵרֵעֵהוּ רָחֲקוּ.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן מֵרֵעָיו וְשׁוֹשְׁבִינָיו וְאוֹהֲבָיו:


**surely his friends distance themselves** Surely his friends, his companions, and his lovers.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:3)


**מְרַדֵּף אֲמָרִים.** לֹא הֵמָּה אוֹמֵר: פְּלוֹנִי וּפְלוֹנִי קְרוֹבַי, וּפְלוֹנִי וּפְלוֹנִי מֵרֵעַי, וְהַכֹּל דִּבְרֵי הֶבֶל. וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּמִי שֶׁהוּא רָשׁ בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְמַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים:


**he pursues statements that are fit for him** He says, “So-and-so and so-and-so are my relatives. So-and-so and so-and-so are my friends.” And all are words of futility. This can be interpreted as concerning one who is poor in Torah and in good deeds.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:7:4)


**מְרַדֵּף אֲמָרִים לֹא הֵמָּה.** מְחַזֵּר לְהוֹרוֹת הֲלָכָה וְלָאו בְּיָדוֹ, וְהַמִּדְרָשׁ פּוֹתְרוֹ בְּיוֹסֵף שֶׁהָיָה מוֹצִיא עַל אֶחָיו דִּבָּה, וְאָמַר לְאָבִיו שֶׁהֵן חֲשׁוּדִין עַל אֵבֶר מִן הַחַי, לְכָךְ שְׂנֵאוּהוּ וְנִקְרָא רָשׁ לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מוֹצִיא דִּבַּת שֶׁקֶר עֲלֵיהֶם. וּמְחַזֵּר אַחַר **אֲמָרִים לֹא הֵמָּה.** לוֹ קְרִינָן **וּמְרַדֵּף אֲמָרִים** לַהֲנָאָתוֹ, וּלְעִנְיַן יוֹסֵף לוֹ הֵמָּה, כִּי עָלָיו חָזְרוּ דִּבָּתוֹ הוּא הָיָה אוֹמֵר שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹכְלִים אֵבֶר מִן הַחַי וְהַכָּתוּב מֵעִיד עֲלֵיהֶם אֲפִלּוּ בִּשְׁעַת הַקַּלְקָלָה הָיוּ שׁוֹחֲטִין שְׂעִיר עִזִּים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּשְׁחֲטוּ שְׂעִיר עִזִּים״ (בראשית לז:לא). הוּא אָמַר שֶׁהָיוּ מְזַלְזְלִין בִּבְנֵי הַשְּׁפָחוֹת לְכָךְ ״לְעֶבֶד נִמְכַּר יוֹסֵף״ (תהלים קה:יז), הוּא אָמַר שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹתְנִים עֵינֵיהֶם בִּבְנוֹת הָאָרֶץ אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא חַיֶּיךָ שֶׁאֲנִי מְגָרֶה בְּךָ אֶת הַדֹּב לְכָךְ ״וַתִּשָּׂא אֵשֶׁת אֲדֹנָיו וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית לט:ז) (בראשית רבה פד:ז):


**he pursues statements which are futile** He seeks to promulgate the halachah, but it is not in his possession. The midrash interprets it as concerning Joseph, who spread slander about his brothers. He would say to his father that they were suspected of [eating] limbs from a living animal. Therefore, they hated him, and he was called a poor man because he would spread false rumors about them and look for statements that were not true (לא המה). We read לו, “to him,” for he would pursue statements for his own benefit, and in Joseph’s matter, they were his, because his slander returned upon him. He would say that they ate limbs from a living animal, but Scripture testifies about them that even in the time of sin, they would slaughter a kid, as it is said: (Gen. 37:31) “and they slaughtered a kid.” He would say that they degraded the sons of the maidservants. Therefore, Joseph was sold as a slave. He would say that they gazed upon the women of the land. Said the Holy One, blessed be He, “By your life, I will incite a bear upon you.” Therefore, (ibid. 39:7) “his master’s wife lifted, etc.” [This appears] in Genesis Rabbah [see 84:7].



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:10:1)


**לֹא נָאוֶה לִכְסִיל תַּעֲנוּג אַף כִּי לְעֶבֶד.** קַל וָחֹמֶר הוּא, שֶׁאֵין נָאֶה שֶׁעֶבֶד יִמְשֹׁל בִּנְדִיבִים:


**Pleasure does not befit a fool, much less a slave** It is derived by an a fortiori conclusion that it does not befit a slave to rule over princes.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:11:1)


**שֵׂכֶל אָדָם הֶאֱרִיךְ אַפּוֹ.** כְּמוֹ לְהַאֲרִיךְ, אִם עָשָׂה כֵּן שֵׂכֶל הוּא לוֹ:


**It is good sense for a man to be slow to anger** Heb. האריך, like להאריך, to be slow. If he did so, it is good sense for him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:11:2)


**וְתִפְאַרְתּוֹ.** הוּא שֶׁיַּעֲבֹר עַל מִדּוֹתָיו:


**and it is his glory** that he pass over his retaliations.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:12:1)


**נַהַם כַּכְּפִיר זַעַף מֶלֶךְ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**The King’s fury is like a lion’s roar** [The fury of] the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:13:1)


**וְדֶלֶף טוֹרֵד.** דֶּלֶף גְּשָׁמִים הַיּוֹרֵד בַּבַּיִת וּמַטְרִיחַ וְטוֹרֵד לְיוֹשְׁבֵי הַבַּיִת, כָּךְ **מִדְיְנֵי אִשָּׁה** רָעָה:


**a constant dripping** lit. a driving drip. [As] the dripping of the rain that falls in the house disturbs and drives out the inhabitants of the house, so are the quarrels of a bad wife.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:15:1)


**עַצְלָה תַּפִּיל תַּרְדֵּמָה.** הָעַצְלוּת מְבִיאָה לִידֵי שֵׁינָה, זוֹ הִיא הַמְּלִיצָה. אֲבָל הַמָּשָׁל – עַל הַמִּתְעַצֵּל בְּתַלְמוּדוֹ; סוֹף שֶׁשּׁוֹאֲלִין דְּבַר חָכְמָה מִמֶּנּוּ וְהוּא נִרְדָּם:


**Laziness causes one to fall into a deep sleep** Laziness causes one to fall asleep. This is the figure, but the allegory is that it concerns one who neglects his studies; it will ultimately come about that people will ask him words of wisdom, and he will be sound asleep.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:15:2)


**וְנֶפֶשׁ רְמִיָּה תִרְעָב.** סוֹף שֶׁיְּהֵא עָנִי; וְכֵן הָרַמַּאי בְּתַלְמוּדוֹ, לַעֲשׂוֹת תּוֹרָתוֹ חֲבִילוֹת, סוֹף שֶׁהוּא יִשְׁכַּח:


**and a deceitful soul shall suffer hunger** He will ultimately become poor. Similarly, one who is deceitful in his studies, making his Torah into “bundles,” will ultimately forget.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:16:1)


**שׁוֹמֵר מִצְוָה שׁוֹמֵר נַפְשׁוֹ בּוֹזֵה דְרָכָיו יָמוּת.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן לִבּוֹ לְשָׁקְלָן:


**He who keeps a commandment keeps his soul; he who despises his ways will die** He does not take heed to weigh them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:17:1)


**מַלְוֵה ה׳ חוֹנֵן דָּל וּגְמוּלוֹ יְשַׁלֶּם לוֹ.** כְּשֶׁיֶּחֱלֶה וְיִטֶּה לָמוּת, צִדְקָתוֹ מְלִיצָה עָלָיו לִפְנֵי מִדַּת הַדִּין, לוֹמַר: הֶעָנִי הָיְתָה נַפְשׁוֹ מְפַרְכֶּסֶת לָצֵאת בָּרָעָב וְזֶה פִּרְנְסָהּ וְהֶחֱזִירָהּ לְגוּפָהּ, אַף אֲנִי אַחֲזִיר לוֹ נַפְשׁוֹ:


**He who is gracious to a poor man lends to the Lord, and He will repay him his reward** When he becomes ill and is close to death, his charity defends him before the Divine standard of justice, saying, “The poor man’s soul was struggling to leave him because of hunger, and this one sustained it. I, too, will return his soul to him.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:18:1)


**יַסֵּר בִּנְךָ כִּי יֵשׁ תִּקְוָה וְאֶל הֲמִיתוֹ אַל תִּשָּׂא נַפְשֶׁךָ.** אַל תַּכֵּהוּ מַכַּת מָוֶת:


**Chastise your son for there is hope, but do not set your heart on his destruction** Do not deal him a lethal blow.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:19:1)


**גְּדָָל חֵמָה נֹשֵׂא עֹנֶשׁ וְגוֹ׳.** אִם תַּעֲבִיר עַל חֲמָתְךָ וְתַצִּיל אֶת שׂוֹנַאֲךָ אִם רָאִיתָ רָעָה בָּאָה לוֹ, **עוֹד תּוֹסִיף** יָמִים וְטוֹבָה:


**A hot-tempered man incurs punishment, etc.** But if you pass over your anger and save your enemy if you see harm coming to him, you will yet increase your days and have much goodness.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:22:1)


**תַּאֲוַת אָדָם חַסְדּוֹ.** עִקָּר שֶׁהַבְּרִיּוֹת מִתְאַוִּים לָאָדָם בַּעֲבוּר חַסְדּוֹ:


**The attraction to a man is his kindness** The main reason why people are attracted to a person is because of his kindness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:22:2)


**וְטוֹב רָשׁ מֵאִישׁ כָּזָב.** וְאִם אִישׁ כָּזָב הוּא וּמַבְטִיחַ וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה, טוֹב אִישׁ רָשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ:


**and a poor man is better than a liar** And if he is a liar, who promises but does not perform, a poor man is better than he.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:23:1)


**וְשָׂבֵעַ יָלִין.** הַיָּרֵא אֶת הַשֵּׁם, שֶׁלֹּא **יִפָּקֵד** בְּשׁוּם רָעָה:


**and he will rest satisfied** He who fears the Lord will rest, satisfied that he will not be visited by any evil.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:24:1)


**טָמַן עָצֵל יָדוֹ בַּצַּלָּחַת.** בְּיוֹרָה חַמָּה, שֶׁכְּשֶׁיַּעַבְרוּהָ מִן הָאוּר וּפִנּוּהָ רֵיקָנִית, מְחַמֵּם יָדוֹ בְּתוֹכָהּ. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״בִּשְּׁלוּ בַּסִּירוֹת וּבַדְּוָדִים וּבַצֵּלָחוֹת״ (דברי הימים ב לה:יג). וּמִשּׁוּם רַבֵּינוּ יִצְחָק הַלֵּוִי שָׁמַעְתִּי: **בַּצַּלָּחַת.** מִלְּשׁוֹן תַּרְגּוּם ״וַיְבַקַּע״ (בראשית כב:ג): ״וְצָלַח״, בִּשְׁעַת הַצִּנָּה וְהַגְּלִיד, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַקּוֹר וְהַגְּלִיד מְבַקְּעִין וְצוֹלְחִים הַיָּדַיִם. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי: **בַּצַּלָּחַת.** קֶרַע הֶחָלוּק, שֶׁקּוֹרִין פאנדאדור״י בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּלוֹמַר: טוֹמְנָהּ בְּחֵיקוֹ (סא״א):


**A lazy man hid his hand in a kettle** Heb. בצלחת, in a hot kettle, in which, when removed from the fire and emptied, one may warm his hand. Similar to it in Chronicles (II 35:13): “they cooked in pots, in cauldrons, and in kettles (בצלחות),” In the name of Rabbi Isaac HaLevi I heard that בצלחת is derived from the expression of the Aramaic translation of ויבקע “and he split,” which is וצלח. At the time of the cold and the frost, since the cold and the frost crack and split the hands. [Hence, we render: A lazy man hid his hand in winter.] But I heard that is a rend of a cloak called fenditura [in Italian, in German Riss, Spalt, Schlitz], i.e., he hides it in his bosom.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:25:1)


**לֵץ תַּכֶּה וּפֶתִי יַעְרִם.** עַל מַכּוֹת פַּרְעֹה וּמִלְחֶמֶת עֲמָלֵק הֶחְכִּים יִתְרוֹ וְנִתְגַּיֵּר (תנחומא, יתרו ג):


**Beat a scorner, and a simple man will gain cunning** Through the plagues visited upon Pharaoh and the war with Amalek, Jethro gained cunning and converted.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:26:1)


**מְשַׁדֵּד אָב יַבְרִיחַ אֵם.** מַבְרִיחַ אֵם:


**A son who causes shame and disgrace robs his father, puts his mother to flight** Heb. יבריח, puts his mother to flight.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:26:2)


**בֵּן מֵבִישׁ וּמַחְפִּיר.** דְּרָכָיו. עַל יְדֵי שֶׁרָאֲתָה שָׂרָה אֶת בֶּן הָגָר הַמִּצְרִית מְשַׂחֵק לַעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים וְגִלּוּי עֲרָיוֹת, גָּרַם זֶה שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּלְּחָה אִמּוֹ, ״וַיֵּרַע הַדָּבָר מְאֹד בְּעֵינֵי אַבְרָהָם״ (בראשית כא:יא):


**A son who causes shame and disgrace** Whose ways are disgraceful. Since Sarah saw the son of the Egyptian maidservant making sport with idolatry and adultery, he caused his mother to be sent away, “and the matter displeased Abraham” (Gen. 21:11).



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:27:1)


**חֲדַל בְּנִי לִשְׁמֹעַ וְגוֹ׳.** מִקְרָא קָצָר הוּא וְזֶהוּ פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: **חֲדַל בְּנִי לִשְׁגּוֹת מֵאִמְרֵי דַעַת** בִּשְׁבִיל **לִשְׁמֹעַ מוּסָר**:


**My son, cease to stray, etc.** This is an elliptical verse, and this is its explanation: My son, cease to stray from words of knowledge in order to hear discipline.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:27:2)


**חֲדַל לִשְׁגּוֹת** כְּמוֹ מִלִּשְׁגּוֹת, וְכֵן ״וְחָדַל לַעֲשׂוֹת הַפֶּסַח״ (במדבר ט:יג), וְכֵן ״כִּי חָדַל לִסְפֹּר״ (בראשית מא:מט):


**cease to stray** Heb. לשגות, like מלשגות, from straying. Likewise, (Num. 9:13): “and refrains (וחדל) from performing (לעשות) the Passover service.” Also, (Gen. 41:49): “they stopped counting (לספר).”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:28:1)


**עֵד בְּלִיַּעַל יָלִיץ מִשְׁפָּט.** יָעִיד לְאָדָם מְלִיצַת מִשְׁפְּטֵי יִסּוּרִין וּמִיתָה:


**A godless witness testifies judgment** He testifies against a man [with] the rhetoric of the judgments of tortures and death.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:28:2)


**וּפִי רְשָׁעִים יְבַלַּע אָוֶן.** הָאָוֶן יִבְלָעֵהוּ:


**but violence will devour the mouth of the wicked** The violence will swallow him up.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 19:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/19:29:1)


**נָכוֹנוּ לַלֵּצִים.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא זִמֵּן לוֹ שְׁפָטִים שֶׁל צָרַעַת לָזֶה הַלֵּץ הַמְסַפֵּר בְּלָשׁוֹן הָרָע (במדבר רבה יג:ד):


**are prepared for the scorners** The Holy One, blessed be He, prepared punishments of zaraath for this scorner who slanders.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:2:1)


**נַהַם כַּכְּפִיר אֵימַת מֶלֶךְ מִתְעַבְּרוֹ.** מְכַעֲסוֹ:


**Fear of a king is like a lion’s roar; he who provokes him** Who angers him.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:3:1)


**כָּבוֹד לָאִישׁ שֶׁבֶת מֵרִיב.** לָנוּחַ מִמְּרִיבָה:


**It is honor for a man to refrain from quarreling** To rest from quarrel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:3:2)


**וְכָל אֱוִיל.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁבַת מֵרִיב, תִּגָּלֶה קְלוֹנוֹ:


**and every fool** who does not refrain from quarreling—his disgrace will be exposed.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:4:1)


**מֵחֹרֶף עָצֵל לֹא יַחֲרֹשׁ.** מִפְּנֵי הַצִּנָּה יוֹשֵׁב הֶעָצֵל וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה מְלָאכָה וְאֵינוֹ עוֹסֵק בַּתּוֹרָה:


**Because of the winter, a lazy man does not plow** Because of the cold, a lazy man sits and does no work, neither does he engage in Torah.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:5:1)


**מַיִם עֲמֻקִּים עֵצָה בְלֶב אִישׁ.** הֲלָכָה בְּלֵב חָכָם סְתוּמָה:


**Counsel in man’s heart is like deep water** A halachah in a wise man’s heart is sealed.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:5:2)


**וְאִישׁ תְּבוּנָה יִדְלֶנָּה.** וְתַלְמִיד נָבוֹן בָּא וְדָלָה אוֹתָהּ מִמֵּעָיו:


**but a man of understanding will draw it out** An understanding pupil comes and draws it out of his innards.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:1)


**רָב אָדָם יִקְרָא אִישׁ חַסְדּוֹ.** הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בְּנֵי אָדָם הַבּוֹטְחִים אֶל אוֹהֲבֵיהֶם הַמַּבְטִיחִים אוֹתָם חֶסֶד וְקוֹרְאִים לָהֶם בְּעֵת דָּחְקָם:


**Many people call upon the man who promises them kindness** There are many people who rely on their friends who promise them kindness, and they call them at the time of their straits.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:2)


**אִישׁ חַסְדּוֹ.** הַמַּבְטִיחַ לוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת חֶסֶד:


**but who can find a trustworthy man?** who promises and keeps his promise.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:6:3)


**וְאִישׁ אֱמוּנִים מִי יִמְצָא.** הַמַּבְטִיחַ וְעוֹשֶׂה:


**who promises them kindness** Heb. איש חסדו, lit. a man of his kindness, who promises him to do kindness.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:8:1)


**מֶלֶךְ יוֹשֵׁב עַל כִּסֵּא דִין.** כְּפִשׁוּטוֹ, אוֹ יֵשׁ לְפָרְשׁוֹ כְּנֶגֶד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וְיֵשׁ לְפָרְשׁוֹ כְּנֶגֶד שׁוֹפְטֵי אֱמֶת:


**A king sits upon a throne of judgment** This may be explained as referring to the Holy One, blessed be He, or it may be explained as referring to the true judges.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:12:1)


**אֹ֣זֶן שֹׁמַ֤עַת וְעַ֣יִן רֹאָ֭ה וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: מַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו הֵם, וְהוּא חָפֵץ בְּאֹזֶן שׁוֹמַעַת מוּסָר וְעַיִן הָרוֹאָה אֶת הַנּוֹלָד:


**The hearing ear and the seeing eye, etc.** They are His handiwork, and He desires an ear that hears reproof and an eye that sees what will develop.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:13:1)


**אַל תֶּאֱהַב שֵׁנָה פֶּן תִּוָּרֵשׁ.** כִּי תֵעָשֶׂה רָשׁ:


**lest you become poor** Heb. תורש you become poor (רש).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:14:1)


**רַע רַע יֹאמַר הַקּוֹנֶה.** הַקּוֹנֶה תּוֹרָה עַל יְדֵי הַדְּחָק וְיִסּוּרֵי רְעָבוֹן, אוֹמֵר: אוֹי לִי עַל רָעָה זֹאת וְהַצָּרָה הַזֹּאת. וּכְשֶׁהוֹלֵךְ לוֹ מָלֵא חָכְמָה **אָז יִתְהַלָּל** עַל הַצַּעַר שֶׁנִּצְטַעֵר:


**“It is bad, it is bad,” says the buyer** If one acquires Torah through poverty and the pains of hunger, he says, “Woe is to me for this evil and for this trouble,” but when he goes away full of wisdom... **then he boasts** about the pain he suffered.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:14:2)


**וְאֹזֵל לוֹ.** וְהוֹלֵךְ לוֹ:


**but when he goes away** Heb. ואזל, but when he goes away.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:17:1)


**עָרֵב לָאִישׁ לֶחֶם שָׁקֶר.** נִיאוּף אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ:


**Bread of falsehood is sweet to a man** Adultery with a married woman.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:17:2)


**וְאַחַר יִמָּלֵא פִיהוּ חָצָץ.** אֲבָנִים דַּקּוֹת, וְכֵן ״וַיַּגְרֵס בֶּחָצָץ״ (איכה ג:טז):


**but afterwards his mouth will be filled with gravel** Heb. חצץ, fine pebbles. Similarly, (Lam. 3:16): “And He has made my teeth grind on gravel (בחצץ).”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:18:1)


**מַחֲשָׁבוֹת בְּעֵצָה תִכּוֹן וּבְתַחְבֻּלוֹת עֲשֵׂה מִלְחָמָה.** אִם בָּאתָ לְהִלָּחֵם כְּנֶגֶד הַשָּׂטָן, בֹּא בְּתַחְבּוּלוֹת תְּשׁוּבָה וּתְפִלָּה וְתַעֲנִית:


**Plans with counsel will be established, and with strategies wage war** If you come to wage war against Satan, come with strategies of repentance, prayer and fasting.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:19:1)


**גּוֹלֶה סוֹד הוֹלֵךְ רָכִיל וּלְפֹתֶה שְׂפָתָיו.** הַמְדַבֵּר חֲלָקוֹת לְפַתּוֹתְךָ וּלְהַסִּיתְךָ:


**with one who divulges secrets, one who gossips, or with one who entices with his lips** who speaks smoothly to seduce you and entice you.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:20:1)


**מְקַלֵּל אָבִיו וְאִמּוֹ יִדְעַךְ נֵרוֹ בֶּאֱשׁוּן חֹשֶׁךְ.** בְּהַנְשִׁיף וּבְהַשְׁחִיר הַחֹשֶׁךְ, כְּלוֹמַר: בְּבֹא הָרָעָה:
