**If one curses...his lamp will flicker in the blackest darkness** When the darkness becomes blacker and blacker—when evil befalls him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:21:1)


**נַחֲלָה מְבֹהֶלֶת בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** שֶׁנִּבְהַל לְמַהֵר וְלִטּוֹל תְּחִלָּה, כְּגוֹן בְּנֵי גָד וּבְנֵי רְאוּבֵן שֶׁמִּהֲרוּ לִטּוֹל חֶלְקָם בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן וְדִבְּרוּ בְּבֶהָלָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״גִּדְרֹת צֹאן נִבְנֶה לְמִקְנֵנוּ פֹּה וְעָרִים לְטַפֵּנוּ״ (במדבר לב:טז) – עָשׂוּ אֶת הָעִקָּר טָפֵל, שֶׁהִקְדִּימוּ צֹאנָם לְטַפָּם (תנחומא, מטות ז):


**An inheritance may be acquired hastily in the beginning** Which one hastened to take hurriedly, e.g. the sons of Gad and the sons of Reuben, who hastened to take their share on the other side of the Jordan, and they spoke hastily, as it is said (Num. 32:16): “We want to build sheepfolds for our cattle here and cities for our children.” They made the main thing of secondary import, for they placed their flocks before their children.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:21:2)


**וְאַחֲרִיתָהּ לֹא תְבֹרָךְ.** שֶׁגָּלוּ כַּמָּה שָׁנִים קֹדֶם שְׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כב) וּבְמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, מסעי י); ״בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְאָחָז וַיָּעַר ה׳ אֶת רוּחַ מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים א ה:כו), וּשְׁאָר שְׁבָטִים גָּלוּ בִּשְׁנַת שֵׁשׁ לְחִזְקִיָּהוּ הִיא הַתְּשִׁיעִית לְהוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה (מלכים ב יז:א-ו):


**but its end will not be blessed** For they were exiled many years before the rest of the tribes, as is explained in Seder Olam (ch. 22), and in the Midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (see Mattoth 7): “In the second year of Ahaz, the Lord aroused the desire of the king of Assyria, etc. (I Chron. 5:26),” but the rest of the tribes were exiled in the sixth year of Hezekiah, which is the ninth year of Hoshea the son of Elah.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:25:1)


**מוֹקֵשׁ אָדָם יָלַע קֹדֶשׁ.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם נִכְשָׁל וְנוֹקֵשׁ בַּעֲבֵירוֹת, מְקַלְקֵל אֶת קְדֻשָּׁתוֹ; כְּמוֹ ״וְשָׁתוּ וְלָעוּ״ (עובדיה א:טז):


**When a man is trapped, he impairs his sanctity** Heb. ילע. When a man stumbles and is trapped in sins, he impairs his sanctity, as in (Obadiah verse 16) “and they shall drink and be stunned (ולעו).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:25:2)


**וְאַחַר נְדָרִים לְבַקֵּר.** צָרִיךְ אָדָם לַחֲזֹר אַחַר קָרְבָּנוֹת, לִנְדֹּר וּלְהָבִיא וּלְבַקֵּשׁ עַל נַפְשׁוֹ:


**and he must seek vows** Man must seek sacrifices to vow and to bring and to beg for his life.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:1)


**מְזָרֶה רְשָׁעִים.** פַּרְעֹה וְחֵילוֹ (שמות יד:כח):


**scatters the wicked** Pharaoh and his host.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:2)


**מֶלֶךְ חָכָם.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**The Wise King** The Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:26:3)


**וַיָּשֶׁב עֲלֵיהֶם אוֹפָן.** גַּלְגַּל מִדָּתָם הֵשִׁיב עֲלֵיהֶם, ״וַיְנַהֲגֵהוּ בִּכְבֵדֻת״ (שמות יד:כה) כְּנֶגֶד ״וְהַכְבֵּד אֶת לִבּוֹ״ (שמות ח:יא):


**and turns the wheel over them** The wheel of their traits He turned over them. “And drove it heavily (Ex. 14:25),” corresponding to (ibid. 8: 11) “and he made his heart heavy.”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:27:1)


**נֵר ה׳ נִשְׁמַת אָדָם.** הַנְּשָׁמָה שֶׁבְּקִרְבּוֹ מְעִידָה עָלָיו בַּדִּין (פסיקתא רבתי ח):


**Man’s soul is the Lord’s lamp** The soul in his midst testifies concerning him in judgment.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:29:1)


**תִּפְאֶרֶת בַּחוּרִים כֹּחָם.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁתִּפְאֶרֶת בַּחוּרִים כֹּחָם, כֵּן הַדְרַת **זְקֵנִים שֵׂיבָה**:


**The glory of young men is their strength** Just as the glory of young men is their strength, so is the beauty of elders their hoary head.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:30:1)


**חַבֻּרוֹת פֶּצַע תַּמְרוּק בְּרָע.** דָּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (ילקוט שמעוני משלי תתקנט): הַמְמָרֵק עַצְמוֹ לִדְבַר עֲבֵירָה, סוֹף בָּא לִידֵי פְּצָעִים וְחַבּוּרוֹת:


**Contusions and wounds come for outpouring in evil** Our Sages expounded: Whoever “pours himself” out into sinful things, will ultimately come to wounds and contusions.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 20:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/20:30:2)


**וּמַכּוֹת חַדְרֵי בָטֶן.** זֶה הַהִדְרוֹקָן (בבא מציעא קז:):


**and plagues in the innermost parts** This is dropsy.



## Chapter 21


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:2:1)


**כָּל דֶּרֶךְ אִישׁ יָשָׁר בְּעֵינָיו וְתוֹכֵן.** וּמוֹנֶה:


**Man’s every way is straight in his own eyes, but the Lord counts** Heb. ותכן.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:4:1)


**רוּם עֵינַיִם.** גַּסּוּת הָרוּחַ:


**Haughty eyes** A haughty spirit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:4:2)


**וּרְחַב לֵב.** חָפֵץ לְמַלֹּאות תַּאֲוַת לִבּוֹ, הוּא נִיר שֶׁל רְשָׁעִים – חֲרִישׁוּתָם וּמַחְשְׁבוֹתָם הוּא חַטָּאתָם:


**and unlimited lust** The desire to satisfy the lusts of his heart; that is the tillage of the wicked. Their “plowing” and their thoughts is their sin.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:1)


**מַחְשְׁבוֹת חָרוּץ.** אָדָם יָשָׁר הַמִּתְהַלֵּךְ בֶּאֱמֶת וּבְמִשְׁפָּט חָרוּץ:


**The plans of a diligent man** Heb. חרוץ, an honest man who walks in truth and according to the established judgment.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:2)


**אַךְ לְמוֹתָר.** לְהַצְלָחָה וּלְרֶוַח בָּאוֹת:


**lead only to advantage** They come to prosperity and profit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:5:3)


**וְכָל אָץ.** דּוֹחֵק אֶת הַשָּׁעָה:


**whereas every hasty one** One who forces the time.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:6:1)


**פֹּעַל אוֹצָרוֹת.** לְהַרְבּוֹת הוֹן. **בִּלְשׁוֹן שָׁקֶר**:


**Gathering treasures** to increase wealth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:6:2)


**הֶבֶל נִדָּף וְגוֹ׳.** מוֹקְשֵׁי (ס״א: מְבַקְּשֵׁי) מָוֶת הֵם לוֹ:


**with a lying tongue is like a driven vapor, etc.** They are snares of death (seekers of death) for him.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:7:1)


**יְגוֹרֵם.** יָגוּר אוֹתָם:


**The plunder of the wicked frightens them** Heb. יגורם, [like] יגור אותם.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:8:1)


**הֲפַכְפַּךְ דֶּרֶךְ אִישׁ וָזָר.** אִישׁ וְהוּא זָר, סָר מִן הַמִּצְוָה, דַּרְכּוֹ הֲפַכְפַּךְ:


**A man’s way is changeable and he is strange** A man who is strange, turning away from the commandment—his way is changeable.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:9:1)


**וּבֵית חָבֶר.** שֶׁנִּכְנָס לְבֵית חֲבֵירוֹ לְדַבֵּר עִם אִשְׁתּוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (אבות דרבי נתן לד,ו; סנהדרין קג:): נִיבָּא שֶׁסּוֹף הַשְּׁכִינָה לְהִסְתַּלֵּק מֵעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהֵם כְּאֵשֶׁת מִדְיָנִים:


**and the house of a friend** That he enters his friend’s house to converse with his wife. And the Midrash Aggadah states: He prophesied that the Shechinah would ultimately leave Israel, who is like a quarrelsome wife.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:9:2)


**וּבֵית חָבֶר.** בַּיִת שֶׁמְּחַבְּרִים בּוֹ רֵעִים לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּגוֹן צֶלֶם שֶׁהֶעֱמִיד מְנַשֶּׁה בְּהֵיכַל ה׳ (מלכים ב כא:ז):


**and the house of a friend** A house in which they associate friends with the Holy One, blessed be He; for example, the image that Manasseh erected in the Temple of the Lord.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:11:1)


**בַּעֲנֹשׁ לֵץ יֶחְכַּם פֶּתִי.** עַל יְדֵי יִסּוּרֵי הַלֵּצִים הַפְּתָאִים מִתְחַכְּמִים וְשָׁבִים בִּתְשׁוּבָה:


**When a scorner is punished, a fool gains wisdom** Through the chastisements of scorners, fools gain wisdom and return [to God] in repentance.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:12:1)


**מַשְׂכִּיל צַדִּיק.** צַדִּיקוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, הוּא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, נוֹתֵן לֵב לְהַכְרִית בֵּית רָשָׁע, כְּגוֹן זֵכֶר עֲמָלֵק (שמות יז:יד):


**The Righteous One considers** The Righteous One of the world sets His heart to destroy the house of the wicked, e.g. the remembrance of Amalek.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:14:1)


**מַתָּן בַּסֵּתֶר.** צְדָקָה:


**A gift in secret** Charity.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:14:2)


**וְשֹׁחַד בַּחֵק.** אַף זוֹ צְדָקָה. **יִכְפֶּה אַף**, יִכְפֶּה **חֵמָה עַזָּה**:


**and a bribe in the pocket** This too refers to charity. It will appease even strong wrath.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:15:1)


**שִׂמְחָה לַצַּדִּיק עֲשׂוֹת מִשְׁפָּט.** שִׂמְחָה לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַעֲשׂוֹת דִּין בַּצַּדִּיקִים כְּדֵי לְזַכּוֹתָם לְחַיֵּי הָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, וְשִׂמְחָה לַצַּדִּיק שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵבִיא יִסּוּרִין עֲלֵיהֶם כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּזְכּוּ לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא. כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּמִדְרַשׁ תְּהִלִּים:


**It is a joy for a righteous man to do judgment** It is a joy for the Holy One, blessed be He, to inflict judgment upon the righteous in order to bestow upon them the life of the world to come, for the Holy One, blessed be He, brings pains upon them in order that they merit the world to come. In this manner, it is expounded in Midrash Psalms (3:1).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:15:2)


**וּמְחִתָּה.** הִיא **לְפֹעֲלֵי אָוֶן**, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין נוֹתְנִין לֵב לָשׁוּב וְאֵין הַיִּסּוּרִין מוֹעִילִין לָהֶן, וּמִצְטַעֲרִין וְלֹא לְהוֹעִיל:


**and a ruin** It is [a ruin] **for those who commit violence** Because they do not put their hearts to repent and the pains do not avail them, but they suffer to no avail.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:16:1)


**אָדָם תּוֹעֶה מִדֶּרֶךְ הַשְׂכֵּל.** פּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


**A man who strays from the way of understanding** From the Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:16:2)


**בִּקְהַל רְפָאִים.** בַּעֲדַת גֵּיהִנֹּם (בבא בתרא עט.):


**in the congregation of the shades** in the congregation of Gehinnom.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:17:1)


**אִישׁ מַחְסוֹר.** מִי שֶׁאוֹהֵב שִׂמְחַת מִשְׁתֶּה תָּמִיד:


**shall be a poor man** He who loves the joy of always banqueting.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:18:1)


**כֹּפֶר לַצַּדִּיק רָשָׁע וְתַחַת יְשָׁרִים בּוֹגֵד.** צַדִּיק נֶחְלָץ וְרָשָׁע בָּא תַּחְתָּיו (ירושלמי ברכות ט:א), כְּגוֹן מָרְדְּכַי וְהָמָן (אסתר ז:ט-י):


**A wicked man shall be ransom for a righteous man, and a treacherous one instead of the upright** A righteous man is rescued, and a wicked man comes in his stead (above 11:8), such as Mordecai and Haman.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:19:1)


**טוֹב שֶׁבֶת בְּאֶרֶץ מִדְבָּר.** גַּם זֶה עַל סִלּוּק הַשְּׁכִינָה נֶאֱמַר:


**It is better to dwell in a desert land** This too was stated regarding the withdrawal of the Shechinah.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:20:1)


**וּכְסִיל אָדָם.** וּכְסִילוּת שֶׁל אָדָם:


**but man’s foolishness** Heb. וכסיל אדם foolishness of a man.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:22:1)


**עִיר גִּבּוֹרִים עָלָה חָכָם.** זֶה מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ שֶׁעָלָה לְבֵין הַמַּלְאָכִים גִּבּוֹרֵי כֹחַ (תהלים קג:כ). **וַיּוֹרֶד וְגוֹ׳.** תּוֹרָה:


**A wise man ascended to a city of mighty men** This refers to Moses, our teacher, who went up among the angels, who are mighty in strength. (**and brought down, etc.** The Torah.)



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:24:1)


**יָהִיר.** גַּס רוּחַ, סוֹפוֹ לִהְיוֹת **לֵץ** שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁ לִשְׁמוֹעַ מוּסָר:


**haughty** Heb. יהיר, one who is haughty; he will ultimately become a scorner, for he does not care to hear reproof.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:26:1)


**וְצַדִּיק יִתֵּן.** כָּל צָרְכֵי בֵיתוֹ שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מְזַמֵּן לוֹ:


**but the righteous gives** all the necessities of his house, for the Holy One, blessed be He, prepares them for him.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:27:1)


**אַף כִּי בְזִמָּה יְבִיאֶנּוּ.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן כְּשֶׁהוּא מְבִיאוֹ לְהַרְשִׁיעוֹ, וּבְעֵצָה רָעָה, כְּגוֹן קָרְבְּנוֹת בִּלְעָם וּבָלָק, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מְבִיאִים אֶלָּא לְקַלֵּל אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל (במדבר כג):


**how much more if he brings it with plans of wickedness!** How much more if he brings it to be wicked and with evil counsel, such as the sacrifices of Balaam and Balak, who brought them only to curse Israel.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:28:1)


**וְאִישׁ שׁוֹמֵעַ.** שֶׁקִּבֵּל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה ״לֹא תַעֲנֶה בְרֵעֲךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות כ:יג):


**but an obedient man** who obeys what is written in the Torah: “You shall not bear false witness against your neighbor.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:28:2)


**לָנֶצַח.** תָּמִיד:


**forever** Always.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:29:1)


**הֵעֵז אִישׁ רָשָׁע בְּפָנָיו.** הָרָשָׁע מַרְאֶה עַזּוּתוֹ בִּשְׁעַת כַּעֲסוֹ:


**A wicked man shows his brazenness in his anger** A wicked man shows his brazenness at the time of his anger.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:29:2)


**בְּפָנָיו.** מִלְּשׁוֹן ״אַף״ (משלי כט:כב):


**in his anger** Heb. בפניו, an expression of anger.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 21:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/21:30:1)


**אֵין חָכְמָה וְגוֹ׳.** אֵין כָּל חָכָם וְנָבוֹן חָשׁוּב **לְנֶגֶד ה׳**, כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ חִלּוּל הַשֵּׁם, אֵין חוֹלְקִין כָּבוֹד לָרַב (ברכות יט:):


**There is neither wisdom, etc.** No wise or understanding man is of any importance against the Lord. Wherever there is a desecration of the Name, we do not pay regard to a teacher.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:1:1)


**נִבְחָר שֵׁם מֵעֹשֶׁר רָב.** שֵׁם טוֹב:


**A name is chosen** A good name above great wealth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:1:2)


**מִכֶּסֶף וּמִזָּהָב.** נִבְחָר חֵן טוֹב:


**good favor over silver and gold** Good favor is chosen.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:2:1)


**עוֹשֵׂה כֻלָּם ה׳.** כְּשֶׁעָנִי אוֹמֵר לֶעָשִׁיר: פַּרְנְסֵנִי, וְהוּא עוֹנֶה קָשׁוֹת, **עוֹשֵׂה כֻלָּם ה׳.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָם חֲדָשִׁים, לָזֶה עָנִי וְלָזֶה עָשִׁיר (תמורה טז.):


**the Lord is the Maker of them all** When the poor man says to the rich man, “Sustain me,” and he answers him harshly, the Holy One, blessed be He, makes them new: this one poor and this one rich.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:3:1)


**עָרוּם רָאָה רָעָה.** עֹנֶשׁ שֶׁל עֲבֵרָה, וְנִסְתָּר וְלֹא עָבַר עֲבֵרָה:


**A cunning man saw harm** The punishment for a sin, and hid, not committing the sin.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:4:1)


**עֵקֶב עֲנָוָה.** בִּשְׁבִיל הָעֲנָוָה **יִרְאַת ה׳** בָּאָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: עֲנָוָה עִקָּר, וְהַיִּרְאָה טְפֵלָה וְעָקֵב לָהּ, מִדְרָס לְרַגְלֶיהָ (ירושלמי שבת א,ג):


**In the wake of humility** Because of humility, fear of the Lord comes. Another explanation: Humility is the main attribute, and fear is secondary to it and a “heel” to it, a mat for its feet.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:1)


**צִנִּים פַּחִים.** כְּמוֹ ״לִצְנִינִם בְּצִדֵּיכֶם״ (במדבר לג:נה), ״וּבָאוּ עָלַיִךְ הֹצֶן״ (יחזקאל כג:כד), לְשׁוֹן גְּדוּדִים וְלִסְטִים:


**Troops [and] snares** Heb. צנים, as in (Num. 33:55) “troops (לצנינים) in your sides”; (Ezek. 23: 24) “And they will come upon you, a band (הצן),” an expression of bands and brigands.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:2)


**צִנִּים פַּחִים.** הֵם טְמוּנִים בְּדַרְכֵי הַמְּעַקֵּשׁ דְּרָכָיו, כְּלוֹמַר: יִסּוּרִים מוּכָנִים לוֹ:


**Troops and snares** are hidden on the ways of the one who perverts his ways; i.e., torments are prepared for him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:5:3)


**שׁוֹמֵר נַפְשׁוֹ יִרְחַק מֵהֶם.** שֶׁמְּיַשֵּׁר מַעֲשָׂיו יִנָּצֵל מֵהֶם:


**he who preserves his soul will distance himself from them** He who is upright in his deeds will be saved from them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:6:1)


**חֲנוֹךְ לַנַּעַר.** לְפִי מַה שֶּׁתְּלַמֵּד לַנַּעַר וּתְחַנְּכֵהוּ בִּדְבָרִים, אִם לְטוֹב אִם לְרַע, **גַּם כִּי יַזְקִין לֹא יָסוּר מִמֶּנָּה**:


**Train a child** According to what you teach a child and train him in matters, either for good or bad, even when he grows old, he will not turn away from it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:7:1)


**עָשִׁיר בְּרָשִׁים יִמְשׁוֹל.** עַם הָאָרֶץ צָרִיךְ לְתַלְמִיד חָכָם לְעוֹלָם (שבת קלט.):


**A rich man will rule over the poor** An ignoramus always needs a Torah scholar.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:8:1)


**זוֹרֵעַ עַוְלָה.** יִקְצָר אָוֶן לְפִי זְרִיעָתוֹ קְצִירָתוֹ, לְפִי פְּעֻלָּתוֹ יִהְיֶה קַבָּלַת מַשְׂכֻּרְתּוֹ:


**He who sows injustice will reap violence** According to his sowing will be his reaping; according to his deed will be the reception of his reward.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:8:2)


**וְשֵׁבֶט עֶבְרָתוֹ יִכְלֶה.** קְנֵה הַשִּׁבֹּלֶת שֶׁלּוֹ כָּלֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ לוֹ. יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים (סא״א): שֵׁבֶט חֲמָתוֹ, שֶׁרוֹדֶה בְּנֵי אָדָם, כָּלֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ לוֹ, לְפִי שֶׁמַּפְסִיד אֶת יְכָלְתּוֹ:


**and the rod of his wrath will fail** The rod of his ear of grain will fail more and more [interpreting עברתו as עבור, grain]. Some say: The rod of his wrath with which he rules over people will become progressively lessened because he impairs his ability.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:10:1)


**גָּרֵשׁ לֵץ.** יֵצֶר הָרָע:


**Banish a scorner** The evil inclination.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:11:1)


**אוֹהֵב טְהַר לֵב חֵן שְׂפָתָיו.** מִי שֶׁשְּׂפָתָיו חֵן:


**He who loves one pure of heart with charm on his lips** One whose lips have grace.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:11:2)


**רֵעֵהוּ מֶלֶךְ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אוֹהֲבוֹ וּמְחַבְּבוֹ (בראשית רבה מא, ה):


**the King is his friend** The Holy One, blessed be He, loves him and endears him.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:13:1)


**אָמַר עָצֵל אֲרִי בַחוּץ.** אֵיךְ אֵצֵא לִלְמֹד תּוֹרָה?


**The lazy man says, “There is a lion outside...”** How will I go out to learn Torah?



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:1)


**שׁוּחָה עֲמֻקָּה פִּי זָרוֹת.** פִּי עוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים:


**The mouth of strange women is [like] a deep pit** An idolatress.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:2)


**זְעוּם ה׳.** מִי שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שׂוֹנְאוֹ, נוֹפֵל וְנִכְשָׁל בָּהּ:


**the one abhorred by the Lord** Heb. זעום. He whom the Holy One, blessed be He, hates, falls and stumbles upon her.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:14:3)


**זְעוּם.** נָזוּף מִלְּפָנָיו:


**the one abhorred** rebuked from before Him.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:16:1)


**עוֹשֵׁק דָּל לְהַרְבּוֹת לוֹ.** עֹשֶׁר:


**He who exploits a poor man to increase for himself** money.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:16:2)


**נוֹתֵן לְעָשִׁיר.** סוֹף שֶׁנּוֹתֵן מָמוֹנוֹ לְעוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים הָעֲשִׁירִים, וְ**אַךְ לְמַחְסוֹר** הוּא בָּא:


**will give to a rich man** He will ultimately give his money to the rich pagan kings, and it will be only to want.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:17:1)


**הַט אָזְנְךָ שְׁמַע דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים.** לִלְמוֹד תּוֹרָה מֵחָכָם כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


**Incline your ear and hearken to the words of the wise** to learn the Torah from a sage of any stature.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:17:2)


**וְלִבְּךָ תָּשִׁית לְדַעְתִּי.** וְאִם רַבְּךָ רָשָׁע, לֹא תִלְמוֹד מִמַּעֲשָׂיו (חגיגה טו:):


**and put your heart to my knowledge** But if your teacher is wicked, do not learn from his deeds.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:18:1)


**כִּי נָעִים.** יְהִי לְךָ לְאַחַר זְמַן אִם **תִּשְׁמְרֵם** וְתִצְפְּנֵם **בְּבִטְנֶךָ**, שֶׁלֹּא תִּשְׁכָּחֵם. וְאֵימָתַי יִהְיוּ שְׁמוּרִים בְּלִבְּךָ? בִּזְמַן **שֶׁיִּכּוֹנוּ עַל שְׂפָתֶיךָ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר תּוֹצִיאֵם בְּפִיךָ (עירובין נד.):


**for it is pleasant** It will be [pleasant] for you at a later time if you preserve them and hide them in your innards lest you forget them. Now when will they be preserved in your heart? When they are established on your lips, when you utter them with your mouth.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:19:1)


**לִהְיוֹת בַּה׳ מִבְטַחֶךָ הוֹדַעְתִּיךָ.** אֲנִי מוֹדִיעֲךָ שֶׁתִּבְטַח בַּה׳ וְתַעֲסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה, וְלֹא תֹאמַר: אֵיךְ אֲבַטֵּל מִמְּלַאכְתִּי וְאֵיךְ אֶתְפַּרְנֵס?


**That your trust shall be in the Lord, I have made known to you** I make known to you that you should trust in the Lord and engage in the Torah, and you shall not say, “How will I be idle from my work, and how will I earn a livelihood?”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:20:1)


**הֲלֹא כָתַבְתִּי לְךָ שָׁלִשִׁים.** תּוֹרָה וּנְבִיאִים וּכְתוּבִים:


**Have I not written to you thirds** The Torah, the Prophets, and the Hagiographa.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:21:1)


**לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ קֹשְׁטְ.** **שָׁלִישִׁים כָּתַבְתִּי לְךָ** כְּדֵי שֶׁתָּבִין מִתּוֹכָם **קֹשְׁטְ אִמְרֵי אֱמֶת.** לְהָשִׁיב אֲמָרִים אֱמֶת:


**to make known to you the certainty** I wrote thirds for you in order that you should understand from them the certainty of the true words.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:21:2)


**לְשֹׁלְחֶיךָ.** לְשׁוֹאֲלְךָ הוֹרָאָה:


**to respond with words of truth to those who send you** To those who ask you for instruction.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:22:1)


**אַל תִּגְזָל דָּל.** אַל תִּגְזְלֶנּוּ כִּי תִרְאֶנּוּ דַּל, בַּעֲבוּר שֶׁהוּא דַּל, וְאֵין לוֹ כֹּחַ לַעֲמֹד נֶגְדְּךָ:


**Do not rob a poor man** Do not rob him because you see that he is poor and has no strength to resist you.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:23:1)


**כִּי ה׳ יָרִיב רִיבָם וְקָבַע.** וְיִגְזֹל אֶת הַנֶּפֶשׁ:


**For the Lord will plead their cause and rob** Heb. וקבע. And will rob the life.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:23:2)


**קוֹבְעֵיהֶם.** גּוֹזְלֵיהֶם. וּבִלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי קְבִיעָה – גְּזֵלָה בְּמַסֶּכֶת רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה (ראש השנה כו,א-ב): אֲתָא הַהוּא גַּבְרָא לְקַמֵּיהּ אָמַר לֵיהּ: קַבְּעַן פְּלַנְיָא וְכוּ׳:


**those who rob them** Heb. קבעים, those who rob them; in Aramaic קביעה means robbery. In tractate Rosh Hashanah (26b): A certain man came before him and said to him, “So-and-so robbed me (קבען)” etc.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:24:1)


**אַל תִּתְרַע.** אַל תִּתְחַבֵּר, לְשׁוֹן רֵעוּת:


**Do not befriend** Heb. תתרע, do not befriend, an expression of friendship (רעות)



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:25:1)


**פֶּן תֶּאֱלַף.** פֶּן תִּלְמַד:


**lest you learn** Heb. תאלף.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:26:1)


**אַל תְּהִי בְתוֹקְעֵי כָף בָּעוֹרְבִים מַשָּׁאוֹת.** הַלְוָאוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״מַשַּׁאת מְאוּמָה״ (דברים כד:י):


**Do not be one of those who give their hands, who stand surety for debts** Loans, as in (Deut. 24: 10), “When you lend you fellow [Jew] any item.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 22:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/22:28:1)


**אַל תַּסֵּג גְּבוּל עוֹלָם.** אַל תָּשֵׁג אָחוֹר מִנְהַג אֲבוֹתֶיךָ. וְאָמְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (תורת כהנים קדושים פרשתא א פרק ג): הַמַּנִּיחַ כַּלְכָּלָה תַּחַת הַגֶּפֶן בִּשְׁעַת בְּצִירָה כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפּוֹל בְּתוֹכוֹ הַפֶּרֶט, עַל זֶה נֶאֱמַר **אַל תַּסֵּג גְּבוּל וְגוֹ׳**. **תַּסֵּג** כְּמוֹ ״יִסּוֹגוּ אָחוֹר״ (תהלים קכט:ה):


**Do not remove an ancient boundary** Do not turn away from the custom of your forefathers. Our Sages of blessed memory said: If one places a basket under a vine at the time of vintage so that the fallen grapes should fall into it, concerning this was stated: “Do not remove an ancient boundary, etc.” **remove** Heb. תסג, as in (Ps. 129:5) “and shall draw backwards (ויסגו).”



## Chapter 23


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:1:1)


**כִּי תֵשֵׁב לִלְחוֹם.** לֶאֱכוֹל:


**If you sit down to dine** Heb. ללחום, to eat.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:1:2)


**בִּין תָּבִין.** תֵּן לִבְּךָ לָדַעַת מִי הוּא, אִם עֵינוֹ צָרָה אוֹ יָפָה:


**you should understand well** Give thought to know who he is, whether he is stingy or generous.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:2:1)


**וְשַׂמְתָּ שַׂכִּין בְּלֹעֶךָ.** בִּלְחָיֶיךָ; אִם רָאִיתָ עֵינוֹ צָרָה, אַל תֹּאכַל מִשֶּׁלּוֹ:


**and you shall put a knife into your jaw** Heb. בלועך, into your jaw. If you see that he is stingy, do not eat of his [food].


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:2:2)


**אִם בַּעַל נֶפֶשׁ אָתָּה.** אִם רַעַבְתָּן אַתָּה וְתָאֵב לֶאֱכוֹל, טוֹב לִתְחוֹב סַכִּין בֵּין שִׁנֶּיךָ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דָּרְשׁוּ (חולין ו.) בְּתַלְמִיד הַיּוֹשֵׁב לִפְנֵי רַבּוֹ; אִם יוֹדֵעַ בְּרַבּוֹ שֶׁיַּחֲזִיר לוֹ תְּשׁוּבָה עַל כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁיִּשְׁאָלֵהוּ, יְדַקְדֵּק וְיִשְׁאַל שְׁמוּעָתוֹ, וְאִם לָאו – יִשְׁתּוֹק:


**if you are a man with a hearty appetite** If you are a glutton and long to eat, it is better that you thrust a knife between your teeth. Our Sages expounded this as referring to a disciple sitting before his teacher. If he knows that his teacher will give him an answer for everything he asks, let him investigate the subtle points and ask concerning his tradition, but if not, let him keep his peace.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:3:1)


**אַל תִּתְאָו לְמַטְעַמּוֹתָיו.** אֶלָּא פְּרוֹשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ וְלֵךְ לִפְנֵי רַב הָגוּן, וְלֹא תְּבַיְּשֵׁהוּ בִּשְׁאֵלוֹת, אַחַר שֶׁלֹּא יֵדַע לְהָשִׁיב:


**Do not desire his delicacies** but leave him and go [to study] before a competent teacher, and do not embarrass him with questions, since he does not know what to answer.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:4:1)


**אַל תִּיגַע לְהַעֲשִׁיר.** לַעֲשׂוֹת גִּירְסָתְךָ חֲבִילוֹת חֲבִילוֹת, כִּי סוֹף שֶׁתִּשְׁכָּחֶנָּה:


**Do not weary yourself to grow rich** To make your learning into bundles because you will ultimately forget it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:5:1)


**הֲתָעִיף עֵינֶיךָ בּוֹ.** כְּרֶגַע, שֶׁתִּכְפּוֹל עֵינֶיךָ לְעָצְמוֹ, וְאֵין אוֹתָהּ גִּירְסָא מְצוּיָה בְּךָ:


**Should you blink your eyes at it** The moment you blink your eyes to close them, that study will no longer be found with you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:5:2)


**הֲתָעִיף.** כְּמוֹ ״וְכָפַלְתָּ״ (שמות כו:ט): ״וְתָעֵיף״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


התעיף, like “and you shall fold” (Ex. 26:9), which Onkelos renders: ותעיף.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:7:1)


**כִּי כְּמוֹ שָׁעַר בְּנַפְשׁוֹ.** שָׁעַר זֶה נָקוּד חֶצְיוֹ פַּתָּח וְחֶצְיוֹ קָמַץ וְטַעֲמוֹ לְמַטָּה, לְכָךְ הוּא פֹּעַל וְלֹא שֵׁם דָּבָר; שֶׁאִם הָיָה שֵׁם דָּבָר הָיָה קָמַץ וְטַעֲמוֹ לְמַעְלָה כְּכָל שַׁעַר שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא. וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ: כְּאִלּוּ שָׁפַךְ הָאוֹכֵל הַזֶּה מְרָה בְּנַפְשׁוֹ שֶׁל רַע הָעַיִן כֵּן הוּא **שָׁעַר** מִן לְשׁוֹן ״הַתְּאֵנִים הַשֹּׁעָרִים״ (ירמיהו כט:יז) הַמָּרִים:


**for it is as though it poured gall** שער. This שער is vowelized half with a “patach” and half with a “kamatz,” and the accent is at the end of the word; therefore, it is a verb and not a noun; for were it a noun, it would be [vowelized completely with] a “pattah” and the accent would be at the beginning of the word, like every שער in Scriptures. Its meaning is that it as though this food poured gall into the soul of this stingy man, so it is. **gall** Heb. שער, from the expression of (Jer. 29:17): “the loathsome (השערים) figs,” the bitter ones.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:8:1)


**פִּתְּךָ.** שֶׁאֲכַלְתּוֹ בְּבֵיתוֹ, סוֹף שֶׁתְּקִיאֶנָּה מִפְּנֵי הַבּוּשָׁה:


**your morsel** that you ate in his house, you will ultimately vomit because of embarrassment.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:8:2)


**וְשִׁחַתָּ דְּבָרֶיךָ הַנְּעִימִים.** חַנּוֹת שֶׁהֶחֱזַקְתָּ וְדִבַּרְתָּ לוֹ דְּבָרִים רַכִּים, הַכֹּל אִבַּדְתָּ:


**and you will lose your sweet words** The gratitude that you extended and spoke to him tender words—you lost everything.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:10:1)


**וּבִשְׂדֵי יְתוֹמִים.** בְּלֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה הָרָאוּי לָהֶם:


**and...the fields of the orphans** The gleanings, the forgotten sheaves, and the corner that is due them.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:16:1)


**בְּדַבֵּר שְׂפָתֶיךָ מֵישָׁרִים.** עַל יְדֵי שֶׁחָכַם לִבֶּךָ:


**when your lips speak right things** since your heart has grown wise.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:17:1)


**אַל יְקַנֵּא לִבְּךָ בַּחַטָּאִים.** בְּהַצְלָחָתָם, לִהְיוֹת רָשָׁע כְּמוֹתָם (ברכות ז:):


**Let your heart not envy the sinners** their success, to be wicked like them.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:18:1)


**כִּי אִם יֵשׁ אַחֲרִית.** אִם זֶה מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲשֶׁר, כְּלוֹמַר: אֲשֶׁר בַּעֲבוּר זֶה יֵשׁ אַחֲרִית תִּקְוָה אֵלֶיךָ:


**for because of this, there is a future** Heb. כי אם. This [word] אם is used as an expression of “that”; i.e., for because of this there is a future and hope for you.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:19:1)


**שְׁמַע אַתָּה בְנִי וַחֲכָם וְאַשֵּׁר בַּדֶּרֶךְ לִבֶּךָ.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁתִּתְחַכֵּם תּוּכַל לָלֶכֶת בְּדַרְכֵי לִבְּךָ, כִּי לֵב חָכָם לֹא יַשִּׁיאֲךָ לַעֲבֵרָה:


**Hear you, my son, and grow wise, and walk in the way of your heart** Since you will grow wise, you will be able to walk in the ways of your heart, for the heart of a wise man will not entice you to sin.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:21:1)


**כִּי סוֹבֵא וְזוֹלֵל יִוָּרֵשׁ.** ״יִתְמַסְכֵּן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**for the guzzler and the glutton will become impoverished** Heb. יורש, will become impoverished.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:21:2)


**וּקְרָעִים תַּלְבִּישׁ נוּמָה.** הָעַצְלוּת וְהַתְּנוּמָה תַּלְבִּישׁ אוֹתְךָ בְּגָדִים קְרוּעִים:


**and slumber will clothe [you] with tatters** Laziness and slumber will clothe you with tattered clothing.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:23:1)


**אֱמֶת קְנֵה.** וְאִם לֹא תִמְצָא לִלְמֹד בְּחִנָּם לְמֹד בְּשָׂכָר, וְאַל תֹּאמַר: כְּשֵׁם שֶׁלָּמַדְתִּי בְּשָׂכָר, כָּךְ אֲלַמְּדֶנָּה בְּשָׂכָר (בכורות כט.):


**Buy truth** If you do not find to learn for nothing, learn for pay [pay to learn], but do not say, “Just as I learned for pay, so will I teach it for pay.”



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:27:1)


**וּבְאֵר צָרָה.** אֶשְׁטְרֵיינָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a narrow well** (etroitte in French, in German, eng, schmal.)



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:28:1)


**אַף הִיא כְּחֶתֶף.** פִּתְאוֹם:


**suddenly** Heb. כחתף, suddenly.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:28:2)


**וּבוֹגְדִים בְּאָדָם תּוֹסִיף.** מְרַבָּה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל בּוֹגְדִים לַה׳, וְעַל הָאֲפִיקוֹרְסִין מְדַבֵּר:


**and she will increase the faithless among men** She increases in Israel those who are faithless to God. He is referring to apostasy.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:1)


**לְמִי אוֹי לְמִי אֲבוֹי.** לְשׁוֹן צְעָקָה וִילָלָה:


**Who cries, “Woe!” Who, “Alas!”** An expression of crying and yelling.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:2)


**לְמִי מִדְיָנִים לְמִי שִׂיחַ.** לְמִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מִדְיָנִים עִם הַבְּרִיּוֹת וְשִׂיחָה יְתֵרָה, לוֹ אוֹי וַאֲבוֹי, כִּי בְּרֹב דְּבָרִים רֹב פֶּשַׁע (משלי י:יט):


**He who has quarrels. He who talks too much** To him who has quarrels with the people and talks too much, woe and alas, for transgression does not stop with much speech.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:29:3)


**לְמִי חַכְלִילוּת עֵינָיִם.** מֵרוֹב יַיִן מַאֲדִימִים עֵינָיו, וּגְנַאי הוּא (תנחומא, שמיני ה):


**Who has bloodshot eyes?** From drinking much wine, the eyes become red, and that is embarrassing.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:30:1)


**מִמְסָךְ.** מִמֶּזֶג, וְהוּא שֵׁם דָּבָר כְּמוֹ ״מִמְכָּר״ (ויקרא כה:יד):


**for mixed wine** [as translated,] for mixed wine; this word is a noun, like ממכר, merchandise.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:30:2)


**לַחְקֹר מִמְסָךְ.** שֶׁחוֹקְרִין וּבוֹדְקִין הֵיכָן מוֹכְרִין יַיִן טוֹב:


**to search for mixed wine** Who search and hunt where good wine is sold.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:1)


**אַל תֵּרֶא יַיִן כִּי יִתְאַדָּם.** אַל תִּתֵּן בּוֹ עֵינֶיךָ:


**Do not look at wine when it is red** Do not put your eye on it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:2)


**כִּי יִתֵּן בַּכּוֹס עֵינוֹ יִתְהַלֵּךְ בְּמֵישָׁרִים.** הַמַּרְבֶּה שִׁכְרוּת, כָּל עֲבֵרוֹת דּוֹמוֹת לוֹ לְמִישׁוֹר, כָּל הַדְּרָכִים יְשָׁרִים בְּעֵינָיו:


**when he puts his eye on the cup, it goes smoothly** Whoever is habitually drunk—all transgressions appear to him to be straight; all ways appear straight.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:31:3)


**בַּכּוֹס.** בַּכִּיס כְּתִיב, כְּלוֹמַר: הַשּׁוֹתֶה נוֹתֵן עַיִן בַּכּוֹס וְהַחֶנְוָנִי – בְּכִיסוֹ שֶׁל זֶה (יומא עד:-עה.):


**on the cup** Heb. בכוס. The masoretic text reads: בכיס, on the pocket; i.e., the drinker puts his eye on the cup, and the storekeeper puts his eye on this one’s pocket.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:32:1)


**יַפְרִישׁ.** לְשׁוֹן עֲקִיצָה, פויינ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, ״בְּמַלְמַד הַבָּקָר״ (שופטים ג:לא) תַּרְגּוּם: ״בְּפָרַשׁ תּוֹרֵי״ (תרגום יונתן), וּבַגְּמָרָא (בבא מציעא פ.): דְּנָקִיט פַּרְשָׁא, אגוילו״ן בְּלַעַ״ז. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין: מַפְרִישׁוֹ מִן הַחַיִּים:


**and sting** Heb. יפרש, an expression of stinging (pointe in French, in German stechend). (Jud. 3: 31): “With an ox-goad” is translated into Aramaic as בפרש תורי, and in the Gemara (Baba Mezia 80a): “The one who holds the colter (פרשא),” aiguillon in Old French, in German stachel, Gewohneisen, and so in Jud. 3:31, II Sam. 8:1, and Jer. 31:18. Others interpret the word to mean that it separates him from life.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:33:1)


**עֵינֶיךָ יִרְאוּ זָרוֹת.** כְּשֶׁתִּשְׁתַּכֵּר, הַיַּיִן בּוֹעֵר בְּךָ וּמַשִּׂיאֲךָ לְהַבִּיט בְּזוֹנוֹת:


**Your eyes will see strange women** When you will become drunk, they will burn in you and entice you to ogle harlots.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:34:1)


**וְהָיִיתָ.** מְשֻׁגָּע (דברים כח:לד):


**And you shall be** mad like one lying in the midst of the sea.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:34:2)


**כְּשׁוֹכֵב בְּלֶב יָם וּכְשׁוֹכֵב בְּרֹאשׁ חִבֵּל.** תֹּרֶן הַסְּפִינָה נוֹחַ לִפּוֹל מִמֶּנּוּ:


**and like one lying at the top of a mast** The mast of a ship sways to fall from it.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Proverbs 23:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/23:35:1)


**הִכּוּנִי בַל חָלִיתִי וְגוֹ׳.** כְּשֶׁיִּקַץ מִיֵּינוֹ אֵינוֹ מַרְגִּישׁ בְּכָל הָרָעוֹת שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלָיו, וְחוֹזֵר לִשְׁתּוֹת:


**“They struck me but I did not become ill, etc.”** When he sobers himself up from his wine, he does not feel any of the evils that passed over him, and he drinks again.



## Chapter 24


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:5:1)


**גֶּבֶר חָכָם בְּעוֹז.** אִישׁ חָכָם הוּא תָּמִיד בְּעוֹז:


**A wise man is with strength** A wise man is always with strength.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:7:1)


**רָאמוֹת לֶאֱוִיל חָכְמוֹת.** מִין אֶבֶן יְקָרָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְצוּיָה, כְּמָה דְתֵימָא ״רָאמוֹת וְגָבִישׁ״ (איוב כח:יח). כְּלוֹמַר: כָּל חָכְמָה דּוֹמָה לַכְּסִיל כְּאֶבֶן יְקָרָה וּכְמַרְגָּלִיּוֹת לִקְנוֹת, לוֹמַר: אֵיךְ אֶעֱסֹק בַּתּוֹרָה, מָתַי תַּעֲלֶה בְּיָדִי? אֲבָל הֶחָכָם שׁוֹנֶה הַיּוֹם מְעַט וּלְמָחָר מְעַט (דברים רבה ח:ג):


**Wisdom is as pearls to the fool** Heb. ראמות, a type of rare precious stone, as it is stated (Job 28: 18): “Pearls (ראמות) and beryls.” All wisdom appears to the fool as unattainable, [as difficult] to purchase as precious stones and pearls, saying, ‘’How will I learn Torah? When will I attain it?” But the wise man studies a little today and a little tomorrow.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:8:1)


**מְחַשֵּׁב לְהָרֵעַ.** חוֹרֵשׁ תַּחְבּוּלוֹת רֶשַׁע:


**He who plots to do evil** He plans strategies of wickedness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:8:2)


**לוֹ בַּעַל מְזִמּוֹת יִקְרָאוּ.** גּוֹרֵם לוֹ רַע, שֶׁהַבְּרִיּוֹת קוֹרְאִים לוֹ: **בַּעַל מְזִמּוֹת.** עֲצַת רְשָׁעִים:


**is called a man of wicked designs** He brings harm to himself, for people call him a man of wicked designs, a counsel of the wicked.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:9:1)


**זִמַּת אִוֶּלֶת.** עֲצַת אִוֶּלֶת **חַטָּאת** לִבְעָלֶיהָ:


**The counsel of folly** Heb. זמת אולת, the counsel of folly **is sin** for those who perpetrate it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:9:2)


**וְתוֹעֲבַת לְאָדָם לֵץ.** לֵיצָנוּת הִיא הַמְּתַעֶבֶת אֶת הָאָדָם עַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא וְעַל הַבְּרִיּוֹת:


**and what makes man an abomination is scorn** Scorn is what makes man an abomination to the Holy One blessed be He, and to people.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:1)


**הִתְרַפִּיתָ.** מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


**If you have become lax** in the Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:2)


**בְּיוֹם צָרָה צַר כֹּחֶכָה.** מַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת לֹא יִהְיוּ מְאַמְּצִין כֹּחֲךָ. זֶה מִדְרַשׁ חֲכָמִים (ברכות סג.). וּלְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ: הִתְרַפִּיתָ בְּיוֹם צָרַת אֲהוּבְךָ לַעֲמוֹד מִנֶּגֶד:


**on a day of trouble your strength will be weak indeed** The ministering angels will not maintain your strength. This is the midrash of the Sages (Midrash Mishle ad loc., Berachoth 63a). But according to its simple meaning, you have become lax on the day of your friend’s trouble, and stand from afar.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:10:3)


**צַר כֹּחֶכָה.** בְּיוֹם פּוּרְעָנוּתְךָ, דִּכְתִיב ״אִם הַחֲרֵשׁ תַּחֲרִישִׁי וְגוֹ׳״ (אסתר ד:יד):


**your strength will be weak indeed** on the day of your punishment, as it is written (Esther 4:14): “For if you remain silent, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:11:1)


**הַצֵּל לְקוּחִים וְגוֹ׳.** לְהַצִּיל אֶת הַלְּקוּחִים לַמָּוֶת, אֶת הַ**מָּטִים לַהֶרֶג**:


**rescuing those taken, etc.** To rescue those taken to death.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:11:2)


**אִם תַּחְשׂוֹךְ.** מִלְּהַצִּילָם, עַל זֶה אָמַרְתִּי ״צַר כֹּחֲךָ״ (משלי כד:י):


**If you refrain** from rescuing them, concerning this I said, “Your strength will be weak indeed.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:12:1)


**כִּי תֹאמַר.** שֶׁמָּא תֹּאמַר, וְזֶהוּ כִּי מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּלְשׁוֹן דִּלְמָא:


**will you say** Heb. כי תאמר. And this instance of כי is used as an expression of “perhaps.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:13:1)


**אֱכָל בְּנִי דְבַשׁ.** כְּלוֹמַר: דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לֶאֱכֹל דְּבַשׁ **כִּי טוֹב** הוּא. אַף **כֵּן דְּעֵה חָכְמָה לְנַפְשֶׁךָ.** וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁאַתָּה רוֹדֵף לֶאֱכֹל דְּבַשׁ, כֵּן תְּהֵא רוֹדֵף לָדַעַת חָכְמָה:


**Eat honey, my son** It is customary for people to eat honey because it is good; so too know wisdom for your soul, and just as you run to eat honey, so should you run to know wisdom.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:14:1)


**אִם מָצָאתָ** אוֹתָהּ, אָז יֵשׁ אַחֲרִית:


**if you have found** it, then you will have a future.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:19:1)


**אַל תִּתְחַר.** לִהְיוֹת כְּמוֹתָם, כְּמוֹ ״אֵיךְ תְּתַחֲרֶה״ (ירמיהו יב:ה), ״כִּי אַתָּה מִתְחַרֶה בָאָרֶז״ (ירמיהו כב:טו):


**Do not compete** to be like them, as in (Jer. 12:5): “How will you compete (תתחרה)?” Also, (ibid. 22:15): “for you compete (מתחרה) with the cedar.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:21:1)


**יְרָא אֶת ה׳ בְּנִי וָמֶלֶךְ.** יְרָא אֶת הַמֶּלֶךְ, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יְסִירְךָ מִיִּרְאַת ה׳, וּלְעוֹלָם יִרְאַת ה׳ קוֹדֶמֶת:


**My son, fear the Lord and the king** Fear the Lord and fear the mortal king, but only if he does not turn you away from the fear of the Lord, for the fear of the Lord is always first.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:21:2)


**עִם שׁוֹנִים אַל תִּתְעָרָב.** הָאוֹמְרִים: שְׁתֵּי רָשֻׁיּוֹת יֵשׁ:


**do not mingle with dualists** Heb. שונים, who say that there are two powers [governing the world].



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:22:1)


**וּפִיד שְׁנֵיהֶם.** שֶׁבֶר עֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים וְשֶׁבֶר עוֹבְדֶיהָ:


**and the ruin of both of them** The ruin of the idol and the ruin of its worshippers.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:23:1)


**גַּם אֵלֶּה לַחֲכָמִים.** כָּל הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁבָּעִנְיָן שֶׁלְּמַטָּה אֲמוּרִים לַחֲכָמִים הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּדִין, שֶׁלֹּא יַכִּירוּ פָנִים בְּמִשְׁפָּט כִּי לֹא **טוֹב** (תנחומא, משפטים ד):


**These too are for the wise** All the statements in the following section are addressed to the wise who sit in judgment, that they should not show respect to persons in judgment, for it is not good.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:26:1)


**שְׂפָתַיִם יִשָּׁק.** רְאוּיִים כָּל שְׂפָתַיִם לְנַשְּׁקוֹ:


**Lips should kiss** It is fitting that all lips kiss him.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:1)


**הָכֵן בַּחוּץ.** זֶה מִקְרָא:


**Prepare...outside** This refers to Scripture.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:2)


**וְעַתְּדָהּ בַּשָּׂדֶה.** זוֹ מִשְׁנָה:


**and make it fit in the field** This is Mishnah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:27:3)


**אַחַר וּבָנִיתָ בֵיתֶךָ.** זוֹ גְּמָרָא (סוטה מד.). דָּבָר אַחֵר: כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, בַּתְּחִלָּה קַח לְךָ שָׂדוֹת וּכְרָמִים, וְאַחַר כָּךְ **וְעַתְּדָהּ**, לְשׁוֹן ״עַתּוּדִים״ (בראשית לא:י) – שִׂים לְךָ בְּהֵמוֹת **בַּשָּׂדֶה**, וְ**אַחַר וּבָנִיתָ בֵיתֶךָ.** תִּקַּח אִשָּׁה:


**afterwards you shall build your house** This is Talmud (Sotah 44a). Another explanation according to its apparent meaning: In the beginning, buy yourself fields and vineyards, and afterwards make it fit, ועתדה, an expression concerning goats (עתודים). Stock your field with livestock, and afterwards you shall build a house, meaning that you shall take a wife.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:28:1)


**וְהַפִּתִּיתָ בִּשְׂפָתֶיךָ.** תִּתְפַּתֶּה לְרֵעֲךָ בְּדִבּוּרְךָ:


**that you should be enticed with your lips** You should be enticed by your friend with your speech.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:30:1)


**אִישׁ עָצֵל.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר עַל גִּרְסַת תַּלְמוּדוֹ:


**a lazy man** who does not review the memorization of his studies.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:1)


**קִמְּשׂוֹנִים.** חוֹחִים:


**thistles** Heb. קמשונים, thistles.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:2)


**כָּסּוּ.** נִכְסוּ:


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:3)


**חֲרֻלִּים.** גְּדוֹלִים מִקִּמְּשׂוֹנִים, וַהֲרֵי הֵם חַדִּים כְּקוֹצִים:


**nettles** חרלים. They are larger than thistles, and they are as sharp as thorns.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:31:4)


**וְגֶדֶר אֲבָנָיו נֶהֱרָסָה.** כָּךְ מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר עַל תַּלְמוּדוֹ, מִתְּחִלָּה מְשַׁכֵּחַ רָאשֵׁי פְרָקִים, וְסוֹף מְחַלֵּף דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים מִזֶּה לָזֶה (אבות דרבי נתן כד,ו), שֶׁאוֹמֵר עַל טָהוֹר טָמֵא וְעַל טָמֵא טָהוֹר, וְהוּא מַחֲרִיב אֶת הָעוֹלָם:


**and its stone fence had been torn down** Thus, one who does not review his studies first forgets the beginnings of the chapters and at the end confuses the names of the Sages from this one to that one, thereby saying “unclean” for something that is clean and “clean” for something that is unclean, and he destroys the world.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Proverbs 24:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/24:34:1)


**מִתְהַלֵּךְ רֵישֶׁךָ.** הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁאַתָּה רָשׁ מֵהֶם יָבוֹאוּ לְךָ בִּמְרוּצָה:


**your poverty will come strolling** The things that impoverish you will come to you quickly.



## Chapter 25


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:1:1)


**גַּם אֵלֶּה מִשְׁלֵי שְׁלֹמֹה אֲשֶׁר הֶעְתִּיקוּ.** חָזַר לְדוֹרְשָׁם, כְּשֶׁנִּתְמַנָּה חִזְקִיָּהוּ לְמֶלֶךְ וְהוּא הוֹשִׁיב תַּלְמִידִים בְּכָל עִיר, כְּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק חֵלֶק (סנהדרין צד:): בָּדְקוּ מִדָּן וְעַד בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע וְלֹא מָצְאוּ עַם הָאָרֶץ:


**These too are Solomon’s proverbs, which...maintained** When Hezekiah was appointed king, he returned to expound upon them [Solomon’s proverbs]. Now he placed disciples in every city, as it is stated in chapter “Chelek” (Sanh. 94b): “They searched from Dan to Beersheba and could not find an ignoramus.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:1:2)


**הֶעְתִּיקוּ.** הֶחֱזִיקוּ:


**maintained** Heb. העתיקו they strengthened.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:2:1)


**כְּבוֹד אֱלֹהִים הַסְתֵּר דָּבָר.** כְּגוֹן מַעֲשֵׂה מֶרְכָּבָה (יחזקאל א) וּמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית (בראשית א):


**The honor of God is to conceal a matter** For instance, the account of the Merkavah and the account of the Creation.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:2:2)


**וּכְבוֹד מְלָכִים חֲקֹר דָּבָר.** כְּשֶׁאַתָּה דּוֹרֵשׁ בִּכְבוֹד מְלָכִים אוֹ בִּכְבוֹד חֲכָמִים שֶׁעָשׂוּ סְיָגִים לַתּוֹרָה וּבַגְּזֵרוֹת שֶׁגָּזְרוּ בָּהֶם, יֵשׁ לְךָ לַחֲקֹר וְלִדְרֹשׁ וְלִשְׁאֹל טַעַם לַדָּבָר. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁתִּדְרֹשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה מֶרְכָּבָה וּבְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית וּבַחֻקִּים הַכְּתוּבִים בַּתּוֹרָה, כְּגוֹן חֻקִּים וּדְבָרִים שֶׁהַשָּׂטָן מְקַטְרֵג עֲלֵיהֶם וּמֵשִׁיב עֲלֵיהֶם, בַּאֲכִילַת חֲזִיר וְכִלְאֵי כֶּרֶם וְשַׁעַטְנֵז, אֵין לְךָ לַחֲקֹר רַק לְהַסְתִּיר, וְלוֹמַר: גְּזֵרַת מֶלֶךְ הוּא:


**whereas the honor of kings is to search out a matter** When you expound on the honor of the Sages and on the safeguards that they enacted to the Torah, and on the decrees that they decreed upon them, you should search, seek, and ask the reason for the matter. When you expound on the account of the Merkavah or on the account of the Creation, or on the statutes written in the Torah—like the statutes and things that Satan denounces and refutes, such as eating pork, mingled species in a vineyard, and shaatnes—you should not search, but only conceal [the reason] and say, “It is the King’s decree.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:3:1)


**אֵין חֵקֶר.** לְרוּם שָׁמַיִם וּלְעוֹמֶק הָאָרֶץ וּלְלֵב מְלָכִים אֵין חֵקֶר, שֶׁכַּמָּה דִינִים בָּאִים לִפְנֵיהֶם וְכַמָּה מִלְחָמוֹת, וּצְרִיכִים לָתֵת לֵב לְכֻלָּם. וְאִם כָּל הַלְּשׁוֹנוֹת מְדַבְּרִים וְכָל הַיָּדַיִם כּוֹתְבִין, אֵינָן יְכוֹלִין לִכְתּוֹב חֲלָלָהּ שֶׁל רְשׁוּת:


**are unsearchable** The height of the heaven and the depth of the earth and the heart of kings are unsearchable, for many judgments come before them, as well as many wars, and they must apply their heart to all of them. And if all tongues would speak, and all hands would write, they would not be able to inscribe the intricacy of political government.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:4:1)


**הָגוֹ סִיגִים מִכָּסֶף.** מָשׁוּךְ, כְּמוֹ ״כַּאֲשֶׁר הֹגָה מִן הַמְּסִלָּה״ (שמואל ב כ:יג); כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין הַכְּלִי שֶׁל כֶּסֶף יוֹצֵא לִמְלֶאכֶת צוֹרֵף עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ סִיגִים שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁבּוֹ, כָּךְ אֵין צִבּוּר נִפְטָרִין מֵעֹנֶשׁ עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיאוּ מִתּוֹכָם הָרְשָׁעִים וְיַעֲשׂוּ בָּהֶם דִּין:


**Remove dross from silver** Heb. הגו, draw away, as in (II Sam. 20:13): “When he was removed (הגה) from the highway.” Just as the silver vessel does not emerge for the work of the refiner until the copper dross is removed from it, similarly, the community cannot be freed from punishment until they remove the wicked from their midst and execute judgment upon them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:6:1)


**אַל תִּתְהַדַּר לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ.** לְהֵרָאוֹת כְּבוֹדְךָ וּלְהִתְגָּאוֹת לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ אוֹ לַעֲמֹד לִפְנֵי מִי שֶׁגָּדוֹל מִמְּךָ, כִּי טוֹב שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ לְךָ עֲלֵה הֵנָּה מִשֶּׁתָּבוֹא בְּלֹא רְשׁוּתוֹ וְיֹאמְרוּ לְךָ צֵא:


**Do not glorify yourself before a king** to show your honor and to be proud before one who is greater than you, because it is better that they say to you, “Come up here,” than that you should go without his permission, and they should tell you, “Go down, get out.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:7:1)


**אֲשֶׁר רָאוּ עֵינֶיךָ.** שֶׁדָּבָר זֶה אֱמֶת:


**as your eyes have seen** that this thing is true.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:8:1)


**פֶּן מַה תַּעֲשֶׂה.** פֶּן תָּבֹא לִידֵי לֹא תֵדַע מַה לַּעֲשׂוֹת בְּאַחֲרִיתָהּ:


**lest [you will not know] what you will do at its end** Lest you come to a situation that you will not know what to do at its end.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:9:1)


**רִיבְךָ רִיב אֶת רֵעֶךָ.** וְאִם עַל כָּרְחֲךָ אַתָּה צָרִיךְ לָרִיב וּלְהוֹכִיחַ עִם רֵעֶךָ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם, **סוֹד אַחֵר אַל תְּגַל.** לֹא תַזְכִּיר לוֹ דֹּפִי אֲבוֹתָיו שֶׁמֵּתוּ, שֶׁאֵין הַכֹּל יוֹדְעִין אוֹתוֹ וְאַתָּה מְגַלֵּהוּ (סנהדרין מד:):


**Have your quarrel with your friend** But if, perforce, you must quarrel and debate with your friend, in any event, **do not divulge another’s secret** Do not mention to him the disgrace of his ancestors, which not everyone knows, and you are revealing it.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:10:1)


**פֶּן יְחַסֶּדְךָ שׁוֹמֵעַ.** ״חֶרְפַּת מִצְרַיִם״ (יהושע ה:ט) מְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״חֲסוּדָא דְּמִצְרָאֵי״, **פֶּן יְחַסֶּדְךָ**, הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ וְיִקְרָאֲךָ מוֹצִיא דִּבָּה:


**lest one who hears embarrass you** Heb. יחסדך (Josh. 5:9) “The reproach of the Egyptians” is rendered: חסודא דמצראי. Lest one who hears embarrass you, and they will call you a slanderer.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:11:1)


**תַּפּוּחֵי זָהָב.** כְּעֵין כַּפְתּוֹרִין מְצֻיָּרִין עַל **מַשְׂכִּיּוֹת כָּסֶף**. **מַשְׂכִּיּוֹת** כֵּלִים הַמְצֻפִּין בְּכֶסֶף, כְּמוֹ ״וְשַׂכֹּתִי כַפִּי״ (שמות לג:כב):


**golden apples** Like knobs depicted on silverplated vessels. **silverplated vessels** Heb. משכיות כסף, vessels plated with silver, as in (Ex. 33:22): “and I will cover (ושכתי) you with My hand.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:11:2)


**דָּבָר דָּבוּר עַל אָפְנָיו.** עַל כַּנּוֹ, וְדוּגְמָתוֹ ״נָשָׂאתִי אֵימֶיךָ אָפוּנָה״ (תהלים פח:טז) – מְבֻסֶּסֶת וּמְיֻשֶּׁבֶת בְּקִרְבִּי. וְאֵין זֶה מִגִּזְרַת ׳אוֹפַן וְגַלְגַּל׳ (ישעיהו כח:כז-כח), שֶׁאִלּוּ כֵּן, הָיָה נָקוּד פַּתָּ״ח תַּחַת הַפֵּ״א כְּמוֹ ״הָאוֹפַנִּים״ (מלכים א ז:לב), וְלֹא יִפֹּל בּוֹ נְקֻדַּת חֲטַף קָמַץ:


**a word spoken with proper basis** Heb. אפניו, on its basis. An example of it is (Ps. 88:16): “I bear your terrors, it is settled (אפונה) [in my heart],” based and settled within me. This is not from the root of (Isa. 28: 27f): אפן and גלגל, wheel, for, were it so, it would be vowelized with a “pattach” under the “fe” as in (Ezek. 1:16) “האופנים the wheels.” The vowelization of the short kamatz is inappropriate for it.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:12:1)


**נֶזֶם זָהָב וַחֲלִי כָתֶם.** עֲדִי קְבוּצַת זָהָב, כְּמוֹ ״וַתַּעַד נִזְמָהּ וְחֶלְיָתָהּ״ (הושע ב:טו), כְּמוֹ ״חֲלָאִים״ (שיר השירים ז:ב):


**Like a golden earring and jewelry of finest gold** A collection of golden jewelry, as in (Hos. 2: 15): “and adorned herself with her earrings and her jewelry (וחליתה),” and like (Song 7:3): “jewels (חלאים).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:12:2)


**כֶּתֶם.** לְשׁוֹן תַּכְשִׁיט זָהָב, וְכֵן ״כֶּתֶם פָּז״ (שיר השירים ה:יא):


**finest gold** Heb. כתם an expression of a golden ornament, and likewise (Song 5:11): “His head is as a jewel (כתם) of the finest gold.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:13:1)


**כְּצִנַּת שֶׁלֶג.** כְּקוֹר יְמֵי שֶׁלֶג שֶׁאָדָם מִתְאַוֶּה לוֹ בִּימֵי קָצִיר, וְלֹא שֶׁלֶג מַמָּשׁ, שֶׁהַשֶּׁלֶג לֹא טוֹב הוּא בִּשְׁעַת קָצִיר:


**Like the cold of snow** Like the cold of the days of snow (winter), for which a person longs at harvest, but not actual snow, because snow is not beneficial during harvest.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:14:1)


**נְשִׂיאִים וְרוּחַ וְגוֹ׳.** כַּאֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה תּוֹחֶלֶת שָׁוְא כְּשֶׁהַשָּׁמַיִם מִתְקַשְּׁרִים בֶּעָבִים וְהָרוּחַ מְנַשֶּׁבֶת וְאָדָם מְצַפֶּה שֶׁיָּבֹא גֶשֶׁם וְלֹא בָא, וְהֵם מִצְטַעֲרִים וְכָלוֹת עֵינֵיהֶם, כָּךְ אִישׁ מִתְפָּאֵר לוֹמַר: כָּךְ וְכָךְ צְדָקָה אֶתֵּן לְיַד גַּבַּאי, וְהוּא מְשַׁקֵּר, וְכָלוֹת עֵינֵי עֲנִיִּים לְמַתְּנָתוֹ וְאֵינָהּ בָּאָה:


**Clouds and wind, etc.** When there is a false hope—when the heavens thicken with clouds and the wind blows—people hope that it will rain. Now if it does not rain, they are troubled and their eyes languish. So is a man who boasts, saying, “So much and so much charity I will give to the collector,” but he lies, and the eyes of the poor languish for his gift, but it does not come.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:1)


**בְּאֹרֶךְ אַפַּיִם יְפֻתֶּה קָצִין.** בְּעוֹד שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מַאֲרִיךְ אַפּוֹ וְאֵינוֹ נִפְרָע, יִתְּנוּ לֵב הַחוֹטְאִים לְפַתּוֹתוֹ בִּתְשׁוּבָה וּתְפִלָּה:


**The Ruler is won over with slowness to anger** As long as the Holy One, blessed be He, holds back His anger and does not requite, the sinners will apply their hearts to win Him over with repentance and prayer.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:2)


**וְלָשׁוֹן רַכָּה.** בִּתְפִלָּה וְתַחֲנוּנִים:


**and a gentle tongue** with prayer and supplications.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:15:3)


**תִּשְׁבָּר גָּרֶם.** עֶצֶם קֹשִׁי הַגְּזֵרָה:


**will break a bone** Heb. גרם a bone, the severity of the decree.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:16:1)


**דְּבַשׁ מָצָאתָ וְגוֹ׳**:


**If you have found honey, etc....**



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:17:1)


**הוֹקַר רַגְלְךָ.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִם מָצָאתָ דְּבַשׁ וְהוּא מָתוֹק לְחִכְּךָ, אַתָּה צָרִיךְ שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאכַל מִמֶּנּוּ פֶּן תִּשְׂבָּעֶנּוּ וַהֲקֵאֹתוֹ, כָּךְ **הוֹקַר רַגְלְךָ מִבֵּית רֵעֶךָ** אַע״פּ שֶׁהוּא מְקָרֶבְךָ, מְנַע מִלָּבֹא שָׁם יוֹם יוֹם **פֶּן יִשְׂבָּעֲךָ** וְיִשְׂנָא אוֹתְךָ. וּלְעִנְיַן מִדְרָשׁוֹ (חגיגה ז.): אַל תְּהִי רָגִיל לַחֲטֹא שׁוֹגֵג וּלְהָבִיא תָּמִיד חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת לְבֵית ה׳ שֶׁנִּקְרָא רֵעַ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, דִּכְתִיב ״זֶה דוֹדִי וְזֶה רֵעִי״ (שיר השירים ה:טז):


**Visit your neighbor sparingly** Just as, if you find honey, and it is sweet to your palate, you must not eat [too much] of it lest you become sated with it and vomit it, so should you visit your neighbor sparingly, although he is from your midst [of your kin], refrain from going there daily lest he become sated with you and hate you. [Bracketed words appear in Salonica edition only.] According to its homiletic interpretation, do not accustom yourself to sin inadvertently and to constantly bring sin- offerings and guilt-offerings to the house of the Lord, Who is called a friend to Israel, as it is written (Song 5:16): “This is my beloved and this is my friend.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:18:1)


**מֵפִיץ וְחֶרֶב.** שֵׁם כְּלֵי מִלְחָמָה:


**like a club, a sword** Names of weapons.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:1)


**שֵׁן רָעָה.** רְצוּצָה:


**a broken tooth** Heb. רעה, broken.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:2)


**מוּעָדֶת.** כְּמוֹ ״וְלֹא מָעֲדוּ קַרְסֻלָּי״ (תהלים יח:לז):


**unsteady** Heb. מועדת, as in (Ps. 18:37): “and my ankles have not slipped (מעדו).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:19:3)


**מִבְטַח בּוֹגֵד.** מְכַזֵּב בּוֹ בְּיוֹם צָרָתוֹ כְּשֵׁן רְצוּצָה וּמוּעֶדֶת:


**trust in a traitor** He disappoints him on the day of his trouble like a broken tooth and an unsteady [foot].



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:20:1)


**מַעֲדֶה בֶּגֶד בְּיוֹם קָרָה.** ״בֶּגֶד עִדִּים״ (ישעיה סד:ה): בֶּגֶד מוּסָר, כְּלִי שֶׁהוּא רָאוּי לַהֲסִירוֹ מֵעָלָיו לְפִי שֶׁהוּא בָּלוּי; כְּדִמְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״וַיָּסַר״ (בראשית ח:יג): ״וְיַעְדֵּי״ (תרגום אונקלוס). וְזֶה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: בֶּגֶד בָּלוּי הֲרֵי הוּא בְּיוֹם קָרָה **כְּחֹמֶץ עַל נָתֶר** .**נָתֶר.** מִין אֲדָמָה רַכָּה, כְּגוֹן אֲדָמָה שֶׁלָּנוּ הַנִּקְרָא קְרִידָ״א, וְהָיוּ חוֹקְקִים אוֹתָהּ וְעוֹשִׂין כֵּלִים; וְאִם יִפֹּל בּוֹ חֹמֶץ, מְמַסְמְסוֹ וְנִשְׁחָת. אַף **שָׁר בַּשִּׁירִים עַל לֶב רָע.** דּוֹמֶה לִשְׁנֵיהֶם. וּמַה הוּא שָׁר בַּשִּׁירִים? זֶה הַמְּלַמֵּד תּוֹרָה לְתַלְמִיד רָשָׁע אֲשֶׁר אֵין בְּלִבּוֹ לְקַיְּמָהּ (חולין קלג.):


**A worn-out garment on a cold day** Heb. מעדה בגד, synonymous with (Isa. 64:5): “בגד עדים, a discarded garment,” a worn-out garment that is fit to be taken off since it is worn out, as we translate into Aramaic “ויסר” (Ex. 7:24) as ויעדי, and He shall remove. Now this is its interpretation: A worn-out garment on a cold day is like vinegar on chalk. **chalk** Heb. נתר, a kind of soft earth, like our earth that is called creide, which they would hew and make vessels therefrom, and if vinegar falls on it, [the earth] is dissolved and ruined. So... **is one who sings songs to a broken heart** [lit. on a bad heart. This one] resembles them both. Now what is one who sings songs? This is one who teaches the Torah to a wicked student, who has no intention in his heart to fulfill it.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:21:1)


**אִם רָעֵב שֹׂנַאֲךָ.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ (סוכה נב,א-ב) עַל יֵצֶר הָרָע: אִם רָעֵב הוּא וְאוֹמֵר לְךָ לְהַשְׂבִּיעוֹ בַּעֲבֵרוֹת, מְשׁוֹךְ אֶת עַצְמְךָ לְבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ **וְהַאֲכִילֵהוּ** מִלַּחְמָהּ שֶׁל תּוֹרָה, וְכֵן **הַשְׁקֵהוּ מָיִם.** מִימֶיהָ שֶׁל תּוֹרָה:


**If your enemy is hungry** [to be explained] according to its apparent meaning. Our Rabbis, however, explained it as referring to the evil inclination. If he is hungry and tells you to sate him with sins, draw yourself into the study hall and feed him the bread of Torah, and likewise, give him the water of Torah to drink.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:1)


**כִּי גֶחָלִים.** הֵם לוֹ שֶׁ**אַתָּה חֹתֶה** וְשׁוֹאֵב מִן הַמְּדוּרָה לָתֵת עַל רֹאשׁוֹ:


**for...coals** they are to him, that you are scooping from the fire to heap upon his head.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:2)


**חוֹתֶה.** כָּל שְׁאִיבַת גֶּחָלִים מִתּוֹךְ הַהֶסֵּק קָרֵי חוֹתֶה, כְּדַאֲמַר ״לַחְתּוֹת אֵשׁ מִיָּקוּד״ (ישעיהו ל:יד):


**scooping** Heb. חותה. Any scooping of coals from a fire is called חותה, as it is stated (Isa. 30:14): “to scoop (לחתות) fire from a hearth.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:22:3)


**וַה׳ יְשַׁלֶּם לָךְ.** יַשְׁלִימֶנּוּ עִמָּךְ, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְגַּבֵּר עָלֶיךָ:


**will reward you** Heb. ישלם לך, will cause him to make peace with you, that he should not overpower you.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:23:1)


**רוּחַ צָפוֹן תְּחוֹלֵל גָּשֶׁם.** תּוֹלִיד וְתִבְרָא אֶת הַגֶּשֶׁם:


**As the north wind begets rain** It begets and creates the rain.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:23:2)
