**וּפָנִים נִזְעָמִים תְּחוֹלֵל לְשׁוֹן סֵתֶר.** לָשׁוֹן הָרָע גּוֹרֵם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ פְּנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא נִזְעָמִים. וְכֵן דִּמְיוֹן פִּתְרוֹן הַמִּקְרָא: **רוּחַ צָפוֹן** עֲשׂוּיָה לְ**חוֹלֵל גָּשֶׁם<b>, וְ<b>לְשׁוֹן סָתֶר** עֲשׂוּיָה **לְפָנִים נִזְעָמִים**:


**an angry countenance** A backbiting tongue begets [an angry countenance]. Slander causes the countenance of the Holy One, blessed be He, to be angry, and this is the comparison [of the interpretation] of the verse. [The bracketed words do not appear in the Salonica edition, which seems more correct than our edition.] The north wind is accustomed to beget rain and a backbiting tongue is accustomed to [beget] an angry countenance.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:1)


**טוֹב שֶׁבֶת עַל פִּנַּת גָּג.** עַל סִלּוּק הַשְּׁכִינָה נֶאֱמַר (איכה רבה פתיחתא כה):


**It is better to sit on the corner of a roof** This is stated concerning the withdrawal of the Shechinah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:2)


**מֵאֵשֶׁת מְדִינִים.** כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהִרְשִׁיעוּ אֶת מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם וְהִקְנִיטוּ לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**than a quarrelsome wife** The nation of lsrael, who dealt wickedly and provoked the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:24:3)


**וּבֵית חָבֶר.** בַּיִת שֶׁחִבְּרוּ בּוֹ גִּלּוּלִים לַשְּׁכִינָה:


**and a house of companionship** A house in which they associated idolatry with the Shechinah.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:25:1)


**עַל נֶפֶשׁ עֲיֵפָה.** עֲשׂוּיָה לְהָשִׁיב נֶפֶשׁ עֲיֵפָה:


**to a faint soul** It [cold water] is accustomed to revive a faint soul.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:25:2)


**וּשְׁמוּעָה טוֹבָה.** אַף הִיא שָׁוָה לָהֶם, וְכֵן יַעֲקֹב: ״וַתְּחִי רוּחַ יַעֲקֹב אֲבִיהֶם״ (בראשית מה:כז):


**so is good news** That too is equal to it, and so was it with Jacob (Gen. 45:27): “And the spirit of Jacob their father was revived.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:26:1)


**מַעְיָן נִרְפָּשׂ.** עָכוּר בָּרַגְלַיִם:


**a muddied spring** Muddied by the feet.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:26:2)


**צַדִּיק מָט לִפְנֵי רָשָׁע.** כְּשֶׁהַצַּדִּיק מָט לִפְנֵי הָרָשָׁע וְיָרֵא לְהוֹכִיחַ עַל פָּנָיו דַּרְכּוֹ, שָׂנאוּי הַדָּבָר **כְּמַעְיָן נִרְפָּשׂ וּמָקוֹר** נִשְׁחָת:


**A righteous man slipping before a wicked man** When a righteous man slips before a wicked man and is afraid to reprove him for his way to his face, the matter is hateful like a muddied spring and a ruined fountain.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:27:1)


**אָכוֹל דְּבַשׁ.** לֶאֱכוֹל דְּבַשׁ יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי. רֶמֶז הַדָּבָר לְדוֹרֵשׁ בְּמַעֲשֵׂה מֶרְכָּבָה (יחזקאל א) וּבְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית (בראשית א) לְגַלּוֹת לָרַבִּים, וְעַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ מְלַגְלְגִים עַל הַדְּבָרִים, וְשׁוֹאֲלִים מַה לְּמַעְלָה מַה לְּמַטָּה:


**Eating honey** To eat honey to excess. The topic symbolizes one who expounds on the account of the Merkavah and the account of the Creation to the public; the ignoramuses will ridicule the words and ask what is above and what is below.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 25:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/25:27:2)


**וְחֵקֶר כְּבוֹדָם כָּבוֹד.** וְהֵיכָן הַחֵקֶר רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת? בְּדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים, אֲשֶׁר כְּבוֹדָם כָּבוֹד בִּגְזֵרוֹתֵיהֶם, יֵשׁ לִשְׁאוֹל מָה טַעַם גָּזְרוּ כָּךְ, וְלָמָּה סִיְּגוּ בְּכָל גְּזֵרָה וּגְזֵרָה:


**but fathoming their honor is honor** But when is fathoming appropriate? In the words of the Sages, whose honor is honor: We may ask why they decreed such and such a decree, and why they enacted a safeguard in each decree.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:1:1)


**כַּשֶּׁלֶג בַּקַּיִץ.** בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁשּׁוֹטְחִין הַתְּאֵנִים בַּחַמָּה לְיַבְּשָׁן וְלַעֲשׂוֹת קְצִיעוֹת, כְּדַאֲמַר ״הַלֶּחֶם וְהַקַּיִץ לֶאֱכֹל הַנְּעָרִים״ (שמואל ב טז:ב):


**Like snow in the summer** when they spread the figs in the sun to dry them and to make fig cakes, as is stated (II Sam. 16:2): “the bread and the dried figs for the young men to eat.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:1)


**כַּצִּפּוֹר לָנוּד.** שֶׁהוּא נָד, וְ**כַדְּרוֹר** שֶׁהוּא חוֹזֵר לְקִנּוֹ:


**Like a wandering sparrow** that wanders and like a swallow that returns to its nest, so...


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:2)


**כֵּן קִלְלַת חִנָּם לֹא תָבוֹא.** לְמִי שֶׁהוֹצִיאָהּ בְּפִיו:


**will a vain curse come home** to the one who uttered it with his mouth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:2:3)


**דְּרוֹר.** הוּא עוֹף הַנִּקְרָא רונדל״ע בְּלַעַ״ז, וְנִקְרָא דְּרוֹר – שֶׁדָּרָה בַּבַּיִת כְּבַשָּׂדֶה:


**swallow Heb.** דרור. This is the bird known as arondele in Old French, [hirondelle in modern French, schwalbe in German,] which is called דרור, because it lives (דר) in a house as in a field.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:3:1)


**שׁוֹט לַסּוּס.** הוּא עָשׂוּי, וְאַף שֵׁבֶט נָכוֹן **לְגֵו כְּסִילִים.** יִסּוּרִין מוּכָנִין לָרָשָׁע (שמות רבה ט:ח):


**A whip is for the horse** [It] is made for the horse, and also a rod is prepared for the body of fools. Tortures are prepared for the wicked.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:4:1)


**אַל תַּעַן כְּסִיל.** בְּדִבְרֵי רִיב וּמַצָּה, **פֶּן תִּשְׁוֶה לּוֹ**:


**Do not answer a fool** with words of quarrel and contention lest you become like him.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:5:1)


**עֲנֵה כְסִיל.** הַבָּא לַהֲסִיתְךָ לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, הוֹדַע לוֹ אִוַּלְתּוֹ:


**Answer a fool** who comes to win you over to idolatry; let him know his folly.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:5:2)


**פֶּן יִהְיֶה חָכָם בְּעֵינָיו.** וְטַעַם שְׁנֵי פְסוּקִים אֵלּוּ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּתוֹכָם: **אַל תַּעַן** בְּדָבָר שֶׁתִּשְׁוֶה לוֹ אִם תַּעֲנֵהוּ, **עֲנֵה כְסִיל** בְּדָבָר שֶׁאִם לֹא תַעֲנֵהוּ יִהְיֶה חָכָם בְּעֵינָיו:


**lest he be wise in his sight** The meaning of these two verses is explained in [the verses] themselves: **Do not answer** in a matter in which you will become like him if you answer him. **Answer a fool** in a matter in which he will be wise in his sight if you do not answer him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:6:1)


**מְקַצֶּה רַגְלַיִם חָמָס שׁוֹתֶה.** מִי שֶׁשּׁוֹלֵחַ דְּבָרִים בְּיַד כְּסִיל הוּא מְקַצֶּה רַגְלֵי שְׁלוּחִים הַרְבֵּה, לַחֲזוֹר וְלִשְׁלֹחַ לְתַקֵּן מַה שֶּׁעִוֵּת הָרִאשׁוֹן, אֲשֶׁר שְׁלָחוֹ בַּתְּחִלָּה; **וְשׁוֹתֶה חָמָס.** שֶׁחֲבֵרוֹ זוֹעֵף עָלָיו עַל שְׁלִיחוּת הַכְּסִיל:


**wears out legs and “drinks violence”** He who sends a message by the hand of a fool wears out the legs of many messengers by repetitively sending [them] to rectify what the first one, whom he sent at the beginning, distorted. And he “drinks violence,” for his fellow is wroth with him for sending a fool.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:7:1)


**דַּלְיוּ שׁוֹקַיִם מִפִּסֵּחַ.** גָּבְהוּ, כְּמוֹ ״דָּלּוּ עֵינַי״ (ישעיהו לח:יד); הַשּׁוֹקַיִם שֶׁל כָּל אָדָם נִרְאִין לַפִּסֵּחַ גְּבוֹהִים מִמֶּנּוּ. וְדָבָר זֶה מָשָׁל בְּפִי כְסִיל, הָאוֹמֵר אוֹתוֹ עַל לִמּוּד הַחָכְמָה: אֵיךְ אָנוּ בָּאִים לִלְמֹד הַחָכְמָה? נִפְלָאָה וּגְבוֹהָה הִיא מִמֶּנּוּ! הַמָּשָׁל אוֹמֵר: **דַּלְיוּ שׁוֹקַיִם מִפִּסֵּחַ**:


**thighs seem raised to a lame man** Heb. דליו, as in (Isa. 38:14): “My eyes were lifted (דלו).” Every man’s thighs appear to the lame to be higher than he. This phrase is a parable in the mouth of the fool, who says it in reference to the study of wisdom, “How can we come to study wisdom? It is hidden and raised up higher than I am. The parable states, ‘Thighs seem raised to a lame man.’”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:8:1)


**בְּמַרְגֵּמָה.** פְרוֹנְדוֹלָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, שֶׁאֶבֶן שֶׁצּוֹרְרִין בּוֹ לֹא לְהִתְקַיֵּם הִיא שָׁם, שֶׁעוֹמֶדֶת לְהִזָּרֵק. כָּךְ הַנּוֹתֵן לִכְסִיל כָּבוֹד אֵינוֹ שֶׁל קַיָּמָא. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה דָּרְשׁוּ (חולין קלג.) עַל הַמְּלַמֵּד תּוֹרָה לְתַלְמִיד שֶׁאֵינוֹ הָגוּן, שֶׁהוּא כְּזוֹרֵק אֶבֶן לְמַרְקוּלִיס:


**in a slingshot** Heb. במרגמה fronde in French, Schleuder in German, in which the stone tied will not remain, for it is destined to be thrown, so is it with one who gives honor to a fool—it is not permanent. And our Sages (Hullin 133a) explained it homiletically as referring to one who teaches Torah to an unfitting student, that he is tantamount to casting a stone into a merculis.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:9:1)


**חוֹחַ עָלָה בְיַד שִׁכּוֹר.** כְּחוֹחַ שֶׁהוּא נִדְבָּק בְּיַד שִׁכּוֹר, כֵּן הַמָּשָׁל הָאָמוּר לְמַטָּה (משלי כו:י) נִדְבָּק בְּפִי הַכְּסִיל לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם לְחוֹחַ וּלְקוֹץ מַכְאִיב. וּמַהוּ הַמָּשָׁל?


**A thorn came up in a drunkard’s hand** Just like a thorn that is stuck to a drunkard’s hand, so is the parable stated below stuck in the mouth of a fool to be for him as a thorn—yea, a painful thorn. Now what is the parable?...



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:10:1)


**רַב מְחוֹלֵל כֹּל.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בָּרָא אֶת הַכֹּל וְדָן אֶת הַכֹּל; כַּכְּסִיל כֶּחָכָם, אֵין אָנוּ צְרִיכִין לְשׁוּם חָכְמָה:


**The Master created all** The Holy One, blessed be He, created all and sustains all, the fool like the wise man. We do not need any wisdom.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:10:2)


**וְשׂוֹכֵר כְּסִיל.** לֹא כְּבָשָׂר וָדָם שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׂכֹּר אֶלָּא פּוֹעֲלִים בְּקִיאִים וְלֹא יִשְׂכֹּר אֶלָּא הָעוֹסְקִים בִּמְלַאכְתּוֹ, אֲבָל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא **מְחוֹלֵל כֹּל וְשׂוֹכֵר הַכְּסִילִים וְשׂוֹכֵר** עוֹבֵר דְּרָכִים הַבַּטְלָנִים מִכָּל מְלָאכָה. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: **וְשׂוֹכֵר כְּסִיל** מִלְּשׁוֹן ״וַיִּסָּכְרוּ מַעְיְנוֹת תְּהוֹם״ (בראשית ח:ב), וּלְשׁוֹן ״עֹשֵׂי שֶׂכֶר״ (ישעיהו יט:י): הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא סוֹכֵר מַזַּל כְּסִיל הַמְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּימוֹת הַחַמָּה מִתִּשְׁרֵי וְאֵילָךְ, וּמֵאָז הוּא סוֹכֵר וְסוֹגֵר כָּל עוֹבְרֵי הַיָּם מִלֵּילֵךְ בּוֹ עַד הַפֶּסַח. וּבְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי מֹשֶׁה רָאִיתִי: **רַב מְחוֹלֵל כֹּל** עָשִׁיר יֵשׁ לוֹ פְּעֻלּוֹת הַרְבֵּה, וְאִם **שׂוֹכֵר כְּסִיל** בִּמְלַאכְתּוֹ הִנֵּה הוּא כְּשׂוֹכֵר כָּל עוֹבְרֵי דֶרֶךְ שֶׁרוֹאִין בְּקִלְקוּל הַמְּלָאכָה, לְהוֹרוֹת אֵיךְ יִתָּכֵן וְאֵיךְ יֵשׁ לוֹ לִפְעֹל; וְדָבָר רֵיק הוּא, וְאֵינוֹ עִנְיָן כָּאן:


**and He hires a fool** Unlike a mortal, who hires only expert workers, and who hires only those engaged in his work, the Holy One, blessed be He, creates all and hires the fools and hires wayfarers, who are idle from all work. [According to] Midrash Aggadah, ושכר כסיל is from an expression of (Gen. 8:2) “And the fountains of the deep were closed (ויסכרו),” and an expression of (Isa. 19:10) “who make dams (שכר).” The Holy One, blessed be He, closes up the constellation of Orion (כסיל), which serves in the summer from Tishri on, and from then on, He closes off all who traverse the sea from going into it until Passover. And in the words of Rabbi Moses, I saw: רב מחולל כל, A wealthy man has many activities, and if he hires a fool, it is as though he is hiring all wayfarers who witness the deterioration of the work, to instruct how to rectify it and how he should work. This is, however, an empty thing and has no connection here.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:15:1)


**בַּצַּלָּחַת.** בְּיוֹרָה חַמָּה נוֹתֵן יָדוֹ, מִפְּנֵי הַקֹּר:


**in the cauldron** He buries his hand in a hot cauldron because of the cold.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:16:1)


**מְשִׁיבֵי טָעַם.** חֲכָמִים:


**who give advice** wise men.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:17:1)


**מַחֲזִיק בְּאָזְנֵי כָלֶב.** הָעוֹבֵר לְהִתְעַבֵּר עַל רִיב לֹא לוֹ הֲרֵי הוּא כְּאוֹחֵז **בְּאָזְנֵי כָּלֶב**, הַגּוֹרֵם שֶׁיִּשְּׁכֶנּוּ עַל חִנָּם:


**one who grabs a dog by its ears** One who passes by and becomes embroiled in a quarrel that is not his is like one who grabs a dog by his ears, who causes him to bite him for no reason.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:18:1)


**כְּמִתְלַהְלֵהַּ.** כְּמִתְיַגֵּעַ לִירוֹת זִיקִים שֶׁל אֵשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״וּבְזִיקוֹת בְּעַרְתֶּם״ (ישעיה נ:יא), מִלְּשׁוֹן ׳זִיקוּקִים דִּינוּר׳ (ברכות נח:). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **זִיקִים.** ׳פְרוֹנְזוֹלָ״שׁ׳, מִלְּשׁוֹן ״אַבְנֵי קָלַע״ (זכריה ט:טו), וְכֵן בַּגְּמָרָא (בבא מציעא צד.): זִיקְתָא פְּסִיק לָן; וְיוֹרֶה **חִצִּים וָמָוֶת**:


**Like one who wearies himself** Like one who wearies himself shooting sparks of fire, as in (Isa. 50: 11): “and in the flames (ובזיקות) you have kindled”; from the expression זיקוקים דינור (Ber. 58b), flames of fire. Another explanation. זקים, frondeles in Old French, slings, from an expression of slingstones (Zech. 9:15), and so in the Talmud (Baba Mezia 94a) זיקתא פסוק לן, “slingers are assigned to us.” He shoots arrows and death.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:19:1)


**כֵּן אִישׁ רִמָּה.** מְפַתֶּה וּמֵסִית אֶת חֲבֵירוֹ מִדַּרְכֵי חַיִּים לְדַרְכֵי מָוֶת, וּכְשֶׁחֲבֵירוֹ מַרְגִּישׁ שֶׁהוּא מַתְעֶה אוֹתוֹ, אוֹמֵר: מְצַחֵק אָנִי (בראשית רבה נג,יא):


**so is a man who deceives** He wins over and entices his friend from ways of life to ways of death, and when his friend realizes that he is misleading him, he says, “I am joking.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:20:1)


**בְּאֶפֶס עֵצִים.** כְּלוֹמַר שְׁנֵי דְבָרִים אֵלּוּ שָׁוִין: כְּשֵׁם שֶׁ**בְּאֶפֶס עֵצִים תִּכְבֶּה אֵשׁ**, כָּךְ **בְּאֵין נִרְגָּן** שֶׁל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע הַמְסַכְסֵךְ בַּעֲלֵי רִיב, **יִשְׁתֹּק מָדוֹן**:


**without wood** That is to say: These two things are analogous; just as without wood the fire goes out, so without a grumbler, who slanders and incites quarrelers, strife quiets down.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:21:1)


**פֶּחָם לְגֶחָלִים וְעֵצִים לְאֵשׁ.** פֶּחָם שֶׁל אֵשׁ עָשׂוּי לְהַבְעִיר גֶּחָלִים עֲמוּמוֹת וְעֵצִים עֲשׂוּיִן לְהַסִּיק אֵשׁ, **וְאִישׁ מָדוֹן לְחַרְחַר רִיב**:


**As charcoal is for coals and wood is for fire** Charcoal on fire is capable of igniting dying embers, and wood is capable of kindling fire, and a quarrelsome man of kindling strife.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:22:1)


**כְּמִתְלַהֲמִים.** לְשׁוֹן מִתְלַחֲמִים. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה פֵּרְשׁוּ (ספרי דברים כד) כְּמִתְלַהֲמִים: כְּמֵת לָהֶם. דִּבְרֵי הַמְרַגְּלִים הָיוּ לָהֶם לְמִיתָה:


**as though waging battle** Heb. כמתלהמים, an expression of מתלחמים. Our Sages however, explained כמתלהמים, כמתלהם, like death to them. The words of the spies became their death.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:22:2)


**חַדְרֵי בָטֶן.** הִיא מִיתַת הַדְּרוֹקָן:


**the innermost parts of the body** lit. they go down into the chambers of the stomach. This is the death of dropsy.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:1)


**מְצֻפֶּה עַל חָרֶשׂ.** כְּסִיגֵי כֶסֶף הַמְּדֻבָּקִים עַל הַחֶרֶשׂ שֶׁצּוֹרְפִין אוֹתוֹ בּוֹ, וְהוּא מַבְהִיק כְּאִלּוּ הוּא כֶּסֶף, וְאֵין בּוֹ תּוֹעֶלֶת, כֵּן **שְׂפָתַיִם דֹּלְקִים וְלֶב רָע.** הָרוֹדְפִים אַחַר הַבְּרִיּוֹת לַהֲסִיתָם בַּחֲלָקוֹת וּמְדַבְּרִים אֶחָד בַּפֶּה וְאֶחָד בַּלֵּב:


**overlaid on earthenware** Like silver dross attached to the earthenware in which it is refined, making the utensil shine as though it were silver although it has no use, so are burning lips and wicked heart, which pursue people to entice them with smooth and hypocritical talk.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:2)


**וְלֵב רָע.** נִרְאִין כְּאוֹהֲבִין וְהֵם אוֹיְבִים:


**and a wicked heart** They appear to be friends, but they are enemies.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:23:3)


**דּוֹלְקִים.** כְּמוֹ ״דָּלַקְתָּ אַחֲרָי״ (בראשית לא:לו):


**burning** Heb. דולקים, as in “you pursued (דלקת) me so hotly” (Gen. 31:36).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:24:1)


**יִנָּכֵר שׂוֹנֵא.** בְּדִבּוּרוֹ מִתְנַכֵּר הַשּׂוֹנֵא, שֶׁלֹּא יַכִּירוּ שֶׁהוּא שׂוֹנֵא. דִישְׁקְנוֹיִישְׁ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**An enemy dissembles** With his speech, an enemy dissembles, so that it is not recognizable that he is an enemy. ינכר, deconnoitre in French, [verkennen in German, and so did Redak write, that it is an expression of denial and making oneself strange, the opposite of recognition (הכרה)].



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:26:1)


**תְּכֻסֶּה שִׂנְאָה.** הָעוֹשֶׂה מַעֲשָׂיו בַּמַּחְשָׁךְ וּמְכַסֶּה בְּמַשָּׁאוֹן, שֶׁהוּא חֹשֶׁךְ (ס״א: שׁוֹאַת חֹשֶׁךְ), אֶת הַדָּבָר הַשָּׂנאוּי לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, סוֹף שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא **מְגַלֶּה רָעָתוֹ בְקָהָל**, שֶׁיַּכִּירוּ בּוֹ שֶׁהוּא רָשָׁע (סוטה ט.):


**When the hatred is covered with darkness** If one performs his deed in the darkness and conceals with משאון, which is pitch darkness (שואת חשך) the thing that is hated by the Holy One, blessed be He, [then] ultimately the Holy One, blessed be He, will reveal his evil in public, so that they should recognize that he is wicked.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:1)


**כּוֹרֶה שַׁחַת.** כְּגוֹן בִּלְעָם, שֶׁהִשִּׂיא עֵצָה לְבָלָק עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְהִפִּיל מֵהֶם עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה אֶלֶף (במדבר כה:ט), וּבָא לְמִדְיָן לִתְבּוֹעַ שְׂכָרוֹ וְנֶהֱרַג בְּיַד יִשְׂרָאֵל (במדבר לא:ח):


**He who digs a pit** For example, Balaam, who persuaded Balak to cause Israel to sin and caused twenty-four thousand of them to fall. He came to Midian to demand his pay and was slain by Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:2)


**וְגוֹלֵל.** כְּלוֹמַר: סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל הַמַּעֲמִיד מִכְשׁוֹל, שֶׁיִּכָּשֵׁל בּוֹ:


**and he who rolls** That is to say that whoever places a stumbling block will ultimately stumble on it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:27:3)


**וְגוֹלֵל אֶבֶן.** מְגַלְּלוֹ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם, שֶׁיִּכָּשְׁלוּ בּוֹ בְּנֵי אָדָם. וְאַגָּדָה פּוֹתְרוֹ (תנחומא בובר, וירא כח) בַּאֲבִימֶלֶךְ שֶׁהָרַג שִׁבְעִים אֶחָיו עַל אֶבֶן אַחַת, וְסוֹף מֵת בָּאֶבֶן, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַתַּשְׁלֵךְ אִשָּׁה אַחַת פֶּלַח רֶכֶב וַתָּרִץ גֻּלְגָּלְתּוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ (שופטים ט:נג):


**and he who rolls a stone** He rolls it from place to place so that people will stumble on it. The Aggadah (Tanhuma Buber, Vayera 28, see footnote 157) interprets it in reference to Abimelech, who slew his seventy brothers on one stone and his end was that he died by a stone, as it is said (Jud. 9:53): “And a certain woman cast a piece of an upper millstone upon Abimelech’s head and crushed his skull.”



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:1)


**יִשְׂנָא דַכָּיו.** מִי שֶׁמְּקַבֵּל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע שׂוֹנֵא אֶת הַנִּדְכָּאִים תַּחְתָּיו, שֶׁכֵּן עַל לָשׁוֹן הָרָע רָדַף שָׁאוּל לְדָוִד וְהָרַג נוֹב (שמואל א כב:יח-יט):


**hates those crushed under him** He who accepts slander hates those crushed under him, for Saul pursued David because of slander and slew Nob.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:2)


**וּפֶה חָלָק.** פֶּה שֶׁל חֲלַקְלַקּוּת:


**and a smooth mouth** A mouth of flattery.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 26:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/26:28:3)


**יַעֲשֶׂה מִדְחֶה.** עוֹשֶׂה דִּיחוּי, שֶׁדּוֹחֶה אֶת מְקַבְּלוֹ מֵעַל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**effects rejection** It makes him rejected, for it rejects the one who accepts it from upon the Holy One, blessed be He.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:1:1)


**אַל תִּתְהַלֵּל.** אַל תִּתְפָּאֵר עַכְשָׁיו בְּדָבָר שֶׁעָתִיד לִהְיוֹת לְ**יוֹם מָחָר**:


**Do not boast** Do not boast now concerning a thing that is destined to be tomorrow.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:1:2)


**כִּי לֹא תֵדַע מַה יֵּלֶד יוֹם.** שֶׁמָּא הַיּוֹם יִוָּלֵד שׁוּם רָעָה, שֶׁיְּבַטֵּל מַחְשְׁבוֹת מָחָר:


**for you do not know what the day will bear** Perhaps the day will bear some evil that will nullify it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:3:1)


**וְנֵטֶל.** מַשָּׂא:


**and the burden** Heb. ונטל, a burden.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:3:2)


**וְכַעַס אֱוִיל.** שֶׁהָאֱוִיל מַכְעִיס לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, גּוֹרֵם לְהָבִיא כַּעַס לָעוֹלָם:


**the anger of a fool** that a fool angers the Holy One, blessed be He, causes [Him] to bring anger to the world.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:4:1)


**וְשֶׁטֶף אַף, וּמִי יַעֲמֹד.** לִפְנֵי קִנְאָתוֹ שֶׁל קַנָּא וְנַקָּם:


**but who can stand up before jealousy** Who can stand up before the jealousy of He Who is jealous and vengeful?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:6:1)


**וְנַעְתָּרוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן גֹּדֶל, כְּמוֹ ״הַעְתַּרְתֶּם עָלַי״ (יחזקאל לה:יג):


**are burdensome** Heb. נעתרות, an expression of largeness, as in (Ezek. 35: 13): “You have multiplied (נעתרות) against Me.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:7:1)


**תָּבוּס נֹפֶת.** תִּרְמוֹס בְּרֶגֶל, כְּמוֹ ״נָבוּס קָמֵינוּ״ (תהלים מד:ו):


**tramples honeycomb** Heb. תבוס, tramples by foot, as in (Ps. 44:6): “we will trample (נבוס) those who rise up against us.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:7:2)


**כָּל־מַר מָתוֹק.** כָּל דָּבָר מַר, מָתוֹק לָהּ. וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בְּתַלְמוּד תּוֹרָה: הַמַּרְאֶה אֶת עַצְמוֹ כְּשָׂבֵעַ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְאַוֶּה לְדִבְרֵי תוֹרָה לְתַאֲוַת נֶפֶשׁ, **תָּבוּס נֹפֶת.** אַף הַטְּעָמִים הַמְיֻשָּׁבִים עַל הַלֵּב אֵינָם חֲשׁוּבִים עָלָיו; וְהַמִּתְאַוֶּה לָהּ, אֲפִלּוּ דְּבָרִים הַבָּאִים לוֹ בִּמְרִירוּת וּבִיגִיעָה הֵם מְתוּקִים לוֹ:


**all bitter is sweet** Every sweet thing is bitter to it. This can be interpreted in regard to the study of the Torah. If one shows himself to be sated, that he has no desire for words of the Torah as a desire of his soul, he tramples honeycomb. [That is,] even the reasons that are acceptable to the heart are not important to him, but he who yearns for it, even the things that come to him with bitterness and toil are sweet to him.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:8:1)


**נוֹדֶדֶת מִן קִנָּהּ.** שֶׁהוֹלֶכֶת וּמִטַּלְטֶלֶת, כֵּן הָאִישׁ **הַנּוֹדֵד מִמְּקוֹמוֹ.** תַּלְמִיד חָכָם הַנּוֹדֵד מִן הַלִּמּוּד, מִלַּחֲזֹר עַל גִּרְסָתוֹ:


**wandering from its nest** that goes and wanders, so is a man who wanders from his place, i.e., a Torah scholar who wanders away from his studies, from reviewing what he learned.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:9:1)


**שֶׁמֶן וּקְטֹרֶת.** רֵיחַ שֶׁמֶן אֲפַרְסְמוֹן וְרֵיחַ קְטֹרֶת מְשַׂמְּחִין לֵב:


**Oil and incense** The scent of balsamum oil and the scent of incense cause the heart to rejoice.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:9:2)


**וּמֶתֶק רֵעֵהוּ מֵעֲצַת נָפֶשׁ.** מִי שֶׁחֲבֵרוֹ מְקָרְבוֹ וּמְמַתֵּק לוֹ בִּדְבָרָיו, הוּא טוֹב מִמַּה שֶׁנַּפְשׁוֹ יוֹעַצְתּוֹ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וּמֶתֶק רֵעֵהוּ** הַמַּכְשִׁיר מַעֲשָׂיו, שֶׁהֵן מְתוּקִין לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, טוֹב לוֹ מִמַּה שֶׁהוּא מְמַלֵּא תַּאֲוַת לִבּוֹ:


**and the sweet words of his friend more than one’s own counsel** He whose friend draws him near with words is better than what his [own] soul advises him. Another explanation: **and the sweetness of his friend** who improves his deeds, that they should be sweet to the Holy One, blessed be He, is better for him than gratifying the desires of his heart.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:1)


**רֵעֲךָ.** וְרֵעַ אָבִיךָ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁנִּקְרָא רֵעַ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְרֵעַ אָבִיךָ שֶׁחִיבֵּב אֶת אֲבוֹתֶיךָ:


**your friend and your father’s friend** The Holy One, blessed be He, Who is called a friend to Israel, and your father’s friend, for He endeared your forefathers.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:2)


**אַל תַּעֲזֹב.** אִם עָזַבְתָּ, יָבֹא עָלֶיךָ פֻּרְעָנוּת:


**Do not forsake** And, if you do forsake Him, retribution will befall you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:3)


**וּבֵית אָחִיךָ.** אַל תִּבְטַח בִּבְנֵי עֵשָׂו וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל שֶׁיְּקָרְבוּךָ. מָצִינוּ (תנחומא, יתרו ה): כְּשֶׁגָּלוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְבָבֶל הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים לְמוֹלִיכֵיהֶם בְּקוֹלָר: בְּבַקָּשָׁה מִכֶּם, הוֹלִיכוּנוּ בְּדֶרֶךְ אַחֵינוּ בְּנֵי עֵשָׂו וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל, וְהָיוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׁמָעֵאל יוֹצְאִים לִקְרָאתָם וּמְקַדְּמִין אוֹתָם בְּמִינֵי מְלוּחִים וְנוֹדוֹת נְפוּחִים:


**and...your brother’s house** Do not rely on the children of Esau and Ishmael that they should befriend you. We find that when Israel was exiled to Babylon, they would say to those who led them in neck irons, “We beg of you, lead us on the way of our brethren, the sons of Esau and Ishmael,” and the sons of Esau went out toward them and welcomed them with various kinds of salty foods and blown up flasks.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:10:4)


**מֵאָח רָחוֹק.** טוֹב שֶׁיִּשְׁכּוֹן בֵּינֵיכֶם הַקָּרוֹב לְקוֹרְאָיו (תהלים קמה:יח), מִשֶּׁתָּבוֹאוּ אֵצֶל אָח שֶׁנִּתְרַחֵק, לֵאמֹר ״יִקְרְבוּ יְמֵי אֵבֶל אָבִי וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית כז:מא):


**than a distant brother** It is better that the One Who is near to those who call Him dwell among you than that you should come to your brother who distanced himself by saying (Gen. 27:41): “When the days of mourning for my father are at hand, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:11:1)


**חֲכַם בְּנִי.** הִתְחַכֵּם בְּנִי:


**Be wise, my son** Heb. חכם, become wise, my son.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:11:2)


**וְשַׂמַּח לִבִּי.** וּתְהֵא לִבִּי שָׂמֵחַ בְּךָ:


**and cause my heart to rejoice** and let my heart be happy with you.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:12:1)


**עָרוּם רָאָה רָעָה.** רוֹאֶה פֻּרְעָנוּת הַבָּאָה עַל הָאָרֶץ וְנִסְתָּר הֵימֶנָּה, שֶׁמָּשַׁךְ יָדוֹ מִן הָעֲבֵירָה. וּפְתָאיִם לֹא נִסְתְּרוּ אֶלָּא **עָבְרוּ** בְּדֶרֶךְ רָעָה:


**A cunning man saw harm** He sees the retribution coming upon the earth and hides from it by withdrawing from sin, but the fools did not hide and passed on the way of harm.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:12:2)


**וְנֶעֱנָשׁוּ.** וְנִפְסְדוּ:


**and were punished** They suffered loss.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:13:1)


**כִּי עָרַב זָר.** אָדָם שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה עָרֵב, גּוֹרֵם שֶׁאָמַר הַדַּיָּן לַמַּלְוֶה: **קַח בִּגְדוֹ**! (בבא מציעא קטו.):


**because he stood surety for a stranger** A man who stood surety brings about that the judge should say to the creditor, “Take his garment.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:14:1)


**מְבָרֵךְ רֵעֵהוּ.** יֵשׁ מְשַׁבֵּחַ אֶת חֲבֵירוֹ יוֹם יוֹם, וְהַבְּרָכָה נֶהֱפֶכֶת לִקְלָלָה, שֶׁאוֹמְרִים עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא וַתְרָן בְּמָמוֹנוֹ וְעָשִׁיר, וְהַכֹּל בָּאִין וְשׁוֹאֲלִין מִמֶּנּוּ, וְהַמַּלְכוּת מִתְגָּרֶת בּוֹ לִגְבּוֹת מָמוֹן. כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲרָכִין (ערכין טז.). וְעוֹד יֵשׁ בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בלק כד): כְּנֶגֶד בִּלְעָם שֶׁהָיָה מְבָרֵךְ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּקוֹל רָם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּשָּׂא מְשָׁלוֹ וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר כג:ז), לְשׁוֹן נְשִׂיאוּת קוֹל, וְסוֹפוֹ יָעַץ לְהַחֲטִיאָם:


**He who blesses his friend** Someone blesses his friend daily, and the blessing is converted into a curse, for people say about him that he is generous with his money and wealthy, and everyone comes and borrows from him, and the government provokes him to collect his money. In this manner, it is interpreted in tractate Arachin (16a). Besides this, it appears in the midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (Balak 15) as regards Balaam, who blessed Israel with a loud voice, as it is stated (Num. 23:7): “And he took up his discourse, etc.,” an expression of raising the voice, and he later counseled to cause them to sin.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:1)


**דֶּלֶף טוֹרֵד.** גֶּשֶׁם הַנּוֹטֵף מִן הַגַּג לְתוֹךְ הַבַּיִת וְטוֹרֵד אֶת בְּנֵי הַבַּיִת:


**A constant dripping** Rain that drips from the roof into the house and drives out the inhabitants of the house.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:2)


**בְּיוֹם סַגְרִיר.** יוֹם הַגֶּשֶׁם, שֶׁהַכֹּל נִסְגָּרִים בְּבָתֵּיהֶם:


**on a rainstormy day** Heb. סגריר, a rainy day, when all are shut in their houses.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:15:3)


**וְאֵשֶׁת** מְדִינִים נִשְׁתָּוָה. שְׁנֵיהֶם שָׁוִין (יבמות סג:):


**and a quarrelsome woman, are alike** They are both the same...



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:16:1)


**צוֹפְנֶיהָ צָפַן רוּחַ.** מִי שֶׁסָּבוּר לְשָׁמְרָהּ מִתְּזְנוּתֶיהָ, צְפוּנַת רוּחַ הוּא; כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִצְפֹּן הָרוּחַ, כֵּן הִיא, לֹא תִצָּפֵנֶה:


**He who guards her guards the wind** He who thinks to watch her from her lewdness guards the wind; just as it is impossible to guard the wind, so will she not be guarded.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:16:2)


**וְשֶׁמֶן יְמִינוֹ יִקְרָא.** קוֹרֵא הוּא הַצָּרַעַת לָבוֹא עָלָיו עַד שֶׁיְּגָרְשֶׁנָּה, כַּמְּצֹרָע הַמִּטַּהֵר בְּשֶׁמֶן בְּבֹהֶן יָדוֹ הַיְמָנִית (ויקרא יד:יז):


**and he calls “the oil of his right hand”** He calls “tzaraath” to come upon him until he drives her out like a “metzora,” who is purified with oil on the thumb of his right hand [as in Lev. 14:17].



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:17:1)


**יַחַד פְּנֵי רֵעֵהוּ.** יַחַד תַּלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים מְחַדְּדִין זֶה לָזֶה בַּהֲלָכָה (תענית ז.):


**sharpens the countenance of his friend** Heb. יחד, sharpens. Torah scholars sharpen each other in halachah.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:18:1)


**יֹאכַל פִּרְיָהּ.** וְכֵן שׁוֹמֵר אֲדוֹנָיו יְכֻבָּד, וְיֹאכַל פְּרִי מַעֲלָלָיו:


**shall eat its fruit** and so will one who guards his master be honored and eat the fruit of his deeds.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:19:1)


**כַּמַּיִם.** הַלָּלוּ **הַפָּנִים** שֶׁאַתָּה מַרְאֶה לְתוֹכָן, הֵן מַרְאוֹת לְךָ:


**As in water** the face that you show it, it shows you.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:19:2)


**כֵּן לֵב הָאָדָם לָאָדָם.** חֲבֵרוֹ, לְפִי מַה שֶּׁאָדָם יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁחֲבֵרוֹ אוֹהֲבוֹ, כֵּן הוּא מַרְאֶה לוֹ פָּנִים:


**is the heart of a man to a man** his friend. According to how much a man knows that his friend loves him, so he will show him his face.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:20:1)


**שְׁאוֹל וַאֲבַדָּה.** לֹא תִשְׂבַּעְנָה מִלְּקַבֵּל אֶת הָרְשָׁעִים לְתוֹכָם, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁעֵינֵי הָאָדָם רָשָׁע לֹא תִשְׂבַּעְנָה מִלָּשׁוּט אַחַר יֵצֶר הָרָע, מִלְּמַלְּאוֹת תַּאֲוָתוֹ:


**The grave and Gehinnom will not be sated** from receiving the wicked therein, just as the eyes of a wicked man will not be sated from wandering after temptation and gratifying his lust.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:21:1)


**מַצְרֵף.** כְּלִי עָשׂוּי לְצָרֵף כֶּסֶף וְהַכּוּר עָשׂוּי לִבְחוֹן זָהָב, וְאִישׁ נִצְרָף וּמִתְחַשֵּׁב **לְפִי מַהֲלָלוֹ.** עַל יְדֵי שֶׁהַבְּרִיּוֹת מְהַלְּלוֹת אוֹתוֹ בְּמַעֲשָׂיו הַטּוֹבִים, נִבְחָן לָרַבִּים, אִם טוֹב אִם רַע:


**The refining pot** A vessel made to refine silver. The furnace is made to refine gold. A man is refined and considered according to his praise; according to how people praise him for his good deeds, he is tested by the public, whether good or bad.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:1)


**הָרִיפוֹת.** חִטִּים הַנִּדּוֹכִים בַּמַּכְתֵּשׁ:


**grain** Heb. הריפות, wheat crushed in a mortar.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:2)


**מַכְתֵּשׁ.** אֲסִיתָא (חולין נב.), מוֹרְטִיוֹ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**in a mortar** Heb. במכתש, a mortar, in French mortier; Morser in German.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:22:3)


**בַּעֱלִי.** הוּא בּוּכְנָא, פּוֹלִי״ן מוֹרְטְר״וֹ בְּלַעַ״ז. עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמַּעֲלִין אוֹתוֹ וּמַכִּין בּוֹ תָּמִיד נִקְרָא עֱלִי:


**with a pestle** Heb. בעלי, a pestle, pilon in French, Stossel in German. Since they lift it up (מעלין) and beat with it, it is called עלי.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:23:1)


**פְּנֵי צֹאנֶךָ.** אַל יֵקַלּוּ בְּעֵינֶיךָ לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן בָּם תָּמִיד מַה הֵם צְרִיכִים:


**the condition of your flocks** lit. the face of your flocks. Do not think lightly of always paying attention to them to determine what they require.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:24:1)


**כִּי לֹא לְעוֹלָם חֹסֶן.** שֶׁאִם אַתָּה עָשִׁיר בְּכֶסֶף וּבְזָהָב, שֶׁמָּא לֹא יִתְקַיֵּם לְעוֹלָם. לְכָךְ אַל תְּבַזֶּה דְּבָרִים קְטַנִּים שֶׁלְּךָ:


**For riches are not forever** For, if you are rich with silver and gold, perhaps it will not last forever. Therefore, do not despise your small things.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:25:1)


**גָּלָה חָצִיר.** כְּשֶׁגָּלָה הֶחָצִיר בִּימֵי נִיסָן וְיִרְאָה הַדֶּשֶׁא וְנֶאֶסְפוּ עֲשָׂבִים לִצְמֹחַ, אָז יֵיטִיב לְךָ, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ גִּזּוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל כְּבָשִׂים לִלְבוּשֶׁךָ:


**When the hay is carried away** When the hay is carried away in the days of Nissan, the grass becomes visible, and the grasses are gathered to grow (?), then it will be beneficial to you, for you will have the fleece of the lambs for your clothing.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:26:1)


**וּמְחִיר שָׂדֶה.** יִשְׁווּ לְךָ הָעַתּוּדִים, כִּי תֹּאכַל הַבָּשָׂר וְתִמְכֹּר הָעוֹרוֹת:


**and... the worth of the field** The he-goats will be worth as much as a field because you will eat the flesh and sell the hides.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:1)


**וְדֵי.** פַּרְנָסָתְךָ:


**and enough** for your sustenance.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:2)


**לְלַחְמְךָ וּלְלֶחֶם בֵּיתֶךָ.** תִּסְתַּפֵּק בַּחֲלֵב הָעִזִּים. הֲרֵי מְלִיצָתוֹ. וְהַמָּשָׁל כֵּן הוּא: פְּנֵי צֹאנְךָ – הָרַב הַמִּתְמַנֶּה עַל הַצִּבּוּר, יִשָּׂאֵם בְּחֵיקוֹ וִינַהֲלֵם לְאַט (ישעיהו מ:יא), כִּי לֹא לְעוֹלָם חֹסֶן, וּבָזֹאת יֹאכַל פֵּרוֹת, וְהַקֶּרֶן קַיֶּמֶת:


**For your food and the food of your household;** you will have sufficient goat milk. This is the figure. The allegory is: **the condition of your flocks** The teacher who is appointed over the congregation is required to carry them in his bosom and to lead them slowly. **For riches are not forever** and thereby, he eats the fruit, and the principal remains.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 27:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/27:27:3)


**גָּלָה חָצִיר.** כְּשֶׁיִּתְפַּשְּׁטוּ שְׁמוּעוֹתָיו וְתִגְדַּל הַתּוֹרָה עַל יָדוֹ, אָז יִהְיוּ הַכְּבָשִׂים הָאֵלֶּה לְבוּשׁוֹ, יִהְיוּ הַתַּלְמִידִים לוֹ לְשֵׁם וּלְבוּשׁ הוֹד וְהָדָר:


**When the hay is carried away** When his laws are disseminated and the Torah is magnified through him, then these lambs will be his clothing, and the disciples shall be to him for a name and for raiment of glory and beauty.



## Chapter 28


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:1:1)


**נָסוּ וְאֵין רוֹדֵף רָשָׁע.** נָסוּ הָרְשָׁעִים כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא אֵידָם וְיִפְּלוּ בְּדָבָר קַל מֵאֵין רוֹדֵף:


**The wicked flee without a pursuer** The wicked flee when their misfortune comes, and they fall on an insignificant thing without a pursuer.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:1:2)


**וְצַדִּיקִים.** אַמִּיץ לִבָּם (עמוס ב:טז) בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא **כִּכְפִיר** אֲשֶׁר יִבְטַח בִּגְבוּרָתוֹ:


**but the righteous** are courageous [through their trust] in the Holy One, blessed be He, **as a young lion** which trusts its might.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:2:1)


**בְּפֶשַׁע אֶרֶץ רַבִּים שָׂרֶיהָ.** שֶׁזֶּהוּ פֻּרְעָנוּת לָאָרֶץ, בִּהְיוֹת שָׂרֶיהָ רַבִּים הָרוֹדְפִים רַק אַחַר בִּצְעָם:


**Because of the sin of a land, its princes are many** This is the punishment of a land, viz. that it has many princes, and they do not respond to any man.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:2:2)


**וּבְאָדָם מֵבִין.** וּבִשְׁבִיל אָדָם מֵבִין יִתְאָרֵךְ הַפּוּרְעָנוּת מִלָּבוֹא:


**but because of an understanding... man** But because of an understanding man, the retribution will be delayed from coming.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:1)


**גֶּבֶר רָשׁ.** דַּיָּן עַם הָאָרֶץ:


**A poor man** An ignorant judge.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:2)


**עוֹשֵׁק דַּלִּים.** בְּדִין עַל שֶׁאֵינוֹ מָתוּן בְּדִין, הֲרֵי הוּא **כְּמָטָר הַסּוֹחֵף** אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה **וְאֵין** עוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת:


**who oppresses the poor** in judgment because he does not take his time in arriving at a verdict. He is like rain that floods a field, and it does not produce fruit.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:3:3)


**סוֹחֵף.** שׁוֹטֵף, וּבַמִּשְׁנָה (כתובות ז,ח): ״נִסְתַּחֲפָה שָׂדֵהוּ״, וְכֵן ״נִסְחַף אַבִּירֶיךָ״ (ירמיהו מו:טו) – נִשְׁטְפוּ וּמָעֲדוּ וְכָשְׁלוּ:


**flooding** Heb. סחף and the language of the Mishnah (Keth. 1:6): “his field was ruined (נסתחפה)” and similarly (Jer. 46: 15): “Have your mighty men been swept away (נחסף)?” They were flooded, they slipped and stumbled.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:5:1)


**אַנְשֵׁי רָע לֹא יָבִינוּ מִשְׁפָּט.** אֵינָם מְשִׂימִים לֵב לְפוּרְעָנִיּוֹת הָעֲתִידוֹת, שֶׁיִּתְבּוֹנְנוּ בָּהּ וְיָשׁוּבוּ מִדַּרְכָּם וְיִנָּצְלוּ:


**Men of evil do not understand judgment** They do not give attention to the ultimate retribution, that they should think about it and repent of their way and be saved.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:5:2)


**וּמְבַקְשֵׁי ה׳ יָבִינוּ כֹל.** טוֹבָה וְרָעָה. **מִשְׁפָּט.** יוֹשְׁטִיצָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז; כָּךְ פֵּרֵשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בראשית לז) עַל דּוֹר הַמַּבּוּל:


**but those who seek the Lord understand everything** Good and evil. משפט is justise in Old French. In this manner, Rabbi Tanhuma explained it as referring to the generation of the Flood.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:6:1)


**טוֹב רָשׁ.** אַף עָנִי שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה:


**A poor man...is better** Even one who is poor in Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:6:2)


**וְהוֹלֵךְ בְּתֻמּוֹ.** בְּמַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים:


**who walks innocently** with good deeds.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:7:1)


**וְרוֹעֶה זוֹלְלִים.** מְחַבֵּר לוֹ זוֹלְלִים, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן רֵעִים:


**but he who befriends gluttons** Heb. ורעה. He associates with gluttons. This is an expression of friends (רעים).



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:8:1)


**מַרְבֶּה הוֹנוֹ בְּנֶשֶׁךְ וְתַרְבִּית לְחוֹנֵן דַּלִּים.** הָרְשׁוּת שׁוֹמְעִין עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא מִתְעַשֵּׁר בְּלֹא מִשְׁפָּט, וְנוֹטְלִין מָמוֹנוֹ, וּבוֹנִין בּוֹ גְּשָׁרִים וּמְתַקְּנִין בּוֹ הַדְּרָכִים, וְזֶהוּ חֲנִינַת דַּלִּים. כָּךְ דְּרָשׁוֹ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, משפטים ה):


**He who increases his riches with usury and interest...for him who is gracious to the poor** The government hears about him, that he is becoming wealthy by illegal means, and they confiscate his money, with which they build bridges and repair highways. This is being gracious to the poor. In this manner, Rabbi Tanhuma expounded on it.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:10:1)


**בִּשְׁחוּתוֹ.** מִלְּשׁוֹן שַׁחַת:


**into his own pit** Heb. בשחותו, from an expression of שחת, a pit.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:11:1)


**אִישׁ עָשִׁיר וְדַל מֵבִין.** כְּנֶגֶד רַב וְתַלְמִיד הוּא מְדַבֵּר, שֶׁהַתַּלְמִיד חוֹקֵר וְעַל יָדוֹ הָרַב מִתְחַכֵּם:


**A rich man...but a discerning poor man** Scripture speaks about a teacher and a pupil, that the pupil searches out [the understanding of the subject matter,] and through him, the teacher gains wisdom.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:12:1)


**יְחֻפַּשׂ אָדָם.** יִתְחַפֵּשׂ בְּכָל מִינֵי עֲלִילוֹת:


**person will be searched out** He will be searched out with all kinds of false accusations.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:13:1)


**וּמוֹדֶה וְעוֹזֵב.** מוֹדֶה פְּשָׁעָיו וְעוֹזְבָם מִשּׁוּב בָּם עוֹד:


**but he who confesses and abandons** He confesses his sins and abandons them, never to return to them.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:14:1)


**מְפַחֵד תָּמִיד.** דּוֹאֵג מֵעֹנֶשׁ, וּבְכָךְ מִתְרַחֵק מֵעֲבֵירָה:


**who is always afraid** He is concerned with punishment, and therefore distances himself from sin.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:15:1)


**אֲרִי נוֹהֵם.** הוּא לָהֶם:


**like a roaring lion** he is to them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:15:2)


**וְדֹב שׁוֹקֵק.** נְהִימָה לַאֲרִי וּשְׁקִיקָה לַדֹּב, שְׁנֵיהֶם לְשׁוֹן צְעָקָה:


**and a growling bear** נהימה, roaring, to a lion, and שקיקה, growling, to a bear, are both expressions of a cry.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:16:1)


**חֲסַר תְּבוּנוֹת.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהוּא חֲסַר תְּבוּנָה הוּא **רַב מַעֲשַׁקּוֹת**, כִּי אֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן לֵב עַל חַיָּיו וּמַרְבֶּה לַעֲשֹׁק; שֶׁהֲרֵי הַ**שׂוֹנֵא בֶצַע יַאֲרִיךְ יָמִים**:


**who lacks understanding** As he lacks understanding, he is a great oppressor because he does not give any thought to his life. [Therefore,] he oppresses excessively, for one who hates unlawful gain will live long. [Consequently, he who oppresses others will die young.]



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:1)


**אָדָם עָשׁוּק בְּדַם נָפֶשׁ.** (שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו עֹשֶׁק דָּם) הוּא הַמַּחֲטִיא אֶת חֲבֵרוֹ וְאָבְדָה נַפְשׁוֹ עַל יָדוֹ:


**A man who is guilty of robbing one’s lifeblood** (Upon whom the crime of robbing blood lies.) He is one who causes his fellow to sin, and his soul is lost because of him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:2)


**עַד בּוֹר יָנוּס.** עַד יוֹם מוֹתוֹ יָנוּס לְעֶזְרָה, שֶׁיִּתְכַּפֵּר לוֹ:


**will flee to the pit** Until his dying day, he will flee for aid, that it should be expiated for him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:17:3)


**וְאַל יִתְמְכוּ בוֹ.** מִשָּׁמַיִם לְהַסְפִּיק בְּיָדוֹ לָשׁוּב, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא הוּא בְּגַן עֵדֶן וְתַלְמִידוֹ בְּגֵיהִנֹּם. כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא (יומא פז.):


**but no one will support him** from Heaven to give him the possibility to repent, so that he will not be in Paradise while his pupil is in Gehinnom. In this manner, it is expounded upon in Tractate Yoma (87a).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:18:1)


**יִפּוֹל בְּאַחַת.** בְּרָעָה אַחַת, וְאֵין תְּקוּמָה לְמַפַּלְתּוֹ:


**will fall in one** In one evil, and there will be no rising up after his downfall.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:19:1)


**יִשְׂבַּע רִישׁ.** עֲנִיּוּת:


**will be sated with poverty** Heb. ריש, poverty.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:20:1)


**אִישׁ אֱמוּנוֹת.** הַנּוֹתֵן מַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו בֶּאֱמוּנָה, שֶׁאֵין עֵד בַּדָּבָר אֶלָּא הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, רוֹאֶה וּמַרְבֶּה לוֹ בְּרָכָה:


**A trustworthy man** who gives his tithes faithfully, for there is no witness to the matter, but the Holy One, blessed be He, sees and lavishes blessings upon him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:20:2)


**וְאָץ לְהַעֲשִׁיר.** דּוֹחֵק הַשָּׁעָה לְהִתְעַשֵּׁר בְּשֶׁל עֲנִיִּים:


**but he who hastens to become rich** He hastens the time to become rich from the belongings of the poor.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:21:1)


**יִפְשַׁע גָּבֶר.** הַמַּטֶּה מִשְׁפָּט:


**a man will sin** one who perverts justice.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:22:1)


**נִבְהָל לַהוֹן.** מְמַהֵר לְהַרְבּוֹת הוֹן וְגוֹזֵל תְּרוּמוֹתָיו וּמַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו (תנחומא, בהר א):


**He who hastens to [acquire] wealth** He who hastens to increase wealth and steals his heave- offerings and tithes.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:22:2)


**כִּי חֶסֶר יְבוֹאֶנּוּ.** חִסָּרוֹן יָבֹא לוֹ, שֶׁהַמְּאֵרָה מִשְׁתַּלַּחַת בְּמַעֲשֵׂי יָדָיו:


**that want will come upon him** Want will come to him, for a curse will be sent upon the work of his hands.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:23:1)


**מוֹכִיחַ אָדָם וְגוֹ׳.** עַל עֲבֵירוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ וּמַפְרִישׁוֹ מֵהֶם:


**He who admonishes man** for the sins he has committed and separates him from them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:23:2)


**אַחֲרֵי חֵן.** לְאַחַר זְמַן חֵן יִמְצָא בְּעֵינָיו יוֹתֵר **מִמַּחֲלִיק לָשׁוֹן**. מָצִינוּ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה מד:ה): כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ׳אַחֲרֵי׳ אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מֻפְלָג, ׳אַחַר׳ סָמוּךְ. בְּתַנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, הוספה לפרשת דברים ב) עַל ״אֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים״ (דברים א:א): **מוֹכִיחַ אָדָם אַחֲרֵי**, זֶה מֹשֶׁה שֶׁהוֹכִיחַ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל אַחַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁנִּקְרְאוּ ׳אָדָם׳, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״אָדָם אַתֶּם״ (יחזקאל לד:לא), וְנֶאֱמַר בּוֹ ״כִּי מָצָאתָ חֵן בְּעֵינַי״ (שמות לג:יז); **מִמַּחֲלִיק לָשׁוֹן.** זֶה בִּלְעָם, הָאוֹמֵר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְּבָרִים מְתֻקָּנִים ״מַה טֹּבוּ אֹהָלֶיךָ יַעֲקֹב״ (במדבר כד:ה). מֹשֶׁה מַכְרִיז: ״הִשָּׁמְרוּ לָכֶם פֶּן יִפְתֶּה״ (דברים יא:טז), וּבִלְעָם מַכְרִיז וּמַחֲנִיף: עֲשׂוּ תַּאֲוַתְכֶם וְאֵינוֹ מַקְפִּיד! ״לֹא אִישׁ אֵל וִיכַזֵּב״ (במדבר כג:יט) – בָּאֻמּוֹת, אֲבָל בָּכֶם אוֹמֵר וְלֹא יַעֲשֶׂה (ראה שם). וּשְׁלֹמֹה צָוַח עַל שְׁנֵיהֶם: ״נֶאֱמָנִים פִּצְעֵי אוֹהֵב וְנַעְתָּרוֹת נְשִׁיקוֹת שׂוֹנֵא״ (משלי כז:ו):


**afterwards will find more favor** After a time, he will find more favor in his eyes than one who speaks with flattery. We find in Gen. Rabbah (44:5): “Wherever it says אחרי, it means only long after; אחר, it is soon after. In Tanhuma, in the portion entitled “These are the words”: “He who admonishes man after Me”—this refers to Moses, who admonished Israel after the Holy One, blessed be He, who were called “man,” as it is stated (Ezek. 34:31): “You are men,” and it stated concerning him (Ex. 33: 17): “For you have found favor in My sight.” **more favor than he who speaks with flattery** This refers to Balaam, who said fine things to Israel (Num. 24:5): “How goodly are your tents, O Jacob!” Moses announces (Deut. 11:16): “Beware lest your heart be deceived,” and Balaam announces and flatters, “Do what your heart desires; He does not care: (Num. 23:19) ‘God is not a man that He should lie,’ concerning the nations, but concerning you, ‘He says but He does not do.’” And Solomon cries about both of them, (above 27:6): “Wounds of a lover are faithful, whereas kisses of an enemy are burdensome.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:1)


**גּוֹזֵל אָבִיו.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**He who robs his father** The Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:2)


**וְאִמּוֹ.** כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל: הַמַּחֲטִיא אֶת הָרַבִּים, שֶׁגּוֹזֵל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, מַפְרִיד מִמֶּנּוּ בָּנָיו וְגוֹזֵל מֵהֶם טוֹבָה:


**or his mother** The people of Israel. He who causes the people to sin by robbing the Holy One, blessed be He, estranges His children from Him and robs them of goodness.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:24:3)


**מַשְׁחִית.** יָרָבְעָם (סנהדרין קב.):


**destroyer** Jeroboam.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:25:1)


**רְחַב נֶפֶשׁ.** לְהַשִּׂיג כָּל תַּאֲוָתוֹ:


**A greedy man** to attain all his desire.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:25:2)


**יְגָרֶה מָדוֹן.** מְגָרֶה עָלָיו מִדַּת הַדִּין:


**stirs up quarrels** He incites the Divine standard of justice upon himself



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:27:1)


**נוֹתֵן לָרָשׁ.** צְדָקָה:


**He who gives to a poor man** charity.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 28:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/28:27:2)


(**אֵין מַחְסוֹר.** לְהַרְבּוֹת לוֹ צְדָקָה סא״א). וְכֵן הָרַב שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹנֵעַ תּוֹרָה מִפִּי הַתַּלְמִיד:


**will suffer no want** (to give him much charity.) And so it is with the teacher who does not withhold Torah from his pupil.



## Chapter 29


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:1:1)


**מַקְשֶׁה עֹרֶף.** מִלִּשְׁמֹעַ:


**but stiffens his nape** not to listen.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:1:2)


**פֶּתַע יִשָּׁבֵר.** מַהֵר יִשָּׁבֵר:


**will suddenly be broken** He will quickly be broken.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:3:1)


**וְרוֹעֶה.** מְחַבֵּר לוֹ זוֹנוֹת. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (עירובין סד.): הָאוֹמֵר שְׁמוּעָה זוֹ נָאָה – אֶשְׁנֶנָּה, וְזוֹ אֵינָהּ נָאָה – לֹא אֶשְׁנֶנָּה. וְסִיּוּעַ בַּמִּקְרָא, שֶׁאֵין ״זוֹנוֹת״ בַּמִּקְרָא מָלֵא אֶלָּא זֶה בִּלְבַד:


**but one who keeps company with harlots** Heb. ורעה. He joins harlots to himself, as its apparent meaning. Our Sages, however, said: “He who says, ‘This tradition is acceptable, I will learn it, but this one is not acceptable, I will not learn it.’” The support [for this view] from the verse is that זונות throughout the Scriptures is not spelled fully [with two “vavim”] except this once only.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:4:1)


**יַעֲמִיד אָרֶץ.** אִם שׁוֹפֵט אֱמֶת הוּא, **יַעֲמִיד אָרֶץ**:


**establishes the country** If he is an honest judge, he establishes the country.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:4:2)


**וְאִישׁ תְּרוּמוֹת.** אִישׁ גֵאוּת, שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁ לִהְיוֹת מָתוּן בַּדִּין. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (כתובות קה:): אִם הַדַּיָּן דּוֹמֶה לְמִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לִקְנוֹת אוֹהֲבִים וְלִיקַּח שֹׁחַד, הוּא **יַעֲמִיד אָרֶץ**, וְאִם דּוֹמֶה לְכֹהֵן הַשּׁוֹאֵל תְּרוּמוֹת עַל הַגְּרָנוֹת, הוּא **יֶהֶרְסֶנָּה**:


**but a haughty man** Heb. תרומות, a haughty man, who does not care to take time in judgment. Our Rabbis (Keth. 105b) stated: “If the judge is like a king, who does not have to acquire friends and accept bribes, he establishes the country. But if he is like a priest, who asks for terumoth in the threshing floors, he tears it down.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:5:1)


**מַחֲלִיק.** מְדַבֵּר חֲלָקוֹת:


**flatters** Heb. מחליק, talks smoothly.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:6:1)


**בְּפֶשַׁע אִישׁ רָע.** יָבֹא מוֹקֵשׁ:


**When a wicked man sins** a snare will come.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:6:2)


**וְצַדִּיק.** שֶׁלֹּא הָלַךְ בִּדְרָכָיו, **יָרוּן וְשָׂמֵחַ**:


**and a righteous man** who did not go in his ways, sings and rejoices.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:7:1)


**דִּין דַּלִּים.** יִסּוּרֵי דַלִּים וּמַה הֵם צְרִיכִים, וְנוֹתֵן לָהֶם לֵב:


**the judgment of the poor** The torments of the poor and what they require, and puts his mind to them.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:8:1)


**יָפִיחוּ.** יַלְהִיבוּ, כְּלַהֶבֶת אֵשׁ הַנָּפוּחַ בָּרוּחַ:


**inflame** Heb. יפיחו, lit. they blow. They inflame it like a flame of fire that is fanned by the wind.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:9:1)


**אִישׁ חָכָם נִשְׁפָּט.** מִתְוַכֵּחַ עִם הָאֱוִיל:


**a wise man contends** Heb. נשפט, debates with the fool.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:9:2)


**וְאֵין נָחַת.** בֵּין מַרְאֶה לוֹ פָּנִים זְעוּפוֹת בֵּין שֶׁמַּרְאֵהוּ פָּנִים שׂוֹחֲקוֹת, אֵין נַחַת לֹא בָזוֹ וְלֹא בָזוֹ, אֵינוֹ מוֹצֵא קוֹרַת רוּחַ. מָצִינוּ בַאֲמַצְיָה, שֶׁהֶרְאָה לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא פָּנִים שׂוֹחֲקוֹת וּמָסַר בְּיָדוֹ אֱדוֹם, וּבְשׁוּבוֹ מֵהַכּוֹת (דברי הימים ב כה:יד) וַיָּבֵא אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם וְלָהֶם הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה. לְאָחָז הֶרְאָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא פָּנִים זוֹעֲפוֹת וּמְסָרוֹ בְּיַד מַלְכֵי אֲרָם, וַיִּזְבַּח לֵאלֹהֵי דַמֶּשֶׂק כִּי אָמַר אֱלֹהֵי אֲרָם מְעַזְּרִים לָהֶם (דברי הימים ב כח:כג):


**he will have no contentment** Whether he shows him an angry countenance or he shows him a laughing countenance, there is no contentment either in this or in that. He finds no satisfaction. We find in the case of Amaziah that the Holy One, blessed be He, showed him a laughing countenance and delivered Edom into his hands, and when he returned from defeating them, (11 Chron 25:14) “he brought their (sic) gods, etc. and prostrated himself before them.” To Ahaz, the Holy One, blessed be He, showed an angry countenance and delivered him into the hands of the kings of Aram (ibid. 28:23): “And he sacrificed to the gods of Damascus, etc. for he said, ‘The gods of Aram are helping them.’”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:10:1)


**יְבַקְשׁוּ נַפְשׁוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן חִבָּה, וְנִרְאֶה שֶׁכֵּן הוּא, כְּמָה שֶׁאָמַר דָּוִד לְאֶבְיָתָר: ״אֲשֶׁר יְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת נַפְשִׁי יְבַקֵּשׁ אֶת נַפְשֶׁךָ״ (שמואל א כב:כג) – מִי שֶׁיִּגְמֹל לִי חֶסֶד יִגְמֹל לְךָ. וְאֵין הַפּוֹתְרִים מוֹדִים לִי:


**seek his soul** [This is] an expression of love, and it appears that it is so, as David said to Abiathar (I Sam. 22:23): “for he who seeks my soul seeks your soul.” He who will deal kindly with me will deal kindly with you. The exegetes, however, do not agree with me.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:11:1)


**בְּאָחוֹר יְשַׁבְּחֶנָּה.** כְּשֶׁיּוֹצִיא הַכְּסִיל כָּל רוּחוֹ, בָּא הֶחָכָם וְיַשְׁפִּילֶנּוּ בְּמַעֲנֵה לְשׁוֹנוֹ. וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״מַשְׁבִּיחַ שְׁאוֹן יַמִּים״ (תהלים סה:ח); ״בְּשׂוֹא גַלָּיו אַתָּה תְשַׁבְּחֵם״ (תהלים פט:י):


**will quiet it** When the fool has let out all his wind, the wise man comes and humbles it with the reply of his mouth. Similar to this is (Ps. 65:8): “Who stills (משביח) the noise of the seas”; (ibid. 89: 10): “when its waves rise up, You still (תשבחם) them.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:13:1)


**וְאִישׁ תְּכָכִים.** אִישׁ מְזִמּוֹת, בַּעַל תּוֹרָה. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ (תמורה טז.; ויקרא רבה לד,ד) פֵּרְשׁוּ: תַּלְמִיד שֶׁאָמַר לָרַב הַשְׁנֵינִי פֶּרֶק אֶחָד, וּמְלַמְּדוֹ:


**and a man of deep thoughts** A man of thoughts, a Torah scholar. And our Sages explained it concerning a disciple who said to a teacher, “Teach me.”



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:15:1)


**וְנַעַר מְשֻׁלָּח.** שֶׁשְּׁלָחוֹ אָבִיו לָלֶכֶת בְּדַרְכֵי לִבּוֹ, סוֹפוֹ **מֵבִישׁ אִמּוֹ.** יִשְׁמָעֵאל, עַל שֶׁהָיָה רָשָׁע, גָּרַם לְשָׂרָה שֶׁאָמְרָה לְאַבְרָהָם ״גָּרֵשׁ אֶת הָאָמָה וְגוֹמֵר״ (בראשית כא:י):


**but a child left free** Whom his father set free to follow the dictates of his heart, ultimately... **brings shame to his mother** This is Ishmael; since he was wicked, he brought about that Sarah should say to Abraham (Gen. 21:10): “Expel this bondwoman and her son.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:18:1)


**בְּאֵין חָזוֹן יִפָּרַע עָם.** כְּשֶׁגּוֹרְמִין יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהַנְּבוּאָה מִסְתַּלֶּקֶת מֵהֶם, עַל יְדֵי שֶׁהֵם מַלְעִיבִים בַּנְּבִיאִים (דברי הימים ב לו:טז), יִפְרְצוּ בָם פְּרָצוֹת וְיוֹצְאִין לְתַרְבּוּת רָעָה:


**Without vision the people become unrestrained** When Israel causes prophecy to withdraw from them by mocking the prophets, they will become unrestrained and will degenerate.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:19:1)


**כִּי יָבִין וְאֵין מַעֲנֶה.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁרוֹאֵהוּ שֶׁמּוֹכִיחוֹ, שׁוֹתֵק, וְהוּא חוֹזֵר וּמְקַלְקֵל; לְכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְיַסְּרוֹ בְּמַכּוֹת וְעוֹנְשִׁין. וְלֹא דִּבֵּר עַל עֶבֶד מַמָּשׁ, אֶלָּא עַל כָּל הַמַּמְרִים דִּבְרֵי הַשּׁוֹפְטִים:


**because he will understand, but without response** As soon as he sees him (sic) that the one who was reproving him is silent, he reverts to his sins; therefore, one must chastise him with blows and punishments. He does not speak of an actual slave, but about all who disobey the words of the judges.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:20:1)


**אָץ בִּדְבָרָיו.** מְמַהֵר וְנִבְהָל לְהָשִׁיב:


**hasty with his words** Who hurries and rushes to reply.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:20:2)


**תִּקְוָה לִכְסִיל מִמֶּנּוּ.** יֵשׁ לַכְּסִיל תִּקְוָה יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנּוּ:


**there is more hope for a fool than for him** A fool has more hope than he.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:21:1)


**מְפַנֵּק מִנֹּעַר עַבְדּוֹ.** יֵצֶר הָרָע:


**If one pampers his slave from childhood** [This refers to] the evil inclination.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:21:2)


**מָנוֹן.** שַׁלִּיט, וְכֵן ״לִפְנֵי שֶׁמֶשׁ יִנּוֹן שְׁמוֹ״ (תהלים עב:יז); וְכֵן כָּל ׳נִין׳ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא, שֶׁהַנִּין קָם תַּחַת אָבִיו לִשְׁלוֹט בִּנְכָסָיו:


**a ruler** Heb. מנון, a ruler, and so is (Ps.72:17): “May His name be magnified (ינון) as long as the sun exists.” Similarly, every instance of נין in the Bible, since the son rises in his father’s stead to rule over his property.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:22:1)


**אִישׁ אַף יְגָרֶה מָדוֹן.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ. **מָדוֹן** מִדַּת הַדִּין:


**stirs up strife** According to its apparent meaning, מדון is the Divine standard of justice.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:23:1)


**יִתְמֹךְ כָּבוֹד.** יִקְרַב אֶל הַכָּבוֹד וְתוֹמֵךְ בּוֹ תָּמִיד:


**will grasp honor** Heb. יתמך, will come near the honor and constantly grasp it.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:24:1)


**אָלָה יִשְׁמַע.** שֶׁמַּשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתוֹ: אִם רָאִיתָ פְּלוֹנִי שֶׁגָּנַב לִי כָּךְ וְכָךְ; וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהוּא חוֹלֵק עִמּוֹ, אֵינוֹ מַגִּיד:


**he hears an oath** that they adjure him, “Did you see so and so who stole so much and so much from me?” And since he shares with him, he does not testify.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:25:1)


**חֶרְדַּת אָדָם יִתֵּן מוֹקֵשׁ.** מְסֹרָס הוּא: מוֹקֵשׁ, עֲבֵרָה, יִתֵּן חֲרָדָה לָאָדָם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאָדָם צַר עַיִן, וְחָרֵד, אִם יַעֲשֶׂה צְדָקָה, יִצְטָרֵךְ אֶל הַבְּרִיּוֹת; חֲרָדָה זוֹ תִּתֵּן לוֹ מוֹקֵשׁ. וְהָרִאשׁוֹן נוֹחַ לִי:


**A snare brings terror to a person** It is transposed. A snare of sin brings terror to a person. Another explanation: According to its apparent meaning, if a person is stingy and fears that if he gives charity he will require help from people, this fear will be a snare for him. I prefer the former [interpretation], however.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:26:1)


**פְּנֵי מוֹשֵׁל.** לָדוּן לְפָנָיו:


**the countenance of a ruler** to litigate before him.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:26:2)


**וּמֵיְהוָה מִשְׁפַּט אִישׁ.** אִם יִזְכֶּה אוֹ אִם יִתְחַיֵּב. וּבַמּוֹשְׁלִים דִּבֵּר הַכָּתוּב:


**but the judgment of a person** Any [judgment], whether he will be adjudged innocent or guilty. Scripture [here] speaks of the gentile kings.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 29:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/29:27:1)


**יְשַׁר דָּרֶךְ.** אָדָם שֶׁהוּא יָשָׁר בִּדְרָכָיו:


**whose way is straight** A man who is straight in his ways.



## Chapter 30


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:1)


**אָגוּר בֶּן יָקֶה.** דִּבְרֵי שְׁלֹמֹה שֶׁאָגַר אֶת הַבִּינָה וְהֵקִיאָהּ, כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ חֲכָמִים (תנחומא, וארא ה):


**The words of Agur, the son of Jakeh** אגור בן יקה, the words of Solomon, who gathered (אגר) understanding (בינה) and vomited it (והקיאה). The Sages interpreted it in this manner.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:2)


**הַמַּשָּׂא.** נְבוּאָה זוֹ אָמַר עַל כֵּן:


**the prophecy** He said this prophecy on that matter.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:3)


**נְאֻם הַגֶּבֶר לְאִיתִיאֵל.** אָמַר הַגֶּבֶר, זֶה שְׁלֹמֹה, הַמַּשָּׂא הַזֶּה עַל עַצְמוֹ, בִּשְׁבִיל אִיתִיאֵל – עַל שֶׁסָּמַךְ עַל חָכְמָתוֹ לְהַרְבּוֹת זָהָב וְסוּסִים וְנָשִׁים שֶׁהוּזְהַר מִלְּהַרְבּוֹת. וְכֵן אָמַר: **אִיתִיאֵל וְאֻכָל**, אַרְבֶּה נָשִׁים וְלֹא יָסוּרוּ אֶת לְבָבִי, אַרְבֶּה זָהָב וְלֹא אָסוּר, אַרְבֶּה סוּסִים וְלֹא אָשׁוּב אֶת הָעָם מִצְרַיְמָה (דברים יז:טז-יז):


**the words of the man concerning, “God is with me;** said the man that is Solomon this prophecy concerning himself because of איתיאל because he relied on his wisdom to increase gold, horses and wives, which he was forbidden to increase, and so he said, “God is with me, and I will be able. I will increase wives, and they will not turn my heart away; I will increase gold, and I will not turn away; I will increase horses, and I will not take the people back to Egypt.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:1:4)


**לְאִיתִיאֵל וְאֻכָל.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאָמַר: אִתִּי אֵל, וְאוּכַל לַעֲשׂוֹת וְלֹא אֶכָּשֵׁל. **לְאִיתִיאֵל**, עַל אִיתִיאֵל, כְּמוֹ ״וְאָמַר פַּרְעֹה לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (שמות יד:ג) – עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**yea, God is with me, and I will be able”** Since he said, “God is with me, and I will be able to do it, and I will not stumble.” לאיתיאל, because of “God is with me,” as in (Ex. 14:3): “For Pharaoh will say of the children of Israel (לבני),” meaning of the children of Israel.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:2:1)


**כִּי בַעַר אָנֹכִי.** עַל שֶׁסָּמַכְתִּי עַל חָכְמָתִי בְּדָבָר שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא דָּאַג, שֶׁמָּא יָבוֹא לִידֵי עֲבֵירָה:


**For I am more boorish** Because I relied on my wisdom in a matter that the Holy One, blessed be He, is concerned lest one come to sin.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:3:1)


**וְלֹא לָמַדְתִּי חָכְמָה.** וְלֹא דַעַת קְדוֹשִׁים יָדַעְתִּי, שֶׁגָּרַעְתִּי אוֹ הוֹסַפְתִּי עַל דִּבְרֵי מֹשֶׁה:


**Neither have I learned wisdom** nor do I know the knowledge of the holy ones, for I subtracted or added to the words of Moses.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:1)


**מִי עָלָה שָׁמַיִם.** כְּמֹשֶׁה (שמות יט:ג):


**Who ascended to heaven** like Moses?


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:2)


**מִי אָסַף רוּחַ.** פִּיחַ הַכִּבְשָׁן (שמות ט:י):


**Who gathered wind** The soot of the furnace.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:3
