[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:3)


**מִי צָרַר מַיִם.** ״קָפְאוּ תְהוֹמוֹת, נִצְּבוּ כְמוֹ נֵד״ (שמות טו:ח) בִּתְפִלָּתוֹ שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה:


**Who wrapped the waters** (Ex. 15:8): “The depths were congealed”; (ad loc.): “The floods stood upright like a heap,” through Moses’s prayer.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:4)


**מִי הֵקִים.** אֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן, שֶׁבַּהֲקָמָתוֹ נִתְבַּסְּסוּ כָּל אַפְסֵי אָרֶץ. כָּךְ נִדְרַשׁ בַּפְּסִיקְתָּא (פסיקתא רבתי א,ד):


**Who established** the Tabernacle, through whose establishment all the ends of the earth were firmly established. In this way, it is expounded in the Pesikta.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:5)


**מַה שְּׁמוֹ וּמַה שֶּׁם בְּנוֹ.** אִם תֹּאמַר: כְּבָר הָיָה דֻּגְמָתוֹ, אֱמוֹר: **מַה שֵּׁם בְּנוֹ**? אֵיזוֹ מִשְׁפָּחָה יָצְאָה מִמֶּנּוּ, וְנֵדַע מִי הוּא?


**What is his name and what is the name of his son** If you say that there already was one like him, tell me what his son’s name is; i.e., what family is descended from him, and we will know who he is.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:4:6)


**כִּי תֵדָע.** אִם תֵּדַע מִי הוּא, וְאֵיךְ לֹא יָרֵאתָ לַעֲבֹר עַל דְּבָרָיו?


**if you know** if you know who he is. Now how did you not fear to transgress His words?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:5:1)


**כָּל אִמְרַת אֱלוֹהַּ צְרוּפָה.** מְזֻקָּקָה, וְלֹא כָתַב דָּבָר שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ, וְהָיָה לִי לִיזָּהֵר:


**Every word of God is refined** Heb. צרופה, refined, and He did not write anything unnecessary. [Therefore,] I should have been careful.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:6:1)


**פֶּן יוֹכִיחַ בְּךָ וְנִכְזָבְתָּ.** יוֹכִיחַ עַל פָּנֶיךָ, שֶׁעַל יְדֵי תוֹסַפְתְּךָ אַתָּה בָּא לִידֵי עֲבֵירָה, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן אִם תִּגְרַע:


**lest He prove to you, and you be found a liar** He will prove to your face that through your addition you have come to sin, and certainly if you subtract.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:7:1)


**שְׁתַּיִם שָׁאַלְתִּי מֵאִתָּךְ.** עַכְשָׁיו מְדַבֵּר לִפְנֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**I ask two [things] of You** Now he addresses the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:8:1)


**רֹאשׁ.** דַּלּוּת:


**poverty** Heb. ראש, poverty.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:8:2)


**הַטְרִיפֵנִי.** לְשׁוֹן מָזוֹן, וְכֵן ״טֶרֶף נָתַן לִירֵאָיו״ (תהלים קיא:ה):


**provide me** Heb. הטריפני, an expression of food, and similarly (Ps. 111: 5): “He has given food (טרף) to those who fear Him.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:1)


**פֶּן אֶשְׂבַּע.** מִתּוֹךְ עֹשֶׁר, וְכִחַשְׁתִּי בַּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵרוֹב גַּאֲוָה. וּמַהוּ הַכְּחָשָׁה?


**lest I become sated** from wealth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:2)


**וְאָמַרְתִּי מִי ה׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: אֵין אֱלֹהִים:


**and deny** the existence of the Holy One, blessed be He, out of my extreme haughtiness. Now what is the denial? And I say, “Who is the Lord?” Meaning, there is no God.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:9:3)


**וְתָפַשְׂתִּי שֵׁם אֱלֹהָי.** לְהַרְגִּיל לְהִשָּׁבַע בּוֹ לַשֶּׁקֶר:


**and take hold of the name of my God** to become accustomed to swearing by it falsely.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:10:1)


**אַל תַּלְשֵׁן עֶבֶד.** אַל תִּמְסֹר דִּין עַל אָדָם לִצְעֹק עָלָיו לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא רָשָׁע אֲשֶׁר אָבִיו יְקַלֵּל, וְכָל הַתּוֹעֵבוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת כָּאן בּוֹ. וּרְאָיָה לַדָּבָר מֵהוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן בְּאֵרִי, כִּדְאִיתָא בִּפְסָחִים בְּפֶרֶק ׳הָאִשָּׁה׳ (פסחים פז.), שֶׁהִלְשִׁין אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאָמַר: הַחֲלִיפֵם בְּאֻמָּה אַחֶרֶת! אָמַר לוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: ״קַח לְךָ אֵשֶׁת זְנוּנִים״ (הושע א:ב):


**Do not inform** Do not deliver your case against a person to complain about him to the Holy One, blessed be He, even if he is wicked, who curses his father and possesses all the abominations mentioned here, and the proof of the matter is from Hosea son of Beeri, as is stated in Pesachim (87b) in the chapter entitled “The Woman,” that he informed on lsrael and said, “Exchange them for another nation.” Replied the Holy One, blessed be He, “Go, take yourself a wife of harlotry” (Hos. 1:2).



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:13:1)


**דּוֹר מָה רָמוּ עֵינָיו.** זֶה גַּסּוּת הָרוּחַ:


**A generation—how lofty etc.** This refers to haughtiness.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:1)


**דּוֹר חֲרָבוֹת שִׁנָּיו.** הַחִיצוֹנוֹת:


**A generation whose teeth are [like] swords** [These are] the outer [teeth].


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:2)


**מְתַלְּעֹתָיו.** שִׁנָּיו הַפְּנִימִיּוֹת:


**and its molars** Its inner teeth.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:14:3)


**וּמַאֲכָלוֹת.** סַכִּינִים:



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:15:1)


**לַעֲלוּקָה.** מְנַחֵם פָּתַר לַעֲלוּקָה כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁהוּא עֲרָבִי. וְהַפּוֹתְרִים אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן שְׁאוֹל וּמוֹרָד. וְכֵן בְּמִדְרַשׁ תְּהִלִּים לָמַדְנוּ כֵּן, שֶׁפּוֹתֵר שְׁתֵּי בָנוֹת – גַּן עֵדֶן וְגֵיהִנֹּם; זוֹ אוֹמֶרֶת: תְּנוּ לִי צַדִּיקִים, וְזוֹ אוֹמֶרֶת: תְּנוּ לִי רְשָׁעִים:


**The leech has** Menachem (Machbereth p. 134) interprets עלוקה according to its apparent meaning. We learn that it is Arabic, but the commentators say that it is an expression of the grave and the descent. Indeed, we learn this in Midrash Psalms (31:9), which interprets the “two daughters” as Paradise and Gehinnom. This one says, “Give me righteous people!” and this one says, “Give me wicked people!”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:15:2)


**אַרְבַּע לֹא אָמְרוּ הוֹן.** הַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ לָנוּ:


**four that do not say, “Enough!”** Heb. הון, lit. wealth. We have much [wealth].



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:16:1)


**וְעֹצֶר רָחַם.** תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


**the confined womb** Sexual intercourse.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:17:1)


**לִיקֲּהַת אֵם.** לִקְמָטִים הַנִּקְבָּצִים וְנִקְהִין בִּפְנֵי אִמּוֹ, לְשׁוֹן ״יִקְּהַת עַמִּים״ (בראשית מט:י) – קְבוּצַת עַמִּים. וְהַיּוֹ״ד יְסוֹד, כְּמוֹ ״יִפְעַת״ (יחזקאל כח:ז), ״וְיַעֲלַת חֵן״ (משלי ה:יט):


**the mother’s wrinkles** Heb. ליקהת, the wrinkles that gather (נקהין) in his mother’s face, from the expression of (Gen. 49:10): “a gathering (יקהת) of peoples.” The “yud” is a radical, like יפעת, splendor; (יעלת חן) (Prov. 5:19), a graceful mountain goat.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:17:2)


**יִקְּרוּהָ.** לְשׁוֹן ״תְּנַקֵּר״ (במדבר טז:יד), ״נִקְרַת הַצּוּר״ (שמות לג:כב), פוריי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז. יָבֹא הָעוֹרֵב שֶׁהוּא אַכְזָרִי עַל בָּנָיו, **וְיִקְּרוּהָ**, וְלֹא יֹאכְלֶנָּה וְלֹא יֵהָנֶה בָּהּ, וְיָבֹא נֶשֶׁר שֶׁהוּא רַחֲמָנִי עַל בָּנָיו **וְיֹאכְלוּהָ** וְיֵהָנֶה בָּהּ. הָעוֹרֵב אַכְזָרִי הוּא, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לִבְנֵי עֹרֵב אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָאוּ״ (תהלים קמז:ט), וְהַנֶּשֶׁר רַחֲמָנִי, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״יִשָּׂאֵהוּ עַל אֶבְרָתוֹ״ (דברים לב:יא; ראה תנחומא עקב ב):


**pick it out** Heb. יקרוה, an expression of (Num. 16: 14): “will you pick (תנקר)” (Ex. 33:22) “In the cleft (בנקרת) of the rock.” Forer in French, to bore through. Let the raven, which is cruel to its young, come and pick it and not eat it and not derive benefit from it, and let the eagle, which is compassionate with its young, come and eat it and derive benefit from it. The raven is cruel, as it is stated (Ps. 147:9): “to the young ravens which cry,” and the eagle is compassionate, as it is stated (Deut. 32: 11): “It bears its young on its wing.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:18:1)


**נִפְלְאוּ מִמֶּנִּי.** נִכְסוּ מִשֶּׁחָלְפוּ מֵעֵינַי, וְאֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ לְהֵיכָן הָלְכוּ, שֶׁמְּמַהֲרִין לְהִסָּתֵר מִן הָעַיִן:


**are concealed from me** They are covered after they passed from my eyes, and I do not know where they went, because they hasten to hide from the eye.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:20:1)


**וּמָחֲתָה.** כּוֹפָה פִּיהָ לְמַטָּה, כְּדִכְתִיב ״כַּאֲשֶׁר יִמְחֶה הַצַּלַּחַת מָחָה וְהָפַךְ עַל פָּנֶיהָ״ (מלכים ב כא:יג):


**and [she] wipes** Heb. ומחתה she turns over her lower orifice, as it is written (II Kings 21:13): “as one wipes (ימחה) a dish, he wipes (מחה) and turns it upside down.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:20:2)


**אָכְלָה.** לָשׁוֹן נְקִיָּה דִּבֵּר הַכָּתוּב (יומא עה.):


**she eats** Scripture speaks euphemistically.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:28:1)


**שְׂמָמִית.** אַרִינְיָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The spider** Erinee in Old French. [Araignee in modern French.]


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:28:2)


**בְּיָדַיִם תְּתַפֵּשׂ.** בְּיָדָהּ הִיא אוֹחֶזֶת וּמִתְדַּבֶּקֶת בַּכְּתָלִים (בראשית רבה סו,ז):


**grasps with [her] hands** With her hands she grasps and clings to the walls.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:29:1)


**מְטִיבֵי צָעַד.** הוֹלְכִים וּמַצְלִיחִים בִּגְבוּרָתָם:


**outstanding in their step** They walk and succeed with their might.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:1)


**זַרְזִיר מָתְנַיִם אוֹ תָיִשׁ.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַהוּ, וּלְפִי הַמַּשְׁמָע הִיא חַיָּה תְּשׁוּשָׁה מָתְנַיִם:


**The greyhound** Heb. זרזיר מתנים. I do not know what it is, but from the context appears to be an animal with weak loins.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:2)


**וּמֶלֶךְ אַלְקוּם.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַה הוּא לְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה פּוֹתְרִין חָמֵשׁ פָּרָשִׁיּוֹת אֵלּוּ שֶׁל אַרְבַּע דְּבָרִים כְּנֶגֶד אַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁנִּתְאַמְּצָה מֶמְשַׁלְתָּן עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּעָוֹן שֶׁעָבְרוּ עַל חֲמִשָּׁה חוּמְשֵׁי תּוֹרָה, הִזְכִּירָם חֲמִשָּׁה פְּעָמִים:


**and the king against whom no one dares to rise up** Heb. ומלך אלקום I do not know what it is according to its simple meaning, but the Aggadic midrashim interpret these five sections of four as corresponding to the four kingdoms. Since their rule over Israel was strengthened because of the iniquity of having transgressed the five Books of the Pentateuch, Scripture mentions them five times.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:3)


(טז) **שְׁאוֹל וְעֹצֶר רָחַם.** שְׁאוֹל מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר ״הִרְחִיב כַּשְּׁאוֹל נַפְשׁוֹ״ (חבקוק ב:ה). **וְעֹצֶר רָחַם** זוֹ הִיא מָדַי שֶׁבִּימֵיהֶם עָצְרוּ רַחֲמִים מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לְהַשְׁמִיד לַהֲרֹג וּלְאַבֵּד״ (אסתר ג:יג):


**[16] The grave and the barren womb** The grave represents the kingdom of Babylon, for it is stated regarding Nebuchadnezzar (Hab. 2:5): “who widened his desire like the nether- world.” And the barren womb, that is Media, in whose time mercy (רחמים) was held back from Israel, as it is said (Esther 3:13): to destroy, kill and cause to perish.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:4)


**אֶרֶץ לֹא שָׂבְעָה מַּיִם.** זוֹ יָוָן, שֶׁלֹּא שָׂבְעָה מִלִּגְזֹר גְּזֵרוֹת רָעוֹת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**the earth, which is not sated with water** This represents Greece, which was not sated with issuing decrees on Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:5)


**וְאֵשׁ לֹא אָמְרָה הוֹן.** נֶגֶד עֵשָׂו, שֶׁעָשָׂה בְּחֵמָה בּוֹעֶרֶת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁאָמְרוּ לְהַשְׁמִיד טַף וְנָשִׁים בְּיוֹם אֶחָד (אסתר ג:יג). וְכֵן:


**and fire, which does not say, “Enough!”** Corresponding to Esau, who acted with burning wrath against Israel, for he said to destroy children and women in one day. And likewise...


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:6)


(יט) **דֶּרֶךְ הַנֶּשֶׁר.** זוֹ בָּבֶל, ״הַנֶּשֶׁר הַגָּדוֹל... אֶרֶךְ הָאֵבֶר״ (יחזקאל יז:ג):


**[19] The way of the eagle** This is Babylon, the great eagle, with the long wings (Ezek. 17:3).


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:7)


**נָחָשׁ.** זוֹ מָדַי (דניאל ח:כ):


**serpent** This is Media.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:8)


**דֶּרֶךְ אֳנִיָּה בְלֶב יָם.** זוֹ יָוָן, שֶׁהִיא קַלָּה בִּגְזֵרוֹתֶיהָ (משלי ל:יט):


**the way of a ship in the heart of the sea** This represents Greece, who was swift with its decrees.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:9)


**דֶּרֶךְ גֶּבֶר בְּעַלְמָה.** זוֹ פָּרַס שֶׁאָמְרָה: ״לְעוֹלָם אֶהְיֶה גְבֶרֶת״ (ישעיהו מז:ז):


**the way of a man with a young woman** This represents Edom, who said, “I will be a mistress forever.” [גבר is interpreted as גברת, a mistress, and בעלמה as לעולם, forever.]


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:10)


(כ) **כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ אִשָּׁה מְנָאָפֶת.** זוֹ הָרָעָה גָּרְמָה לָהֶם לִכְנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנָּאֲפָה בַּעֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים, וּכְדַאי הוּא הַפּוּרְעָנִיּוֹת לָבוֹא לָהּ:


**[20] So is the way of an adulterous woman** The people of Israel brought this evil upon themselves because they played the adulteress with idolatry, and they deserved that the retribution should befall them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:11)


**וְאָמְרָה לֹא פָעַלְתִּי אָוֶן.** כְּדַאֲמַר ״הִנְנִי נִשְׁפָּט אוֹתָךְ עַל אָמְרֵךְ לֹא חָטָאתִי״ (ירמיה ב:לה). [עד כאן לשוֹן רש״י שבמק״ג]:


**and she says, “I have no committed no sin.”** As it says (Jeremiah 2:35), “Behold, I contend with you concerning because you say, I have not sinned.” (Until this point are the words of Rashi [as they appear] in Mikraoth Gedoloth.)


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:12)


(כא) **רָגְזָה אֶרֶץ.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**[21] the earth quakes** This refers to Eretz Israel.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:13)


(כב) **תַּחַת עֶבֶד.** זֶה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שֶׁהָיָה עֶבֶד לִמְרוֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן וְכוֹתֵב אִגְּרוֹתָיו, כְּדִגְרָסִינַן בְּחוּלִין (סנהדרין צו.):


**under a slave** This refers to Nebuchadnezzar, who was the slave and secretary of Merodach-baladan, as appears in Hullin. [To my knowledge, this does not appear in Hullin, but in Helek, the eleventh chapter of Sanhedrin, 96a.]


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:14)


**וְנָבָל כִּי יִשְׂבַּע לָחֶם.** זֶה אֲחַשְׁוֵרוֹשׁ שֶׁעָשָׂה מִשְׁתֶּה מֵאָה וּשְׁמוֹנִים יוֹם (אסתר א:ד):


**and a wretch who is sated with food** This refers to Ahasuerus, who made a banquet for one hundred and eighty days


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:15)


(כג) **שְׂנוּאָה כִּי תִבָּעֵל.** כְּנֶגֶד יָוָן:


**[23] a hated woman who is married** Corresponding to Greece.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:16)


**וְשִׁפְחָה כִּי תִירַשׁ גְּבִרְתָּהּ.** זֶה עֵשָׂו, שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לַעֲבֹד לְיַעֲקֹב וְנֶהְפַּךְ הַדְּבָרִים:


**and a maidservant who inherits her mistress** This refers to Esau, who should have served Jacob, but the matter was reversed.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:17)


(כה) **הַנְּמָלִים עַם לֹא עָז.** זוֹ בָּבֶל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״הֵן אֶרֶץ כַּשְׂדִּים זֶה הָעָם לֹא הָיָה״ (ישעיהו כג:יג):


**[25] The ants are a people not strong** This refers to Babylon, as it is stated (Isa. 23:13): “Behold the land of the Chaldees, this people has never been.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:18)


**וַיָּכִינוּ בַקַּיִץ לַחְמָם.** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר עָשָׂה כָבוֹד אַחַר לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בִּימֵי מְרֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן אֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח סְפָרִים לְחִזְקִיָּהוּ וְכָתַב שָׁם: שְׁלָם לְמַלְכָּא חִזְקִיָּהוּ, שְׁלָם לְקַרְתָּא דִירוּשְׁלֵם, שְׁלָם לֶאֱלָהָא רַבָּא. וּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר כּוֹתֵב אִגְּרוֹתָיו הָיָה, וְלֹא הָיָה שָׁם אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם; וּכְשֶׁבָּא וְסִפְּרוּ לוֹ מַה שֶּׁעָשׂוּ, אָמַר לָהֶם: אֱלָהָא רַבָּא קְרִיתוּ לֵיהּ וּכְתַבִיתוּ לֵיהּ בְּסוֹף? רָץ אַחַר הַשָּׁלִיחַ וְהֶחֱזִירוֹ. בִּשְׁבִיל אוֹתוֹ מְרוּצָה זָכָה לַמַּלְכוּת; הֲרֵי שֶׁהֵכִין בַּקַּיִץ לַחְמוֹ כְּדֶרֶךְ הַנְּמָלָה (סנהדרין צו.):


**yet they prepare their food in the summer** [This refers to] Nebuchadnezzar, who performed one [act of] honor to the Holy One, blessed be He, in the days of Merodach-baladan, when he sent letters to Hezekiah, and wrote therein, “Peace to King Hezekiah; peace to city of Jerusalem; peace to the great God.” Nebuchadnezzar was the one who wrote his letters, but he was not there that day, and when he came and they told him what they had done, he responded, “You call Him the great God, yet you address Him last!” He ran after the messenger and brought him back. For that [act of] pursuit, he merited the kingship. This is how he “prepared his bread in the summer,” like the ant.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:19)


(כו) **שְׁפַנִּים עַם לֹא־עָצוּם.** זֶה מָדַי וּפָרָס:


**[26] The hyraxes are a people not strong** This refers to Media and Persia.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:20)


**וַיָּשִׂימוּ בַסֶּלַע בֵּיתָם:** שֶׁבָּנוּ בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**yet they make their home in the rock** For they built the Temple.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:21)


(כח) **שַׁמָּמִית בְּיָדַיִם תְּתַפֵּשׂ.** זֶה עֵשָׂו שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְהַיָּדַיִם יְדֵי עֵשָׂו״ (בראשית כז:כב):


**[28] The spider grasps with its hands** This refers to Esau, as it is stated (Gen. 27:22): “The voice is the voice of Jacob, but the hands are the hands of Esau.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:22)


**בְּהֵיכְלֵי מֶלֶךְ.** שֶׁנִּכְנַס לְהֵיכַל מֶלֶךְ וְהֶחֱרִיבוֹ:


**in a king’s palaces** That he [Esau] entered the Temple of the King and destroyed it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:23)


(ל) **לַיִשׁ גִּבּוֹר בַּבְּהֵמָה.** זֶה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו ד:ז) ״עָלָה אַרְיֵה מִסֻּבְּכוֹ״:


**[30] The lion is the mightiest of the beasts** This is Nebuchadnezzar, as it is stated (Jer. 4:7): The lion has come up from his thicket.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:24)


**זַרְזִיר מָתְנַיִם.** זוֹ מָדַי וּפָרַס, שֶׁזֵּרְזוּ מָתְנֵיהֶם וְהָרְגוּ לְבֵלְשַׁצַּר וְנָטְלוּ מַלְכוּת בָּבֶל (דניאל ה:ל):


**the one who girds his loins** This refers to Media and Persia, who girded their loins and assassinated Belshazzar and seized the kingdom of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:25)


**אוֹ תָיִשׁ.** זֶה יָוָן שֶׁנִּקְרָא תַּיִשׁ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וְהַצָּפִיר הַשָּׂעִיר״ הוּא ״מֶלֶךְ יָוָן״ (דניאל ח:כא):


**[31] and the he-goat** This refers to Greece, as it is stated (Dan. 8:21): “And the rough he-goat is the king of Greece.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:31:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:31:26)


**וּמֶלֶךְ אַלְקוּם עִמּוֹ.** זוֹ אֻמַּת אֲרָם, שֶׁאָמַר ״אֲנִי וְאַפְסִי עוֹד״ (ישעיה מז:ח), אֵין נִצָּב לְעֻמָּתוֹ, אַלְקוּם – אֵין עוֹמֵד עִמּוֹ:


**and the king against whom no one dares to rise up** This is Edom, who says, “I am it, and there is none besides me.” No one opposes him. אלקום, no one stands with him.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:32:1)


**אִם נָבַלְתָּ בְהִתְנַשֵּׂא.** אִם נִתְנַבַּלְתָּ עַל יְדֵי חֲבֵירְךָ שֶׁחֵירְפְךָ (ס״א: עַל יְדֵי דַּבּוּרְךָ שֶׁחֵרַפְתָּ וְגִדַּפְתָּ) אֶת עַצְמְךָ, סוֹפְךָ לְהִנָּשֵׂא בַּדָּבָר:


**If you have been put to shame, you will be in your ascendency** If you were put to shame through your speech, that you derided yourself, you will ultimately ascend in the matter.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:32:2)


**וְאִם זַמּוֹתָ.** בְּלִבְּךָ לְהִתְקוֹטֵט, שִׂים יָד עַל פֶּה וּשְׁתוֹק:


**and if you thought evil** in your heart to quarrel, put your hand onto your mouth and remain silent.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Proverbs 30:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/30:33:1)


**כִּי מִיץ חָלָב.** כִּי כַּאֲשֶׁר תֵּצֵא חֶמְאָה עַל יְדֵי מִיץ חָלָב וְדָם עַל יְדֵי מִיץ הַחוֹטֶם יוֹתֵר מִדַּי, כֵּן יֵצֵא רִיב עַל יְדֵי **מִיץ אַפַּיִם** שֶׁל כַּעַס. **מִיץ** סְחִיטָה פרימדור״א בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּמֶץ טַל וְגוֹ׳״ (שופטים ו:לח). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (ברכות סג:): אִם נִבַּלְתָּ עַצְמְךָ עַל דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה, לִדְרוֹשׁ וְלִשְׁאוֹל סְפֵקוֹתֶיךָ לְרַבְּךָ, וַאֲפִלּוּ נִרְאֵיתָ לוֹ כְּשׁוֹטֶה בְּלֹא לֵב, סוֹפְךָ שֶׁתְּהֵא מְנֻשָּׂא; וְאִם זַמּוֹתָ – אִם שַׂמְתָּ זְמָם עַל פִּיךָ וְחָסַמְתָּ אוֹתוֹ וְלֹא שָׁאַלְתָּ לוֹ הַכֹּל, סוֹפְךָ כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁאָלְךָ דְּבַר הֲלָכָה, תִּתֵּן יָד לַפֶּה וְתֵאָלֵם, שֶׁלֹּא תֵדַע לַעֲנוֹת כְּלוּם בּוֹ. כַּאֲשֶׁר מִיץ חָלָב יוֹצִיא חֶמְאָה, כֵּן מִיץ אַפַּיִם. שֶׁרַבְּךָ כּוֹעֵס בְּךָ שֶׁלֹּא הֲבִינוֹתָ מַהֵר, וְאַתָּה מִתְנַבֵּל עָלֶיהָ – סוֹף יוֹצִיא מִפִּיךָ אַחַר זְמַן הֲלָכוֹת רַבּוֹת וְהוֹרָאוֹת:


**For pressing milk** For, just as butter will come out by pressing milk and blood by pressing the nose too much, so will strife come out of pressing the nostrils of anger. **pressing** Heb. מיץ, pressing, preindre in Old French, as in (Jud. 6:38): “and wrung (וימץ) dew, etc.” And our Sages explained (Ber. 63b): If you were put to shame because of the words of Torah, by seeking and asking your doubts of your mentor, even if you appear to him as a fool without intelligence, you will ultimately be exalted; ואם זמות, but if you placed a muzzle on your mouth and muzzled it, and you did not ask him anything, your end will be that when they ask you a matter of halachah, you will put your hand to your mouth and you will be dumb, for you will not know to reply anything about it. Just as pressing milk gives out butter, so will pressing anger that your mentor is wroth with you for not understanding readily and you were put to shame because of it, eventually bring out of your mouth after a time many (רבות) halachoth and instructions.



## Chapter 31


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:1)


**דִּבְרֵי לְמוּאֵל מֶלֶךְ.** דִּבְרֵי שְׁלֹמֹה הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁאָמַר לְמוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, כְּלוֹמַר: בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁהֶעֱוָה כְּנֶגֶד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. **לְמוּאֵל** לָאֵל, כְּמוֹ ״לְמוֹ פִי״ (איוב מ:ד). דְּבָרִים לִשְׁמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁאָמַר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**The words of Lemuel the king** The words of King Solomon, that he said for the Holy One, blessed be He, because he sinned against the Holy One, blessed be He. **Lemuel** for God, like (Job 40:4): “to (למו) my mouth.” The words that the king said for the sake of the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:2)


**מַשָּׂא אֲשֶׁר יִסְּרַתּוּ אִמּוֹ.** כְּשֶׁהִתְחַתֵּן עִם בַּת פַּרְעֹה בְּיוֹם חֲנֻכַּת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְהִכְנִיסָה לוֹ כַּמָּה מִינֵי כְּלֵי זֶמֶר, וְנֵעוֹר כָּל הַלַּיְלָה, וְיָשַׁן לְמָחֳרָת עַד אַרְבַּע שָׁעוֹת; כִּדְאִיתָא בִּפְסִיקְתָּא (ויקרא רבה יב, ה), וְהָיוּ מַפְתְּחוֹת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ תַּחַת מְרַאֲשׁוֹתָיו. וְעַל אוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה שָׁנִינוּ (עדיות ו, א): עַל תָּמִיד שֶׁל שַׁחַר שֶׁקָּרַב בְּאַרְבַּע שָׁעוֹת, וְנִכְנְסָה אִמּוֹ וְהוֹכִיחַתּוּ עַל הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה:


**a prophecy that his mother chastised him** When he married the daughter of Pharaoh on the day of the dedication of the Temple, she brought in for him many kinds of musical instruments, and he was awake all night and slept on the next day until four hours [after dawn], as is related in Pesikta (unknown, but found in Mid. Mishle and in Num. Rabbah 10:8), and the keys of the Temple were under his head. Regarding that time, we learned (Eduyoth 6:1): “Concerning the daily morning burnt- offering, that it was offered up at [the conclusion of] four hours. Then his mother entered and chastised him with all this prophecy.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:1:3)


**מַשָּׂא אֲשֶׁר יִסְּרַתּוּ אִמּוֹ.** מַשָּׂא שֶׁל מָשָׁל שֶׁיִּסְּרַתּוּ אִמּוֹ (סנהדרין ע:):


**a prophecy that his mother chastised him** An allegorical prophecy with which his mother chastised him.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:1)


**מַה בְּרִי.** מֶה זֹּאת עָשִׂיתָ, וְהִגַּדְתָּ שֶׁאַתָּה בְּנִי וְלֹא בֶן אָבִיךָ? הַכֹּל יוֹדְעִין שֶׁאָבִיךָ צַדִּיק גָּמוּר הָיָה, וְאִם אַתָּה רָשָׁע, יֹאמְרוּ: אִמּוֹ גָּרְמָה לוֹ:


**What, my son** What is this that you have done and told that you are my son, and [you did] not [tell] that you are the son of your father. Everybody knows that your father was completely righteous, and if you are wicked, they will say, “His mother made him that way.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:2)


**בַּר בִּטְנִי.** כָּל נְשֵׁי אָבִיךָ, כֵּיוָן שֶׁמִּתְעַבְּרוֹת אֵינָן שָׁבוֹת לְתַשְׁמִישׁ; וַאֲנִי דָּחַקְתִּי וְנִכְנַסְתִּי כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא לִי בֵּן מְלֻבָּן וּמְזֹרָז, שֶׁהַתַּשְׁמִישׁ יָפֶה כָּל שִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים אַחֲרוֹנִים:


**the son of my womb** All your father’s wives, as soon as they conceived, would not return for marital relations, but I pushed and entered in order to have a son well-formed and of strong vitality, because marital relations are beneficial during the last six months.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:2:3)


**וּמֶה בַּר נְדָרָי.** כָּל נְשֵׁי אָבִיךָ הָיוּ נוֹדְרוֹת שֶׁיְּהֵא לָהֶן בֵּן הָגוּן לַמַּלְכוּת, וַאֲנִי נָדַרְתִּי שֶׁיְּהֵא לִי בֵּן מְלֻבָּן בַּתּוֹרָה:


**and what, the son of my vows?** All your father’s wives would vow that they would have a son fit for the throne, but I vowed that I would have a son bright in Torah.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:3:1)


**אַל תִּתֵּן.** אַל תַּתִּישׁ:


**Do not give** Do not weaken.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:3:2)


**לַנָּשִׁים חֵילֶךָ.** כֹּחֲךָ:


**your strength to women** Heb. חילך, your strength.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:1)


**אַל לַמְּלָכִים לְמוֹאֵל.** אֵין זֶה דָּבָר הָגוּן לַמְּלָכִים, שֶׁהֵם לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא. **לְמוֹאֵל** כְּמוֹ ״לְמוֹ פִי״ (איוב מ:ד):


**It is not for kings, Lemoel** This matter is not fit for kings who are for the Holy One, blessed be He. Lemoel is like (Job 40:4): “to (למו) my mouth.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:2)


**אַל לַמְּלָכִים שְׁתוֹ יָיִן.** אֵין נָאֶה לָהֶם לְהִשְׁתַּכֵּר:


**it is not for kings to drink wine** It is not proper for them to become intoxicated.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:4:3)


**אֵי שֵׁכָר.** כְּמוֹ אֵין שֵׁכָר:


**neither is strong drink** Heb. אי שכר, like אין שכר, there is no strong drink.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:5:1)


**מְחֻקָּק.** הַכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן מַה שֶּׁהוּא בְּגִרְסָא:


**what was made law** What is written in the Torah—and surely the tradition that is committed to memory.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:6:1)


**לְאוֹבֵד.** לְמִי שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לְאִבּוּד, לָרְשָׁעִים (עירובין סה.):


**to the one who is perishing** To him who will ultimately perish—to the wicked.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:6:2)


**לְמָרֵי נָפֶשׁ.** הַמִּצְטַעֲרִין עַל עָנְיָם וְעַל אֶבְלָם (במדבר רבה י:ד):


**to those of bitter soul** Who suffer pain because of their poverty and their mourning. To give them pleasure in this world, so that they receive the reward for the precepts they kept, in this world, [leaving them with nothing in the hereafter.]



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:8:1)


**כָּל בְּנֵי חֲלוֹף.** אֵלּוּ הֵן הַיְתוֹמִים, שֶׁחָלְפָה עֶזְרָתָם וְהָלְכָה לָהּ:


**all whose help has passed** Heb. בני חלוף. These are the orphans, whose help has passed and gone away.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:11:1)


**וְשָׁלָל לֹא יֶחְסָר.** כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא יֶחְסַר טוֹב:


**and he will lack no gain** lit. no plunder, i.e., he will lack no good.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:15:1)


**טֶרֶף.** מָזוֹן:


**food** Heb. טרף.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:15:2)


**וְחֹק.** הוּא מָזוֹן הַקָּצוּב לָהֶם:


**and an allotted share** Heb. וחוק. This too is the food that is allotted to them.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:19:1)


**בַּכִּישׁוֹר.** שֶׁקּוֹרִין וִוירְטוֹי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז, הַמַּכְשִׁיר אֶת הַפֶּלֶךְ לִטְווֹת. **פָלֶךְ** פּוּשִׁי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז:


**onto the distaff** Heb. כישור that is called vertel, verteil, vertay in Provencal, which prepares (מכשיר) the spindle to spin. **the spindle** Heb. פלך, fusele in French.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:1)


**לֹא תִירָא לְבֵיתָהּ.** לִבְנֵי בֵיתָהּ:


**She fears not for her house hold** For those who live in her house.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:2)


**מִשָּׁלֶג.** מִצִּנָּה:


**for snow** For the cold.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:21:3)


**לְבוּשׁ שָׁנִים.** בִּגְדֵי צִבְעוֹנִין:


**are dressed in crimson** Colored clothing.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:22:1)


**מַרְבַּדִּים.** מַצָּעוֹת נָאוֹת לַמִּטָּה, כְּמוֹ ״מַרְבַּדִּים רִבַּדְתִּי״ (משלי ז:טז):


**beautiful bedspreads** Heb. מרבדים, beautiful spreads for the bed, as in (above 7:16) “I have bedecked my couch with covers (מרבדים רבדתי).”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:23:1)


**נוֹדָע בַּשְּׁעָרִים בַּעְלָהּ.** נִכָּר הוּא בֵּין חֲבֵירָיו מִפְּנֵי מַלְבּוּשָׁיו שֶׁהֵם נָאִים:


**Her husband is known in the gates** He is recognizable among his peers because of his garments, which are beautiful.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:24:1)


**לַכְּנַעֲנִי.** תַּגָּר:


**to the trafficker** Heb. כנעני, a merchant.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:25:1)


**לְיוֹם אַחֲרוֹן.** בְּיוֹם מוֹתָהּ נִפְטֶרֶת בְּשֵׁם טוֹב:


**at the last day** On the day of her death, she departs with a good name.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:25:2)


**וַתִּשְׂחַק.** כָּל יָמֶיהָ עַל יוֹם מִיתָתָהּ, שֶׁיְּהֵא נִכְבָּד בְּשֵׁם טוֹב:


**and she laughs** all her life about the day of her death, that it should be honored with a good name.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:27:1)


**צוֹפִיָּה.** הִיא בְּבֵיתָהּ נוֹתֶנֶת לֵב עַל צָרְכֵי בְּנֵי בֵיתָהּ, אֵיךְ יִנְהֲגוּ בֶּאֱמֶת וּצְנִיעוּת:


**She supervises** In her house, she pays attention to all the needs of her household, how they should act with truth and with modesty.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:29:1)


**רַבּוֹת בָּנוֹת.** כָּךְ מְאַשְּׁרִין אוֹתָהּ בַּעְלָהּ וּבָנֶיהָ:


**“Many women, etc.”** In this way, her husband and children call her fortunate.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:30:1)


**שֶׁקֶר הַחֵן.** אֵשֶׁת הַחֵן וְיוֹפִי אֵין מְהַלְּלִין אוֹתָהּ, אֶלָּא הַכֹּל הֶבֶל וְשֶׁקֶר; אֲבָל **אִשָּׁה יִרְאַת ה׳ הִיא** לְבַד **תִתְהַלָּל**:


**Charm is false** No one praises a woman of charm or beauty; everything is futility and false, but a God-fearing woman alone is praised.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:1)


**מַעֲשֶׂיהָ.** כִּשְׁרוֹן מַעֲשֶׂיהָ מֵעִיד עָלֶיהָ, לְהַלְּלָהּ בְּשַׁעַר הָעִיר כָּל יוֹצֵא וָבָא. זוֹ הַמְּלִיצָה שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי. אֲבָל לְפִי הַמָּשָׁל עַל הַתּוֹרָה וְלוֹמְדֶיהָ הַפָּרָשָׁה מִתְבָּאֶרֶת:


**her deeds** The skill of her deeds testifies for her so that all passersby praise her in the gate of the city. This is the figure, as I explained it, but according to the allegory, the chapter is explained as referring to the Torah and those who study it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:2)


(י) **אֵשֶׁת חַיִל.** הִיא הַתּוֹרָה:


**[10] a woman of valor** This is the Torah.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:3)


**מִי יִמְצָא.** אַשְׁרֵי הַזּוֹכֶה לִמְצֹא אוֹתָהּ:


**who can find** Fortunate is he who merits to find it.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:4)


**מִפְּנִינִים.** מַרְגָּלִיּוֹת:


**than pearls** Heb. מפנינים, [also referred to as] מרגליות.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:5)


(יא) **וְשָׁלָל לֹא יֶחְסָר.** אוֹכֵל פֵּרוֹת בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**[11] and he lacks no gain** He eats the fruit in this world and in the next


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:6)


(יג) **דָּרְשָׁה צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים.** לְפִי שֶׁהִמְשִׁילָהּ לְאִשָּׁה, דִּבֵּר לְפִי הַמְּלִיצָה כְּצָרְכֵי מְלֶאכֶת הַנָּשִׁים, וְהַמָּשָׁל כָּךְ הוּא: דּוֹרֶשֶׁת הַתּוֹרָה מִקְרָא, מִשְׁנָה, מִדְרָשׁ, וּמְחַזֶּרֶת אַחֲרֵיהֶם, שֶׁהֵם צָרְכֵי הַתַּלְמִידִים:


**[13] She seeks wool and flax** Since it [the Torah] is compared to a woman, Scripture speaks according to the figure concerning the requirements for the work of women, and the allegory is as follows: The Torah seeks Scripture, Mishnah and Midrash, and searches for them, as they are the requirements of the students.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:7)


(יד) **הָיְתָה כָּאֳנִיּוֹת סוֹחֵר.** מְבִיאָה הִיא לְלוֹמְדֶיהָ בְּרָכָה וּמָזוֹן:


**[14] She is like the merchant ships** The Torah brings those who study it blessing and sustenance.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:8)


(טו) **וַתָּקָם בְּעוֹד לַיְלָה.** מַשְׁכִּימִים בָּאַשְׁמוֹרֶת (מסכת ברכות ג:):


**[15] She rises when it is still night** They rise early in the [morning] watch.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:9)


**וְתִתֵּן טֶרֶף לְבֵיתָהּ.** שׁוֹנֶה הָרַב לַתַּלְמִידִים הַחֹק הַקָּצוּב לָהֶם:


**she gives food to her household** The teacher teaches the pupils the lesson allotted to them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:10)


(טז) **זָמְמָה שָׂדֶה.** זָמְמָה בְּזֶמֶם וּבְרֶסֶן אֶת ״עֵשָׂו אִישׁ שָׂדֶה״ (בראשית כה:כז):


**[16] She contemplates a field** Heb. זממה. The Torah muzzles Esau, the “man of the field,” with a muzzle and a bridle.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:11)


**וַתִּקָּחֵהוּ.** מִן הָעוֹלָם, לְהַאֲבִידוֹ:


**and purchases it** lit. and takes him from the world to destroy him;


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:12)


**מִפְּרִי מַעֲשֶׂיהָ נָטְעָה כֶרֶם.** יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְקַיְּמָם לְחַיֵּי הָעוֹלָם הַבָּא:


**from the fruit of her deeds she plants a vineyard** Israel, to keep them alive for the fire of the world to come.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:13)


(יח) **טָעֲמָה.** דְּבוּרָהּ:


**[18] She advised** Its speech.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:14)


**לֹא יִכְבֶּה בַלַּיְלָה נֵרָהּ.** בְּלֵיל כְּתִיב, חָסֵר ה׳: בְּלֵיל שִׁמּוּרִים שֶׁנִּגְּפוּ הַמִּצְרִיִּים (שמות יב:מב) הֵאִירָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְהֵגִינָה עֲלֵיהֶם:


**her lamp does not go out at night** It is written בליל, with the “hey” missing; on the night of watching (ליל שמורים), when the Egyptians were plagued, it shone for Israel and protected them.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:15)


(יט) **בַּכִּישׁוֹר.** בְּכִשְׁרוֹן מַעֲשֶׂיהָ:


**[19] onto the distaff** Heb. בכישור, through the skill of its deeds.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:16)


**תָּמְכוּ פָלֶךְ.** ״מַשְׁעֵן וּמַשְׁעֵנָה״ (ישעיה ג:א), כְּמוֹ ״מַחֲזִיק בַּפֶּלֶךְ״ (שמואל ב ג:כט):


**support the spindle** Heb. פלך, a support, as in (II Sam. 3:29): “one who leans on a staff (מחזיק בפלך).”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:17)


(כ) **כַּפָּהּ פָּרְשָׂה.** לְמִי שֶׁמֵּשִׂים עַצְמוֹ עָלֶיהָ כְּעָנִי, בּוֹ הִיא מִתְקַיֶּמֶת (אבות ו:ד):


**[20]She spreads out her hand** Whoever makes himself like a poor man on its account—in him it [the Torah] endures.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:18)


(כא) **לֹא תִירָא לְבֵיתָהּ מִשָּׁלֶג.** שֶׁדָּנִין בּוֹ הָרְשָׁעִים מֵאֵשׁ לְשֶׁלֶג (גֵּיהִנֹּם):


**[21] She fears not for her household for snow** with which they judge the wicked from fire to snow.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:19)


**לָבֻשׁ שָׁנִים.** בְּרִית דַּם מִילָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: מְלֻבָּשִׁים בְּכֶפֶל ״נָתֹן תִּתֵּן״ (דברים טו:י), ״פָּתֹחַ תִּפְתַּח״ (דברים טו:ח), ״הַעֲנֵיק תַּעֲנִיק״ (דברים טו:יד). כָּל אֵלּוּ מַצִּילִין אוֹתָן מִשֶּׁלֶג גֵּיהִנֹּם. כָּךְ הוּא נִדְרָשׁ בַּתַּנְחוּמָה (יל״ש משלי תתקסד):


**dressed in crimson** The covenant of the blood of circumcision. Another explanation: They are dressed with the commandments expressed in double language. (Deut. 15:10): “You shall surely give (נתון תתן),” (ibid. verse 8): “you shall surely open (פתוח תפתח),” (ibid. verse 14): “you shall surely furnish him liberally (הענק תעניק).” All these save them from the snow of Gehinnom. So it is expounded in Tanhuma.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:20)


(כד) **סָדִין עָשְׂתָה.** לְבוּשׁ תִּפְאֶרֶת נוֹתֶנֶת לַחֲכָמִים:


**[24] She makes a cloak** It grants a glorious [raiment] to the Sages.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:21)


**וַחֲגוֹר נָתְנָה לַכְּנַעֲנִי.** (ס״א:) (לְמִי) לְפִי שֶׁהוּא חָגוּר בִּסְחוֹרָתָהּ, נוֹתְנָה אֵזוֹר לְמָתְנֵיהֶם:


**and she gives a belt to the trafficker** To the one who is girded with its merchandise, she gives a belt for their loins.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:22)


(כה) **לְיוֹם אַחֲרוֹן.** אֵין לָהֶם לְהִתְעַצֵּב מִמִּדַּת הַדִּין כִּי יִנָּצְלוּ מִמֶּנּוּ, וְכָל יְמֵי חַיֵּיהֶם יִשְׂחֲקוּ לְיוֹם הַדִּין. מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לִדְאֹג נוֹפֵל בַּדָּבָר לְשׁוֹן צְחוֹק, כְּמוֹ ״וְיִשְׂחַק לְרַעַשׁ כִּידוֹן״ (איוב מא:כא):


**[25] to the last day** They need not grieve over the day of judgment because they will be saved from it, and all the days of their life they will rejoice at the day of judgment. An expression of צחוק, laughter, applies to anyone who need not be concerned, as in (Job 41:21): “and he rejoices at the din of a spear.”


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:23)


(כז) **צוֹפִיָּה הֲלִיכוֹת בֵּיתָהּ.** הַתּוֹרָה מְלַמֶּדֶתָם דֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹב, לִפְרוֹשׁ מֵעֲבֵירָה:


**[27] She supervises the ways of her household** The Torah teaches them the good way, to separate from sin.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:24)


(כח) **קָמוּ בָנֶיהָ.** הַתַּלְמִידִים:


**[28] Her children** The pupils.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:25)


**בַּעְלָהּ.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**her husband** The Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:26)


(ל) **שֶׁקֶר הַחֵן.** שָׁב עַל כָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי גִּלּוּלִים:


**[30] Charm is false** [The charm] of the kings of the nations.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:27)


**וְהֶבֶל.** גְּדוּלָּתָן וְיָפְיָן:


**and...is futile** Their greatness and their beauty.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:28)


**תְּנוּ לָהּ.** לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא:


**[31] Give her** in the future.


###### Rashi on Proverbs 31:31:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Proverbs/r/31:31:29)


**מִפְּרִי יָדֶיהָ.** תִּפְאֶרֶת וּגְדוּלָּה, עֹז, פְּאֵר וּמֶמְשָׁלָה:


**of the fruit of her hands** Glory and greatness, strength, beauty and ruling power.
